Abstract: Direct Multisearch (DMS) is a class of direct-search algorithms designed for multiobjective derivative-free optimization. Its framework consists of an optional search step and a poll step, the latter ensuring the corresponding theoretical convergence properties. Recently, a search strategy based on the minimization of quadratic polynomial models, constructed from previously evaluated points, was proposed to improve the numerical efficiency of the method. While the construction of these surrogate models has been investigated, considerably less attention has been devoted to how they should be jointly minimized, with min-max scalarization typically being adopted.
  This work investigates how different model-minimization strategies influence the performance of DMS. To this end, alternative strategies for exploring the quadratic polynomial models within the search step of DMS are proposed and numerically assessed, including one based on the recently proposed Improved Front Steepest Descent algorithm.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献缺乏将初值向量理论应用于LEO环境中各类摄动（如地球非球形引力J2和大气阻力Drag）下的最优脉冲机动研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于最优控制理论和初值向量理论的回顾，将推力控制扩展为测度并推广至保守/非保守摄动模型；提出笛卡尔坐标系下的脉冲多重打靶优化方案，采用分段方法求解；分别在Keplerian、J2和J2+Drag模型下求解已知机动场景并进行轨迹对比。

Result: 针对J2（保守摄动）和J2+Drag（非保守摄动）两种模型，验证了多种初值向量计算方法的有效性，并证明初值向量是降低轨道机动成本的有效工具。

Conclusion: 初值向量理论可成功应用于LEO摄动环境下的最优脉冲机动，为降低成本提供了实用分析工具。

Abstract: This work sets out to apply primer vector theory to optimal impulse maneuvers in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) and adapt it to the types of perturbations encountered in this environment, which is not readily available in the literature. A review of the theory of optimal control and orbital maneuvering is made and in particular, primer vector theory is laid out in detail based on the extension of the thrust control to a measure, including its generalization to conservative and non-conservative perturbation models. An impulsive multiple shooting optimization scheme in Cartesian coordinates is presented, through a piecewise approach to the problem. Then, some maneuver scenarios with known solutions under the Keplerian model are solved under perturbed orbital dynamics with the help of the primer vector, and the resulting trajectories are compared. The perturbation models include a J2 model, representing the class of conservative perturbations by modeling Earth's nonspherical gravity field, and a J2+Drag model, representing the class of non-conservative perturbations by including the effects of atmospheric drag in LEO\@. For each model, some valid methods of primer vector calculation have been tried and validated between each other, and the primer vector is proven to be a useful tool in reducing the cost of orbital maneuvers.

</details>

Result: 在分析型和基于仿真的约束测试问题集上评估性能，并与包括MADS框架下的PB方法在内的最先进黑箱优化求解器进行比较。

Abstract: This work introduces ADS-PB, an extension of the Adaptive Direct Search (ADS) framework for solving constrained blackbox optimization problems. With ADS, iterates progress without relying on mesh structures or sufficient decrease conditions on the objective function value. Unlike the extreme barrier approach used in ADS, where only unrelaxable constraints are considered, the proposed method also handles quantifiable and relaxable constraints using a Progressive Barrier (PB) mechanism that exploits both constraint and objective function values. A convergence analysis of the proposed framework under mild assumptions is presented. The performance of the proposed method is assessed using sets of analytical and simulation-based constrained test problems and is compared with state-of-the-art blackbox optimization solvers, including the PB approach within the Mesh Adaptive Direct Search (MADS) framework.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储能运营商在日前市场中预留容量时面临价格 spikes、可再生能源预测误差、调用惩罚等不确定性，导致收益分布呈现非对称、重尾特征而非高斯分布，现有基于正态假设的模型无法准确刻画此类风险。

Method: 构建三目标存储-报童模型：①最大化期望利润；②最小化CVaR尾部损失；③最小化候选非正态分布上的最大遗憾值。推导临界分位数形式的期望利润最优解，并提出有限场景凸标量化方法联合求解三个目标。

Result: 在均值和方差匹配的log-NMVM实验中，基于正态假设的策略会过量预留容量达1.337单位；在lognormal策略对比中，CVaR和最小最大遗憾策略有效降低尾部损失和候选集遗憾值，但以期望利润下降为代价。

Conclusion: 储能容量预留决策必须考虑收益分布的非对称性和重尾特征，三目标框架提供了兼顾利润与风险的多维度权衡工具，为运营商在非正态不确定环境下的鲁棒决策提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Energy storage operators often reserve usable capacity before uncertain market opportunities are realized, for example when a day-ahead operator commits capacity for an evening peak-spread or ancillary-service window. Price spikes, renewable forecast errors, activation calls and imbalance penalties can make the decision-relevant opportunity distribution asymmetric and heavy-tailed rather than Gaussian. This paper develops a tri-objective storage-newsvendor framework for this capacity-reservation decision. The model jointly maximizes expected profit, minimizes CVaR tail loss and minimizes maximum regret over candidate non-normal distributions. We derive a critical-fractile expected-profit solution and a finite-scenario convex scalarization that solves the three objectives together. In stylized log-NMVM experiments with matched mean and variance, a moment-matched normal policy over-reserves capacity by up to 1.337 units. In a lognormal policy comparison, the CVaR and maximum-regret policies reduce tail loss and candidate-set regret at the cost of lower expected profit.

### [100] [Van Vleck spectra of high-order Heun operators:\ finite-band universality and exterior asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02700)
*Boris Shapiro*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高阶Heun算子的Van Vleck多项式和谱多项式的性质，证明了有限带行列式表示和普适性定理：归一化后谱多项式零点的固定幂和极限仅依赖于算子的首项系数Q_k，而与低阶系数无关。还证明了可定性准则、外域Cauchy变换的全纯性以及Picard-Fuchs方程，并提出了复情形的母体猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究经典Fuchs指数为1的Heun算子的高阶类似物，揭示高阶Heun算子谱理论的普适性行为，特别是谱多项式根计数测度的弱极限仅由算子最高阶项系数决定这一深刻现象。

Method: 利用有限带行列式表示、Cauchy变换、对数势、WKB周期和Picard-Fuchs方程等工具，结合Van Vleck根的局部化定理，从解析和代数两方面展开分析。

Result: 1. 建立了Sp_n的有限带行列式表示和普适性定理；2. 证明了谱根渐近有界时的可定性准则；3. 当Q_k的根共线时，Sp_n的根计数测度弱收敛到该线段上的概率测度，且与低阶系数无关；4. 证明了外域Cauchy变换的全纯性，导出了k=3情形的三阶Picard-Fuchs方程。

Conclusion: 高阶Heun算子的谱理论具有显著的普适性，谱多项式的渐近行为主要由最高阶项控制，低阶项仅产生高阶修正。论文以精确的母体猜想作为结尾，为复情形指明了未来研究方向。

Abstract: We study high-order analogues of the classical Heun operator of Fuchs index one, \[
  \dq=\sum_{i=1}^k Q_i(z)\frac{d^i}{dz^i},
  \qquad °Q_i\le i+1,
  \qquad °Q_k=k+1. \] For a fixed degree $n$ we consider the linear Van Vleck polynomials $V$ for which $\dq+V$ has a polynomial solution of degree $n$, and we form the spectral polynomial $Sp_n$ whose zeros are the zeros of these Van Vleck polynomials. The main result is a finite-band determinant representation and the resulting universality theorem: after normalization, all fixed power sums of the zeros of $Sp_n$ have limits given by explicit constant-term formulae depending only on the leading coefficient $Q_k$. The lower coefficients of $\dq$ enter only lower order correction terms. Combining this with the localization theorem for Van Vleck roots, we strengthen the usual germ-at-infinity conclusion to locally uniform convergence of the normalized Cauchy transforms and logarithmic potentials on the whole exterior of the convex hull of the zeros of $Q_k$.
  We also prove a determinacy criterion: if the spectral roots are asymptotically confined to a compact set with empty interior and connected complement, then the finite-band moments determine the actual weak limit. In particular, when the zeros of $Q_k$ are collinear the root-counting measures of $Sp_n$ converge weakly to a probability measure supported on the corresponding segment; this limit is independent of all lower coefficients of $\dq$. Finally, we prove holonomicity of the exterior Cauchy transform and derive Picard--Fuchs equations for the WKB periods, with an explicit third-order equation in the first non-classical case $k=3$. The paper ends with a precise mother-body conjecture for the genuinely complex case, clearly separated from the unconditional results.

</details>


### [101] [Transition-Set Morphometry for Inter-Scale Contacts in Digital Sandstones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02737)
*O. M. Kiselev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种过渡集形态计量方法，用于量化数字砂岩中的尺度间接触，通过定义孔隙体积、孔喉子系统和裂缝孔隙度等几何子系统及其过渡集，构建了新的结构描述符，显著提升了渗透率预测的精度，并在裂缝数据集上验证了方法的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字岩石物理中，不同尺度几何子系统（如孔隙、孔喉、裂缝）之间的接触关系缺乏有效的定量描述方法，现有形态计量手段难以捕捉跨尺度的结构信息对宏观物性（如渗透率）的影响。

Method: 将孔隙体积、孔喉子系统和裂缝孔隙度视为不同几何子系统，用过渡集（如 $T_{VL}$, $T_{VF}$, $T_{LF}$）表示它们的有限邻域接触；计算过渡集的密度、有限尺度维数和加权接触测度；提出自适应度量 $\widehat C_{VL}^{(d+1)}=μ_{VL}(\overline D_{VL}/D_*)^{d_{VL}+1}$。

Result: 在21个数字砂岩样本上，单一过渡维数 $d_{VL}$ 预测增益有限；结合过渡集密度与局部孔隙半径的接触测度更有效；自适应度量 $\widehat C_{VL}^{(d+1)}$ 使留一法 $R^2$ 从0.752提升至0.881（合并样本），在DRP-317子集中从0.287提升至0.807；裂缝数据集验证了方法的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 过渡集是可重复的尺度间接触形态计量描述符，能够有效表征孔隙-孔喉-裂缝系统的跨尺度结构信息，并为渗透率预测提供有物理意义的独立预测变量。

Abstract: This paper introduces a transition-set morphometry for quantifying inter-scale contacts in digital sandstones. Pore volume, a medial pore-throat subsystem, and fracture porosity are treated as distinct geometric subsystems. Their finite-neighborhood contacts are represented by transition sets such as $T_{VL}$, $T_{VF}$, and, when a residual skeleton is resolved, $T_{LF}$. The method measures the density, finite-scale dimension, and weighted contact measures of these sets. A matrix-mode test was performed on 21 digital sandstone samples from the Imperial College micro-CT collection and Digital Porous Media Portal dataset DRP-317. Permeability, expressed as $\log_{10}K$, served as an external response for assessing how much structural information the descriptors carry. The transition dimension $d_{VL}$ alone produced little independent predictive gain, whereas contact measures combining transition-set density with local pore radius were more informative. The adaptive measure $\widehat C_{VL}^{(d+1)}=μ_{VL}(\overline D_{VL}/D_*)^{d_{VL}+1}$ increased leave-one-out $R^2$ from 0.752 to 0.881 in the combined sample and from 0.287 to 0.807 within DRP-317. Fractured datasets DRP-5, DRP-31, and DRP-285 were then used to test the transfer of the same transition-set construction to fracture-related contacts. The results support transition sets as reproducible morphometric descriptors of inter-scale contact.

</details>


### [102] [A new PBW basis for the alternating central extension of the $q$-Onsager algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02849)
*Haoran Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为$q$-Onsager代数的交错中心扩张$\mathcal A_q$建立了新的PBW基，证明了交错生成元在块序$\mathcal W^-<\mathcal G<\widetilde{\mathcal G}<\mathcal W^+$下也构成PBW基，并验证了Terwilliger的猜想，即乘法诱导出向量空间同构$\mathcal W^-\otimes\mathcal G\otimes\widetilde{\mathcal G}\otimes\mathcal W^+ \longrightarrow \mathcal A_q$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Terwilliger提出的猜想，即$\mathcal A_q$的交错生成元能否在不同块序下构成PBW基，并建立相应的向量空间同构。

Method: 通过分析块序$\mathcal W^-<\mathcal G<\widetilde{\mathcal G}<\mathcal W^+$下交错生成元的代数关系，证明其满足PBW基的线性无关性和生成性，从而建立新的PBW基。

Result: 证明了交错生成元在块序$\mathcal W^-<\mathcal G<\widetilde{\mathcal G}<\mathcal W^+$下构成PBW基，并建立了向量空间同构$\mathcal W^-\otimes\mathcal G\otimes\widetilde{\mathcal G}\otimes\mathcal W^+ \longrightarrow \mathcal A_q$。

Conclusion: 本文证实了Terwilliger的猜想，为$\mathcal A_q$提供了另一种PBW基结构，拓展了对$q$-Onsager代数交错中心扩张的代数结构的理解。

Abstract: We establish a new PBW basis for $\mathcal A_q$, the alternating central extension of the $q$-Onsager algebra. Terwilliger showed that the alternating generators form a PBW basis in the block order $\mathcal G<\mathcal W^-<\mathcal W^+<\widetilde{\mathcal G}$. We prove that they also form a PBW basis in the different block order $\mathcal W^-<\mathcal G<\widetilde{\mathcal G}<\mathcal W^+$. Consequently, multiplication induces a vector-space isomorphism \[ \mathcal W^-\otimes\mathcal G\otimes\widetilde{\mathcal G}\otimes\mathcal W^+ \longrightarrow \mathcal A_q, \] thereby confirming a conjecture of Terwilliger.

</details>


### [103] [The Oracle Theorem for Matrix-Valued Jacobi Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03507)
*Silas L. Carvalho,Douglas C. P. Freitas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了Jacobi算子的矩阵值反射less与Oracle框架，证明了全多重绝对连续谱下ω-极限集的反射less性质及Oracle定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将标量Jacobi算子的反射less与Oracle理论推广到矩阵值情形，处理更一般的多通道量子系统。

Method: 从Siegel上半空间的矩阵值Weyl-Titchmarsh m-函数出发，研究转移矩阵的距离递减作用、矩阵值调和测度及值分布收敛性。

Result: 建立了ω-极限集的反射less性质，并证明了全多重绝对连续谱下矩阵值Jacobi算子的Oracle定理。

Conclusion: 成功将反射less与Oracle框架推广至矩阵值Jacobi算子，为多通道谱分析提供了理论工具。

Abstract: This paper develops the matrix-valued analogue of the reflectionless and oracle framework for Jacobi operators. Starting from matrix-valued Weyl--Titchmarsh $m$-functions on the Siegel upper half-space, we study the distance-decreasing action of transfer matrices, matrix-valued harmonic measures and value distribution convergence. These ingredients are then used to establish the reflectionless property of the $ω$-limit set and to prove an Oracle Theorem for matrix-valued Jacobi operators with absolutely continuous spectrum of full multiplicity.

</details>


### [104] [Anomalous rate of eigenstate thermalisation at singularities of the density of states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03546)
*László Erdős,Joscha Henheik,Volodymyr Riabov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一般相关结构的大N×N平均场随机矩阵的本征态热化假说（ETH）/量子唯一遍历性（QUE），确定了微正则系综，并建立了本征向量重叠的最优波动尺度。研究否定了量子混沌物理学中基于半经典理论的Feingold-Peres预测，揭示了依赖于多预解局部律的真正机制。意外发现，在规则谱边缘，波动率仍保持N^{-1}的体相速率，而非如预期般增大，这源于矩阵Dyson方程解中的精细抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证平均场随机矩阵的ETH/QUE，并澄清量子混沌领域中关于本征向量波动尺度的长期争议——特别是检验和修正Feingold-Peres基于半经典理论的预测。

Method: 利用多预解局部律（multi-resolvent local laws）分析一般相关结构的平均场随机矩阵，通过求解矩阵Dyson方程（Matrix Dyson Equation）研究本征向量重叠的波动尺度，并在体相、规则谱边缘和尖点区域分别建立最优波动界限。

Result: 1) 否定了Feingold-Peres预测；2) 在尖点区域波动尺度为N^{-1/2}；3) 在规则谱边缘意外发现波动率仍为N^{-1}（与体相同），而非增大；4) 本征基整体波动尺度与Haar酉矩阵相同。

Conclusion: 一般相关结构的平均场随机矩阵满足ETH/QUE，且本征向量波动存在精细的尺度结构：尖点处增大但规则谱边缘因Dyson方程解中的抵消效应而保持与体相一致的N^{-1}速率。

Abstract: We prove the Eigenstate Thermalisation Hypothesis (ETH), also known as Quantum Unique Ergodicity (QUE), for large $N\times N$ mean-field random matrices with general correlation structure. We identify the microcanonical ensemble and establish the optimal fluctuation scale of eigenvector overlaps around it. Our results invalidate the prediction by Feingold and Peres [Phys. Rev. A 34, 591 (1986)] in the physics literature of quantum chaos, based upon popular semiclassical theory, and uncover the genuine mechanism which relies on multi-resolvent local laws. Although fluctuations are expected to increase as the density of states vanishes, and indeed scale as $N^{-1/2}$ in the special cusp regime, rather than $N^{-1}$ in the bulk, we find, unexpectedly, that the same $N^{-1}$ rate persists at regular spectral edges. Hence, generically, in the absence of cusps, the entire eigenbasis fluctuates on the same scale as a Haar unitary. This anomaly stems from delicate cancellations in the solution of the underlying matrix Dyson equation, which form the core of our analysis.

</details>


### [105] [The Dirichlet Laplacian with a point interaction on unbounded Lipschitz domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04349)
*Diego Noja,Francesco Raso Stoia*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了二维和三维无界区域上Dirichlet Laplacian的一点相互作用（点相互作用），重点分析了外部区域和特殊Lipschitz区域。通过Kreĭn公式给出了自伴扩展的显式表达式，完整刻画了负谱结构，建立了Weyl函数、临界耦合和负特征值的区域单调性，导出了均匀C^{1,1}几何中临界耦合的近边界渐近估计，证明了非正谱在相互作用中心靠近Dirichlet边界时消失，并在外部区域和特殊Lipschitz区域中证明了极限吸收原理和纯绝对连续正谱。还深入分析了阈值现象，包括零能Green函数远场行为对临界耦合的控制、外部区域的阈值共振、半空间的阈值特征值、半平面的p波共振以及平面楔形中的孔径依赖阈值态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对于无界区域上Dirichlet Laplacian的一点相互作用缺乏系统研究，特别是外部区域和特殊Lipschitz区域中这类奇异摄动的谱性质尚未被完整刻画。

Method: 采用自伴扩展理论，通过Kreĭn公式显式表示预解式；利用Weyl函数建立标量方程刻画负谱；运用区域单调性分析和渐近估计技术。

Result: 建立了Weyl函数、临界耦合和负特征值的区域单调性；导出了临界耦合在均匀C^{1,1}边界附近的渐近行为；证明了外部区域和特殊Lipschitz区域中极限吸收原理和纯绝对连续正谱；揭示了阈值现象与区域几何的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 本文是该领域首个系统性工作，为无界区域中Dirichlet Laplacian点相互作用的谱理论提供了完整框架。

Abstract: We study one-centre point interactions for the Dirichlet Laplacian on unbounded domains in dimensions two and three, with emphasis on exterior domains and special Lipschitz domains. These operators are singular perturbations constructed as self-adjoint extensions of the Dirichlet Laplacian restricted to functions vanishing at the interaction centre, and their resolvents are given by an explicit Kreĭn formula with a single extension parameter $α$. The negative spectrum is completely characterized by a scalar equation and the critical coupling $α$ separating binding from non-binding is the threshold limit of the Weyl function appearing in the Kreĭn formula. We establish domain monotonicity of the Weyl function, of the critical coupling, and of the unique negative eigenvalue when existing, and we derive sharp near-boundary asymptotics of the critical coupling in uniformly $C^{1,1}$ geometries. These estimates imply that, for every fixed coupling, nonpositive spectrum disappears when the interaction centre approaches the Dirichlet boundary. We also prove limiting absorption principles and purely absolutely continuous positive spectrum for a point-interaction in exterior domains case and in classes of special Lipschitz domains. We also analyze in depth several threshold phenomena. We show that the critical coupling is governed by the far-field behavior of the zero-energy Green function: exterior domains give threshold resonances, domains contained in a three-dimensional half-space give threshold eigenvalues, the half-plane gives a $p$-wave resonance, and planar wedges exhibit types of threshold states that are aperture-dependent. Finally, low-energy resolvent expansions are computed in the model cases and persistence or disappearance of eigenvalues at threshold are studied. The present paper seems to be the first systematic work on the subject.

</details>


### [106] [Hyperbolic Completion of Newton's Off-Center Orbit Problem: $SO(2,1)$ Symmetry, Inversion Duality, and Magnetic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04521)
*Dipesh Bhandari*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了奇异势V(r) = -α/(R² - r²)²的双曲离心轨道问题。零能量下，Jacobi度量在r=R两侧具有恒定负曲率，内部为Poincaré盘度量的常数倍，外部通过圆反演等距映射到带孔圆盘。所有零能轨道被分类：非径向轨道是与r=R正交的欧几里得圆弧，径向轨道为过原点的直线，力心位于所有非径向支撑圆之外。Runge-Lenz型矩映射闭合为so(2,1)，其Casimir为双曲测地线Hamilton量。反演的余切提升保持对称生成元，并将零能流映射到自身（正时间重参化下）。奇异圆在有限牛顿时间内可达，但位于无穷Jacobi距离处。量子力学上区分了Stäckel耦合变换与真正酉等价，后者在欧几里得表象中是散度型算子而非朴素平坦Schrödinger算子。双曲连续谱底部映射到逆方边界模型的Hardy/振荡阈值。对称保持的径向磁场变为双曲平面上的恒定内禀磁场，其移位Casimir将轨迹分类为闭合磁圆、horocycles或开放超循环，零场测地线为极限情形，并在Q²=8mαR²处发生转变。数值积分证实了轨道方程和守恒量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决奇异势V(r) = -α/(R² - r²)²中的双曲离心轨道问题，该势在r=R处具有奇异性，导致经典和量子力学中的非平凡几何结构。

Method: 利用Jacobi度量几何方法，将零能量动力学映射到双曲几何（Poincaré盘），运用圆反演等距变换、Runge-Lenz型矩映射、so(2,1) Lie代数及Casimir不变量，结合量子力学中的Stäckel变换和酉等价分析，以及数值积分验证。

Result: 完成了零能轨道的完整分类；证明了反演对称性保持零能流；揭示了奇异圆的有限牛顿时间/无穷Jacobi距离特性；建立了量子力学中Stäckel耦合变换与真酉等价的区分；确定了磁场下轨迹的完整分类（闭磁圆、horocycles、超循环）及临界转变点Q²=8mαR²。

Conclusion: 该奇异势系统展现出丰富的双曲几何结构，经典和量子行为均可通过双曲几何框架统一理解，磁场引入导致轨迹拓扑分类的相变。

Abstract: We resolve the hyperbolic off-center-orbit problem for the singular potential \[ V(r)=-\fracα{(R^2-r^2)^2},\qquad α>0. \] At zero energy, the Jacobi metric has constant negative curvature on both components separated by $r=R$. The interior Jacobi metric is a constant multiple of the Poincaré disk metric, while circular inversion maps the exterior isometrically to the punctured disk. We classify all zero-energy trajectories: nonradial orbits are arcs of Euclidean circles orthogonal to $r=R$, radial trajectories lie on lines through the origin, and the force center lies outside every nonradial supporting circle. An explicit Runge--Lenz-type moment map closes into $\mathfrak{so}(2,1)$, whose Casimir is the hyperbolic geodesic Hamiltonian. The canonical cotangent lift of inversion preserves the symmetry generators and maps the zero-energy flow to itself up to positive time reparametrization.
  The singular circle is reached in finite Newtonian time but lies at infinite Jacobi distance. Quantum mechanically, we distinguish the Stäckel coupling transform from a genuine unitary equivalence, whose Euclidean representative is a divergence-form operator rather than the naive flat Schrödinger operator. The bottom of the hyperbolic continuum maps to the Hardy/oscillation threshold of the inverse-square boundary model. Finally, the symmetry-preserving radial magnetic field becomes a constant intrinsic field on the hyperbolic plane. Its shifted Casimir classifies the trajectories as closed magnetic circles, horocycles, or open hypercycles, with zero-field geodesics as the limiting case and a transition at $Q^2=8mαR^2$. Numerical integrations confirm the orbit equations and conserved quantities.

</details>


### [107] [Long-range interactions and Anderson localisation for one-dimensional high-contrast resonator chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04971)
*Habib Ammari,Silvo Barandun,Jiayu Qiu,Alexander Uhlmann*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维高对比度谐振子无限周期链的电容算子的谱与输运性质。主要结果包括：(1) 精确估计了电容算子的非对角衰减率，发现其为长程且临界性的：当|n-m|→∞时，C(n,m)~1/(|n-m| log²|n-m|)，该衰减率虽然ℓ¹可和，但比二次衰减更慢；(2) 基于上述估计，证明了截断链的有限电容算子强收敛到无限链电容算子，并给出了谱收敛速率的严格估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高对比度谐振子系统中电容算子的长程相互作用性质，特别是其临界衰减行为如何影响Anderson局域化现象和谱收敛性分析。

Method: 通过数学分析推导电容算子非对角元的精确衰减率，并基于该估计建立截断有限系统到无限系统的强收敛性及谱收敛速率估计。

Result: (1) 电容算子非对角元具有临界长程衰减率~1/(|n-m| log²|n-m|)；(2) 证明了有限截断电容算子的强收敛性，并改进了前人关于谱收敛速率的估计。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有临界长程相互作用的经典波系统中的Anderson局域化提供了新的物理洞察，同时为高对比度谐振子系统的谱分析奠定了更坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: Spectral and transport properties of high-contrast resonator systems can be described in the subwavelength regime in terms of the so-called capacitance operator. In this paper, we consider an infinitely periodic chain of high-contrast resonators in three dimensions. The first result is a precise estimate of the off-diagonal decay rate of the capacitance operator $C$. Importantly, we demonstrate that the decay rate is long-range and critical: as $|n-m|\to\infty$, \begin{equation*} C(n,m)\sim \frac{1}{|n-m|\log^2|n-m|}, \end{equation*} which is $\ell^1$ summable but slower than quadratic. This borderline decay of the off-diagonal entries makes the present proof of Anderson localisation with arbitrary disorder, which is observed numerically in this paper, out of reach; we hope that this physical example of classical wave systems with critical long-range interactions provides new insight in the field of Anderson localisation. As the second main result, based on the off-diagonal decay estimate, we prove a strong convergence of the finite capacitance operator, which corresponds to a truncated chain, to the capacitance operator as the size of the truncated chain grows to infinity. Using this strong convergence, we improve the results of [Ammari et al., SIAM J. Math. Anal., 2023 and Bull. London Math Soc., 2025] by presenting a rigorous estimate of the convergence rate of the spectrum.

</details>


### [108] [Collision geometry of relativistic spinning particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05254)
*Simone Calogero*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在狭义相对论框架下研究相对论性自旋粒子的弹性二元碰撞，通过将自旋表示为反对称二阶张量，并在四动量与自旋张量守恒条件下，将碰撞问题简化为圆上二次方程的求解，实现了末态变量的完整分类与重构，发现末态最多有8种可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相对论碰撞理论多集中于无自旋粒子，而对自旋粒子的相对论碰撞缺乏完整的几何理解和解析分类，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用全洛伦兹协变形式，以反对称二阶张量表示自旋，基于四动量守恒和总自旋张量守恒，结合质壳条件与自旋约束，将守恒律系统几何化，转化为圆上的二次方程求解问题。

Result: 碰撞问题可简化为圆上二次方程；末态状态数量有限，一般情形下最多有8个可能的后碰撞状态；给出了所有后碰撞变量的显式重构公式。

Conclusion: 相对论自旋粒子的弹性碰撞问题具有简洁的几何结构，通过圆上二次方程即可完整刻画，且末态解的数量有限（最多8个），为相对论自旋碰撞提供了完整的分类与求解方案。

Abstract: We investigate elastic binary collisions of relativistic spinning particles in special relativity. The spin of each particle is represented by an antisymmetric second order tensor. Assuming the conservation of total four momentum and total spin tensor, together with the mass shell and spin constraints, we formulate the collision problem in a fully Lorentz covariant setting. We show that the relativistic collision problem admits a simple geometric formulation, reducing the original system of conservation laws to the solution of a quadratic equation on a circle. This reduction yields a complete classification of the postcollisional states together with explicit reconstruction formulas for all postcollisional variables from the conserved quantities. In particular, there are generically only finitely many postcollisional states, with the maximal number equal to eight.

</details>


### [109] [Lower bound on the energy-momentum relation of the polaron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05286)
*Steffen Polzer*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文针对极化子类模型，建立了能量-动量关系的下界（依赖于真空重叠和零总动量哈密顿量的谱隙），证明了关联路径测度的重标均方位移收敛到有效质量的倒数，并推导了在大总动量下基态缺失的概率性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究极化子类模型中能量-动量关系、有效质量以及大动量下基态存在性等核心物理问题的严格数学基础。

Method: 通过建立能量-动量下界与真空重叠和谱隙的关系，利用路径测度的重标均方位移分析有效质量，并基于概率性判据判断基态缺失条件。

Result: 给出了能量-动量关系的下界表达式，证明了均方位移收敛到有效质量倒数，并得到了大动量下基态缺失的概率性判据。

Conclusion: 该工作为极化子类模型的基态性质和有效质量行为提供了严格的数学框架，特别是对大动量区域的物理行为给出了新的理解。

Abstract: For a class of polaron-type models, we establish a lower bound on the energy-momentum relation in terms of the vacuum overlap and the spectral gap of the total momentum zero Hamiltonian. We show convergence of the rescaled mean square displacement of the associated path measure to the inverse of the effective mass. We derive a probabilistic criterion for the absence of ground states at large total momentum.

</details>

### [110] [Correlation-Induced Topological Reconstruction in a Periodically Driven Kagome Mott Insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02717)
*Rahul Ghosh,Subhajyoti Pal,Ganesh C Paul*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究Kagome晶格Hubbard模型在周期性驱动下的电子关联与拓扑效应，通过Floquet理论推导有效哈密顿量，发现驱动可重复诱导拓扑相变和平带，并在考虑相互作用时产生非单调的Mott跃迁及拓扑重构的自旋子激发，最终实现无法通过单独驱动或相互作用获得的关联拓扑平带绝缘体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索周期性驱动（圆偏振光）对Kagome晶格中电子关联效应的影响，特别是驱动与相互作用的协同作用能否产生新的拓扑平带物理。

Method: 采用Brillouin-Wigner微扰论推导有效Floquet哈密顿量，并用slave-rotor平均场理论处理电子关联。

Result: 驱动重正化跳跃并产生手性近邻和次近邻项，导致重复拓扑相变和连续可调平带；Mott转变随驱动振幅呈非单调行为，出现重复金属-绝缘体转变；Mott相中自旋子激发具有与非相互作用Floquet能带不同的拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 周期性驱动Kagome系统可作为工程化非平衡关联拓扑平带物理的平台，建议在超冷原子光晶格中实现。

Abstract: We study the interplay between electronic correlations and circularly polarized periodic driving in the Hubbard model on a Kagome lattice. Using Brillouin-Wigner perturbation theory, we derive an effective Floquet Hamiltonian in which the drive renormalizes the bare hopping and generates chiral nearest- and next-nearest-neighbor terms, producing repeated topological transitions and a flat band whose position is continuously tunable across the spectrum. Within slave-rotor mean-field theory, we show that the resulting Mott transition is strongly non-monotonic in the drive amplitude, yielding repeated metal-insulator transitions, and that the spinon excitations inside the Mott phase acquire a band topology distinct from that of the non-interacting Floquet bands. This correlation-driven topological reconstruction produces emergent flat-band spinon insulators inaccessible to either driving or interactions alone. Our results establish periodically driven Kagome systems as a platform for engineering correlated topological flat-band physics out of equilibrium, with proposed realizations in ultracold atomic lattices.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum magnetism of the spin-1 kagome-lattice antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03086)
*Katsuhiro Morita*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模Lanczos对角化和有限温度Lanczos方法研究了自旋-1 kagome晶格海森堡反铁磁体，揭示了零温磁化过程中m=0、1/3、7/9和8/9的磁化平台，其中m=0为三聚体价键晶体态，高场平台为磁振子晶体态（m=8/9为精确局域磁振子晶体态），并分析了有限温度下的比热双峰结构和磁化曲线的热效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自旋-1 kagome晶格海森堡反铁磁体在零温和有限温度下的磁化行为，为候选自旋-1 kagome晶格材料的热力学和高场磁化测量提供基准数据。

Method: 采用大规模Lanczos对角化方法计算零温基态性质，结合有限温度Lanczos方法研究热力学性质；使用高斯核平滑方法构建零温磁化曲线。

Result: 零温磁化过程出现m=0、1/3、7/9和8/9四个平台；m=0为三聚体价键晶体态，m=7/9和8/9为磁振子晶体态（8/9为精确局域磁振子晶体态）；磁化曲线在1/3平台低场侧和7/9、8/9平台高场侧出现跳跃；比热在T/J≈0.1和1.1处呈现双峰结构；有限温度下仅1/3平台保持可见，高场平台被热效应迅速抹除。

Conclusion: 自旋-1 kagome晶格反铁磁体展现了丰富的磁化平台现象，包括价键晶体态和磁振子晶体态，为实验研究提供了理论基准。

Abstract: We investigate the spin-1 kagome-lattice Heisenberg antiferromagnet using large-scale Lanczos diagonalization and the finite-temperature Lanczos method. The zero-temperature magnetization process exhibits plateaus at $m=0$, $1/3$, $7/9$, and $8/9$, where $m$ is the normalized magnetization. The $m=0$ plateau is identified as a trimer valence-bond-crystal state, while the high-field plateaus at $m=7/9$ and $8/9$ are identified as magnon crystals. In particular, the $m=8/9$ plateau corresponds to the exact localized-magnon crystal state. A smoothed zero-temperature magnetization curve constructed using the Gaussian-kernel smoothing method indicates magnetization jumps at the lower-field edge of the $m=1/3$ plateau and at the upper-field edges of the $m=7/9$ and $8/9$ plateaus. At finite temperatures, the specific heat exhibits a double-peak structure with peaks around $T/J\simeq0.1$ and $T/J\simeq1.1$, and the low-temperature peak may be related to trimer valence-bond-crystal ordering. The finite-temperature magnetization curves show that the $m=1/3$ plateau remains visible at low temperatures, whereas the high-field plateaus are rapidly smeared out by thermal effects. These results provide benchmark data for thermodynamic and high-field magnetization measurements in candidate spin-1 kagome-lattice materials.

</details>


### [112] [Winding charge density wave: intertwining of structural chirality and phase topology of electronic order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03102)
*Shun Asano,Youichi Yanase*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一类“缠绕电荷密度波”（winding CDWs），其通过单一有序波矢在螺旋对称手性晶体中展现宏观手性，并扩展至非手性晶体中实现自发对称性破缺的手性相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索手性晶体中电子有序相与晶格几何结构之间的深层联系，揭示电荷密度波如何通过特定手性声子驱动的Peierls失稳获得宏观手性。

Method: 基于螺旋对称手性晶体中手性声子驱动的Peierls失稳理论，利用电子-声子耦合选择定则确定晶体角动量通道，赋予CDW整数方位角相位缠绕；并推广至具有离散旋转对称性的非手性晶体，通过自发对称性破缺稳定缠绕CDW。

Result: 在手性晶体中实现了单一波矢下的宏观手性CDW；在非手性晶体中通过自发对称性破缺实现了从非手性到手性的相变。

Conclusion: 揭示了手性结构的几何与电子有序相相位拓扑之间的基本联系，为理解手性电子有序相提供了新框架。

Abstract: We propose a class of chiral charge density waves (CDWs), dubbed winding CDWs, that exhibit macroscopic chirality despite a single ordering wavevector. In screw-symmetric chiral crystals, chiral phonons drive a Peierls instability that selects a definite crystal angular momentum channel, thereby endowing the CDW with an integer azimuthal phase winding dictated by the selection rule governing electron-phonon coupling. We further extend this framework to achiral crystals with discrete rotational symmetry and demonstrate that spontaneous symmetry breaking stabilizes a winding CDW with either handedness, realizing an achiral-to-chiral phase transition. Our results reveal a fundamental link between the geometry of chiral structures and the phase topology of electronic orders.

</details>


### [113] [What Makes Three-Dimensional Quantum Spin Liquids Possible?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03352)
*Yasir Iqbal,Ronny Thomale*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文论证三维量子自旋液体并非脆弱例外，而是可通过局域约束、相干隧穿和规范缺陷拓扑在三维空间中稳定存在，甚至在某些情况下三维比低维更有利于退禁闭相的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统低维直觉——即量子自旋液体仅存在于低维或弱配位系统中，解释为何三维量子自旋液体可以稳定存在而不被磁有序压制。

Method: 围绕三个关键机制展开分析：(1) 局域约束驱动的库仑相；(2) 弱谐波序-by-无序选择；(3) 自旋-轨道或多极相互作用产生的微观动力学。通过对比不同维度的规范理论行为，论证三维的优势。

Result: 三维紧致规范理论中，原本在低维不稳定或需精细调谐的某些相，在三维参数空间中可在有限区域内稳定存在。三维量子自旋液体可通过约束-隧穿-拓扑的组合机制实现稳定性。

Conclusion: 三维量子自旋液体具有坚实的理论基础，不应被视为例外。实验诊断、候选材料和开放理论问题为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Quantum spin liquids are often introduced through a low-dimensional intuition: weak coordination and strong zero-point motion frustrate conventional magnetic order. This view has shaped much of the field, but it misses another route to quantum disorder. In many frustrated magnets, the more natural starting point is not a fluctuating version of an ordered state, but a locally constrained manifold of low-energy configurations. Within such a manifold, quantum dynamics can generate an emergent gauge theory, with fractionalized excitations and collective modes absent in ordinary magnets. Here we ask why such phases can be stable in three spatial dimensions. We argue that three-dimensional quantum spin liquids need not be regarded as fragile exceptions to the tendency of 3D magnets to order. They can arise when local constraints suppress premature order selection, coherent tunneling processes connect the constrained manifold, and the topology of gauge defects permits a deconfined regime. For compact gauge theories, three dimensions can even reverse the usual dimensional intuition: regimes that are unstable or fine-tuned in lower dimensions may become stable over finite regions of parameter space. We organize the discussion around constraint-driven Coulomb phases, weak harmonic order-by-disorder selection, and the role of spin-orbit or multipolar interactions in generating the required microscopic dynamics. We close with experimental diagnostics, candidate materials, and open theoretical questions for three-dimensional quantum spin liquids

</details>


### [114] [Pressure-Driven Structural Transitions without a Displacive Charge-Density Wave in La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_7$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03363)
*J. Huang,Sitaram Ramakrishnan,P. Rodière,P. Toulemonde,Z. Rahmany,V. Balédent,B. Vignolle,Sourav Marik,P. Fertey,P. Foury-Leylekian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了双层镍氧化物La₂SmNi₂O₇在压力和温度下的结构性质，发现了与以往不同的单斜超结构，在低温下未检测到电荷密度波有序，并观察到压力诱导的结构相变序列（单斜→正交→四方），在超导穹顶内进行了结构精修以理解超导电性的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双层镍氧化物La₂SmNi₂O₇在压力和温度下的结构性质，特别是其超导相的结构特征，以理解超导电性的起源。

Method: 通过X射线衍射实验，在不同压力和温度条件下对La₂SmNi₂O₇进行结构表征，分析其晶体结构随压力和温度的变化。

Result: 发现该材料在常压下为单斜超结构；低温下未观察到电荷密度波有序；压力诱导结构相变：单斜→正交（15 GPa）→四方（21 GPa）；在超导穹顶内获得了可用于结构精修的高质量衍射数据。

Conclusion: La₂SmNi₂O₇的结构性质与先前报道不同，压力诱导的结构相变序列与其超导性相关，高质量结构数据为理论模型研究超导电性提供了实验基础。

Abstract: We investigated the structural properties of bilayer nickelate La$_2$SmNi$_2$O$_7$ as a function of pressure and temperature. At ambient conditions, we show that the material crystallizes as a monoclinic superstructure distinct from the one previously reported and close to the pseudo-orthorhombic structure of pristine La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$. No signatures of satellite reflections associated with charge density wave (CDW) ordering are detected at low temperature. Upon compression, a sequence of pressure-induced structural transitions from monoclinic to orthorhombic 15 GPa and then tetragonal 21 GPa symmetry is observed. Within the superconducting dome, the quality of the X-ray diffraction data enables structural refinements enabling theoretical models to understand the emergence of superconductivity.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum Destabilization of Skyrmions in Centrosymmetric Frustrated Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03378)
*Amit Kumar,Kalpataru Pradhan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用神经网络量子态方法研究了自旋量子数s对J1-J2-J3中心对称量子海森堡模型中方晶格斯格明子稳定性的影响。结果表明：从半经典极限到极端量子极限（s=1/2），斯格明子稳定性Q严重退化直至消失；Q随1/s呈幂律衰减，但s=1/2时偏离该标度行为；建立了斯格明子稳定性与纠缠（二阶Rényi熵）和局域自旋幅度的定量对应关系；发现经典鲁棒的斯格明子相仅限于高自旋材料，自旋矩至少需s=3/2才能实现稳定的原子尺度拓扑纹理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自旋量子数s对中心对称量子海森堡模型中斯格明子稳定性的影响，明确从半经典到极端量子极限的过渡过程中斯格明子的行为变化。

Method: 采用神经网络量子态（neural network quantum states）方法，对J1-J2-J3中心对称量子海森堡模型在方晶格上进行数值模拟。

Result: 斯格明子稳定性Q随自旋矩减小（1/s增大）呈幂律衰减；极端量子极限s=1/2时Q完全消失，且显著偏离幂律标度行为；Q的消失与局域二阶Rényi熵增大和局域自旋幅度抑制呈线性关系。

Conclusion: 经典鲁棒的斯格明子相在阻挫晶格中本质上局限于高自旋材料，自旋矩至少需要s=3/2才能实现稳定的原子尺度拓扑纹理。

Abstract: We investigate the role of the spin quantum number $s$ on the stability of skyrmions in a $J_1-J_2-J_3$ centrosymmetric quantum Heisenberg model on a square lattice using the neural network quantum states method. Our results reveal that the skyrmion stability $Q$ is severely degraded when transitioning from the semiclassical regime to the extreme quantum limit ($s=1/2$), where it ultimately vanishes. We demonstrate that this destabilization is driven by quantum longitudinal fluctuations, with $Q$ exhibiting a power-law decay as a function of the reciprocal spin moment $1/s$. Notably, the extreme quantum limit ($s=1/2$) deviates drastically from this scaling behavior, exhibiting distinct physics compared to larger spin moments. Furthermore, we reveal the microscopic origin of this decay by establishing a quantitative correspondence between skyrmion stability, entanglement, and local spin magnitude: as the local second Rényi entropy (an indicator of entanglement) increases and the local spin magnitude is suppressed, the skyrmion stability vanishes linearly. This regime marks a quantum state where the skyrmion number $C$ remains as remanent geometric feature of the spin orientations, yet the skyrmion stability $Q$ vanishes due to the longitudinal suppression of the local spin magnitude. Our findings suggest that classically robust skyrmion phases in frustrated lattices are fundamentally restricted to high-spin materials, indicating that a spin moment must of at least $s = 3/2$ is required for the realization of stable, atomic-scale topological textures.

</details>


### [116] [Fixed-point tensor network for compactified boson conformal field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03534)
*Gong Cheng,Dong-Yu Bao,Zheng-Cheng Gu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用边界（开弦）数据和共形边界条件，为一般紧致半径下的二维紧致玻色子理论构建了定点张量网络，成功再现了闭弦谱、生成了稳定的重整化群流，并实现了沿c=1模空间的连续可调边际形变，为描述二维无理CFT提供了具体的格点框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定点张量网络提供了共形场论的离散时空表示，有望理解全息对偶、广义对称性乃至量子引力；然而，构建无理CFT（如一般紧致半径下的紧致玻色子理论）的FP张量仍是一个挑战，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用边界（开弦）数据和共形边界条件，为一般紧致半径下的2D紧致玻色子理论构造FP张量，并通过张量复重整化算法进行数值验证。

Result: 所构造的FP张量以高精度再现了闭弦谱，生成了稳定的RG流，并在单张量层面识别出可控的恰好边际形变，实现了沿c=1模空间的连续流动。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了描述广泛2D无理CFT的具体格点级路径，为相关理论（如全息对偶、广义对称性）提供了新的数值工具和理论框架。

Abstract: Fixed-point (FP) tensor networks provide a discrete spacetime representation of conformal field theories (CFTs), offering a new route toward understanding holographic duality, generalized symmetries, and even quantum gravity. In this work, we construct FP tensors for the 2D compactified boson theory at a generic compactification radius, an archetypal irrational CFT, using boundary (open-string) data with conformal boundary conditions. We show that the resulting tensors reproduce the closed-string spectrum with high accuracy and generate stable renormalization-group (RG) flows under the tensor complex renormalization algorithm. Moreover, we identify a controllable exactly marginal deformation at the level of a single tensor, enabling flows that move continuously along the $c=1$ moduli space. This framework establishes a concrete lattice-level route toward describing a broad class of 2D irrational CFTs.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond-adiabatic flat Chern bands from a double-helix skyrmion crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03707)
*Bin Xi,Ken Chen,Qiang Luo,Jie Lu,Jia-Wei Mei,Hong-Gang Luo,Jize Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双螺旋斯格明子晶体（DHSKX）结构，通过相位聚类机制在抑制动能的同時保留贝里曲率，实现了平坦Chern带和分数Chern绝缘体物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带工程中的一个核心挑战是抑制动能的同时不牺牲贝里曲率。本文旨在寻找一种能够同时实现这两点的实空间组织方式。

Method: 通过受挫蜂窝自旋模型的经典基态得到双螺旋斯格明子晶体（DHSKX），利用π锁定螺旋度将波函数相位缠绕排斥出斯格明子核，借助磁C3对称性将其固定为三个相位锁定簇，通过分布式的相消干涉抑制净输运同时保留贝里曲率。

Result: DHSKX实现了孤立的|C|=1平坦Chern带，在中等耦合区间超越了绝热参考的量子几何性质；在超越绝热区间，带投影精确对角化给出了与ν=1/3 Laughlin型分数Chern绝缘体一致的有限尺寸证据；同时还存在一个更高Chern数（C=-2）的平带。

Conclusion: DHSKX结构仅通过位点分辨的复跳跃即可实现，可直接在拓扑电路、声学和光子学平台中编程实现。

Abstract: A central challenge in flat-band engineering is suppressing kinetic energy without sacrificing Berry curvature. We show that a double-helix skyrmion crystal (DHSKX)--two sublattice-resolved skyrmion textures locked at opposite helicities, obtained here as the classical ground state of a frustrated honeycomb spin model--provides such a route under double exchange. The key mechanism is a single real-space organization, phase clustering: the $π$-locked helicities expel the wave function's phase winding from the skyrmion cores, and the magnetic $C_3$ symmetry pins it into three phase-locked clusters whose distributed destructive interference cancels net transport while preserving the Berry curvature. Ordinary skyrmion crystals, even with the same symmetry, do not develop this organization. Phase clustering yields isolated flat $|C| = 1$ Chern bands over broad coupling windows, one of which surpasses the adiabatic reference in quantum geometry at intermediate coupling. In this beyond-adiabatic window, band-projected exact diagonalization gives finite-size evidence consistent with $ν= 1/3$ Laughlin-type fractional-Chern-insulator physics; the same texture also hosts a higher-Chern ($C = -2$) flat band. Built from site-resolved complex hoppings alone, the DHSKX architecture is directly programmable in topolectric, acoustic, and photonic platforms.

</details>


### [118] [Pauli Spectrum and Stabilizer Rényi Entropy in Gapless Symmetry-Protected Topological Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03762)
*Ying-Lin Li,Po-Yao Chang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究稳定子Rényi熵和Pauli谱在对称保护拓扑(SPT)相中的行为，发现稳定子Rényi熵在相变点附近出现极值但无法区分不同SPT相，而Pauli谱在相变点呈现特征性交叉结构，可反映拓扑不同相之间非局域弦序参量的主导Pauli弦关联交换。对于能隙SPT和非本质无能隙SPT相，该交叉结构可由局域酉对偶性解释；对于本质无能隙SPT相，则需要非可逆对偶变换来实现Pauli谱映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越量子纠缠的视角，利用非稳定子性（量子魔法）这一与稳定子态距离的度量，探索其在表征拓扑物相中的潜力，特别是针对能隙和无能隙SPT相的诊断能力。

Method: 数值计算稳定子Rényi熵在对称性保持微扰下的行为；分析Pauli谱在相变点处的交叉结构；利用局域酉对偶性和非可逆对偶变换解释不同SPT相中的Pauli谱映射关系。

Result: 稳定子Rényi熵在相变点附近出现极值但不能区分不同SPT相；Pauli谱在相变点呈现特征性交叉结构，反映主导Pauli弦关联的交换；能隙和非本质无能隙SPT相可由局域酉对偶解释，本质无能隙SPT相需非可逆对偶变换。

Conclusion: 稳定子Rényi熵对相变仅提供粗略诊断，Pauli谱包含更精细的弦序参量交换信息；量子魔法为表征能隙和无能隙SPT相提供了互补视角。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is widely used as a diagnostic of topological phases of matter. Beyond entanglement, non-stabilizerness captures a distinct aspect of quantum many-body states by quantifying their distance from the manifold of stabilizer states. In this work, we study the stabilizer Rényi entropy in symmetry protected topological (SPT) phases, including both gapped SPT, non-intrinsically gapless SPT, and intrinsically gapless SPT phases. Under symmetry preserving perturbations, we find numerically that the stabilizer Rényi entropy exhibits an extremum near the phase transition. However, the stabilizer Rényi entropy alone cannot distinguish different SPT phases. In contrast, the Pauli spectrum reveals a characteristic crossing structure at the transition point. This crossing reflects the exchange of dominant Pauli-string correlations associated with the non-local string order parameters of the two topological distinct phases. For gapped SPT and non-intrinsically gapless SPT phases, the crossing structure can be understood from a local-unitary duality that maps the Pauli spectrum between the two phases. For intrinsically gapless SPT phases, such a local-unitary mapping is absent. Instead, we find that the Pauli spectrum mapping is generated by a non-invertible duality transformation. These results show that although the stabilizer Rényi entropy provides only a coarse diagnostic of phase transitions, the Pauli spectrum contains finer information about the exchange of string order sectors. Our findings demonstrate that quantum magic offers a complementary perspective for characterizing both gapped and gapless SPT phases.

</details>


### [119] [Cuprate d-wave superconductivity based on Non perturbative many body theory for spin fluctuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04168)
*Jianwei Gong,Junnian Xiong,Ruitao Xiao,Hui Li,Ziyu Li,Huaqing Huang,Dingping Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文采用非微扰多体自旋GW方法和协方差方法，分别计算了超导体Hubbard模型的单粒子格林函数和双体关联函数。研究发现，奈尔温度和超导临界温度Tc与实验数据定量吻合，费米面的演化清晰展示了从赝能隙到奇异金属再到费米液体的相变。该方法在强耦合和低温下经过基准测试，无费米子符号问题，可模拟64×64晶格，为研究强关联系统（特别是铜氧化物超导体）提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数值方法在处理强关联超导系统时面临费米子符号问题、有限尺寸效应或无法同时准确描述单粒子和双体性质的困难，亟需一种非微扰且无符号问题的计算方法来定量研究高温超导机理。

Method: 采用非微扰多体自旋GW方法计算单粒子格林函数，利用协方差方法计算双体关联函数，在Hubbard模型框架下对超导系统进行数值模拟，并在强耦合和低温条件下进行基准测试。

Result: 奈尔温度和超导临界温度Tc与实验数据定量吻合；费米面演化清晰展示了从赝能隙到奇异金属再到费米液体的相变过程；方法无费米子符号问题，可扩展至64×64晶格，能够达到热力学极限。

Conclusion: 该方法为研究强关联系统（特别是铜氧化物超导体）提供了一种新的有效途径，克服了传统方法的关键瓶颈，有望推动高温超导机理的理解。

Abstract: We employ the nonperturbative many-body spin GW method to compute the single-particle Green's function, and the covariance method to evaluate the correlation functions of two body, for the Hubbard model for superconductors. The key transition temperatures, namely the Neel temperature and the superconducting critical temperature Tc are found to be in quantitative agreement with experimental data. The evolution of the Fermi surface in different doping regimes is also presented, which clearly shows transitions from pseudogap to strange metal and to Fermi liquid phase. The method has been benchmarked at strong coupling and low temperature and is free from the fermion sign problem. Simulations on lattices up to 64 times 64 are also feasible, making access to the thermodynamic limit possible. This approach may offer a new route toward investigating strongly correlated systems, especially cuprate superconductors.

</details>


### [120] [Orbital-Selective Mott and Antiferromagnetic Phases in Diagonally Compressed Kagome Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04621)
*Jiewei Ding,Ho-Kin Tang,Wing Chi Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过行列式量子蒙特卡洛模拟，研究了半填充Hubbard模型在对角压缩的kagome晶格上的行为。通过引入指数衰减的长程跳跃和改变晶格角度θ及相互作用U，发现几何压缩导致子晶格分化，驱动选择性Mott转变和轨道选择Mott相，绘制了丰富的U-θ相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究几何压缩对受阻二维体系中晶格几何、磁阻挫与强关联电子之间复杂相互作用的影向，特别是在kagome晶格中由于结构形变导致的子晶格分化与选择性Mott转变机制。

Method: 使用行列式量子蒙特卡洛（DQMC）模拟半填充Hubbard模型，在对角压缩的kagome晶格上引入指数衰减长程跳跃t(r)=t₀ exp(-r/r₀)。通过改变晶格角度θ和原位相互作用U，测量双占据数、电荷压缩率及自旋-自旋关联函数。

Result: 几何压缩诱导明显的子晶格分化：θ≳52°时A子晶格建立长程跳跃，抑制B/C子晶格的金属行为驱动选择性Mott转变；θ≲52°时B-C链发展长程反铁磁关联，抑制A子晶格的金属行为。临界相互作用U^c_A在B-C反铁磁关联起始处急剧下降，而U^c_{B/C}上升，形成竞争序和轨道选择Mott相。

Conclusion: 得到了包含顺磁金属、顺磁Mott、反铁磁金属和反铁磁Mott态的丰富U-θ相图，揭示了晶格几何、磁阻挫与强关联作用在受阻二维系统中的复杂耦合机制。

Abstract: We perform determinant quantum Monte Carlo simulations of the half-filled Hubbard model on a diagonally compressed kagome lattice, introducing exponential decay long-range hopping $t(r) = t_0 \exp\bigl(-r / r_0\bigr)$ to account for the evolving bond length. By varying the lattice angle $θ$ and the on-site interaction $U$, double occupancy, charge compressibility, and spin-spin correlation functions of the whole system and each sub-lattice are measured. We find that geometric compression induces a clear sublattice differentiation: for $θ\gtrsim52^\circ$, the A sublattice establishes long-range hoppings, which in turn suppresses the metallic behavior of the $B/C$ sublattice and drives a selective Mott transition; for $θ\lesssim52^\circ$, the $B$-$C$ chains develop long-range antiferromagnetic correlations within the finite-size simulations, which in turn suppresses the metallic behavior of the $A$ sublattice and drives a selective Mott transition. The critical interaction $U^c_A$ for the $A$ sites decreases sharply near the onset of $B$-$C$ antiferromagnetic correlations, while $U^c_{B/C}$ increases. These competing orders give rise to an orbital-selective Mott phase and a rich $U$-$θ$ phase diagram featuring paramagnetic-metal, paramagnetic-Mott, antiferromagnetic-metal, and antiferromagnetic-Mott states. Our results highlight the complex interplay between lattice geometry, magnetic frustration, and strong correlations in frustrated two-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [121] [Supercritical fluid of quantum electrons in three-dimensional superconducting fullerides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04693)
*Xinying Li,Yuki Matsuda,Chongli Yang,Huaxue Zhou,Takuma Ogasawara,Qin Wang,Liguo Zhang,Hidekazu Shimotani,Hailiang Xia,Khuong K. Huynh,Panagiotis Kotetes,Satoshi Heguri,Katsumi Tanigaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次通过电导率和磁化率双探针实验，在三维超导富勒烯CsxRb3-xC60中识别出热力学平衡的超临界流体（SCF）相及其Mott端点，揭示了两条不同金属-绝缘体转变线汇聚于单一Mott端点，磁化率的双粒子分析显示金属-绝缘体共存SCF具有最大两相混合熵，符合Widom线平衡热力学图像，同时电导率标度给出量子临界预言区域的临界指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mott金属-绝缘体转变中无对称性破缺的量子电子超临界流体是强关联电子物理中最 elusive 的现象之一，先前Cr掺杂V2O3和有机Mott体系的研究报告了不一致的临界指数，且标度分析依赖单一实验观测量，无法处理相共存、非均匀性和逾渗的作用。

Method: 采用电导率和磁化率两种独立探针，对三维超导富勒烯CsxRb3-xC60进行实验测量；使用无假设的双粒子分析（two-particle analysis）处理磁化率数据，提取两相混合熵的最大值以识别SCF相；对电导率数据进行标度分析提取临界指数。

Result: 首次实验识别出热力学平衡的SCF相及其Mott端点；两条不同MET-INS转变线汇聚于单一Mott端点；磁化率双粒子分析显示金属-绝缘体共存SCF具有最大两相混合熵；电导率标度得到的临界指数位于量子临界预言区域；揭示了Mott SCF的特征长度尺度低于当前衍射分辨率；对超导Tc穹顶的起源提供了新解释。

Conclusion: 双探针方法为Mott超临界流体提供了统一的微观图像，解决了先前体系临界指数不一致的问题，并给出了超导Tc穹顶的新物理解释。

Abstract: The supercritical fluid (SCF) of quantum electrons at the Mott metal-insulator transition without symmetry breaking is one of the most elusive phenomena in strongly correlated electron physics. Prior studies of Cr-doped V2O3 and organic Mott systems reported discrepant critical exponents. A key limitation is that the scaling analysis relies on a single experimental observable, leaving the roles of phase coexistence, inhomogeneity, and percolation unaddressed. Here we report the first experimental identification of a thermodynamically equilibrated SCF phase and its associated Mott endpoint in the three-dimensional superconducting fullerides CsxRb3-xC60, using two independent probes of electrical conductivity and magnetic susceptibility, which reveal two distinct metal-insulator transition lines converging at a single Mott endpoint. A hypothesis-free two-particle analysis of magnetic susceptibilities yields a metal-insulator coexisting SCF by exhibiting the maximum two-phase mixing entropy, in agreement with a picture of a thermodynamically equilibrated Widom line. Simultaneously, conductivity scaling yields a critical exponent in the regime of quantum critical predictions. Our new dual-probe approach provides a unified microscopic picture of the Mott SCF with a characteristic length scale below current diffraction resolution, in addition to a new interpretation on the origin of superconducting Tc-dome.

</details>


### [122] [Nonlocal Kondo-exchange-driven intrinsic anomalous Hall effect in localized-$4f$ antiferromagnetic metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04805)
*Akimitsu Kirikoshi,Junya Otsuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了4f反铁磁金属Ce₂CuGe₆中反常霍尔效应的起源，提出局域反铁磁序通过非局域Kondo交换耦合诱导的自旋依赖的跳跃机制，而非传统的Zeeman有效场作用，解释了内禀反常霍尔电导和能带自旋劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁金属中的反常霍尔效应多集中于d电子体系，而在f电子体系中，局域磁矩与巡游电子分属不同轨道，其机制尚不明确。本文旨在建立描述局域反铁磁序对巡游电子影响的理论框架，并解释新发现的4f反铁磁金属Ce₂CuGe₆中的反常霍尔效应。

Method: 发展了一个理论框架处理局域反铁磁序对巡游电子的影响，聚焦于f电子材料中局域磁矩与传导电子分属不同轨道的特性，并通过非局域Kondo交换耦合机制进行分析。

Result: 识别出Ce₂CuGe₆中内禀反常霍尔电导和能带自旋劈裂的起源是自旋依赖的晶格间跳跃，该跳跃由非局域Kondo交换耦合诱导，而非传统的局域Zeeman型有效场作用。

Conclusion: 在f电子反铁磁金属中，非局域Kondo交换耦合导致的跳跃机制是反常霍尔效应的关键，这为理解f电子体系中的反常输运现象提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The anomalous Hall effect in antiferromagnetic metals has attracted considerable attention. Most known realizations involve itinerant $d$ electrons that simultaneously mediate charge transport and magnetic order. Here, we focus on $f$-electron materials, where localized magnetic moments and conduction electrons are hosted in different orbitals. We develop a theoretical framework to describe the impact of localized antiferromagnetic order on itinerant electrons. Applying this approach to the recently discovered $4f$ antiferromagnetic metal $\mathrm{Ce}_{2}\mathrm{Cu}\mathrm{Ge}_{6}$, we identify the origin of both the intrinsic anomalous Hall conductivity and the spin splitting of the energy bands as spin-dependent intersite hopping induced by nonlocal Kondo exchange coupling, rather than a Zeeman-type effective field acting locally on the conduction electrons.

</details>


### [123] [Chirped Floquet linear drives activate forbidden charge-to-spin conversions in Rashba two-dimensional electron gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04946)
*Mohsen Yarmohammadi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出利用啁啾（chirped）Floquet线性驱动，在Rashba二维电子气中激活原本禁戒的Edelstein效应电荷-自旋转换路径，无需静磁场或界面工程，为自旋轨道转矩调控提供新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Rashba二维电子气中，Edelstein效应仅实现横向电荷-自旋转换，纵向或面外通道需要静磁场或界面工程，但这些方法存在杂散场且缺乏可调性。动态圆/椭圆Floquet驱动虽可解锁禁戒通道，但简单的线性Floquet驱动无法实现。

Method: 引入啁啾（chirped）Floquet线性驱动，通过啁啾产生面内Floquet-Zeeman场和奇宇称动量漂移，同时打破旋转对称性和时间反演对称性，从而激活禁戒的Edelstein电荷-自旋转换。

Result: 啁啾线性驱动成功激活了Rashba 2DEG中原本禁戒的Edelstein电荷-自旋转换路径，可通过可编程空间光调制器或光学延迟线实验实现。

Conclusion: 该可调谐的啁啾线性驱动方案为自旋轨道转矩切换和高效率自旋电子学提供了一条广泛适用的路径。

Abstract: In Rashba two-dimensional electron gases (2DEGs), charge-to-spin conversion via the Edelstein effect is conventionally limited to the transverse plane. Accessing longitudinal or out-of-plane pathways typically requires static magnetic fields or interface engineering, which cause stray fields and lack tunability. While dynamic circular or elliptical Floquet drives can also unlock these forbidden pathways, a simple Floquet linear drive cannot. Here, we propose an alternative approach: a \textit{chirped} Floquet linear drive. The chirp induces in-plane Floquet-Zeeman fields and an odd-parity momentum drift, which simultaneously break rotational and time-reversal symmetries. This mechanism activates the forbidden Edelstein charge-to-spin conversions in Rashba 2DEGs. Experimentally accessible via programmable spatial light modulators or optical delay lines, this tunable chirped linear drive offers a broadly applicable route to spin-orbit torque switching and high-efficiency spintronics.

</details>


### [124] [Optical switching of antiferromagnetic domains by nonreciprocal heat current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04975)
*Takeshi Hayashida,Shunsuke Izumikawa,Kenta Kimura,Carl S. Davies,Andrei Kirilyuk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示了非互易光学响应可确定性切换反铁磁畴，并通过LiFePO4中红外光实验验证了基于光生热电流的宽带切换机制，建立了非互易性作为光控对称破缺相的一般原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何区分反铁磁体的前后方向性，并利用非互易光学响应实现反铁磁畴的确定性控制，克服传统共振光学切换方案的频谱和材料限制。

Method: 使用中红外光照射磁电反铁磁体LiFePO4，从相反方向照明选择性地稳定相反的畴态；利用材料固有的非互易性通过光生热电流实现宽带切换。

Result: 实现了反铁磁畴的确定性切换，且切换可在宽波长范围内持续，而非局限于窄的跃迁特定光谱区域。

Conclusion: 非互易性可作为光控对称破缺相确定性切换的通用原理，突破了传统共振方案的频谱和材料限制。

Abstract: What distinguishes front from back? In physics, such directionality emerges only when an underlying symmetry is broken. Antiferromagnets that inherently break both space-inversion and time-reversal symmetries provide a striking example, exhibiting nonreciprocal optical responses that depend on the direction of light propagation. Beyond distinguishing antiferromagnetic domains, we show that this nonreciprocity can deterministically create them. Using mid-infrared light, we demonstrate deterministic switching of antiferromagnetic domains in the magnetoelectric antiferromagnet LiFePO4, where illumination from opposite sides selectively stabilizes opposite domain states. Remarkably, the switching persists over a broad wavelength range rather than being confined to a narrow transition-specific spectral region, overcoming the spectral and material constraints of resonance-based optical switching schemes. The broadband switching originates from the material's intrinsic nonreciprocity through optically generated heat currents. Our results establish nonreciprocity as a general principle for deterministically controlling symmetry-broken phases with light.

</details>


### [125] [Near itinerancy and slow singlet formation in the triangular lattice NaRuO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05287)
*Charles C. Tam,Alon Hendler Avidor,Pritam Bhattacharyya,Yongseong Choi,Daniel Haskel,Sven Luther,Hlynur Gretarrson,Liviu Hozoi,Stephen D. Wilson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: NaRuO₂具有类三水铝石结构，包含由共享边的RuO₆八面体构成的三角子晶格。在低至100 mK的温度下未发现磁有序迹象，且存在持续的自旋涨落，表明其处于量子无序磁基态。通过RIXS、XAS和脉冲强磁场磁化测量，结合随机单态模型，提出该系统是一个近巡游体系，存在强烈的磁和电荷涨落，从而破坏了长程磁有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了表征NaRuO₂中量子无序磁基态产生的物理机制，揭示该体系从何种物理区域（如自旋液体、近巡游金属等）产生这种无序态。

Method: 采用Ru-L₂,₃边的共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）和X射线吸收光谱（XAS）表征局部电子结构和磁相互作用，结合脉冲强磁场磁化测量，并将高场磁化数据与随机单态模型进行拟合分析。

Result: XAS测量表明存在显著的自旋-轨道耦合，但RIXS未观测到自旋-轨道激子（自旋-轨道辅助Mott绝缘体的特征），取而代之的是阻尼的带内激发（金属特征）。高场磁化行为与随机单态模型吻合。

Conclusion: NaRuO₂是一个近巡游体系，强磁和电荷涨落破坏了长程磁有序，形成了量子无序的磁基态。

Abstract: NaRuO$_2$ forms a delafossite-like structure that contains triangular sublattices of edge-sharing RuO$_6$ octahedra. It shows no evidence of magnetic order down to 100 mK and persistent spin fluctuations, suggestive of a quantum disordered magnetic ground state. In order to characterize the physical regime from which this disordered state arises, we use resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) and X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) at the Ru-$L_{2,3}$-edge, along with pulsed high-field magnetization to characterize both the local electronic structure and the magnetic interactions. Despite significant spin-orbit coupling inferred from XAS measurements, a spin-orbit exciton, characteristic of a spin-orbit assisted Mott insulator, was not observed with RIXS due to the presence of damped intraorbital excitations, which are characteristic of a metal. Corroborated by models of the high-field magnetization to a random singlet model, we propose a picture of a nearly itinerant system with strong magnetic and charge fluctuations that destabilize long-range magnetic order.

</details>
