### [126] [Probing Neutron Star Interiors and the Properties of Cold Ultra-dense Matter with the SKAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02597)
*Avishek Basu,Vanessa Graber,Marcus E. Lower,Marco Antonelli,Danai Antonopoulou,Manjari Bagchi,Prasanta Char,Paulo C. C. Freire,Brynmor Haskell,Huanchen Hu,David I. Jones,Banibrata Mukhopadhyay,Micaela Oertel,Nanda Rea,Violetta Sagun,Benjamin Shaw,Jaikhomba Singha,Benjamin W. Stappers,Tinn Thongmeearkom,Anna L. Watts,Patrick Weltevrede,The SKA Pulsar Science Working Group*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了中子星内部超致密物质（密度超过核饱和密度数倍）的物理状态，包括超流性等微观性质，以及通过SKAO等射电望远镜观测脉冲星来约束致密物质状态方程、质量、转动惯量、最大旋转频率、脉冲星glitch和自由进动等全局与非全局可观测量，并讨论了暗物质和修正引力模型对约束的潜在影响以及与其他设施的协同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星内部物质密度远超核饱和密度，且温度低、中子-质子不对称性大，提供了地球上无法复现的极端物理环境。然而冷致密物质的物态方程仍高度不确定，因此需要通过观测脉冲星来揭示其性质。

Method: 综述致密物质物理和超流性的最新进展；基于射电观测（尤其是SKAO的前所未有灵敏度、大规模巡天和子阵列能力）测量中子星全局性质（质量、转动惯量、最大旋转频率）和非全局可观测量（脉冲星glitch、自由进动）；讨论暗物质和修正引力模型的潜在影响；强调SKAO与X射线望远镜及下一代引力波观测站的协同作用。

Result: 总结了当前致密物质物理和超流性的研究现状；指出SKAO的观测能力将显著提升对致密物质的约束；分析了暗物质和修正引力对现有约束的可能影响。

Conclusion: SKAO凭借其极高的灵敏度、大规模巡天和子阵列能力，将在约束超致密物质物态方程方面发挥关键作用，与X射线和引力波设施的协同将进一步提升研究深度。

Abstract: Matter inside neutron stars is compressed to densities several times greater than nuclear saturation density, while maintaining low temperatures and large asymmetries between neutrons and protons. Neutron stars, therefore, provide a unique laboratory for testing physics in environments that cannot be recreated on Earth. To uncover the highly uncertain nature of cold, ultra-dense matter, discovering and monitoring pulsars is essential, and SKAO will play a crucial role in this endeavour. In this chapter, we will present the current state-of-the-art in dense matter physics and dense matter superfluidity, and discuss recent advances in measuring global neutron star properties (masses, moments of inertia, and maximum rotation frequencies) as well as non-global observables (pulsar glitches and free precession). We will specifically highlight how radio observations of isolated neutron stars and those in binaries -- such as those performed with SKAO in the near future -- inform our understanding of ultra-dense physics and address in detail how SKAO's telescopes unprecedented sensitivity, large-scale survey and sub-arraying capabilities will enable novel dense matter constraints. We will also address the potential impact of dark matter and modified gravity models on these constraints and emphasise the role of synergies between SKAO and other facilities, specifically X-ray telescopes and next-generation gravitational wave observatories.

</details>


### [127] [A Shock-based Interpretation of Radio and X-ray Emission in Active Galactic Nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02641)
*Fan Wu,Benzhong Dai*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于激波的框架，统一解释活动星系核（AGNs）的射电和X射线辐射：射电来自吸积流或弱外流中的同步辐射，X射线主导成分取决于吸积状态、非热辐射区位置及种子光子场；该模型自洽解释了非喷流AGNs的观测特征，并用射电噪度和黑洞活动基本面（FP）检验了适用性，发现射电噪度随黑洞质量增加、随爱丁顿比下降，FP斜率取决于吸积率标度关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGNs的射电和X射线辐射起源是开放问题，现有模型无法自洽统一解释不同吸积状态下非喷流和喷流AGNs的多波段辐射特征，需要构建一个更通用的理论框架。

Method: 构建基于激波的物理模型，区分非热电子加速区域（吸积流/弱外流 vs. 喷流），分析不同吸积状态（低光度AGN、塞弗特星系、射电宁静类星体）下的辐射机制，并利用射电噪度和BH活动基本面两个经验诊断检验模型。

Result: 模型自洽解释了非喷流AGNs的射电和X射线辐射；在喷流AGNs中，X射线非热电子不可能来自盘或冕；射电噪度随BH质量增大、随爱丁顿比减小的趋势可自然解释；FP斜率依赖于吸积率标度关系；随吸积率增加，ADA流收缩、薄盘扩张，反映辐射效率更高的吸积结构转变。

Conclusion: 爱丁顿比通过调控吸积结构（ADA流 vs. 薄盘）影响辐射机制，进而塑造观测到的AGN类别；该框架为统一理解AGNs射电-X射线辐射提供了自洽的理论基础。

Abstract: We propose a shock-based framework to interpret the radio and X-ray emission in active galactic nuclei (AGNs), whose origin remains an open problem. In this framework, the radio emission is produced by synchrotron radiation in the accretion flow or a jet/weak outflow, while the dominant X-ray component depends on the accretion state, the location of the non-thermal emission region, and the available seed photon field. The model provides a self-consistent interpretation of radio and X-ray emission in typical non-jetted AGNs, including low-luminosity AGNs, Seyfert galaxies, and radio-quiet quasars. In jetted AGNs, our results disfavor scenarios in which the non-thermal electrons responsible for X-ray emission are accelerated in the disk or the corona. We use two widely discussed empirical diagnostics, radio loudness and the fundamental plane (FP) of black hole (BH) activity, to assess the applicability and limitations of the model. It can naturally explain the observed trends that the radio loudness increases with BH mass and decreases with the Eddington ratio. The observed slope of the FP depends on how the key physical quantities scale with the accretion rate. As the accretion rate increases, the advection-dominated accretion flow region contracts while the thin disk region expands, reflecting a transition toward a more radiatively efficient accretion structure. The Eddington ratio therefore influences the accretion structure, and may in turn shape the observed AGN classes.

</details>


### [128] [High-Energy Neutrino Tomography of the Earth's Interior with IceCube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02644)
*The IceCube Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用IceCube中微子天文台10.7年的高能中微子数据（500 GeV–100 TeV），通过测量中微子穿越地球时的通量衰减（依赖于天顶角和能量），首次以弱相互作用方式约束了地球的径向密度分布，并推导出地球质量和极转动惯量，结果与PREM模型和引力测量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上地球内部密度分布依赖于引力和地震波探测，这些方法受限于弹性力和引力响应。本研究试图利用中微子这种弱相互作用粒子作为独立探针，从全新的物理相互作用角度约束地球内部结构。

Method: 分析10.7年的IceCube缪子中微子数据（500 GeV–100 TeV），包含大气中微子和弥散天体物理中微子通量。通过测量随天顶角和能量变化的通量抑制效应，拟合同心均匀密度壳层模型，结合中微子通量、相互作用截面、探测器响应和冰盖系统不确定性，反演地球径向密度分布。

Result: 得到了地球径向密度后验分布，并推导出地球质量和极转动惯量的弱相互作用测量结果，这是迄今最精确的此类弱相互作用测量，且与PREM模型及独立引力测量结果一致。

Conclusion: 中微子提供了一种通过独特物理相互作用（弱相互作用）探测行星内部的崭新手段，与重力和地震学互补。未来随着探测器和精度的提升，中微子将进一步推动对地球结构的多维度理解。

Abstract: The Earth's interior reflects its geological evolution, from accretion to present-day dynamics. Its structure drives the geodynamo in the outer core, generating the magnetic field that shields the surface from charged cosmic radiation. The primary observables of the Earth's interior are its radial density distribution and derived quantities such as its mass and moment of inertia. These have traditionally been inferred from gravity and seismic wave propagation, which probe the macroscopic response of matter to gravitational and elastic forces. Here we instead constrain the Earth's density profile using high-energy neutrinos observed by the IceCube Neutrino Observatory at the South Pole. We analyze 10.7 years of predominantly muon-neutrino data spanning 500 GeV--100 TeV, including atmospheric neutrinos produced by cosmic-ray interactions in the Earth's atmosphere and the diffuse astrophysical neutrino flux. Neutrino attenuation depends on both the traversed column density and neutrino energy. By measuring the zenith- and energy-dependent flux suppression, we infer the Earth's radial density profile by fitting a concentric uniform-density shell model that incorporates neutrino fluxes, interaction cross sections, detector response, and glacial-ice systematic uncertainties. From the resulting density posteriors, we derive the Earth's mass and polar moment of inertia as measured by neutrinos. These are the most precise weak-interaction measurements of these quantities to date and are consistent with the Preliminary Reference Earth Model and independent gravitational determinations. Our results demonstrate that neutrinos provide a novel probe of planetary interiors via a distinct physical interaction, complementing gravity and seismology. With improved detectors and precision, neutrinos will further contribute to a multifaceted understanding of the Earth's structure.

</details>


### [129] [Non-thermal emission in jets and winds: Expected emission and spectral index distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02656)
*M. Meenakshi,D. Mukherjee,G. Bodo,P. Rossi,C. M. Harrison,L. K. Morabito,P. Kharb,S. Silpa*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟研究紧凑型AGN喷流和风中的宇宙射线电子（CREs）原位演化，发现喷流中谱指数在热点处最平坦、远离热点逐渐变陡；风中谱指数随离马赫盘距离增加而变陡，且高频时更明显。马赫盘比正向激波更高效的CRE加速位点。结合空间分辨谱指数、发射和偏振特征可作为紧凑射电源的有效诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑射电源中同步辐射的起源尚不清楚，需要诊断工具来区分主导过程。

Method: 通过模拟研究CREs在紧凑AGN喷流和风中的原位演化，分析其如何塑造可观测的射电特性。

Result: 喷流中谱指数在热点处最平坦，远离热点变陡；风中谱指数随离马赫盘距离增加而变陡，高频时趋势更显著。马赫盘比正向激波更高效的CRE加速位点。

Conclusion: 空间分辨谱指数结合发射和偏振特征可作为紧凑射电源的有效诊断工具。

Abstract: The origin of synchrotron emission in compact radio sources associated with active galactic nuclei (AGN) remains poorly understood. In a series of papers, we have examined diagnostic tools to disentangle the dominant underlying processes. In this study, we investigate the in situ evolution of cosmic-ray electrons (CREs) in compact AGN jets and winds, and examine how their evolution shapes the resulting observable radio properties. In jets, CREs experience multiple shock interactions as they propagate along the spine toward the hotspot and flow into the cocoon via backflows. In winds, CREs are predominantly accelerated at the Mach disc, with occasional re-acceleration within turbulent cocoon backflows. The continuous mixing of different CRE populations within the cocoon produces observational signatures that cannot be inferred from instantaneous conditions alone. In all jet simulations, spectral indices are flattest near the hotspot and steepen progressively away from the hotspots. In winds, spectra steepen with increasing distance from the Mach disc, with this trend becoming more pronounced at high radio frequencies due to radiative losses. We find the Mach disc to be a significantly more efficient CRE acceleration site than the forward shock in winds, which weakens as the wind expands to large scales. Since morphology, especially at low resolution, can be ambiguous for compact sources, spatially resolved spectral indices, particularly when combined with emission and polarization signatures, can provide a powerful diagnostic.

</details>


### [130] [Evidence for Asymmetric Ejecta and Circumstellar Material in SN 2023ixf Inferred from Extensive Nebular-phase Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02859)
*Brian Hsu,Nathan Smith,K. Azalee Bostroem,Jeniveve Pearson,David J. Sand,Lindsey A. Kwok,Jennifer E. Andrews,Bhagya M. Subrayan,Griffin Hosseinzadeh,Manisha Shrestha,Conor Ransome,Moira Andrews,Collin T. Christy,Yize Dong,Joseph Farah,Alexei V. Filippenko,Noah Franz,Jared A. Goldberg,Estefania Padilla Gonzalez,Claudia P. Gutiérrez,Emily Hoang,D. Andrew Howell,Saurabh W. Jha,Réka Kőnyves-Tóth,Michael Lundquist,Curtis McCully,Darshana Mehta,Nicolas E. Meza Retamal,Megan Newsome,Aravind P. Ravi,Jeonghee Rho,Giacomo Terreran,Stefano Valenti,Sergiy Vasylyev,X. -F. Wang,Kathryn Wynn,Yi Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对II型超新星2023ixf从爆发后+89天到+749天的光学和近红外观测，结合JWST的中红外光谱，分析了其星云阶段的谱线演化。Hα轮廓表现出复杂演化，揭示高速成分与外围抛射物与低密度周周介质相互作用；中间时期Hα轮廓可重构为早期衰变功率和后期激波功率的叠加，并出现额外中间宽度成分。近红外中Mg I、Na I和[Ni I]呈现双峰发射，表明富含Ni物质的非对称分布导致非均匀加热。研究提出盘状CSM几何和至少两个大型Ni-rich plume的抛射物几何模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解II型超新星2023ixf在星云阶段的周周介质分布、抛射物几何结构以及Ni-rich物质的非对称性，从而揭示超新星爆发和演化的物理过程。

Method: 对SN 2023ixf进行长时间跨度的光学和近红外观测（+89天至+749天），并利用JWST获取中红外光谱数据；通过Hα轮廓的分解重构（衰变功率成分+激波功率成分+中间宽度成分）以及近红外谱线双峰轮廓分析来推断抛射物和CSM的几何结构。

Result: 1. Hα发射轮廓显示复杂演化，出现高速成分（外抛射物与低密度CSM相互作用）；2. 中间时期Hα轮廓可由衰变功率+激波功率成分叠加并增加中间宽度成分重构；3. 近红外Mg I、Na I和[Ni I]在+200天至+374天呈现双峰发射，指示Ni-rich物质非对称分布。

Conclusion: SN 2023ixf的周周介质呈盘状几何结构，抛射物中至少存在两个大型富含Ni的plume，导致观测到的谱线轮廓多样性。

Abstract: We present extensive optical and near-infrared (NIR) observations of the nearby Type II supernova (SN II) 2023ixf in the nebular phase from +89 days to +749 days after explosion, supplemented with NIR and mid-infrared (MIR) spectroscopy from the James Webb Space Telescope. The H$α$ emission profile shows complex evolution, with the emergence of high-velocity components consistent with the outer ejecta interacting with extended, low-density circumstellar material (CSM). We find that the H$α$ profile at an intermediate epoch (around +375 d) can be reconstructed by scaling an earlier decay-powered component and a later-phase shock-powered component, which revealed an additional intermediate-width component. This is consistent with the ejecta crashing into the initially aspherical dense CSM that has been swept-up by the forward shock. In the NIR, we find double-peaked emission from Mg I $1.504\ {\rm μm}$, Na I $2.206\ {\rm μm}$, and [Ni I] $3.12\ {\rm μm}$ between +200 d and +374 d, consistent with an asymmetric distribution of Ni-rich material that heats the ejecta inhomogeneously. We posit a disk-like CSM geometry and an ejecta geometry in which at least two large Ni-rich plumes lead to the observed line-profile diversity.

</details>


### [131] [No Strong Evidence for Plasma Lensing in FRB 20240114A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02939)
*Jiarui Niu,Xiaohui Liu,Nan Xu,Songyu Shen,Junshuo Zhang,Tiancong Wang,Pawan Kumar,Yuanhong Qu,Dejiang Zhou,Weiwei Zhu,Bing Zhang,He Gao,Dongzi Li,Jinlin Han,Di Li,Xuelei Chen,Kejia Lee,Ye Li,Weiyang Wang,Qiuyang Fu,Jiawei Jin,Yanqing Cai,Caisong Liu,Shuo Cao,Ziwei Wu,Heng Xu,Dengke Zhou,Longxuan Zhang,Wanjin Lu,Yi Feng,Chenhui Niu,Jiawei Luo,Rui Luo,Chunfeng Zhang,Shiqian Zhao,Chengwei Liang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对FAST和Parkes数据的分析，本文检验了利用一维高斯等离子体透镜模型解释快速射电暴FRB~20240114A的爆发率变化、光谱演化和“碳副本”爆发的假说。研究发现，模型预测的放大峰与消减谷之间存在远大于预期的偏移且缺乏周期性；超过10,000次爆发中偶然出现的“碳副本”对不足为奇；透镜区的爆发带宽并未系统性变窄，能量也未显著增强。因此，没有令人信服的证据表明单一高斯等离子体透镜能解释观测到的变化，其更可能源于源本身的固有活动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了验证FRB~20240114A的爆发率变化、光谱演化和“碳副本”爆发对是否由等离子体透镜效应所致，而非源本身的内在活动。

Method: 利用FAST望远镜数据及公开的Parkes观测数据，采用一维高斯等离子体透镜模型进行拟合和统计分析。

Result: 模型拟合的爆发率增强与预测的放大/消减峰谷存在显著偏移且无周期性；大量爆发中偶然出现少量“碳副本”对的概率很高；透镜期间爆发带宽未系统性变窄，能量也未显著增强。

Conclusion: 没有充分证据支持单一高斯等离子体透镜模型可以解释FRB~20240114A的观测变化，其变异性更可能由源本身的固有活动主导。

Abstract: FRB~20240114A is an extremely active repeating fast radio burst for which plasma lensing has been proposed to explain its burst-rate variations, spectral evolution, and apparently ``carbon-copy'' burst pairs. Using FAST data and publicly available Parkes observations, we test this interpretation with a one-dimensional Gaussian plasma-lens model. Although the burst-rate enhancements can be fitted separately, the corresponding magnification peaks and demagnification troughs are offset by far more than predicted and show no consistent periodicity. Moreover, with more than 10,000 bursts detected, a few apparently ``carbon-copy'' pairs can readily occur by chance. The burst bandwidth is not systematically narrower during the proposed lensing interval, nor are the burst energies significantly enhanced during the predicted magnification interval. These results provide no compelling evidence that a single Gaussian plasma lens explains the observed variability, which is more likely dominated by intrinsic source activity.

</details>


### [132] [FAST Pulsar Database III. Snapshots of nulling, mode-changing and subpulse modulation of 374 pulsars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03047)
*Yi Yan,J. L. Han,J. Xu,N. N. Cai,W. Q. Su,P. F. Wang,C. Wang,T. Wang,D. J. Zhou,W. C. Jing,Z. L. Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于FAST望远镜的L波段观测，分析了374颗脉冲星的单脉冲序列，发现了消零、模式变化、子脉冲调制等现象，其中160颗存在消零（127颗首次发现）、52颗存在模式变化（51颗首次发现）、272颗存在子脉冲调制（180颗新发现）。消零和/或子脉冲调制的脉冲星通常更老、周期更长、自旋能量损失率更低。调制周期P3与脉冲星自转周期、磁场强度和自旋能量损失显著相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用FAST高灵敏度L波段观测数据，系统性地检测并统计脉冲星的消零、模式变化和子脉冲调制现象，揭示这些现象的分布特征及其与脉冲星物理参数的关系。

Method: 使用FAST望远镜在1.0-1.5 GHz频段对脉冲星进行单脉冲序列分析，识别消零（nulling）、模式变化（mode changing）和子脉冲调制（subpulse modulation）现象，并对脉冲星参数进行统计分析。

Result: 在374颗脉冲星中，160颗检测到消零（127颗首次）、52颗检测到模式变化（51颗首次）、272颗检测到子脉冲调制（180颗新发现）。表现出消零和/或子脉冲调制的脉冲星通常更老、周期更长、自旋能量损失率更低。P3与自转周期、磁场强度和自旋能量损失相关。

Conclusion: FAST的高灵敏度观测显著增加了对脉冲星辐射现象多样性的认识，揭示了消零和子脉冲调制现象与脉冲星演化状态之间的关联。

Abstract: Based on sensitive L-band (1.0 to 1.5 GHz) observations of pulsars using the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical radio Telescope (FAST), we analyzed single-pulse sequences from FAST-detected pulsars and identified nulling, mode changing, or subpulse modulation phenomena in 374 sources. Among these, nulling has been detected in 160 pulsars, with 127 cases reported for the first time. Emission mode changes have been observed in 52 pulsars, including 51 first-time detections. Subpulse modulation has been identified in 272 pulsars, 180 of which are newly revealed, with the majority displaying subpulse drifting behavior. Subpulse drifting in some pulsars exhibits distinct modes with varying drift properties, leading to mode changes and divergent mean profiles. Statistics on pulsar parameters show that pulsars exhibiting nulling and/or subpulse modulation tend to be older, with longer periods and lower spin-down energy-loss rates. The modulation period P3 is predominantly correlated with pulsar rotation periods, magnetic field strengths, and spin-down energy loss.

</details>


### [133] [Numerical Simulations of Restarted Jets -- I. Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03061)
*Prathamesh Ratnaparkhi,Dipanjan Mukherjee,Gianluigi Bodo,Paola Rossi,Marisa Brienza,Raffaella Morganti*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过三维相对论磁流体动力学（RMHD）模拟，研究了重启喷流在先前喷流遗迹空腔中的演化过程，揭示了其经历从锥形到圆柱再到蘑菇状形态的多阶段演化，并分析了喷流功率、磁场强度和间歇时间对激波结构与动力学的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重启喷流在先前喷流遗迹空腔（而非均匀环境）中的复杂演化过程，以及喷流功率、磁场和间歇时间等参数对其动力学特征的影响。

Method: 使用PLUTO代码进行高分辨率三维相对论磁流体动力学（RMHD）模拟，空间域50 kpc，分辨率40 pc，设计了三种参数套件分别研究喷流功率、磁场和间歇时间的影响。

Result: （1）重启喷流经历了多阶段演化：残余茧包从初始锥形结构经中间圆柱形相最终演变为蘑菇状形态；（2）初始喷流扩张中由Kelvin-Helmholtz和Rayleigh-Taylor不稳定性夹带的致密物质显著改变了茧包内部条件；（3）重启喷流在稀薄残余茧包中弹道式传播，不产生显著回流，仅在遇到致密结构时才减速并产生回流；（4）重启喷流中的激波结构与强度受喷流功率、磁场强度和间歇时间共同影响。

Conclusion: 重启喷流与初始喷流在动力学上存在本质差异，其演化特征取决于喷流功率、磁场和间歇时间等参数，这些动力学行为对射电星系的观测特征具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: We performed high-resolution three-dimensional relativistic magnetohydrodynamic (RMHD) simulations of restarted jets evolving within the cavity of a previous jet episode, using the PLUTO code. The simulations cover a spatial domain of $50\;\mbox{kpc}$ with a resolution of $40\;\mbox{pc}$. Three suites of simulations were performed to understand the impact of jet power, magnetic fields and quiescence time on the evolution of restarted jets. The restarted jets undergo a complex, multi-stage evolution, with the remnant cocoon relaxing from an initially conical structure to a mushroom-shaped morphology via an intermediate cylindrical phase. As the cocoon of the initial jet expands, dense material entrained by fluid instabilities such as Kelvin-Helmholtz and Rayleigh-Taylor significantly alter the conditions within it. As a result, the interaction of the restarted jet with the cocoon is markedly different from that of the initial jet with the ambient medium. In particular, we find that the restarted jet propagates ballistically through the rarefied remnant cocoon, without creating prominent backflows. Deceleration of the jet and associated backflows are observed only when it encounters dense structures. The structure and strength of the shocks in restarted jets are affected by jet power, magnetic field strength, and quiescence time. Finally, we discuss the implications of the dynamics for observed properties of radio galaxies.

</details>


### [134] [Galactic Centre Pulsars with the SKAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03078)
*F. Abbate,A. Carleo,S. Chatterjee,J. Cordes,P. B. Demorest,G. Desvignes,R. P. Eatough,E. Hackmann,Z. Hu,M. Kramer,J. Lazio,K. J. Lee,K. Liu,I. Rammala-Zitha,S. M. Ransom,G. Saowanit,L. Shao,P. Torne,R. Wharton,J. Wongphechauxsorn,W. Zhu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了在银河系中心超大质量黑洞人马座A*附近搜寻脉冲星的研究进展，指出目前仅探测到7颗脉冲星，而SKA望远镜有望揭示更大的潜在脉冲星种群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测围绕银河系中心超大质量黑洞运行的脉冲星是脉冲星天体物理学的重要目标，因为这类系统展现的相对论效应远超已知双致密星系统，并且能以前所未有的细节研究银河系中心的磁离子特性。

Method: 回顾了自上一版SKAO科学书以来的重要进展，基于更新的SKAO设计方案，重点分析了观测策略和可能的探测结果。

Result: 目前在距人马座A* 100 pc投影距离内仅探测到7颗脉冲星；认为存在更大的隐藏种群，但需等待SKA观测才能揭示。

Conclusion: SKA是揭示银河系中心大脉冲星种群的关键设备，本文提供了基于更新设计方案的观测策略展望。

Abstract: The detection of a pulsar closely orbiting our Galaxy's supermassive black hole - Sagittarius~A* - is one of the ultimate prizes in pulsar astrophysics. The relativistic effects expected in such a system could far exceed those currently observable in compact binaries such as double neutron stars and pulsar white dwarfs. In addition, pulsars offer the opportunity to study the magneto-ionic properties of Earth's nearest galactic nucleus in unprecedented detail. For these reasons, and more, a multitude of pulsar searches of the Galactic Centre have been undertaken, with the outcome of just seven pulsar detections within a projected distance of 100\,pc from Sagittarius~A*. It is currently understood that a larger underlying population likely exists, but it is not until observations with the SKA have started that this population can be revealed. In this chapter, we look at important updates since the publication of the last SKAO science book and offer a focused view of observing strategies and likely outcomes with the updated SKAO design.

</details>


### [135] [Understanding the Neutron Star Population with the SKAO Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03087)
*L. Levin,M. Bagchi,M. Burgay,A. T. Deller,V. Graber,A. Igoshev,M. Kramer,D. Lorimer,B. Posselt,T. Prabu,K. Rajwade,N. Rea,B. Stappers,T. M. Tauris,P. Weltevrede*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文概述了非吸积中子星群体的现状（超过3500颗），阐述了SKAO望远镜将如何通过发现大量射电脉冲星来扩展这一群体，并探讨了由此带来的天体物理研究机遇，包括理解脉冲星演化、磁星与长周期脉冲星的联系、自旋与射电发射关系、核物质状态方程、引力理论测试以及中子星质量分布等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前已知的非吸积中子星数量已超过3500颗且仍在增长，但现有样本对理解中子星物理仍存在局限。SKAO望远镜的高灵敏度、大视场和实时波束合成能力将极大扩展已知脉冲星样本，有望发现新类型源，从而回答孤立脉冲星演化、磁星与高B脉冲星关联、自旋与射电发射关系、核物质状态方程以及引力理论检验等关键科学问题。

Method: 综述了SKAO望远镜的技术优势：高灵敏度、宽视场、大量同时合成实时搜索的绑束波束、宽观测频率范围以及多子阵列合成能力，并讨论了这些技术特性如何应用于中子星观测研究。

Result: SKAO将大幅增加已知射电脉冲星数量，覆盖射电噪中子星家族的各个子类群，包括发现新型源；双星系统中脉冲星数量的增加将提供更多高精度引力理论检验机会，并探测中子星质量分布。

Conclusion: SKAO是研究中子星的优异设施，将显著推进对中子星群体及其相关物理现象的理解。

Abstract: The known population of non-accreting neutron stars is ever growing and currently consists of more than 3500 sources. Pulsar surveys with the SKAO telescopes will greatly increase the known population, adding radio pulsars to every subgroup in the radio-loud neutron star family. These discoveries will not only add to the current understanding of neutron star physics by increasing the known sample, but will undoubtedly also uncover new types of sources that will challenge our theories of a wide range of physical phenomena. A broad variety of scientific studies will be made possible by a significantly increased population of neutron stars, unravelling questions such as: How do isolated pulsars evolve with time; What is the connection between magnetars, high B-field pulsars, and the newly discovered long-period pulsars; How is a pulsar's spin-down related to its radio emission; What is the nuclear equation of state? Increasing the numbers of pulsars in binary systems enables both larger numbers and higher precision tests of gravitational theories and general relativity, as well as probing the neutron star mass distribution. The excellent sensitivity of the SKAO telescopes combined with the wide field of view, large numbers of simultaneous tied-array beams that will be searched in real time, wide range of observing frequencies, and the ability to form multiple sub-arrays will make the SKAO an excellent facility for neutron star research. This chapter presents an overview of different types of neutron stars and discusses how the SKAO will aid in our understanding of the neutron star population.

</details>


### [136] [A Square Kilometre Array Pulsar Census](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03090)
*E. F. Keane,V. Graber,L. Levin,C. M. Tan,O. A. Johnson,C. Ng,C. Pardo-Araujo,M. Ronchi,D. Vohl,M. Xue*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文讨论了利用平方公里阵列（SKA）进行全天盲脉冲星巡天的策略，通过SKA-Low和SKA-Mid的联合观测，预计可探测约10,000颗慢脉冲星和约800颗毫秒脉冲星（MSPs），为致密物质状态方程、强场引力检验和引力波天文学等重大科学问题提供约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SKA大部分脉冲星科学依赖于大量脉冲星的高精度计时和VLBI天体测量，而这一切的前提是先发现它们。因此，需要规划利用SKA进行全天盲脉冲星巡天的策略，以最大化脉冲星发现数量，从而支持后续的科学目标。

Method: 基于对脉冲星种群的理解、SKA组件的性能参数以及射频干扰等实际约束，采用两种互补方法对多种巡天设计方案（结合SKA-Low和SKA-Mid Band 1、2）进行巡天产量预测，评估不同阵列配置（AA*、AA4）和巡天覆盖区域的科学产出。

Result: 最佳策略是使用SKA-Low覆盖尽可能多的天区，并尽量与SKA-Mid区域重叠。AA*阵列的全天盲巡天预计发现~10,000颗慢脉冲星和~800颗MSPs（SKA-Mid覆盖银面±5°，更高纬度由SKA-Low覆盖）。AA4阵列在相同区域产量高约20%，若扩大SKA-Mid Band 1和2的覆盖范围，MSPs产量可达~1,300颗。

Conclusion: SKA的大规模脉冲星巡天将大幅扩充脉冲星样本，从而为整个中子星种群的不确定物理性质设定新约束，这对解决SKA关键科学问题（致密物质状态方程、强场引力检验和引力波天文学）至关重要。

Abstract: Most of the pulsar science case with the Square Kilometre Array (SKA) depends on long-term precision timing of a large number of pulsars, as well as their astrometric measurements using very long baseline interferometry (VLBI). However, before we can time them, or VLBI them, we must first find them. Here, we describe the considerations and strategies needed when planning an all-sky blind pulsar survey using the SKA. Based on our understanding of the pulsar population, the performance of the now-under-construction SKA elements, and practical constraints such as evading radio frequency interference, we project pulsar survey yields; this is done using two complementary methods for a number of illustrative survey designs, combining SKA-Low and SKA-Mid Bands 1 and 2 in a variety of ways. A composite survey using both SKA-Mid and SKA-Low is optimal, with Mid Band 2 focused in the plane. We find that, given its much higher effective area and survey speed, the best strategy is to use SKA-Low to cover as much sky as possible, ideally also overlapping with the areas covered by Mid. We find that an all-sky blind survey with Phase 1 of the SKA with the AA* array assembly will detect $\sim10,000$ slow pulsars and $\sim 800$ millisecond pulsars (MSPs) if SKA-Mid covers the region within $5°$ of the plane, while higher latitudes will be covered with SKA-Low. For the same survey region the yield with AA4 is $\sim 20\%$ higher, but this increases considerably by broadening the range covered by SKA-Mid Bands 1 and 2. In particular one could expect a yield of $\sim 1300$ MSPs with AA4. The pulsar census will enable us to set new constraints on the uncertain physical properties of the entire neutron star population. This will be crucial for addressing major SKA science questions including the dense-matter equation of state, strong-field gravity tests, and gravitational wave astronomy.

</details>


### [137] [Exploring the Magnetic Field Structure of the Milky Way with Pulsars in the SKA Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03314)
*Jun Xu,J. L. Han,Weicong Jing*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用脉冲星探测银河系磁场结构的研究，指出脉冲星是探索银河系三维磁场结构的最佳探针。即将建成的SKA1望远镜（AA4和AA*阵列）将使已知脉冲星数量大幅增加（约3倍或翻倍），通过偏振观测可获得数千条视线方向的旋转测量值，从而详细探索银盘和银晕的磁场结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系磁场结构对理解星系磁场的起源至关重要，但目前对银盘和银晕远区的磁场测量仍然稀疏，需要更有效的探测手段。

Method: 利用脉冲星作为探针，通过其高度偏振的短时标射电脉冲和可忽略的内禀法拉第旋转测量星际介质的法拉第旋转，结合SKA1望远镜（AA4/AA*阵列）的大规模脉冲星巡天和偏振观测来获取数千条视线方向的旋转测量值。

Result: SKA1设计基线（AA4）将使已知脉冲星数量增加约3倍，初始分阶段交付阵列（AA*）将可能使当前脉冲星总数翻倍，从而获得数千条视线方向的旋转测量数据。

Conclusion: 利用脉冲星并借助SKA1望远镜的大规模观测能力，将实现对银河系银盘和银晕磁场结构的详细探测。

Abstract: The magnetic field structure of the Milky Way can offer critical insights into the origin of galactic magnetic fields. Measurements of magnetic structures of the Milky Way are still sparse in far regions of the Galactic disk and halo. Pulsars are the best probes for the three-dimensional structure of the Galactic magnetic field, primarily owing to their highly polarized short-duration radio pulses, negligible intrinsic Faraday rotation compared to the contribution from the medium in front, and their widespread distribution throughout the Galaxy across the thin disk, spiral arms, and extended halo. In this article, we give an overview of Galactic magnetic field investigation using pulsars. The sensitive SKA1 design baseline (AA4) will increase the number of known pulsars by a factor of around three, and the initial staged delivery array (AA*) will probably double the total number of the current pulsar population. Polarization observations of pulsars with the AA* telescopes will give rotation measures along several thousand lines of sight, enabling detailed exploration of the magnetic structure of both the Galactic disk and the Galactic halo.

</details>


### [138] [Multiwavelength properties of short gamma ray bursts with extended emission observed by Swift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03541)
*M. M. Dinatolo,A. Mei,R. Brivio,M. Ferro,M. G. Bernardini,P. D'Avanzo,S. Belova,S. Campana,S. Covino,D. Frederiks,B. Haskell,R. Salvaterra,B. Sbarufatti,A. Tsvetkova*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了短伽马射线暴延展辐射（SGRBEEs）是否构成独立于短暴（SGRBs）和长暴（LGRBs）的独特群体。通过分析SBAT4样本中SGRBEEs的瞬时辐射和X射线余辉数据，发现其初始脉冲（IP）与延展辐射（EE）在时域上分别不同于SGRBs和LGRBs，但能谱特征相似。余辉观测表明，早期EE贡献使SGRBEEs更亮，后期则回归标准SGRBs行为。结论认为SGRBEEs并非中间类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究SGRBEEs究竟是源于与SGRBs相同的 progenitors 但表现为LGRBs的一个特殊子类，还是构成一个独立群体。

Method: 使用Swift/BAT、Fermi/GBM、Konus-WIND的瞬时辐射数据及Swift/XRT的X射线余辉数据，分析SBAT4样本中SGRBEEs的时域和能谱特性，并与扩展SBAT4样本的SGRBs和BAT6样本的LGRBs进行比较。

Result: 1) IP和EE以及SGRBs和LGRBs仅凭硬度比无法区分；2) 所有暴的能谱时延一致为零；3) SGRBEEs的XRT光变曲线比SGRBs更复杂（多拐点、陡衰和平坦段）；4) IP在时域上不同于SGRBs，EE不同于LGRBs；5) EE太暗无法出现在Amati平面上，IP与SGRBs占据相同参数空间；6) 余辉早期SGRBEEs比标准GRBs更亮，后期行为类似SGRBs且均比LGRBs暗。

Conclusion: SGRBEEs并非中间类别：瞬时辐射中IP和EE在时域上分别区别于SGRBs和LGRBs，余辉早期EE贡献使SGRBEEs更亮，后期回归标准SGRBs行为。

Abstract: Short gamma-ray bursts with extended emission (SGRBEEs) are a particular class of long GRBs (LGRBs) which, despite their duration, share several observational features with short GRBs (SGRBs). They are composed by a short, hard initial pulse (IP) followed by a longer and softer extended emission (EE). We investigate whether SGRBEEs originate from the same progenitor as SGRBs despite their duration, representing a peculiar subclass of LGRBs, or if they constitute a distinct population. Given their respective duration, we tested if the IP and the EE share properties with short and long GRBs, respectively. We analysed SGRBEEs from the flux-limited, redshift-complete SBAT4 sample using prompt-emission data from Swift/BAT, Fermi/GBM and Konus-WIND, and X-ray afterglow observations from Swift/XRT. The temporal and spectral properties of the IP and EE components were compared with those of SGRBs from the extended SBAT4 sample and LGRBs from the BAT6 sample. Despite observing a clear spectral evolution during the prompt phase of each SGRBEE, IPs and EEs as well as short and long GRBs can not be distinguished by their hardness ratio only. All bursts analysed have a spectral lag consistent with zero. SGRBEEs XRT light curves are consistently more complex than those of SGRBs, requiring the addition of multiple breaks and showing the presence of steep decays and plateaus. Our results indicate that SGRBEEs are not an intermediate class. During prompt emission, despite common spectral features, IPs and EEs temporally differ from short and long GRBs, respectively. EEs are on average too faint to appear in the Amati plane, but IPs occupy the same parameter space of SGRBs. In the afterglow, SGRBEEs are more luminous than standard GRBs at early-time, suggesting a direct contribution from the EE; at later times, these events behave as standard SGRBs, and both remain systematically less luminous than LGRBs.

</details>


### [139] [Beyond the Impulsive Approximation: The Dynamics and Radio Emission of Tidal Disruption Jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03548)
*Fabio De Colle,Enrico Ramirez-Ruiz*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 潮汐瓦解事件（TDEs）中的相对论性喷流受到持续吸积供能，导致射电辐射比标准瞬时爆发模型更暗、更晚出现，且偏离轴观测时辐射显著压低，说明实际相对论性喷流可能比当前射电观测显示的更常见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上使用Blandford-McKee瞬时爆发解解释TDE射电辐射，但TDE喷流由回落吸积持续供能（数周至数月），不同于伽马射线暴的瞬时爆发，因此需要研究持续能量注入对喷流动力学和射电辐射的影响。

Method: 采用一维和二维相对论流体动力学模拟，耦合同步辐射计算，对比持续供能喷流与瞬时Blandford-McKee爆炸解的动力学和射电辐射特性。

Result: 持续供能喷流驱动更慢的前向激波，产生延迟且更暗的射电辐射；瞬时近似仅当供能时间小于等效BM爆炸减速时间的10%时有效，而相对论性TDE通常不满足此条件；持续供能喷流准直性显著低于瞬时爆炸，导致离轴射电辐射大幅压低。

Conclusion: 相对论性TDE的射电演化保留了中心引擎的记忆，不能用瞬时解描述；持续供能的回落吸积喷流会大幅压低离轴射电辐射，意味着实际存在的相对论性喷流可能比当前射电观测所揭示的要多得多。

Abstract: Radio emission from relativistic tidal disruption events (TDEs) is commonly interpreted using the Blandford--McKee solution for an impulsive relativistic blast wave. Unlike gamma-ray bursts, however, TDE jets are powered by fallback accretion, injecting energy over weeks to months. We investigate how this sustained energy injection modifies the dynamics and radio emission of relativistic TDE jets using one- and two-dimensional relativistic hydrodynamic simulations coupled with synchrotron radiation calculations. For a fixed total energy, fallback-powered jets drive slower forward shocks, producing delayed and systematically fainter radio emission. We show that the impulsive approximation is valid only when the duration of energy injection is less than approximately ten percent of the deceleration time of an equivalent Blandford--McKee blast wave, a condition that is generally not satisfied for relativistic TDEs. As a result, continuously powered outflows are significantly less beamed than impulsive explosions, leading to a strong suppression of off-axis radio emission. These results demonstrate that the radio evolution of relativistic TDEs retains memory of the central engine and cannot, in general, be described by an impulsive solution. By substantially reducing the expected off-axis radio emission, sustained fallback-powered jets imply that relativistic jet production in tidal disruption events may be considerably more common than current radio observations suggest.

</details>


### [140] [Neutrinos from super-Eddington Seyfert galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03559)
*Lucas M. Pasquevich,Gustavo E. Romero,Matías M. Reynoso*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出超爱丁顿吸积活动星系核（特别是赛弗特星系）内部磁重联可加速相对论性粒子，产生10-100 TeV中微子流，而其辐射驱动风有效吸收伴随的伽马射线，解释了中微子与弱伽马射线共存的现象，并以NGC 7469为例验证模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多信使观测表明赛弗特星系是高能中微子的有前景源，但其致密内环境会强烈抑制甚高能伽马射线发射。现有模型难以解释中微子与弱伽马射线共存的现象，需要新的理论框架。

Method: 提出超爱丁顿吸积AGN核心模型：在磁约束区域通过磁重联加速包括强子在内的相对论性粒子，加速后的强子成分与致密盘光子场相互作用产生中微子；同时外围的辐射驱动风有效吸收内核对伽马射线的贡献。以NGC 7469为例进行具体应用。

Result: 模型预测中微子通量峰值在10-100 TeV范围，可被IceCube和KM3NeT等现有观测站探测到；成功应用于已报告两次中微子事件的近邻超爱丁顿赛弗特1星系NGC 7469，为观测提供理论解释。

Conclusion: 超爱丁顿AGN，尤其是经历短暂强烈吸积过程的赛弗特星系，是合理的隐藏中微子源，天然解释了高效中微子产生与强烈衰减的伽马射线对应体共存的现象。

Abstract: Multimessenger observations suggest that Seyfert galaxies are promising sources of high-energy neutrinos, but their dense inner environments can strongly suppress the emerging very-high-energy gamma-ray emission. Active galactic nuclei (AGN) undergoing intense accretion episodes can enter a super-Eddington state, in which the accretion flow becomes geometrically and optically thick within a critical radius and develops strong magnetic fields in its innermost region. At the same time, large amounts of matter are expelled from the disk surface in the form of powerful, radiation-driven winds. In this work, we explore a scenario in which the cores of super-Eddington AGN provide suitable conditions for the acceleration of relativistic particles, including hadrons, via magnetic reconnection in a magnetically confined region close to the supermassive black hole. The accelerated hadronic component interacts with the intense photon field of the disk, leading to a copious neutrino flux peaking at 10-100 TeV that may be detectable with current observatories such as IceCube and KM3NeT, while the surrounding outflow efficiently absorbs the accompanying gamma-ray emission from the inner core. We also apply the model to the nearby super-Eddington Seyfert 1 NGC 7469 as a representative case with two reported neutrino events. In this framework, super-Eddington AGN, in particular Seyfert galaxies undergoing transient intense accretion episodes, emerge as plausible hidden neutrino sources, offering a natural explanation for the coexistence of efficient neutrino production and a strongly attenuated gamma-ray counterpart.

</details>


### [141] [Different dissipation mechanisms of jet underlying variability in blazars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03689)
*Hong-bin Tan,Ruo-yu Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过结合射电核偏移测量、能谱分布（SED）和多波段功率谱密度（PSD），对典型耀变体Mrk~501进行多频率分析。采用守恒磁功率的锥形喷流模型，发现单一径向参数分布可重现核偏移和SED，但无法解释高频射电及光学到γ射线波段的观测变异性。引入内外喷流区（分界~0.1 pc）不同的团块分布后，模拟的PSD与Mrk~501在2017-2019低态的多波段观测一致，表明喷流内外部存在不同的耗散机制。该研究展示了谱-时-天测联合喷流建模对约束喷流分层和尺度依赖耗散的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耀变体的喷流能量耗散机制尚不明确，现有观测（射电平谱、核偏移、多波段光变）各自提供喷流信息，但缺乏统一的理论框架将这些观测量与喷流物理联系起来。

Method: 采用守恒磁功率的锥形喷流模型，对Mrk~501的核偏移、能谱分布（SED）和功率谱密度（PSD）进行联合建模。初始使用单一径向参数分布，后引入内外喷流区（分界~0.1 pc）不同的团块分布来模拟多波段光变。

Result: 单一径向参数分布可重现核偏移和SED，但低估了高频射电及光学到γ射线的变异性；引入内外区不同的团块分布后，模拟的PSD与Mrk~501 2017-2019低态的多波段观测一致。

Conclusion: 喷流内外部（分界~0.1 pc）存在不同的耗散行为。谱-时-天测联合喷流建模（结合SED、多波段PSD和射电核偏移测量）能有效约束喷流分层结构和尺度依赖的耗散机制。

Abstract: Blazars are among the most extreme classes of active galactic nuclei. They are powered by relativistic jets, but the way in which the jet energy is dissipated is still unclear. The flat radio spectrum and the core-shift effect trace the distributions of magnetic fields and relativistic particles along the jet, while variability carries information about time-dependent dissipation. However, a unified framework connecting these observables to the underlying jet physics has been lacking. Here we present a multi-frequency analysis of the prototypical blazar Mrk~501. We model its core-shift measurements, spectral energy distributions (SEDs), and power spectral densities (PSDs) with a conical jet model that conserves magnetic power. The core-shift data localize the radio emitting regions and constrain the electron-density and dissipation-rate profiles along the jet. With a single radial distribution of jet parameters, the model reproduces the core-shift relation and SED, but it underpredicts the observed variability at high radio frequencies and in the optical to $γ$-ray bands. We therefore introduce different blob distributions for the inner ($\lesssim$~0.1\,pc) and outer ($\gtrsim$~0.1\,pc) jet regions. With this extended model, the simulated PSDs are consistent with the multiwavelength observations of Mrk~501 during its 2017--2019 low state. This result points to different dissipation behavior in the inner and outer jet. Our study demonstrates that spectro--timing--astrometric jet modeling, which combines SEDs, multiwavelength PSDs, and radio core-shift measurements, can constrain jet stratification and scale-dependent dissipation in blazars.

</details>


### [142] [A NICER view of the millisecond pulsar PSR J2124$-$3358: evidence for a helium atmosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03721)
*Denis González-Caniulef,Sebastien Guillot,Pierre Stammler,Lucien Mauviard,Christine Kazantsev,Anna L. Watts,Devarshi Choudhury,Bas Dorsman,Mariska Hoogkamer,Daniela Huppenkothen,Yves Kini,Tuomo Salmi,Lucas Guillemot,Ismaël Cognard,Gilles Theureau*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究利用X-PSI软件包对孤立毫秒脉冲星PSR J2124-3358进行脉冲轮廓建模分析，结合NICER和Chandra观测数据，推测其大气成分为氦，并估算质量M=1.8±0.5 M⊙、赤道半径Req=11.7^{+2.6}_{-3.0} km，同时推断出两个略非对跖的热斑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定中子星质量与半径的严格约束对于理解超致密物质的状态方程至关重要。虽然脉冲轮廓建模已成功应用于少数毫秒脉冲星，但仍需分析更多此类脉冲星以加强对状态方程的约束。

Method: 使用X-PSI（X射线脉冲模拟与推理）软件包，对孤立旋转动力的毫秒脉冲星PSR J2124-3358进行相对论光线追踪和贝叶斯参数推断。利用NICER和Chandra观测数据，探索不同的中子星大气成分和热极冠配置。

Result: 氦大气模型更受青睐，得到质量M=1.8±0.5 M⊙、赤道半径Req=11.7^{+2.6}_{-3.0} km（中位数和68%置信区间），并推断出两个略非对跖的热斑。氢大气模型则导致质量和半径分别减少约0.5 M⊙和1 km。

Conclusion: 氦大气成分可能源于吸积及后续蒸发过程，但受限于源本身较暗和数据集信噪比低，后验分布较宽。未来结合射电和伽马射线脉冲发射的多波段研究有望提供更严格的几何与物理性质约束。

Abstract: Pulse profile modeling has proven to be a powerful technique for determining the mass and radius of neutron stars. To date, this method has been applied to a handful of millisecond pulsars observed by the Neutron Star Interior Composition Explorer (NICER). However, analyses of more millisecond pulsars are necessary to determine tight constraints on the equation of state of superdense matter. In this study, we present an analysis of the isolated, rotation-powered millisecond pulsar PSR J2124$-$3358 using the X-ray Pulse Simulation and Inference (X-PSI) package, a publicly available state-of-the-art code for neutron-star relativistic ray tracing and Bayesian parameter inference. We use NICER and Chandra observations of this pulsar, exploring different neutron star atmospheric compositions and different configurations of the hot polar caps responsible for the pulsed X-ray emission. Our analyses favor a helium atmospheric composition, plausibly originating from accretion and subsequent evaporation of a former hydrogen-depleted binary companion. For this composition, and given the faint nature of the source and the low signal-to-noise of the data sets, we obtain broad posterior distributions yielding a mass $M = 1.8\pm0.5\,M_\odot$ and an equatorial radius $R_{\mathrm{eq}} = 11.7^{+2.6}_{-3.0}$ km (medians and $68\%$ credible intervals), and infer a configuration consisting of two slightly non-antipodal hot spots. By contrast, when using a hydrogen atmosphere model, the mass and radius decrease by $\sim 0.5\,M_\odot$ and $\sim 1$ km, respectively. Future multiwavelength studies, particularly those incorporating radio and gamma-ray pulse-emission, may provide tighter constraints on the geometry and physical properties of this source.

</details>


### [143] [Neutron stars with an agnostic Dark sector: Core and Halo configurations from a two-fluid approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03840)
*Asit karan,Asim Kumar Saha,Tuhin Malik,Constança Providência,Ritam Mallick*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文采用无模型预设的物态方程，在双流体框架下构建暗物质与中子星的混合星体。研究发现，轻暗物质形成延展晕增加潮汐形变，重暗物质形成致密核降低形变。基于现有数据，轻暗物质质量分数被约束在约11%以内。具有相近质量但潮汐形变差异显著的中子星可能是暗物质存在的关键信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质与中子星的混合研究有助于理解暗物质粒子候选者，但暗物质粒子质量参数空间巨大，限制了系统性的、模型无关的研究。因此需要一种不依赖于特定暗物质微观物理的通用框架来研究其对中子星可观测性质的影响。

Method: 采用不可知论的强子物态方程和暗物质物态方程，在双流体形式下构建暗物质混合中子星。暗物质建模为费米气体，仅由其低密度物态方程和质量刻画；强子物质在低密度和高密度分别由手征有效场论和微扰QCD约束，中间密度区域采用声速参数化方法。暗物质物态方程仅受热力学一致性约束，无软硬偏好。

Result: 暗物质并不普遍使星体致密化：轻暗物质形成延展晕，提高潮汐形变能力；重暗物质形成致密核，降低潮汐形变能力。轻暗物质主导的晕模型主要受引力波潮汐形变约束，重暗物质主导的核模型主要受NICER质量-半径数据约束。在1σ置信水平下，轻暗物质质量分数约束为f_DM≲0.11。

Conclusion: 该框架几乎不依赖于暗区微观物理假设，给出了保守且广泛适用的中子星暗物质含量上限。具有相近质量但潮汐形变能力显著不同的中子星可能是暗物质存在的确凿证据（smoking-gun signature）。

Abstract: The study of dark matter admixed neutron stars has the potential to advance our understanding of dark matter particle candidates. However, the large parameter space of dark matter particle masses restricts a systematic, model-independent study. In this analysis, we employ agnostic hadronic and dark matter equations of state to construct dark-matter-admixed neutron stars within a two-fluid formalism. Dark matter is characterised solely by its low-density equation of state and mass, and is modelled as a Fermi gas, while hadronic matter is anchored at low and high densities by chiral effective field theory and perturbative quantum chromodynamics calculations. A speed-of-sound parametrisation covers the intermediate density region for hadronic matter and the high-density region for dark matter, so the dark matter equation of state is constrained only by thermodynamic consistency, free from bias toward a softer or stiffer equation of state. Within this agnostic framework, we find that dark matter does not generically compactify the star: light dark matter forms extended halos that raise the tidal deformability, while heavy dark matter forms compact cores that lower it. Consequently, the dominant observational constraint shifts from gravitational-wave tidal deformability for light, halo-dominated models to NICER mass--radius data for heavy, core-dominated models. Using current data at $1σ$, we constrain the dark matter fraction to $f_{\mathrm{DM}} \lesssim 0.11$ for light dark matter. Being almost independent of any assumed dark-sector microphysics, our framework yields conservative, broadly applicable bounds on the dark-matter content of neutron stars. Neutron stars with similar masses but very different tidal deformabilities could be a smoking-gun signature of dark matter in Neutron stars.

</details>


### [144] [Long-period radio transient PSR J0901-4046 is not an Isolated White Dwarf Pulsar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03848)
*Maxim Lyutikov,Mukremin Kilic,Alexander Wolszczan,Craig Heinke*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过Chandra对长周期射电瞬变源PSR J0901-4046的X射线非探测，将其X射线光度上限降至~10^28 erg/s，比之前Swift观测低约50倍。该上限与中子星的自转能相当，但比白矮星的自转能低约四个数量级，从而排除了孤立白矮星作为其中心天体的可能性，支持其由磁耗散（而非自转）供能的磁星模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定PSR J0901-4046（周期75.89秒的长周期射电瞬变源）的中心天体性质，区分其是中子星还是白矮星。

Method: 利用Chandra望远镜进行X射线观测，将X射线光度上限与中子星和白矮星的自转能进行比较。

Result: Chandra非探测给出X射线光度上限约10^28 erg/s，比Swift观测低50倍，与中子星自转能相当，但比白矮星自转能低四个数量级。

Conclusion: 排除孤立白矮星作为PSR J0901-4046中心天体的可能性，认为孤立长周期射电瞬变源由磁耗散（类似磁星射电辐射机制）而非自转供能。

Abstract: We report the {\it Chandra} non-detection of PSR J0901$-$4046, a $P=75.89 $ seconds long-period radio transient (LPT). For a distance of 467 pc, the upper limit on X-ray luminosity is $L_X \leq$ few $\times 10^{28}$ erg s$^{-1}$. For the measured $P$ and $\dot{P}$, this upper limit, approximately 50 times lower than the previous {\it Swift} observations, is comparable to the spin-down luminosity of a neutron star, but would be approximately four orders of magnitude smaller than the spindown power of a white dwarf. Our results disfavor isolated WDs as the central star in PSR J0901$-$4046. We suggest that the isolated LPTs are powered by magnetic dissipation (not rotation), in a way similar to magnetars' radio emission.

</details>


### [145] [Cross-validation of six dispersion measure estimation methods for FRB 20240114A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03877)
*Tonglun Wang,Songbo Zhang,Yuanchuan Zou,Xuan Yang,Pei Wang,Di Xiao,Xianghan Cui,Ye Li,Hao Qiu,Yingze Shan,Junyi Shen,Ya Zeng,Longxuan Zhang,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用FAST望远镜在一次4.4小时观测中从FRB20240114A获取的2874个爆发样本，系统比较了六种色散量（DM）估算方法。研究发现，低信噪比和复杂多成分爆发的爆发会导致方法间DM偏差增大，而射频干扰影响有限。即使在严格控制方法一致性的条件下，FRB20240114A仍表现出显著的DM表观波动，这并非源于视线方向电子柱密度的真实变化，而是由爆发固有的频率依赖性发射时间结构造成的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速射电暴（FRB）作为重要的宇宙学探针，其应用高度依赖于色散量（DM）的精确测定。然而，不同DM估算方法可能导致结果差异，影响FRB的科学应用。FRB20240114A作为目前最活跃的重复暴，提供了在短时间内获得大量均匀样本的独特机会，使研究者能够隔离算法效应对DM测定的影响，系统评估不同方法的可靠性和一致性。

Method: 研究者使用FAST望远镜在2024年3月12日的一次4.4小时观测中收集了FRB20240114A的2874个爆发，采用六种不同的DM估算方法进行比较分析。通过控制观测环境近乎静态的条件，研究了方法间一致性对信噪比、爆发形态和射频干扰的依赖性。对单成分、双成分和多成分爆发分别评估了DM测定偏差，并分析了严格一致性约束下残留DM波动的可能起源。

Result: 1) 低信噪比爆发在方法间表现出显著更大的DM偏差；2) 单成分爆发在各方法间产生高度一致的DM值；3) 具有漂移子结构的双成分和多成分爆发导致方法间DM散射大幅增加，表明DM差异主要由算法对爆发形态的响应引起；4) 射频干扰通过频率通道掩蔽导致形态扭曲，影响密度滤波方法，但不显著改变DM偏差的全局统计行为；5) 在严格一致性约束下，FRB20240114A在15780秒内仍表现出~528-534 pc cm^-3的表观DM波动；6) 形态简单爆发的DM变化远超测量不确定度，且无法归因于视线电子柱的合理变化，而是源于爆发中类似色散的频率依赖性发射时间结构。

Conclusion: DM测定方法的选择对爆发形态复杂的FRB尤为重要，单成分爆发能提供最可靠的DM测量。观察到DM表观波动的真正来源是爆发固有的频率依赖性发射时间结构，而非传播路径上电子柱密度的变化，这对FRB作为宇宙学探针的应用具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) are important cosmological probes, but their applications depend critically on accurate dispersion measure (DM) determinations. We present a systematic comparison of six DM estimation methods using 2,874 bursts from FRB20240114A, the most active repeating FRB currently known, observed by FAST during a single 4.4-hr session on 2024 March 12. This large, homogeneous sample over a short timescale, during which the propagation environment is expected to be nearly static, provides an ideal benchmark for isolating algorithmic effects on DM determination. We investigate the dependence of inter-method consistency on signal-to-noise ratio (S/N), burst morphology, and radio frequency interference (RFI). Low-S/N bursts exhibit significantly larger inter-method deviations, while single-component bursts produce highly consistent DM values across methods. In contrast, complex double- and multiple-component bursts with drifting substructures lead to substantial inter-method scattering, indicating that DM discrepancies are primarily driven by algorithmic responses to burst morphology. RFI does not significantly alter the global statistical behavior of DM deviations, but it affects density-filtering methods through morphology distortion caused by frequency-channel masking. Even after imposing strict inter-method consistency constraints, FRB20240114A still exhibits notable apparent DM fluctuations spanning $\sim$528-534~pc~cm$^{-3}$ over 15,780s. For morphologically simple bursts these variations far exceed the measurement uncertainty and, on second-to-minute timescales, cannot arise from any plausible change in the line-of-sight electron column, pointing instead to a frequency-dependent emission-time structure intrinsic to the bursts that mimics dispersion.

</details>


### [146] [Constraining Pulsar Radiative Geometry via Multi-wavelength Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04083)
*Tong Zhao,Shunshun Cao,Renxin Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出通过联合建模热X射线脉冲轮廓和偏振位置角，可以确定脉冲星相干辐射的偏振取向焦点。X射线脉冲轮廓测量约束热X射线发射中心的余纬（对应极冠内下落粒子中心），而RVM拟合得到偏振取向焦点的倾角。两个独立角度测量的一致性表明RVM拟合能准确恢复等离子体流中心倾角，且该中心与偏振取向焦点重合；不一致则表明射电辐射的偏振态在传播过程中发生变化，因为磁层中波模演化强烈依赖于磁场方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以准确定位脉冲星相干辐射的偏振取向焦点，而联合热X射线脉冲轮廓与偏振位置角建模提供了一种有效手段，可以检验RVM拟合的可靠性并揭示射电辐射传播过程中的偏振态演化。

Method: 通过X射线脉冲轮廓测量约束热X射线发射中心的余纬（对应极冠内下落粒子中心），同时利用旋转矢量模型（RVM）拟合得到偏振取向焦点的倾角，比较这两个独立角度测量的一致性。

Result: 两个独立角度测量的一致性意味着RVM拟合能准确恢复等离子体流中心倾角，且该中心与偏振取向焦点重合；不一致则表明射电辐射的偏振态在传播中发生变化，波模演化依赖于磁场方向。

Conclusion: 联合热X射线脉冲轮廓与偏振位置角建模是确定脉冲星偏振取向焦点的有效方法，可用于检验RVM拟合的可靠性并揭示射电辐射传播过程中的偏振态演化机制。

Abstract: We propose that jointly modeling the thermal X-ray pulse profiles and the polarization position angles offers an effective means of locating the polarization orientation focus of the pulsar's coherent radiation. From the X-ray pulse-profile measurement we constrain the colatitude of the center of the thermal X-ray emission, which corresponds to the center of in-falling particles within the polar cap, while the RVM fitting yields the inclination angle of the focus point of polarization orientations. Thus, consistency between these two independent angle measurements would imply that the RVM fit faithfully recovers the inclination of the plasma flow center, and this center coincides with the polarization orientation focus. Conversely, the discrepancy would suggest that the polarization state of the radio emission changes as it propagates because the evolution of wave modes during wave propagation in the magnetosphere strongly depends on magnetic field orientations.

</details>


### [147] [Measuring the Angular Auto-power Spectrum of Fast Radio Burst Dispersion Measures as a Robust Cosmological Probe and Baryon Tracer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04106)
*Bao Wang,Zhiyu Lu,Yang Liu,Jun-Jie Wei,Xue-Feng Wu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次利用CHIME/FRB Catalog 2中3455个非重复快速射电暴的色散量测量了其角自功率谱，以>3σ置信度探测到与电子密度大尺度涨落相关的角关联信号，并约束了宇宙重子密度与哈勃常数、大尺度结构重子分数等关键参数组合。该方法不依赖单个FRB的红移测量，且对宿主星系等未关联DM成分不敏感，有效缓解了系统误差，确立了DM角功率谱作为稳健宇宙学探针的地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB的色散量携带着宇宙电子密度涨落的信息，传统方法依赖单个FRB的红移—DM关系，但受限于宿主星系DM等不确定因素。本文旨在发展一种不依赖个体红移测量的新方法——DM角功率谱，以更稳健地探测宇宙重子分布并约束宇宙学参数。

Method: 利用CHIME/FRB Catalog 2中3455个非重复FRB的DM数据，计算其角自功率谱，并与理论模型拟合，约束Ω_b h²-H_0和Ω_b h²-f_d参数组合。通过模拟样本评估了FRB红移分布假设、宿主星系DM、银河系晕DM和星际介质DM等系统不确定性对功率谱方法的影响。

Result: 首次探测到FRB DM的角自功率谱信号，显著性>3σ；约束了Ω_b h²-H_0和Ω_b h²-f_d两组参数组合；模拟验证表明该方法对宿主星系DM等未关联成分不敏感，相比传统DM_LSS-z关系可部分打破参数简并。

Conclusion: FRB DM角功率谱是一种稳健的新型宇宙学探针，无需单个FRB的红移测量，可有效减轻宿主星系系统误差的影响，并作为大尺度结构重子示踪剂发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Fluctuations in the cosmic electron density are imprinted on the dispersion measures (DMs) of fast radio bursts (FRBs), making DMs a promising probe of cosmology and the spatial distribution of ionized baryons. In this work, we present the first measurement of the angular auto-power spectrum of FRB DMs, using 3455 apparently non-repeating bursts from the CHIME/FRB Catalog 2. We detect an angular correlation signal at $>3σ$ significance, associated with large-scale electron-density fluctuations. By fitting the measured spectrum to theoretical models, we constrain two key parameter combinations: $Ω_{\rm b}h^2$-$H_0$, which probes the cosmic baryon density and expansion rate, and $Ω_{\rm b}h^2$-$f_{\rm d}$, which traces the baryon fraction in cosmic large-scale structure (LSS). We further assess the robustness of the power-spectrum method against systematic uncertainties arising from the assumed FRB redshift distribution and from the DM contributions of host galaxies (${\rm DM}_{\rm host}$), the Galactic halo (${\rm DM}^{\rm MW}_{\rm halo}$), and the Milky Way interstellar medium (${\rm DM}^{\rm MW}_{\rm ISM}$), using mock samples. Our results demonstrate that the angular power spectrum is largely insensitive to uncorrelated DM components such as ${\rm DM}_{\rm host}$, thereby effectively mitigating the impact of poorly constrained host-galaxy systematics. In contrast to the traditional ${\rm DM}_{\rm LSS}$-$z$ relation, this method does not require individual redshift measurements--it relies only on the overall redshift distribution--and it partially breaks the parameter degeneracies in the $Ω_{\rm b}h^2$-$H_0$ and $Ω_{\rm b}h^2$-$f_{\rm d}$ planes. These findings establish the DM angular power spectrum as a robust cosmological probe and a powerful baryon tracer.

</details>


### [148] [Exploring Hierarchical Merger Scenarios for GW241011 and GW241110](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04663)
*Lei He,Liang-Gui Zhu,Zheng-Yan Liu,Rui Niu,Chao Wei,Ken Chen,Wen Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文分析两个非对称双黑洞并合事件GW241011和GW241110，发现它们更可能来自第二代+第一代(2G+1G)的层级并合，而非第一代双黑洞并合，并搜索了可能的光学对应体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW241011和GW241110具有高速自转主黑洞、非对称质量和非零自旋-轨道倾角等特征，是层级并合的天然候选体，亟需通过统计比较来检验其成因。

Method: 采用贝叶斯框架，比较第一代(1G+1G)双黑洞种群与星团和AGN盘环境下的第二代+第一代(2G+1G)层级并合模型；进一步考虑3G+1G模型；利用ZTF和ATTF强制测光搜索光学对应体。

Result: GW241011的lnℬ^{2G+1G}_{1G+1G}约6.5-8.6，GW241110约3.0-4.5，均支持2G+1G解释；AGN盘模型证据略高于星团模型；3G+1G模型未被稳健偏好；发现一个可能与GW241110相关的候选弱耀发源。

Conclusion: 这两个事件更倾向于层级并合起源，尤其2G+1G场景，但无法明确区分具体环境；光学对应体候选有待进一步确认。

Abstract: GW241011 and GW241110 are asymmetric binary black hole mergers with rapidly spinning primaries, unequal component masses, and nonzero spin--orbit tilts, making them natural candidates for hierarchical mergers. We use a Bayesian framework to compare a fiducial first-generation (1G+1G) binary black hole population with second-generation plus first-generation (2G+1G) hierarchical merger models in star clusters and active galactic nucleus (AGN) disks. Both events favor the 2G+1G interpretation over the 1G+1G hypothesis, with $\ln\mathcal{B}^{\rm 2G+1G}_{\rm 1G+1G}\simeq6.5$--$8.6$ for GW241011 and $\ln\mathcal{B}^{\rm 2G+1G}_{\rm 1G+1G}\simeq3.0$--$4.5$ for GW241110, depending on the waveform model and assumed environment. The AGN disk models yields slightly larger evidence than the star cluster models, mainly due to their spin tilt distribution, but the data do not provide a decisive environmental classification. We further consider a third-generation plus first-generation (3G+1G) interpretation, but it is not robustly preferred over 2G+1G scenarios. Finally, we also search for optical counterparts by examining AGNs within the three-dimensional localization volumes using ZTF and ATLAS forced photometry, and find one candidate source with weak flare, which might be associated with GW241110 event.

</details>


### [149] [Evidence for a Delayed Progenitor Population for CHIME non-repeating Fast Radio Bursts using a Self-Consistent Forward and Backward Inference Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04792)
*Zi-Liang Zhang,Bing Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用CHIME/FRB Catalog 2中超过一千个非重复快速射电暴（FRB）事件，通过结合逆向非参数推断与前向蒙特卡罗种群合成的方法，揭示了FRB的内在红移分布峰值在z~1，显著低于宇宙恒星形成历史（SFH）峰值z~1.7，表明FRB至少部分滞后于SFH；内在能量分布符合幂律（指数α≈1.9）并在高能端变陡，未发现红移-能量分布相关性的显著证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速射电暴（FRB）的物理起源仍不确定，需要通过对大量观测数据进行自洽的统计分析来推断其内在种群属性（红移、能量分布等），以限制理论模型。

Method: 结合两种方法：1）逆向非参数推断（加权Lynden-Bell C⁻估计器），无需假设具体种群模型即可恢复内在红移和能量分布；2）前向蒙特卡罗种群合成，在观测的色散-流量空间进行模拟，将候选内在分布作为假设并与实际观测目录比较。同时考虑了概率性的色散-红移估计、流量校正和模糊多维选择函数。

Result: 内在红移分布峰值在z~1（低于SFH峰值z~1.7），与纯SFH追踪场景存在明显张力；内在能量分布符合幂律（α≈1.9），高能端变陡；未发现红移-能量分布相关性的显著证据。

Conclusion: 至少部分FRB的形成相对于恒星形成历史存在延迟，不能简单地用纯SFH追踪模型解释；研究提供了一个自洽的FRB种群推断框架，有助于限制FRB起源模型。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are luminous extragalactic radio transients whose physical origins remain uncertain. Using over one thousand non-repeating events from CHIME/FRB Catalog 2, we infer the intrinsic FRB demographics with a self-consistent framework that combines backward non-parametric inference and forward population synthesis while accounting for probabilistic dispersion measure--redshift estimates, baseband-to-catalog fluence corrections, and the latest fuzzy multidimensional selection function. We first apply a backward non-parametric method, the weighted Lynden--Bell $C^{-}$ estimator, to recover the intrinsic redshift and energy distributions without assuming any population model. Independently, we perform forward Monte Carlo population synthesis in observable dispersion measure--fluence space, treating candidate intrinsic redshift and energy distributions as population hypotheses and comparing the resulting selected synthetic catalogs with observations. We find that the intrinsic redshift distribution peaks at $z\sim1$, significantly lower than the cosmic star formation history (SFH) peak at $z\sim1.7$, indicating clear tension with a pure SFH-tracking scenario, suggesting that at least some FRBs are delayed with respect to SFH. The intrinsic energy distribution is consistent with a power law of index $α\approx1.9$ and steepens at higher energies. We find no significant evidence for a redshift-energy distribution correlation.

</details>


### [150] [An 18-year Fermi-LAT stacking limit on GeV $γ$-ray emission from particle-accelerating colliding-wind binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05102)
*Youngwan Son*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对61个确认的粒子加速碰撞星风双星（PACWBs）进行17.8年的Fermi-LAT数据分析，未发现集体GeV伽马射线发射的证据。仅在避开明亮伽马射线源后保留6个干净源，其堆叠结果与控制场无显著差异（p=0.83）。每源平均通量上限F(>1 GeV) ≲ 1.1×10⁻¹¹ ph cm⁻² s⁻¹，伽马射线效率η ≲ 4×10⁻⁶ (d/kpc)²，比η Car低约两个数量级。η Car很可能是独特天体而非新兴群体的最亮成员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞星风双星（CWBs）的星风碰撞区域能加速相对论性粒子，非热射电同步辐射和η Car的硬X射线已证实这一点，但CWBs作为一个群体是否发射GeV伽马射线尚不清楚（目前仅η Car被Fermi-LAT明确探测到）。本文旨在系统性地检验CWBs群体是否存在集体性的GeV伽马射线发射。

Method: 使用17.8年的Fermi-LAT数据（>1 GeV），选取De Becker & Raucq目录中61个确认的粒子加速CWB（PACWBs），采用二维（光子指数×通量）似然扫描，与200个在银纬、局部源密度和弥散强度上匹配的控制场进行对比分析。

Result: 移除与明亮伽马射线源视线方向重合的系统后，得到6个干净且有相互隔离的样本，其堆叠结果与控制场零假设一致（p=0.83），无集体GeV发射证据。保留这些系统则会产生>3.5σ的虚假超出。95%置信度下每源平均通量上限为F(>1 GeV) ≲ 1.1×10⁻¹¹ ph cm⁻² s⁻¹ (Γ=2)，伽马射线生产效率η ≲ 4×10⁻⁶ (d/kpc)²，比样本中位距离处的η Car低约两个数量级。

Conclusion: η Car很可能是独特的天体，而非一个新兴CWB伽马射线群体的最亮成员；单区模型将CWB星风碰撞区域的电子加速效率约束在磁-光子等分场值以下（除非磁场足够强）。

Abstract: The wind-collision regions of massive colliding-wind binaries (CWBs) accelerate relativistic particles, as shown by their non-thermal radio synchrotron and, in $η$ Car, hard X-ray emission. Whether CWBs emit GeV $γ$rays as a population is unknown: only $η$ Car is unambiguously detected by the Fermi Large Area Telescope (LAT). We analyze 17.8 yr of Fermi-LAT data above 1 GeV (where the sharp point-spread function controls plane confusion) at the 61 confirmed (list A) particle-accelerating CWBs (PACWBs) of De Becker & Raucq, using a two-dimensional (photon index $\times$ flux) likelihood scan against 200 control fields matched in latitude, local source density, and diffuse intensity. Removing systems whose lines of sight coincide with bright catalogue $γ$-ray sources leaves a clean, mutually isolated sample of 6, whose stack is consistent with the control-field null ($p=0.83$): no evidence for collective GeV emission. Retaining those systems instead yields a spurious $\gtrsim3.5σ$ excess from chance catalogue coincidences, a caveat for Galactic-plane stacking. The resulting 95% limit on the mean per-source flux, $F(>1\,\mathrm{GeV})\lesssim1.1\times10^{-11}~\mathrm{ph\,cm^{-2}\,s^{-1}} (Γ=2)$, implies a $γ$-ray production efficiency $η=L_γ/L_{\rm wind}\lesssim4\times10^{-6}\,(d/\mathrm{kpc})^2$, about two orders of magnitude below $η$ Car at the sample's median distance. A representative single-zone model then bounds the electron acceleration efficiency in CWB wind-collision regions unless their magnetic field is comparable to or above the magnetic-photon equipartition value. Among the high-latitude systems accessible to a clean stack, $η$ Car thus appears to be a singular object rather than the brightest member of an emerging population.

</details>


### [151] [Spectropolarimetric detection of baryonic mass loading in a transient relativistic jet: application to the black hole X-ray binary Swift J1727.8$-$1613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05182)
*A. K. Hughes,R. P. Fender,G. R. Sivakoff,F. J. Cowie,I. Heywood,J. H. Matthews,K. Savard,F. Carotenuto,T. D. Russell,C. M. Wood,M. C. Baglio,S. Corbel,S. E. Motta*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用MeerKAT L波段分光偏振测量技术，对2023年爆发期间的黑洞X射线双星进行观测，发现与射电耀斑相关的瞬态法拉第复杂结构。由于内部法拉第旋转在纯电子-正电子等离子体中被抑制，观测数据支持喷流以电子-质子成分为主。通过法拉第厚度和同步自吸收论证，估算出法拉第旋转质量约为10²¹克，仅占耀斑期间吸积质量的约10⁻³。研究表明，时域分光偏振测量可将瞬态法拉第复杂性转化为诊断X射线双星喷流成分、质量加载和等离子体演化的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射电分光偏振测量可提供磁电离等离子体的互补探测手段，但该技术很少应用于瞬态喷流源。本文旨在通过时域分光偏振测量揭示黑洞X射线双星喷流的等离子体成分和质量加载。

Method: 利用MeerKAT L波段对黑洞X射线双星2023年爆发期间的最亮射电耀斑进行分光偏振测量，采用多种分光偏振技术识别瞬态法拉第复杂结构，并结合同步自吸收论证约束磁场和尺度。

Result: 发现与射电耀斑时间上紧密关联的瞬态法拉第复杂结构，排除星际介质或外部屏的起源，支持喷流本地起源。数据倾向于电子-质子主导的喷流成分，法拉第旋转质量约10²¹克，仅占耀斑期间吸积质量的~10⁻³。

Conclusion: 时域分光偏振测量可将瞬态法拉第复杂性转化为诊断喷流成分、质量加载和等离子体演化的有效工具，适用于X射线双星爆发及其他瞬态喷流源。

Abstract: Radio emission during X-ray binary outbursts is dominated by synchrotron radiation from relativistic jets, but is usually studied through total-intensity diagnostics such as flux density, spectra, variability, and proper motion. Radio spectropolarimetry provides a complementary probe of the magneto-ionic plasma through Faraday rotation and depolarisation. When the Faraday rotating material is local to the source, these effects can constrain the jet plasma composition and mass content, but this approach is rarely applied to transient jetted sources. We present MeerKAT L-band spectropolarimetry of the black hole X-ray binary \src\ during its 2023 outburst, focusing on the brightest radio flaring interval, when relativistic jets were being launched intermittently. Using multiple spectropolarimetric techniques, we identify transient Faraday-complex structure coincident with the major radio flares. The close temporal association with the flaring activity, together with the stability of the foreground Faraday screen, favours an origin local to the jet rather than in the ISM or in a separate local screen external to the emitting plasma. Since internal Faraday rotation is suppressed in a pure electron--positron plasma, the data favour a dominant electron--proton component. Interpreting the characteristic Faraday thickness as internal rotation, and anchoring the magnetic-field and size scales with synchrotron self-absorption arguments, we infer a characteristic Faraday-rotating mass of order $M_{\rm rot}\sim10^{21}{\rm\,g}$, corresponding to only a small fraction, $f_{\rm rot}\sim10^{-3}$, of the accreted mass available during the flare. These results show that time-domain spectropolarimetry can turn transient Faraday complexity into a diagnostic of jet composition, mass loading, and plasma evolution in X-ray binary outbursts, and potentially other transient jetted sources.

</details>


### [152] [Geminga and Monogem in the CTAO Era: Probing TeV Halos and Cosmic-Ray Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05245)
*Youyou Li,Oscar Macias,Manuela Vecchi,Shinichiro Ando*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用GALPROP v57为Geminga和Monogem构建双区扩散模型，模拟脉冲星自转减速注入的电子/正电子在慢扩散泡和星际介质中的传播，并通过CTAO官方响应函数进行正向折叠。研究表明，CTAO在50小时观测下能以13-30σ显著性探测这两个TeV晕，可区分高能注入谱指数变化Δγ₁≈0.2和磁场强度变化ΔB≈2 μG，且结果对银河弥散背景模型的不确定性具有鲁棒性（≤10%差异）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeV晕（如Geminga和Monogem）揭示了中年脉冲星周围存在强抑制扩散区域，确定这些慢扩散区的尺寸和磁场强度对建模轻子输运及评估其局部正电子超额的贡献至关重要。

Method: 使用GALPROP v57构建双区扩散模型，根据脉冲星自转减速历史注入电子和正电子，追踪其在演化中的慢扩散泡和星际介质中的传播；通过HAWC和Fermi-LAT测量校准模型，利用官方CTAO仪器响应函数正向折叠，并纳入弥散和仪器本底。

Result: CTAO在50小时观测下两晕检测显著性达13-30σ；可区分高能注入谱指数变化Δγ₁≈0.2和磁场强度变化ΔB≈2 μG；Monogem的紧凑泡（R_SDZ≈30 pc）可与扩展（≥50 pc）情况区分，但超大泡因视场限制呈简并；替换银河弥散模板对显著性及参数敏感性影响≤10%。

Conclusion: CTAO能够高显著性探测Geminga和Monogem的TeV晕，并以较高精度约束注入谱指数和磁场强度，且对弥散背景模型不确定性具有良好鲁棒性，为理解脉冲星周围粒子输运及其对正电子超额的贡献提供了关键观测能力。

Abstract: TeV halos around Geminga and Monogem (B0656+14) reveal regions of strongly suppressed diffusion near middle-aged pulsars. Determining the sizes and magnetic-field strengths of these slow-diffusion zones is important for modelling lepton transport and assessing their contribution to the local positron excess. The Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO) will provide arcminute-scale imaging of extended gamma-ray emission. We construct two-zone diffusion models for Geminga and Monogem with GALPROP v57, injecting electrons and positrons according to the pulsar spin-down history and following their propagation through an evolving slow-diffusion bubble and the ambient interstellar medium. Calibrating to HAWC and Fermi-LAT measurements, we forward-fold the models through official CTAO instrument response functions, including realistic diffuse and instrumental backgrounds. For 50 h observations, both halos are detected with high significance ($\sim 13$--$30,σ$) from either site. Under optimistic background assumptions, CTAO can distinguish changes of $Δγ_1 \simeq 0.2$ in the high-energy injection index and $ΔB \simeq 2,μ{\rm G}$ in magnetic-field strength. For Monogem, compact bubbles with $R_{\rm SDZ}\approx30,{\rm pc}$ can be distinguished from extended ($\gtrsim50,{\rm pc}$) cases, although very large bubbles remain degenerate because of the finite field of view. Using an alternative Galactic diffuse template in the fit changes detection significances and parameter sensitivities by $\lesssim10%$, indicating robustness against plausible diffuse-background mismodelling.

</details>


### [153] [The emergence of X-ray emission lines during relativistic radio-jet formation in the changing-look active galactic nucleus 1ES 1927+654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05246)
*Dev R. Sadaula,Sibasish Laha,Eileen T. Meyer,Onic I. Shuvo,Main Pal,Ritesh Ghosh,Matteo Guainazzi,Fabio Pacucci,Stefano Bianchi,Luigi Gallo,Rostom Mbarek,Amelia M. Hankla,Fabio La Franca,Tahir Yaqoob,Megan Masterson,Erin Kara,Missagh Mehdipour,Claudio Ricci,Javier A Garcia,Timothy R. Kallman,Ralf Ballhausen,Mitchell C. Begelman,Alexander Philippov,Suvendu Rakshit,Francesca Panessa,Ehud Behar,S. Bradley Cenko,Federica Ricci,Ilaria Villani,James N. Reeves*

TL;DR: 对变脸活动星系核1ES 1927+654在射电喷流形成与演化期间（2022年5月-2025年8月）的多波段监测结果显示：软X射线发射线出现且强度宽度变化，首次探测到Fe K发射线；电离吸收体先增强后减弱；喷流形成时电离外流减弱，表明吸积物质优先被引导入喷流而非外流；软X射线和射电流量大幅增加后趋于稳定；宽光学发射线缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究变脸活动星系核1ES 1927+654在射电喷流形成与演化过程中吸积盘-喷流-外流系统的物理状态变化，特别是内吸积流和喷流之间的能量与物质分配机制。

Method: 利用XMM-Newton、Swift、TNG、ZTF、VLA和VLBA等多波段观测设备，对目标源进行历时约3年的综合监测，通过对EPIC-pn和RGS数据的联合能谱拟合分析发射线、吸收体及其演化特征。

Result: 1) 软X射线0.56 keV和~1 keV发射线出现并随喷流形成而变化；2) 首次在该源中探测到宽Fe K发射线（~6-7 keV，宽度~800 eV）；3) 2022年存在电离吸收体，2023-2025年吸收减弱；4) 发射线出现与电离吸收减弱同步发生；5) 软X射线流量增大约10倍、5 GHz射电流量增大约60倍后趋于稳定；6) 宽光学发射线缺失。

Conclusion: 喷流形成过程中，吸积物质从被电离外流排出转向被优先引导进入喷流；吸积盘、日冕和喷流系统已趋于稳定构型；宽线区可能不在视线方向或未被充分照射。

Abstract: We present results from a comprehensive multi-wavelength monitoring campaign of the changing-look active galactic nucleus 1ES 1927+654 during the onset and evolution of a radio jet (May 2022-August 2025). Using observations from XMM-Newton, Swift, TNG, ZTF, VLA, and VLBA, we characterize the spectral evolution of the source. Soft X-ray emission lines at ~0.56 keV and ~1 keV have appeared with variable strength and width during the formation of a nascent jet, with the ~1 keV feature persisting since the post-2017 flare phase. We also report the detection of a broad (~800 eV) Fe K emission feature at ~(6-7) keV in ~70 ks of stacked EPIC-pn spectra, marking the first such detection in this historically featureless source. Joint spectral fitting of XMM-Newton EPIC-pn and RGS data reveals the presence of ionized absorbers in 2022, followed by weaker absorption from 2023 to 2025. The emergence of emission features concurrent with the decline of ionized absorption suggests a transition in the inner accretion and outflow processes, indicating the reflection and reprocessing of X-rays from the inner accretion disk during jet formation. The apparent weakening of ionized outflows as the jet develops supports a scenario in which accreting material is preferentially channeled into the jet rather than expelled as winds. Furthermore, both the 0.3-2 keV soft X-ray and 5 GHz radio fluxes, which have increased by factors of ~10 and ~60, respectively, since 2022, have recently plateaued at elevated levels. Combined with steady optical emission, this indicates a stabilized accretion disk, corona, and jet configuration. Finally, the absence of broad optical emission lines suggests that the broad-line region is either not along our line of sight or insufficiently illuminated by the central source.

</details>


### [154] [The disk luminosity deficit as a tracer of receding disk during Soft-to-Hard transitions in Black Hole X-ray Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05249)
*Sai-En Xu,Bei You,Yi Long,Zhen Yan,Andrzej A. Zdziarski,Rui-Xiang Hu,Alex Markowitz*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对26个黑洞X射线双星的RXTE/PCA观测数据系统分析，发现在软态到硬态转变过程中，薄吸积盘的外向后退会导致热光度亏缺，这为盘半径截断提供了经验示踪方法，并得到时变分析中特征频率下降的佐证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑洞X射线双星的软态到硬态转变过程中，传统光谱方法对吸积盘内半径的估计变得高度模型依赖，难以追踪薄盘的演化。

Method: 系统分析26个BHXRBs的RXTE/PCA观测数据，通过光度亏缺示踪盘后退，并结合功率密度谱中的宽带噪声和低频QPO特征频率进行时变分析验证。

Result: 24次爆发中软态盘光度呈指数衰减后显著低于基线；特征截断半径在转变过程中快速增大；特征频率随截断半径增大而下降；后退的起始和速度在不同源和爆发间差异显著。

Conclusion: 光度亏缺可作为软态到硬态转变中薄盘后退的实用经验示踪器，当直接光谱半径测量不可靠时尤为有效。

Abstract: Tracing the evolution of the thin accretion disk during the soft-to-hard state transition in black hole X-ray binaries (BHXRBs) remains difficult because conventional spectral estimates of the disk inner radius become highly model-dependent once the thermal component weakens. We present evidence that the thin disk recedes during this transition, obtained from a systematic study of RXTE/PCA observations of 26 BHXRBs. In 24 outbursts, the disk luminosity decays exponentially in the soft state, then drops significantly below the extrapolated baseline. This thermal luminosity deficit is considered a signature of reduced accretion efficiency, caused by the outward receding of the optically thick disk. Under this framework, we found that the estimated characteristic truncation radius increases rapidly as the systems evolve through the soft-to-hard transition. This interpretation is supported by timing analysis: in observations with well-constrained power density spectra, the characteristic frequencies of broadband noise and low-frequency QPOs generally decrease as the inferred truncation radius increases, consistent with the expansion of a hot inner flow. The onset and rapidity of recession vary substantially across different sources and outbursts. Our results demonstrate that luminosity deficits provide a practical empirical tracer of thin disk receding during soft-to-hard transitions, when direct spectral radius measurements become unreliable.

</details>


### [155] [AU or pc? Inferring the distance of magnetized plasma near FRBs from propagation diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05289)
*Wanjin Lu,Dongzi Li,Zhenyin Zhao,Fayin Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新方法，通过联合分析时间散射、去偏振和法拉第旋转测量变化，来估算FRB磁活跃环境的物理尺度。对多个重复爆发源的应用表明，部分FRB倾向于超新星遗迹尺度的磁环境，而另一些则可能与双星结构相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复FRB被发现处于与典型星际介质显著不同的磁活跃环境中，这对其起源和演化路径至关重要。不同模型（如伴星风、年轻超新星遗迹）对磁活跃环境的空间尺度有不同预测，而目前缺乏有效方法来区分这些模型。

Method: 通过联合分析重复FRB的三个观测量——时间散射(τ_scat)、去偏振(σ_RM)和法拉第旋转测量变化(|ΔRM/Δt|)——来推断磁活跃区域的物理尺度。该方法系统应用于所有具有多次RM测量的活跃重复源。

Result: FRB 20190303A、FRB 20190417A和FRB 20190520B的推断距离倾向于超新星遗迹尺度的磁环境；FRB 20180916B和FRB 20201124A在合理假设下仍允许双星尺度的结构。但当前采样较粗且不确定性较大。

Conclusion: 该方法有潜力系统区分FRB磁环境的起源并约束前身星演化，但需要更好的传播效应采样以及未来CHORD和DSA等设备提供的同步宽带多效应测量。

Abstract: Fast Radio Bursts (FRBs) are highly energetic, millisecond-duration radio transients. A significant fraction of repeating FRBs are found in magneto-active environments significantly different from typical interstellar medium, offering important insights into their origins and evolutionary pathways. Possible explanations range from companion winds to young magneto-active supernvae remnants. The spatial scales of the magneto-active environment is a major distinction of different models. In this work, we present a new method to estimate the physical scale of the magneto-active region surrounding FRBs by jointly analyzing measurements of temporal scattering ($τ_\mathrm{scat}$), depolarization ($σ_\mathrm{RM}$), and Faraday rotation measure (RM) variations ($\left|Δ\mathrm{RM}/Δt\right|$) in repeating sources. We systematically apply this method to all active repeaters with multiple RM measurements. Despite the coarse sampling and large uncertainties, the inferred distances tentatively favor SNR-scale magneto-environments for FRB 20190303A, FRB 20190417A, and FRB 20190520B, while still allowing binary-scale structures for FRB 20180916B and FRB 20201124A under plausible assumptions. Better sampling of propagation effects, together with future advances in simultaneous wideband measurements of multiple effects with CHORD and the DSA, has the potential to systematic discrimination among the origins of FRB magneto-environments and constrain progenitor evolution.

</details>
