### [156] [RTL Fault Injection of a Deployed Graph Neural Network Trigger for Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03153)
*Georgios Sotiropoulos,Marc Neu,Tanja Harbaum,Torben Ferber,Jürgen Becker*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次在寄存器传输层对Belle II触发系统中的硬件神经网络触发器GNN-ETM进行故障注入研究，通过注入1,442,840个单粒子翻转，发现现有验证基础设施存在监控不对称性，并提出级间活跃度监控作为更准确的替代方案，两种方法的平均故障时间估计差异高达78.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理实验中，Level-1触发系统基于FPGA实现并越来越多地使用神经网络算法，但其靠近对撞点易受辐射影响，可能导致输出错误、处理流水线停滞或硬件损坏，造成严重的科学和经济后果。现有研究缺乏对部署在真实硬件上的神经网络触发器的故障注入分析。

Method: 采用寄存器传输级故障注入方法，针对实时触发流水线中最关键的三种失效模式（死锁、超时、数据包完整性违规）进行测试。通过两次互补的实验，在211,245个信号上注入1,442,840个单粒子翻转，比较输出端观察与级间活跃度监控两种评估方式。

Result: 发现现有验证基础设施存在监控不对称性问题；级间活跃度监控相比输出端观察更准确，两种方法得出的平均故障时间估计差异最高达78.7%；获得了各阶段的故障数据，识别出最高优先级的加固目标。

Conclusion: 级间活跃度监控是评估辐射对实时触发流水线影响更准确的方法，基于阶段性故障数据可有效指导硬件加固优先级。

Abstract: As particle physics detectors grow in scale, High Energy Physics experiments must process ever-increasing data volumes. Level-1 trigger systems, implemented on Field-Programmable Gate Arrays and increasingly using neural-network algorithms, filter this data in real time. However, their proximity to the interaction point exposes them to radiation, which can corrupt outputs, stall processing pipelines, or damage hardware, with significant financial and scientific consequences. In this work, we present the first Register Transfer Level fault-injection study of a deployed Level-1 hardware neural-network trigger, GNN-ETM in the Belle II trigger system. We target three failure modes most consequential to a real-time trigger pipeline: deadlocks, timeouts, and packet-integrity violations. Through two complementary campaigns, we inject 1 442 840 Single-Event Upsets across 211 245 signals. We find a monitoring asymmetry in the existing verification infrastructure and propose inter-stage liveness monitoring as a more accurate alternative to output-only observation, showing that Mean Time To Failure estimates from the two approaches differ by up to 78.7%. The resulting per-stage data identifies the highest-priority hardening targets.

</details>


### [157] [Searching for Dark Photons with a room-temperature dielectric haloscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03240)
*Siyin Li,Paschos Ioannis,Zhengyin Yang,Alexandros Spiliotis,Husheng Guan,Pavlos G. Savvidis,Shengchao Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文使用室温介质多层谐振腔实验搜索暗光子暗物质，未发现超出，在1.9 eV/c²质量处设定了κ<4.0×10⁻¹³的90%置信度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗光子是暗物质的一种候选粒子，目前尚未被直接探测到。本文旨在利用新型介质多层谐振腔技术，在可见光波段（约2 eV）对暗光子暗物质进行高灵敏度搜索。

Method: 采用室温介质多层谐振腔（dielectric multilayer haloscope）增强约2 eV附近的暗光子-光子转换效率；使用空间分辨CMOS焦平面探测器以单光子级灵敏度记录发射光子；通过原位标定堆叠-透镜-CMOS系统响应，利用标定后的焦平面模式进行模板化统计推断。共采集904小时搜索数据和404小时背景控制数据。

Result: 未观测到超出背景的过量信号，在90%置信水平下，对质量1.9 eV/c²的暗光子暗物质设定耦合强度上限κ<4.0×10⁻¹³。

Conclusion: 该实验在可见光波段对暗光子暗物质给出了迄今最严格的直接探测上限之一，验证了室温介质多层谐振腔方案在亚eV暗物质搜索中的可行性。

Abstract: We present a search for dark-photon dark matter with a room-temperature dielectric multilayer haloscope. The dielectric stack enhances photon conversion near 2 eV, and a spatially resolved CMOS focal plane records the emitted photons with few-photon sensitivity. We calibrate the stack-lens-CMOS response in situ and use the calibrated focal-plane pattern in a template-based inference. With 904 h of search data and 404 h of background-control data, we observe no excess and set a 90% confidence-level upper limit of $κ< 4.0\times10^{-13}$ for dark-photon dark matter with mass 1.9$\,\mathrm{eV}/c^2$.

</details>


### [158] [Flow-Based Surrogates for High-Dimensional Likelihoods in Experimental Neutrino Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03477)
*Mathias El Baz,Lorenzo Giannessi,Adrien Blanchet,Federico Sánchez*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出使用归一化流（Normalizing Flows）为长基线中微子实验的近探测器似然函数构建高保真且可移植的概率模型。通过结合耦合变换与自回归样条流的混合架构，在110个系统不确定性参数（含10个非高斯参数）的测试中，模型有效样本量达98%，远超高斯近似的5%，且保持闭合形式、可采样和逐点评估特性，适用于下游不确定性传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确长基线中微子实验依赖近探测器数据约束未振荡中微子通量的系统不确定性，但高维、非高斯约束似然的处理需要先进的统计方法。现有高斯近似有效样本量低（~5%），无法忠实表征后验分布。

Method: 利用初始高斯近似作为基础，训练一种混合架构：结合耦合变换（Coupling Transformations）与自回归样条流（Autoregressive Spline Flows）的归一化流模型，对110维参数空间（含10个明确引入非高斯性的参数）的近探测器似然副本进行建模。

Result: 训练后的模型相对有效样本量达到98%（高斯近似仅~5%），忠实复现MCMC参考结果，且保持闭合形式、可采样和逐点评估的数学性质。

Conclusion: 归一化流可为高维非高斯似然提供高保真、可移植的近似模型，适用于下游不确定性传播及近-远探测器联合拟合。

Abstract: Precision long-baseline neutrino experiments use near-detector data to constrain systematic uncertainties on the unoscillated neutrino flux, a prerequisite for accurate oscillation parameter measurements at the far detector. When the constrained likelihood is high-dimensional and non-Gaussian, this procedure demands advanced statistical treatment. Here we show that normalizing flows provide faithful and portable likelihood models for this problem. Leveraging an initial Gaussian approximation of the likelihood, we train a hybrid architecture combining coupling transformations and autoregressive spline flows. We demonstrate the method on a representative near-detector likelihood replica with 110 systematic uncertainty parameters, 10 of which explicitly introduce non-Gaussianities in the posterior. The trained model achieves a relative effective sample size of 98%, compared with about 5% for the Gaussian approximation, and reproduces a Markov chain Monte Carlo reference while remaining closed-form, samplable, and pointwise evaluable, making it suited to downstream uncertainty propagation and future near-detector to far-detector fits.

</details>


### [159] [GPU-Accelerated Matrix-Based Hough Transform for Online Track Reconstruction in the STCF MDC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04067)
*Liang Peng,Baolin Zhang,Zhaoli Guo,Jiarui Zhao,Yizhen Ma,Aonan Zhu,Huilin Li,Zhujun Fang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文针对STCF的MDC实时径迹重建问题，提出了一种GPU加速的矩阵化Hough变换方法，通过算法-架构协同设计将不规则参数空间计算重组为规则矩阵运算，在5个模拟物理通道上实现了93.04%的信号保留率、将保留击中体积降至34.92%，GPU处理1000事件仅需0.14秒，较CPU基线加速151.57倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STCF作为下一代高亮度正负电子对撞机，其高事件率、探测器占用率和本底水平对MDC中低横动量粒子的实时径迹重建提出了严苛要求，尤其是强弯曲或多圈轨迹的重建挑战亟需解决。

Method: 采用算法-架构协同设计范式，将共形Hough变换的数据表示和计算流程重构为适合GPU执行的规则矩阵运算，并针对CUDA线程组织和GPU内存层次结构优化核心计算，以利用Hough变换的固有并行性并降低计算和数据传输开销。

Result: 在5个模拟物理通道（含标称本底叠加）上，平均信号保留率93.04%，保留击中体积降至原始水平的34.92%；GPU处理1000事件约0.14秒，相比CPU基线加速151.57倍。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提高了径迹重建吞吐量同时保留径迹关联击中，为未来高亮度粒子对撞机实验的实时径迹重建提供了新的方法论视角。

Abstract: The Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF) is a proposed next-generation high-luminosity electron-positron collider operating at center-of-mass energies of 2-7 GeV for precision studies of tau-charm physics. Its high event rate, detector occupancy, and background level impose stringent requirements on real-time track reconstruction in MDC, particularly for low-transverse-momentum particles with strongly curved or multi-turn trajectories. To address this challenge, we develop a GPU-accelerated matrix-based Hough transform method for online track reconstruction in the STCF MDC. Following an algorithm-architecture co-design paradigm, the data representation and computational workflow of the conformal Hough transform are reformulated for GPU execution. The original irregular parameter-space computations are organized into regular matrix-based operations, and the core computations are adapted to CUDA thread organization and the GPU memory hierarchy to exploit the inherent parallelism of the Hough transform and reduce computational and data-transfer overhead. Tests on five representative simulated physics channels with nominal background overlay show an average signal retention ratio of 93.04%, while reducing the retained hit volume to 34.92% of the original level. The GPU implementation processes 1,000 events in approximately 0.14s, achieving a speedup of 151.57 x compared with the CPU baseline. These results demonstrate that the proposed method substantially improves track reconstruction throughput while preserving track-associated hits, providing a new methodological perspective for real-time track reconstruction in future high-luminosity particle-collider experiments.

</details>


### [160] [Neural-Network-Assisted Binary Template Construction for Matrix-Based Pattern Matching in the STCF MDC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04271)
*Zhaoli Guo,Qingyu Li,Baolin Zhang,Jiarui Zhao,Aonan Zhu,Liang Peng,Huilin Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经网络辅助的二进制模板库构建框架，用于Super Tau-Charm Facility主漂移室局部径迹段查找的矩阵模式匹配。该框架将模板构建转化为可微多目标优化问题，训练后仅导出二进制模板对部署到现有按位模式匹配例程中，无需运行时神经网络推理，在有限探测效率下保持高信号保留率，并可根据实际需求灵活调整覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Super Tau-Charm Facility高亮度运行产生的高事件率和高数据吞吐量，对高速径迹查找和数据压缩算法提出了严格要求。主漂移室局部径迹段查找是高背景率和有限探测效率下容易产生假触发和信号丢失的关键环节，亟需一种既能保持在线算法确定性、快速性和可并行性，又能提升物理性能的优化方案。

Method: 将模板构建形式化为可微多目标优化问题，使用神经网络联合学习多约束下的模板参数；训练完成后仅导出二进制模板对，部署到现有的按位模式匹配例程中，运行时无需神经网络推理，保持在线算法的确定性和高速性。

Result: 基于模拟样本的实验结果表明，在有限探测效率下，生成的模板库在不同横动量范围和背景水平下均保持较高的信号保留率，且可根据实际需求灵活调整覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 该方法通过离线神经网络优化提升物理性能与在线算法的确定性和高速度相结合，解耦了物理性能与计算速度之间的权衡，为高亮度粒子对撞机实验中人工智能增强的在线数据处理提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: The Super Tau-Charm Facility, operating at high luminosity, will produce high event rates and high data throughput, imposing stringent requirements on fast track finding and data reduction and compression algorithms in the High-Level Trigger. Local track segment finding in the Main Drift Chamber underpins subsequent segment combination and full track reconstruction, yet high background rates and limited detection efficiency can significantly increase the risk of false triggers and signal loss in pattern matching algorithms. This paper presents a neural-network-assisted framework for constructing binary template libraries used in matrix-based pattern matching for MDC local track segment finding. The framework formulates template construction as a differentiable multi-objective optimization problem, employing a neural network to jointly learn template parameters under multiple constraints. After training, only binary template pairs are exported and deployed into the existing bitwise pattern matching routine, requiring no neural network inference at runtime and thus preserving the deterministic, fast, and parallelizable nature of the online algorithm. Experimental results based on simulation samples demonstrate that, under limited detection efficiency, the resulting template library maintains relatively high signal retention across different transverse momentum ranges and background levels, and can be flexibly tailored to adjust the coverage range according to practical requirements. The proposed approach decouples the physics performance from the computational speed by combining the improved physics performance brought by offline neural-network-based optimization with the determinism and high speed of a conventional online algorithm, suggesting a new research direction for artificial-intelligence-enhanced online data processing in high-luminosity particle collider experiments.

</details>

Z. Yuan,J. Yuan,L. Yuan,Y. Yusa,L. Zani,M. Zeyrek,B. Zhang,X. Zhao,V. Zhilich,J. S. Zhou,Q. D. Zhou,L. Zhu,R. Žlebčík*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用Belle探测器采集的142.5 fb⁻¹数据样本，在质心能量10.73-11.02 GeV范围内研究了e⁺e⁻→Υ_J(1D)η和e⁺e⁻→Υ_J(1D)π⁺π⁻过程。测量了Υ(10860)共振峰附近（√s=10.866 GeV）的Born截面乘以分支比乘积，以及Υ_J(1D)态的分支比。Υ_1(1D)、Υ_2(1D)和Υ_3(1D)信号显著性分别为4.8σ、>10σ和3.0σ。精确测量了Υ_2(1D)的质量为(10167.0±1.0±0.2) MeV/c²，并给出了不同态之间的质量分裂。还确定了能量依赖的联合截面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究底夸克偶素Υ(1D)态的产生和衰变性质，验证重夸克偶素谱系的量子色动力学（QCD）理论预言，特别是探索Υ(10860)的奇特衰变行为以及可能存在的新的激发态结构。

Method: 使用Belle探测器在KEKB非对称能量e⁺e⁻对撞机上采集的142.5 fb⁻¹数据样本（包含122 fb⁻¹的Υ(10860)共振峰附近数据），分析e⁺e⁻→Υ_J(1D)η和e⁺e⁻→Υ_J(1D)π⁺π⁻过程，通过测量Born截面与分支比的乘积以及Υ(10860)衰变分支比来研究Υ_J(1D)态，并利用截面能量依赖性分析进行联合拟合。

Result: Υ_1(1D)、Υ_2(1D)和Υ_3(1D)信号显著性分别为4.8σ、>10σ和3.0σ（含系统误差）。Υ_2(1D)质量测量为(10167.0±1.0±0.2) MeV/c²。质量分裂Δm₁₂=(11.8±1.5±0.4) MeV/c²，Δm₂₃=(7.6±2.4±0.6) MeV/c²。获得了Born截面×分支比乘积及Υ(10860)衰变分支比的测量值。

Conclusion: 成功观测到e⁺e⁻→Υ_J(1D)η和e⁺e⁻→Υ_J(1D)π⁺π⁻过程，首次对多个Υ(1D)态进行了系统的联合分析，精确测量了Υ_2(1D)质量及其与相邻态的质量分裂，为理解重夸克偶素激发态结构提供了重要实验数据。

Abstract: We study the processes $e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)η$ and $e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)π^+π^-$ at center-of-mass energies $\sqrt{s}$=(10.73 -- 11.02) GeV using a $142.5\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ data sample, including 122~fb$^{-1}$ near the $Υ$(10860) peak ($\sqrt{s}$ = 10.866 GeV), collected with the Belle detector at the KEKB asymmetric-energy $e^+e^-$ collider. From the peak sample, the products of Born cross section times branching fraction are obtained for $σ_{\rm Born}(e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)η)$ or $σ_{\rm Born}(e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)π^+π^-)$ and ${\cal B}(Υ_J(1D)\toχ_{b1}γ)$ or ${\cal B}(Υ_J(1D)\toχ_{b2}γ)$ for each $Υ_J(1D)$ state. The corresponding branching fractions for $Υ(10860)$ decays are also obtained. The significances of the $Υ_1(1D)$, $Υ_2(1D)$, and $Υ_3(1D)$ signals are 4.8$σ$, ${>}10σ$, and 3.0$σ$, respectively, including systematic uncertainties. The mass for $Υ_2(1D)$ is measured to be $(10167.0\pm 1.0\pm 0.2)$ MeV/$c^2$, where the first and second uncertainties are statistical and systematic. The mass splittings $Δm_{12}=m(Υ_2(1D))-m(Υ_1(1D))$ and $Δm_{23}=m(Υ_3(1D))-m(Υ_2(1D))$ are $(11.8\pm1.5\pm0.4)$ MeV/$c^2$ and $(7.6\pm2.4\pm0.6)$ MeV/$c^2$, respectively.~We determine the energy dependence of the cross sections for $e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)η$ and $e^+e^-\toΥ_J(1D)π^+π^-$ for the $Υ_1(1D)$, $Υ_2(1D)$, and $Υ_3(1D)$ states, combined.

</details>


### [162] [Standard Model Soft and Hard QCD at ATLAS and CMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04767)
*Maciej Trzebinski*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了ATLAS和CMS实验在LHC质子-质子碰撞中对QCD的最新测量结果，涵盖从软强子相互作用到硬部分子散射的广泛动量尺度，包括带电粒子事件形状、双喷注产生、双部分子散射以及前向质子探测器在衍射和光子诱导过程中的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC宽广的能量尺度上系统探索QCD的各个方面，从软QCD动力学到微扰QCD检验，以及多部分子相互作用和衍射过程，以全面理解强相互作用。

Method: 利用ATLAS和CMS实验在LHC质子-质子碰撞中收集的数据，对软QCD（带电粒子事件形状可观测量）、硬QCD（双喷注产生）、多部分子相互作用（同号WW产生中的双部分子散射）以及前向质子探测器（衍射和光子诱导过程）进行测量和分析。

Result: 展示了从最小偏差事件中带电粒子事件形状（软QCD）到双喷注产生（微扰QCD检验）的广泛动量尺度测量结果，以及同号WW产生中双部分子散射的新见解，并回顾了前向质子探测器的能力。

Conclusion: ATLAS和CMS实验的QCD测量覆盖了从软到硬的广泛能量尺度，为理解强相互作用提供了全面约束，其中双部分子散射和前向质子探测器为多部分子相互作用和衍射/光子诱导过程提供了新视角。

Abstract: Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) is explored at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) over a wide range of energy scales, from soft hadronic interactions to hard parton scattering. This contribution presents a selection of recent measurements from the ATLAS and CMS experiments probing QCD in proton--proton collisions at the LHC. The results span a broad range of momentum scales, from studies of charged-particle event-shape observables in minimum-bias events, which are sensitive to soft QCD dynamics and hadronisation, to measurements of inclusive di-jet production that provide stringent tests of perturbative QCD. Recent studies of double-parton scattering in same-sign $WW$ production are also discussed, offering new insight into multi-parton interactions inside the proton. Finally, the capabilities of the forward proton detectors operated by the ATLAS and CMS collaborations are briefly reviewed, highlighting their contribution to the LHC physics programme through measurements of diffractive and photon-induced processes.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII探测器采集的约27亿ψ(3686)事件，首次观测到χ_{cJ} (J=0,1,2) → pK⁻Λ̅η + c.c. 衰变过程（统计显著性均超过5σ），测量了分支比，并在不变质量谱中观测到Λ(1520)和Λ̅(1690)超子共振结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次观测χ_{cJ}到包含重子-反重子对的五体衰变过程，为理解P波粲偶素强子衰变、重子产生机制以及非微扰QCD区域的强子化动力学提供新的实验信息。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上收集的(2712.4±14.3)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事件，通过分析χ_{cJ} → pK⁻Λ̅η + c.c. 衰变，采用最大似然拟合提取信号，并对统计和系统误差进行了评估。

Result: 三个χ_{cJ}态的分支比测量结果分别为：χ_{c0} (5.3±0.7±0.5)×10⁻⁵，χ_{c1} (9.8±0.6±0.6)×10⁻⁵，χ_{c2} (9.3±0.6±0.6)×10⁻⁵；在pK⁻和Λ̅η不变质量谱中分别观测到Λ(1520)和Λ̅(1690)超子共振结构。

Conclusion: 这些结果首次提供了χ_{cJ}到pK⁻Λ̅η衰变的实验数据，丰富了P波粲偶素强子衰变的研究，并为非微扰QCD区域的重子产生和强子化动力学提供了新约束。

Abstract: By analyzing $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^{6}$ $ψ(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, the decays $χ_{cJ} \to pK^{-}\barΛη+ \mathrm{c.c.}$ ($J=0,1,2$) are observed for the first time, with statistical significances exceeding $5σ$ for all three $χ_{cJ}$ states. The measured branching fractions are $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c0} \to pK^{-}\barΛη+ \mathrm{c.c.}) = (5.3 \pm 0.7 \pm 0.5) \times 10^{-5}$, $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c1} \to pK^{-}\barΛη+ \mathrm{c.c.}) = (9.8 \pm 0.6 \pm 0.6) \times 10^{-5}$, and $\mathcal{B}(χ_{c2} \to pK^{-}\barΛη+ \mathrm{c.c.}) = (9.3 \pm 0.6 \pm 0.6) \times 10^{-5}$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second are systematic. Structures consistent with the known hyperon resonances $Λ(1520)$ and $\barΛ(1690)$ are seen in the $pK^{-}$ and $\barΛη$ invariant mass spectra, respectively. The reported branching fractions include both resonant and non-resonant contributions. These results provide new experimental information on hadronic decays of $P$-wave charmonium states and contribute to the understanding of baryon production and hadronization dynamics in the nonperturbative QCD regime.

</details>


### [164] [Search for nonresonant triple Higgs boson production in the final state with six bottom quarks in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05145)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验在138 fb⁻¹的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据中搜索非共振三希格斯玻色子（HHH）产生（末态为六个底夸克），未观测到显著超出背景的事例，在95%置信水平上设定信号截面上限为标准模型预期的588倍（观测）/572倍（预期），并对三线性耦合修饰因子κ₃和四线性耦合修饰因子κ₄给出迄今为止最严格的约束，首次排除了部分微扰幺正性允许的(κ₃,κ₄)参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测非共振三希格斯玻色子（HHH）产生过程，检验标准模型中希格斯势能的形状，特别是三线性自耦合（κ₃）和四线性自耦合（κ₄）修饰因子，从而探索超出标准模型的新物理。

Method: 利用CMS探测器收集的138 fb⁻¹积分亮度、质心能量13 TeV的质子-质子对撞数据，选择HHH→6b（六个底夸克）末态进行搜索，通过统计方法将观测数据与标准模型背景预测进行比较，设定95%置信水平上限并约束κ₃和κ₄参数空间。

Result: 未发现显著超出标准模型背景的迹象。观测（预期）信号截面上限为44 (43) fb，对应588 (572)倍标准模型预期。对κ₃的观测（预期）约束为-7.4 < κ₃ < 12.4 (-6.4 < κ₃ < 11.2)（假设κ₄=1）；对κ₄的约束为-177 < κ₄ < 185 (-180 < κ₄ < 190)（假设κ₃=1）。

Conclusion: 该分析提供了迄今为止对非共振HHH产生的最严格约束，并首次排除了部分此前由微扰幺正性边界允许的(κ₃,κ₄)参数空间区域，对理解希格斯势能和电弱对称性破缺机制具有重要意义。

Abstract: A search for nonresonant triple Higgs boson (HHH) production in the final state with six bottom quarks is performed using proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$ recorded by the CMS experiment. No significant excess of events over the background prediction is seen. Observed (expected) 95% confidence level upper limits on the signal cross section are set at 44 (43) fb, corresponding to 588 (572) times the standard model expectation. The observed (expected) constraint on the trilinear coupling modifier $κ_3$ is $-$7.4 $\lt$ $κ_3$ $\lt$ 12.4 ($-$6.4 $\lt$ κ_3 $\lt$ 11.2), assuming the quartic coupling modifier $κ_4$ = 1. The corresponding constraint on $κ_4$ is $-$177 $\lt$ $κ_4$ $\lt$ 185 ($-$180 $\lt$ $κ_4$ $\lt$ 190), assuming $κ_3$ = 1. This analysis provides the most stringent constraint to date on nonresonant HHH production and excludes, for the first time, part of the $(κ_3,κ_4)$ space allowed by the perturbative unitarity bound.

</details>


### [165] [A three-step proposal for searching for light shining through walls in the X-ray band at the High Energy Photon Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05288)
*R. T. Chang,S. Feng,X. P. Geng,C. Y. Hu,H. T. Hu,Y. Y. Hu,Y. H. Huang,B. Liao,F. L. Liu,P. Luan,S. S. Lv,M. L. Qiu,S. K. Shao,Q. L. Shuai,Q. Tang,H. R. Wang,D. Wu,M. Y. Wu,J. S. Xie,Z. H. Zhang,Z. H. Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出利用HEPS同步辐射光源的ID21硬X射线束线，通过三步式"穿墙光"实验方案在1 eV-400 keV质量范围内搜寻暗光子等弱相互作用超轻粒子，可覆盖现有X射线LSW实验未探索的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质的基本物理性质尚不明确，现有观测证据无法揭示其微观本质，需要模型依赖性低、纯实验室的方法来探测暗扇区粒子。

Method: 提出了三步LSW实验方案：(1)短期数天专用曝光实验；(2)长期数年同步伴随实验；(3)强磁场下WISP探测。利用HEPS ID21束线的高亮度、高能X射线作为光源。

Result: 该方案可有效约束1 eV-400 keV质量范围内的暗光子参数，覆盖现有X射线LSW实验尚未探索的参数空间。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了模型依赖性最低、最纯粹的实验室方法来探测暗扇区粒子，可逐步推进标准模型之外的新物理研究。

Abstract: Despite compelling observational evidence for dark matter (DM), its fundamental physical properties remain poorly understood. In this report, we propose a three-step light-shining-through-walls (LSW) experimental scheme utilizing the high-brilliance, high-energy X-rays from the ID21 Hard X-ray Imaging Beamline at the High Energy Photon Source (HEPS) to search for signatures of dark photons (DPs) and other weakly interacting slim particles (WISPs). The scheme includes three steps of LSW experiments: a short-term (several days) dedicated exposure experiment, a long-term (several years) synchronous accompanying experiment, and a WISP detection with strong magnetic fields. Projection results show that this HEPS-based LSW experiment can effectively constrain DP parameters in the 1 eV--400 keV mass range, covering unexploited parameter space of the existing X-ray LSW experiments. It provides a least model-dependent and most purely-laboratory approach for probing dark sector particles and advancing new physics research beyond the Standard Model gradually.

</details>


### [166] [Measurement of the $b$-jet identification efficiency in dileptonic $t\bar{t}$ events using proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV collected with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05322)
*The ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文展示了基于Transformer的GN2算法在ATLAS实验中进行b-喷注标记的性能，利用2022-2023年LHC质子-质子对撞数据（√s=13.6 TeV，积分亮度56 fb⁻¹），在ttbar事件富集样本中同时提取b-喷注识别效率和喷注味组成。GN2算法相比前代DL1d性能显著提升，对轻味和粲味喷注的拒绝因子分别提高至两倍和三倍。测量效率与模拟比较得到0.9-1.3的修正因子，在累计效率区间[65%, 0%]内喷注横动量>60 GeV时总不确定性约1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对基于Transformer的GN2算法进行基于数据的性能标定，测量b-喷注识别效率及喷注味组成，并与前代算法对比，验证新算法在实际数据中的表现。

Method: 利用2022-2023年ATLAS采集的质子-质子对撞数据（√s=13.6 TeV，56 fb⁻¹），在ttbar事件富集样本中通过同时提取方法测量b-喷注识别效率和喷注味组成，效率按喷注横动量20-400 GeV及六个累计效率区间[100%,90%]、[90%,85%]、[85%,77%]、[77%,70%]、[70%,65%]、[65%,0%]进行测量。

Result: GN2算法相比DL1d对轻味喷注拒绝因子提高至两倍，对粲味喷注拒绝因子提高至三倍。数据与模拟比较得到0.9-1.3的修正因子，在累计效率区间[65%,0%]且喷注横动量>60 GeV时总不确定性约1%。

Conclusion: GN2算法在b-喷注识别性能上显著优于前代算法，基于数据的标定结果提供了可靠的效率修正因子，为后续ATLAS物理分析中b-喷注标记的精确应用提供了支持。

Abstract: This paper presents the performance of the identification of jets containing $b$-hadrons ($b$-jets) for the GN2 algorithm, a transformer-based model for jet flavour tagging, using data collected by the ATLAS detector at the LHC. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data recorded in 2022 and 2023 at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 56 fb$^{-1}$. The $b$-jet identification efficiency and jet flavour composition are extracted simultaneously from a sample enriched in top-quark pair events ($t\bar{t}$). This efficiency is measured as a function of the jet transverse momentum in the range of 20-400 GeV and across six intervals of cumulative efficiency as derived in simulated $t\bar{t}$ events: [100%, 90%], [90%, 85%], [85%, 77%], [77%, 70%], [70%, 65%], and [65%, 0%]. The GN2 algorithm demonstrates significant performance improvements over its predecessor DL1d, achieving up to a factor of two (three) higher rejection of light-flavour (charm-flavour) jets at the same $b$-jet efficiency. The measured efficiencies in data are compared with simulation to derive correction factors ranging from 0.9 to 1.3. The total uncertainty is around 1% for jets with transverse momentum larger than 60 GeV in the [65%, 0%] interval of cumulative efficiency.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Homotopy minimal periods for fiber maps on $S^{1}$-bundles over $S^{1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02769)
*Weslem Liberato Silva,Rafael Moreira de Souza*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文针对纤维丛Y→M→B上的纤维映射f，定义了相对于基空间B的**同伦最小周期**概念HBPer(f)，并完全刻画了当底空间和纤维均为S¹（圆）时，HS¹Per(f)的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动力系统与拓扑学中，映射的周期点研究是一个核心问题。现有工作多关注绝对情形下的同伦最小周期，但对于纤维丛上保持纤维结构的映射，缺乏相应的理论工具。本文旨在将同伦最小周期的概念推广到纤维映射的场景，并给出可计算的刻画。

Method: 首先在一般纤维丛框架下定义了HBPer(f)的概念（即相对于基空间B的同伦最小周期集）；然后聚焦于底空间和纤维均为S¹的特殊情形，利用圆上的拓扑性质和纤维映射的结构约束，给出HS¹Per(f)的完整分类与计算描述。

Result: 成功定义了纤维映射的同伦最小周期HBPer(f)；在M为以S¹为底且以S¹为纤维的纤维丛这一特例下，得到了HS¹Per(f)的完整刻画——即所有可能出现的周期集合被完全确定。

Conclusion: 本文的工作将同伦最小周期理论从绝对情形推广到了相对（纤维）情形，并在一个典型的非平凡情形（双圆丛）中给出了完全的答案，为后续研究更一般纤维丛上的周期点问题提供了基础。

Abstract: Given $Y \to M \stackrel{p}{\to} B$ a fiber bundle and $f: M \to M$ a fiber map over $B$, we introduce the definition of homotopy minimal periods for $f$ denoted by $H_{B}Per(f).$ In the case where $M$ is a fiber bundle with base and fiber $S^{1}$ we presented a complete description of $H_{S^{1}}Per(f).$

</details>


### [168] [Homotopic distances and group-like spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03484)
*Ekansh Jauhari,John Oprea*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了同伦距离的多种变体（包括概率化版本和对角化版本），并证明了当目标空间为类群空间或CW H-空间时，所有版本的同伦距离都可以用相应版本的Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴精确计算，并在有理群设定下得到了精细化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同伦距离是一个数值同伦不变量，用于量化两个或多个连续映射之间的同伦差异。本文旨在发展同伦距离的概率化版本和对角化版本理论，并探究其在类群空间和CW H-空间上的计算性质。

Method: (1) 发展了同伦距离的概率化版本和对角化版本理论，并比较了它们的性质；(2) 证明了当目标空间为类群空间或CW H-空间时，所有版本的同伦距离可以用相应版本的Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴精确计算；(3) 在有理群设定下对结果进行了精细化；(4) 研究了映射的环路化以及到类群空间的映射同伦类集合的幂零性。

Result: 成功建立了同伦距离的多种变体与相应版本的Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴之间的等价关系，特别是在类群空间和CW H-空间情形下；在有理群设定下获得了更精细的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作系统地发展了同伦距离的概率化与对角化版本，揭示了其在特殊空间类上与Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴的深刻联系，为同伦距离的精确计算提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Homotopic distance is a numerical homotopy invariant that quantifies the homotopic distinction between two or more continuous maps. In this paper, we look at various versions of homotopic distance between maps and study them on maps to group-like spaces and CW H-spaces. In particular, (1) we develop the theories of probabilistic and diagonalized versions of the homotopic distance and compare their properties, and (2) we show that all versions of the homotopic distance can be calculated precisely in terms of their respective versions of the Lusternik-Schnirelmann category when group-like spaces or CW H-spaces are involved. Our results are refined in the setting of rational groups. On the way, we also study loopings of maps and the nilpotency of the set of homotopy classes of maps to group-like spaces.

</details>


### [169] [A Note on the 2-Local Homotopy Types of $G_2$-Gauge Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03684)
*Dang Vo Phuc*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文对Kameko关于$G_2$-规范群在$S^4$上的2-局部分类定理的证明进行了数学修正，包括将同构断言修正为正确的2-局部形式、解决EHP序列中Hopf不变量的单射问题，以及明确Postnikov层约定，确认修正后分类定理仍然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kameko近期在2-局部分类$G_2$-规范群的工作中存在若干关键步骤需要数学上的精炼和局部化澄清，包括积分同构断言的修正、EHP序列延拓问题以及Leray-Serre谱序列计算中Postnikov层约定的明确化。

Method: 通过将积分同构断言修正为正确的2-局部形式，解决EHP序列中关于Hopf不变量的单射性延拓问题，并明确mod 2 Leray-Serre谱序列计算所需的Postnikov层约定。

Result: 经过上述调整，确认Kameko在文献中的主要2-局部分类定理仍然成立。

Conclusion: 本文提供的修正并未破坏原分类定理的有效性，而是为其提供了必要的数学严谨性支撑。

Abstract: In a recent preprint \cite{Kameko2026} , Kameko presented a substantial completion of the 2-local classification of $G_2$-gauge groups over $S^4$, extending earlier work by Kishimoto, Theriault, and Tsutaya. The central strategy relies on reducing the 2-local classification to the order of the Samelson product $\langle i_3,1\rangle$ and separating specific gauge group homotopy types. The purpose of this note is to provide necessary mathematical refinements and localization clarifications to several key steps in the proof of the classification theorem. Specifically, we refine the integral isomorphism claim for gauge group homotopy to its correct 2-local form, resolve an EHP sequence extension regarding the injectivity of the Hopf invariant, and make explicit the Postnikov layer conventions required for the mod 2 Leray--Serre spectral sequence calculations. We confirm that with these adjustments, the main 2-local classification theorem in \cite{Kameko2026} also holds as claimed.

</details>


### [170] [Galois and separable extensions of Tambara functors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04411)
*Birgit Richter*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文为Tambara函子定义了可分扩张和H-伽罗瓦扩张的概念，证明了平坦可分扩张是形式étale的，伽罗瓦扩张是可分的，并在伽罗瓦理论框架下表达了Nullstellensatzian Tambara函子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典交换环论中的可分扩张和伽罗瓦扩张理论推广到Tambara函子的范畴中，建立Tambara函子的伽罗瓦理论框架。

Method: 通过定义Tambara函子间的可分性和H-伽罗瓦扩张，利用平坦性条件证明形式étale性质，并证明伽罗瓦扩张的可分性。

Result: (1) 定义了Tambara函子的可分扩张和H-伽罗瓦扩张；(2) 证明了平坦可分扩张是Hill意义上的形式étale；(3) 证明了伽罗瓦扩张是可分的；(4) 将Nullstellensatzian Tambara函子置于伽罗瓦理论语境中。

Conclusion: 成功将可分扩张和伽罗瓦扩张理论推广至Tambara函子，建立了基本性质并连接了Nullstellensatzian概念与伽罗瓦理论。

Abstract: For a map of Tambara functors $R \rightarrow T$ we define when $T$ is separable over $R$ and if $T$ carries an action by some finite group $H$ that fixes $R$ we also define when $T$ is an $H$-Galois extension of Tambara functors. We show that flat and separable extensions are (formally) étale in the sense of Hill and we prove that Galois extensions are separable. We express Nullstellensatzian Tambara functors in the context of Galois theory.

</details>


### [171] [Noncommutative Cartier Formulae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05360)
*Semon Rezchikov*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于$\mathbb{E}_1$代数的拓扑霍赫希尔德同调中帽积与轮回结构映射相互作用的公式，并将其推广到相对情形。当特化为$R=\mathbb{F}_p$时，该公式给出了Cartier公式的非交换类比，描述了Cartier同构下内积作用的共轭关系。在辛几何中，该公式解释了辛上同调的轮回结构与量子Steenrod运算之间的相互作用，进而证明了在标准横截性和非退化假设下，Calabi-Yau辛流形的量子联络的$p$-曲率可计算量子Steenrod运算，且射影Calabi-Yau超曲面等镜像对称例子的$p$-曲率可用$\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$-等变亏格0 Gromov-Witten不变量解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的动机源于辛几何：在辛上同调中，需要理解轮回结构与量子Steenrod运算之间的相互作用。这一公式的辛类比（在$R=\mathbb{F}_p$或Novikov类比下）能够解释这种相互作用。

Method: 本文从$\mathbb{E}_1$代数的拓扑霍赫希尔德同调出发，证明帽积与轮回结构映射相互作用的公式，并建立其相对版本。通过特化到$R=\mathbb{F}_p$得到非交换Cartier公式，再将其应用到辛几何中，在Fukaya范畴的标准横截性和非退化假设下，推导出量子联络的$p$-曲率与量子Steenrod运算的关系。

Result: 证明了$\mathbb{E}_1$代数$A$的帽积与轮回结构映射的相互作用公式及其相对版本；得到了非交换Cartier公式；建立了Calabi-Yau辛流形上量子联络的$p$-曲率与量子Steenrod运算的等价关系；将射影Calabi-Yau超曲面等例子的$p$-曲率用$\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$-等变Gromov-Witten不变量解释。

Conclusion: 本文建立的代数公式架起了非交换代数与辛几何之间的桥梁，为理解量子联络的$p$-曲率与量子Steenrod运算的关系提供了统一的代数框架，并在镜像对称中找到了重要应用。

Abstract: We prove, for every $\mathbb{E}_1$ algebra $A$, a formula describing the interaction of the action of the cap product on topological Hochschild homology of $A$ with the cyclotomic structure map, as well as a variant of this result relative to a ring $R$. Specializing to $R = \mathbb{F}_p$ gives a noncommutative analog of a formula of Cartier which describes the conjugation of interior product action on differential forms by the Cartier isomorphism, and which computes the $p$-curvature of the Getzler-Gauss-Manin connection in terms of an equivariant cap product. The motivation for this formula comes from symplectic geometry, where (in the case $R=\mathbb{F}_p$ or a Novikov analog) the symplectic analog of this formula explains the interaction between the cyclotomic structure on symplectic cohomology and the quantum Steenrod operations. We prove, under standard transversality and nondegeneracy assumptions on the Fukaya category, that for a Calabi-Yau symplectic manifold with rational symplectic form, the $p$-curvature of the quantum connection computes the Quantum Steenrod operations. In particular, the $p$-curvature of the quantum connections of projective Calabi-Yau hypersurfaces, and many other examples in mirror symmetry, can be interpreted in terms of $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$-equivariant genus zero Gromov-Witten invariants.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [GigaWorld-1: A Roadmap to Build World Models for Robot Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02642)
*GigaWorld Team,Angyuan Ma,Boyuan Wang,Bohan Li,Chaojun Ni,Guo Li,Guan Huang,Guosheng Zhao,Hao Li,Hengtao Li,Jingyu Liu,Jiwen Lu,Qiuping Deng,Tingdong Yu,Xuancheng Xu,Xinyu Zhou,Xiuwei Xu,Xinze Chen,Xiaofeng Wang,Xiaoyu Tian,Yang Wang,Yifan Chang,Yukun Zhou,Yun Ye,Zhenyu Wu,Zhanqian Wu,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了世界模型在机器人策略评估中的作用，构建了WMBench基准，分析了7个视频世界模型、4种动作表示方案和超过32.4万次模拟策略 rollout，得出关键见解：评估器质量主要取决于长程、动作一致性的 rollout 一致性而非短期视觉真实性；预训练增益不仅来自数据规模，还来自平衡通用知识与机器人特定可控性；架构选择（动作编码、记忆设计、评估器专用后训练）强烈决定与真实机器人行为的一致性。基于此设计了GigaWorld-1，并开源了代码、模型、数据集和工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估具身机器人基础模型仍然是一个关键瓶颈，与可以通过数字基准高效评估的大语言模型不同，机器人策略需要缓慢、昂贵且受硬件和人工监督限制的物理世界 rollout，因此需要世界模型作为替代策略评估器，但使世界模型可靠用于策略评估的关键属性仍不清楚。

Method: 提出了WMBench基准，基于真实机器人遥操作数据和匹配的策略 rollout 构建，涵盖多种操作任务；分析了7个视频世界模型、4种动作表示方案、超过32.4万次模拟策略 rollout，并利用来自CVPR 2026 GigaBrain Challenge的大规模社区提交、合成轨迹和超过12,000小时的训练视频进行分析。

Result: 三大核心发现：(1) 评估器质量取决于长程、动作一致性的 rollout 一致性而非短期视觉真实性；(2) 预训练收益来自数据规模与通用知识和机器人特定可控性之间的平衡；(3) 动作编码、记忆设计和评估器专用后训练等架构选择决定与真实机器人行为的一致性。

Conclusion: 基于实验结果得出了实用的设计路线图，并实现了专门为策略评估优化的大规模世界模型GigaWorld-1，同时开源了代码、模型、数据集和工具包，以推进具身基础模型的可扩展评估研究。

Abstract: Evaluating embodied robot foundation models remains a critical bottleneck; unlike large language models efficiently assessed via digital benchmarks, robotic policies require slow, costly real-world rollouts limited by hardware and human supervision, which has driven interest in world models as surrogate policy evaluators, yet the key properties that make a world model reliable for policy assessment remain poorly understood. This work presents a systematic study of world models for robotic policy evaluation and introduces WMBench, a benchmark constructed from real-robot teleoperation data and matched policy rollouts covering diverse manipulation tasks to enable controlled comparisons across model families, action encodings, rollout horizons, and evaluation metrics. Using WMBench, we analyze 7 video world models, 4 action representation schemes, and over 324,000 simulated policy rollouts paired with real robot executions, further enriching our analysis with large-scale community submissions from the CVPR 2026 GigaBrain Challenge, curated synthetic trajectories, and a training videos spanning more than 12,000 hours. Our experiments deliver three core insights: evaluator quality is dominated by long-horizon, action-faithful rollout consistency rather than short-term visual realism; pretraining gains stem not only from data scale but from balancing general world knowledge with robot-specific controllability; and architectural choices including action encoding, memory design, and evaluator-focused post-training strongly determine alignment with real-world robot behavior. Drawing on these results, we derive a practical design roadmap and realize it in \textit{GigaWorld-1}, a world model specially optimized for policy evaluation, and we fully release our code, models, datasets, and toolkits to advance scalable evaluation research for embodied foundation models.

</details>


### [173] [EVA-Client: A Unified Data Collection, Inference, and Deployment Framework for Embodied Policies on Real Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02646)
*Heqing Yang,Yang Yi,Liyao Wang,Linqing Zhong,Donglin Yang,Ruipu Wu,Zitong Bai,Fengjiao Chen,Manyuan Zhang,Linjiang Huang,Si Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EVA-Client 是一个开源框架，用于在真实机器人上部署、收集数据和评估操作策略，通过组件解耦架构、三种可检查执行工作流以及将每次评估同时作为数据收集的设计，统一了策略迭代循环中的实机阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略部署与评估流程缺乏统一框架，不同机器人后端、推理策略和传输中间件之间耦合度高，且评估结果往往无法直接反馈到下一轮训练中，导致策略迭代效率低下。

Method: 提出组件解耦架构，将机器人后端、推理策略和传输中间件正交分离；设计 Debug、Collect、Eval 三种可检查执行工作流；实现评估运行同时进行数据收集，记录完整 rollout 并生成训练格式数据及对比查看器。

Result: 实现了多种实时推理策略（同步/异步执行、ACT 时序集成、实时分块、朴素异步基线）的统一配置；每次评估均可生成训练就绪格式的数据和详尽日志，支持并排对比查看。

Conclusion: EVA-Client 通过统一的代码库有效衔接策略服务器与物理硬件，简化了从评估到训练的闭环迭代过程，提升了机器人操作策略的实机部署效率。

Abstract: We present EVA-Client, an open-source framework for deployment, data collection, and evaluation of trained manipulation policies on real robots. Sitting between a policy server and the physical hardware, EVA-Client unifies the real-robot stages of the policy iteration loop within a single codebase. It makes three contributions. First, a component-decoupled architecture in which robot backends, inference strategies, and transport middlewares form an orthogonal grid: adding a robot or a strategy touches only its own layer. Second, inspectable execution through Debug, Collect, and Eval workflows, with modes ranging from open-loop simulation to continuous real-time control. Third, every evaluation run doubles as a data collection, recording full rollouts in training-ready format alongside exhaustive logs and a side-by-side comparison viewer, so each evaluation feeds the next round of training rather than ending as an unrecorded impression. EVA-Client further consolidates major real-time inference strategies, synchronous and asynchronous execution, ACT-style temporal ensembling, Real-Time Chunking, and a naive-async ablation baseline, behind a single configuration surface.

</details>


### [174] [MorphQuad: Morphable Quadrotor for Superhuman Maneuverability, Manipulation, and Resiliency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02764)
*J. Diaz Peon Gonzalez Pacheco,J. Xu,A. Zhao,H. Zhou,A. Navsalkar,A. Scheffer,A. Malli Reddi,S. Shankar,Y. Bao,V. Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MorphQuad，一种通过双轴万向节独立铰接四个旋翼系统的可变形四旋翼飞行器，结合硬件与控制协同设计，实现了极致机动性、操控力和鲁棒性，克服了现有飞行器无法同时实现任意方向最大推力矢量化、全局稳定性及紧凑标准设计的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有飞行器受结构和控制限制，无法同时满足"任意方向最大推力矢量化（I）"、"全局稳定性（II）"和"紧凑标准设计（III）"三个要求，从而无法真正实现基础设施维护、接触式检测和应急响应所需的机动性、操控力和鲁棒性（MMR）。

Method: 采用硬件与控制协同设计：硬件上通过双轴万向节独立铰接四个旋翼系统；控制上引入全局稳定控制，以及仅为避免下洗干扰和万向节锁定时才允许旋翼间推力抵消的能量最优推力分配策略。

Result: 在完全机载自主条件下，MorphQuad实现了：多圈旋转同时平移或悬停（管道检测与目标追踪）；以人类级别力量进行阀门转动、栖息、物体按压与推拉（操控力）；任意方向抗风干扰（甚至针对单旋翼）及推拉恢复（鲁棒性）。

Conclusion: MorphQuad通过硬件与控制的协同设计成功实现了MMR能力，验证了其对管道检测、目标追踪、阀门操作及抗风扰动等复杂任务的有效性。

Abstract: Infrastructure maintenance, contact-based inspection, and emergency response can benefit from aerial vehicles that act as a flying human hand with extreme maneuverability, manipulation, and resiliency (MMR): maneuverability to fly in arbitrary orientations to reach remote and tight locations; manipulation to point sensors, turn valves, and press tools at arbitrary orientations; resiliency to maintain accurate motion and force control despite disturbances from arbitrary directions, such as wind, ground effects, and friction. Realizing MMR on aerial vehicles requires not only omnidirectional flight; it also requires (I) vectoring of maximum thrust in any direction, to maximize capacity for contact-force application and disturbance rejection, (II) global stability, to enable control over any orientation/position, and (III) compact, standard designs that build upon platforms such as quadrotors to inherit technological know-how. No current aerial vehicle simultaneously enables I--III, due to structural and control limitations that constrain actuation. We present MorphQuad: a morphable quadrotor that enjoys MMR. Key to our approach is a hardware and control co-design: on hardware, we independently articulate each of the four rotor systems via two-axis gimbals; on control, we introduce globally-stable control, and energy-optimal thrust allocation that permits inter-rotor thrust cancellations only to avoid downwash interference and gimbal lock. With fully-onboard autonomy, MorphQuad demonstrates multi-revolution rotation while translating or hovering, for pipe inspection and target tracking (maneuverability); valve turning, perching, and object pressing and pushing with human-level strengths (manipulation); and wind rejection from any direction, even directed to a single rotor, and push-pull recovery (resiliency).

</details>


### [175] [Metallic Ultrasound Waveguides as a Distributed Tactile Sensing Platform for Contact Localization, Force Estimation, and Material Class Discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02827)
*Alexandros Rosakis,Alessio Tamborini,Basile Fakhoury,Cole Bailey,Morteza Gharib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了金属超声波导作为分布式触觉传感器，通过单个近端换能器实现接触定位、力估计和材料分类，验证了其作为鲁棒触觉感知平台的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人触觉传感在传感面积和系统复杂度之间面临权衡，需要一种既能覆盖大面积又保持系统简洁的分布式触觉方案。

Method: 使用圆柱压头在金属超声波导上进行单点和多点接触实验，分析反射/透射系数比与接触力的线性关系，并扩展到二维金属薄板进行接触定位和材料效应验证。

Result: 单点接触中力与R/T比呈线性关系(R²≥0.95)，校准斜率与有效接触模量强相关(log-log Pearson r=-0.98)；反射能量可独立于负载实现材料分类；双压头实验中两个接触力均与参考值高度吻合(R²=0.97和0.95)。

Conclusion: 金属波导作为一种鲁棒的分布式触觉传感平台，能够同时实现接触定位、力估计和接触体材料分类。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is central to how robotic systems interact with the real world, yet current solutions face a tradeoff between sensing area and system complexity. This work investigates metallic ultrasound waveguides as distributed tactile sensors fully interrogated from a single proximal transducer. Using cylindrical indenters, we characterized the acoustic response to single and multi-point contacts with varying forces and contact materials. For single point indentation, the applied force was well captured by a linear relationship with the ratio of the reflection to transmission coefficients (F = a * R/T) across all nine tested materials (R2 >= 0.95). The calibration slope, a, correlated strongly with the material's effective contact modulus (log--log Pearson r=-0.98). The reflected energy partition was found to be a load-independent parameter related to the contacting material's properties, enabling material classification independent of force. For the two-indenter experiment, both contact forces were recovered from the waveguide signal and were in close agreement with reference load cell measurements (contact 1, R2 = 0.97; contact 2, R2=0.95). The approach was extended to two-dimensional metallic sheets, confirming both contact localization and material-dependent effects. Overall, these results validate metallic waveguides as a robust platform for distributed tactile sensing, providing contact localization, force estimation, and material-class discrimination for the contacting body.

</details>


### [176] [TACO: TActile World Model as a Self-COrrector forScalable VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02840)
*Shengbang Liu,Yueru Jia,Yuyang Yan,Jiaming Liu,Xinran Zhang,Qiuxuan Feng,Yandong Guo,Shiji Zhou,Boxin Shi,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACO提出了一种触觉感知的世界模型驱动框架，用于可扩展的VLA后训练，通过"识别-想象-标注"循环将真实操作失败转化为想象的视觉-触觉校正，实现接触密集型操作的策略提升，在真实任务上获得44%的绝对成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在接触密集型操作中因微小接触扰动导致不可恢复的失败，且仅凭视觉难以检测；触觉感知的后训练虽有效但人工标注成本高昂；现有视觉世界模型可能生成视觉合理但接触不一致的轨迹。

Method: TACO框架包含三个核心组件：1) 统一进度-动作模型通过进度估计识别接近失败的状态；2) 视觉-触觉生成模型想象局部修正片段；3) 进度-动作模型为修正片段标注可执行的动作。结合知识隔离的触觉适配与优势条件训练，在保持预训练视觉-语言先验的同时学习触觉校正。

Result: 真实世界接触密集型操作实验中，TACO相比基础策略绝对成功率提升44%，相比无知识隔离触觉适配的策略提升32%。

Conclusion: TACO通过触觉感知世界模型实现了可扩展的VLA后训练，有效将真实失败转化为可用的视觉-触觉校正信号，显著提升了接触密集型操作的成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising generalization in robotic manipulation, but they still struggle with contact-rich tasks, where minor contact perturbations can cause unrecoverable failures that are hard to detect from vision alone. Since these failures are localized rather than task-level semantic errors, tactile-aware corrective post-training offers an efficient way to improve recovery. However, scaling such supervision through human intervention is costly. Recent works have explored world models to synthesize imagined rollouts for policy improvement, but vision-only world models may produce visually plausible yet contact-inconsistent trajectories. We therefore introduce TACO, a tactile-aware world-model-driven framework for scalable VLA post-training in contact-rich manipulation. Given real robot rollouts, TACO follows a Recognize-Imagine-Label loop with a tactile-aware world model: a unified progress-action model recognizes failure-adjacent states using progress estimates, a visuo-tactile generation model imagines local correction segments, and the progress-action model labels them with executable corrective actions. To incorporate tactile corrective supervision into VLA post-training, TACO combines knowledge-insulated tactile adaptation with advantage-conditioned training, enabling the policy to learn from imagined corrections without degrading pretrained visual-language priors. These components enable TACO to convert real-world failures into imagined visuo-tactile corrections for iterative VLA post-training. Experiments on real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks show that TACO achieves 44% absolute success rate improvement over the base policy and 32% over the policy without knowledge-insulated tactile adaptation.

</details>


### [177] [CLEAR: Closed-Loop Reinforcement Learning at Scale for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02841)
*Yunxiao Shi,Hong Cai,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Fatih Porikli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLEAR提出了一种基于强化学习的闭环训练框架，通过在预训练VLA策略上学习残差航点策略，并采用异构训练管线实现大规模并行仿真，在CARLA等基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA-based端到端自动驾驶策略采用模仿学习进行开环训练，导致开环训练与闭环推理之间的分布偏移，在闭环规划中性能欠佳。

Method: 1) 在预训练VLA策略的航点先验上学习残差航点策略，有效利用预训练知识；2) 设计异构训练管线，将仿真器和VLA学习器分布在不同计算组上，大幅增加并行仿真环境数量，避免资源争用并保持训练稳定性。

Result: 在CARLA longest6 v2和Bench2Drive基准上显著优于此前方法，达到新的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 使用简单的奖励函数，结合残差策略和异构并行管线，强化学习可以有效提升VLA策略的闭环规划能力，弥合开环训练与闭环推理之间的差距。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) aims to directly map raw sensor information to driving actions. Recently, with the rapid advancement of multi-modal large language models (MLLMs), researchers have proposed the paradigm of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for E2E-AD, where it seeks to integrate visual perception, language understanding and action prediction within a single policy. However, existing VLA-based policies largely adopts imitation learning, where it only learns to drive by optimizing distance-based metrics w.r.t. logged expert trajectories. Such distribution shift between open-loop training and closed-loop inference leads to suboptimal performance in closed-loop planning. To close this gap, we present CLEAR, a system that enables closed-loop training using Reinforcement Learning (RL) at scale for E2E-AD. We propose to learn a novel residual waypoint policy around the waypoint prior from pretrained VLA policies, effectively harnessing the knowledge within. On another front, one of the key challenges to scale up RL for vision-based policies is the number of parallel simulation environments since RL is data hungry. To that end, we design a heterogeneous pipeline that places the simulator and the VLA learner on distinct compute groups, which allows us to dramatically increase the number of simulation environments running in parallel while avoiding resource contention and maintaining training stability. We show that with a simple reward, CLEAR significantly outperforms previous methods and sets new state-of-the-art performance on the challenging benchmarks of CARLA longest6 v2 and Bench2Drive.

</details>


### [178] [Differential Amplifier-Inspired AmpAttention for Multi-View Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02845)
*Jin Yang,Ping Wei,Nanning Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对多视角机器人操作中注意力漂移问题，本文提出受差分放大器启发的AmpAttention机制，并构建RVAF模型（集成任务引导的视图内/间注意力），在18个RLBench任务（249变体）上取得最优平均成功率，训练时间减少33.3%；扩展版本RVAF++结合SAM2图像编码器，在"插销"高精度任务上达91%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角机器人操作方法虽在训练效率和任务性能上取得进展，但机器人视图图像存在固有的冗余性、遮挡和视角依赖性问题，导致严重的注意力漂移（attention drift），需要一种能抑制注意力噪声、捕获高信噪比信号的机制。

Method: 提出AmpAttention注意力机制（受模拟电路中差分放大器启发），用于抑制注意力噪声并捕获高信噪比信号；基于此构建RVAF模型，集成任务引导的视图内（intra-view）和视图间（inter-view）AmpAttention；进一步扩展为RVAF++，引入SAM2图像编码器。

Result: 在18个RLBench任务（249变体）上取得最优平均成功率，训练时间减少33.3%；RVAF++在"插销"高精度任务上达91%成功率；真实世界高精度任务中展示了捡起飞镖并精确插入红心靶的能力。

Conclusion: 提出的AmpAttention机制和RVAF模型有效解决了多视角机器人操作中的注意力漂移问题，在性能、效率和精度上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Multi-view robotic manipulation methods with the attention mechanism have recently achieved significant progress in both training efficiency and task performance. However, the inherent redundancy, occlusion, and viewpoint dependency in robotic view images often lead to severe attention drift. To address this challenge, we propose AmpAttention, a novel attention mechanism inspired by differential amplifiers in analog circuits. It aims to suppress attention noise and capture high signal-to-noise ratio signals for more reliable perception. Based on this, we introduce the RVAF model, which integrates task-guided intra-view and inter-view AmpAttention. Compared to previous state-of-the-art methods, RVAF achieves the optimal average success rate across 18 RLBench tasks (249 variations) while reducing training time by 33.3\%. RVAF also demonstrates strong potential in real-world high-precision tasks, exemplified by its ability to pick up a dart and accurately insert it into the red bullseye. Furthermore, we extend RVAF to RVAF++ by incorporating the SAM2 image encoder. RVAF++ achieves substantial gains on high-precision tasks, achieving a 91\% success rate on the `insert peg' task. More qualitative results are provided at the anonymous project website https://anonymous.4open.science/w/RVAF-Anonymization.

</details>


### [179] [DREAMSTEER: Latent World Models Can Steer VLA Policies During Deployment Without Any Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02865)
*Hanchen Cui,Sergio Arnaud,Arjun Majumdar,Daniel Dugas,Elie Aljalbout,Karthik Desingh,Krishna Murthy Jatavallabhula,Franziska Meier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DREAMSTEER是一种无需微调或修改参数的部署时引导框架，通过潜在世界模型和值模型对预训练VLA策略进行引导，在未见物体场景下将任务成功率从23.75%提升至66.25%，指令跟随准确率从38.75%提升至56.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA策略在部署时存在分布偏移，导致鲁棒性下降和指令跟随不一致。现有方法通常需要目标环境中的演示数据进行微调，成本高昂。

Method: DREAMSTEER框架包含三个步骤：1) 从VLA策略和预定义运动基元中采样候选动作块；2) 使用动作条件潜在世界模型想象执行结果；3) 使用语言条件值模型对想象轨迹进行排序，选择最优动作。

Result: 在四个真实世界操作基准测试中，面对未见物体，任务成功率从23.75%提升到66.25%，指令跟随准确率从38.75%提升到56.25%。

Conclusion: DREAMSTEER通过部署时引导而非微调的方式，有效提升了VLA策略在分布偏移下的鲁棒性和指令跟随能力，避免了收集目标环境演示数据的高昂成本。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) policies show promising zero-shot generalization, but often fail under deployment-time distribution shift, leading to decreased robustness and inconsistent instruction following. While prior work commonly tackles this by finetuning on in-distribution data, it assumes demonstrations collected on tasks in the target environment. In this work, we propose DREAMSTEER, a deployment-time steering framework for pretrained VLAs without any finetuning or parameter modifications. The key insight in DREAMSTEER is to leverage a latent world model and a value model to steer pretrained VLA policies. During deployment, DREAMSTEER samples candidate action chunks from a VLA policy and predefined motion primitives, imagines their outcomes using an action-conditioned latent world model, and ranks the imagined trajectories with a language-conditioned value model. Across four real-world manipulation benchmarks with unseen objects, DREAMSTEER improves task success rate from 23.75% to 66.25% and instruction-following accuracy from 38.75% to 56.25% over the base VLA policy.

</details>


### [180] [Continuous-Time Gaussian Belief Trees for Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02884)
*Rayan Mazouz,Qi Heng Ho,Zachary N. Sunberg,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向连续时间随机系统的采样-based运动规划框架，通过离线混合信念传播模型和信念障碍函数安全检测器，确保机器人轨迹在过程与测量不确定性下的概率安全与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样-based运动规划通常假设离散时间节点安全即整体安全，忽略了测量间隔内连续时间状态的不确定性传播，导致节点间机会约束违规被遗漏，在窄通道等场景中安全性不足。

Method: 将机器人动力学建模为连续时间线性随机微分方程，测量为离散时间更新；推导离线混合信念传播模型（连续ODE+离散卡尔曼更新）；引入信念障碍函数实现段级概率安全验证，覆盖整个连续轨迹段。

Result: 集成RRT和SST规划器，在多个基准环境中测试，在成功率与机会约束鲁棒性上均优于离散时间方法，尤其窄通道场景中避免了因遗漏节点间违规导致的失败。

Conclusion: 连续时间信念传播与段级安全验证是保证随机系统采样规划中概率安全的关键，所提方法为兼顾连续时间不确定性传播与安全约束提供了系统化框架。

Abstract: We address sampling-based motion planning for continuous-time stochastic systems under process and measurement uncertainty, with probabilistic guarantees on safety and performance. The robot dynamics are modeled as a continuous-time linear stochastic differential equation, while sensor measurements arrive at discrete time instants. We derive an offline hybrid belief propagation model in which the belief evolves according to continuous-time ODEs between measurements and undergoes discrete Kalman filter update jumps at measurement times. To ensure safety, we introduce a belief-barrier-function-based safety checker for segment-level probabilistic verification. This enables the planner to certify safety over entire continuous trajectory segments and detect inter-sample chance-constraint violations that are missed by conventional node-based checks. Together, these components provide a principled framework for sampling-based belief planning that accounts for both continuous-time uncertainty propagation and continuous-time safety requirements. We integrate the method with RRT and SST planners and evaluate it across multiple benchmark environments. The results show that the proposed method achieves high success rates and robust enforcement of chance constraints, including in narrow-passage scenarios where discrete-time counterparts fail due to missed inter-sample unsafe behavior.

</details>


### [181] [Longitudinal-Motion-Aware Lateral Control for Autonomous Vehicles: A Robust Nonlinear Control Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02924)
*Sixu Li,Nitesh Kumar,Reyshwanth Ganeshan,Sivakumar Rathinam,Swaroop Darbha,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑纵向运动（变速度和加速度）的鲁棒非线性横向控制框架，用于解决自动驾驶中现有控制器在纵向瞬态工况下性能下降及参数不确定性问题。通过反馈线性化建立变参数跟踪误差模型，并分别设计了Lyapunov重设计（LR）和增量非线性动态逆（INDI）两种鲁棒控制方案，理论证明了最终有界性，仿真和实车验证了其跟踪精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有横向控制器大多依赖恒速或工作点线性化假设，在纵向速度/加速度变化时性能下降；且多数方法假设车辆参数精确已知，无法应对实际参数不确定性。

Method: 1) 推导了包含时变纵向速度与加速度的跟踪误差模型；2) 采用反馈线性化得到线性输入-输出关系，将纵向运动嵌入控制律；3) 分析内部动态保证系统稳定性；4) 提出两种鲁棒控制方案：基于滑模思想的Lyapunov重设计（LR）和增量非线性动态逆（INDI）。

Result: 仿真表明该方法在变速度和加速度下具有更高的跟踪精度和一致性，对模型不确定性鲁棒；实车测试验证了实时实现能力和实际路径跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的纵向运动感知的鲁棒非线性横向控制框架有效克服了传统方法在瞬态工况和参数不确定下的局限性，两种鲁棒设计均证明了最终有界稳定性，具有实际工程应用价值。

Abstract: As autonomous vehicles (AVs) operate in increasingly dynamic traffic conditions, lateral control must be performed while longitudinal speed and acceleration vary. Yet many existing lateral controllers rely on constant-speed or operating-point-based assumptions, which can degrade performance during transient longitudinal maneuvers. Moreover, most methods assume precisely known vehicle parameters, despite real-world parametric uncertainties. To address these limitations, this paper presents a longitudinal-motion-aware robust nonlinear lateral control framework for AVs. It first derives a tracking error model that depends on varying longitudinal speed and acceleration. Using this model, feedback linearization is employed to obtain a linear input-output relation for lateral error tracking while embedding longitudinal motion into the control law. The resulting internal dynamics are then analyzed to ensure overall system stability. To address parameter uncertainty, two robust control designs with distinct implementation trade-offs are proposed: (i) a Lyapunov redesign (LR) approach inspired by sliding mode control, and (ii) an incremental nonlinear dynamic inversion (INDI) method. Both are rigorously analyzed and proven to ensure ultimate boundedness, with key robustness-tuning parameters explicitly identified. Simulations demonstrate enhanced tracking accuracy, consistent performance across varying speeds and accelerations, and robustness to model uncertainties, while also examining the effects of the robustness-related parameters. Real-vehicle tests further confirm real-time implementation and practical path-tracking performance on actual hardware.

</details>


### [182] [Function-Space Diffusion for Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02977)
*Zinuo Chang,Yipu Chen,Byoungwoo Park,Hongzhe Yu,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FSD-MP，一种在函数空间中进行扩散的连续运动规划方法，实现了跨分辨率的零样本泛化，在2D点和7-DoF机械臂规划任务上验证了其离散化不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的运动规划器在固定长度路径序列上操作，导致所学模型具有分辨率依赖性，无法实现跨分辨率的零样本泛化。

Method: 将轨迹建模为连续函数，在函数空间中执行扩散；在谱域中定义模态前向过程（使用Matérn型协方差的高斯噪声驱动），并用边界兼容的离散正弦变换傅里叶神经算子（DST-FNO）参数化逆过程，以保持起止点约束不受分辨率影响。

Result: 在2D点机器人和7-DoF Franka机械臂规划基准上，FSD-MP在训练分辨率下达到竞争性性能，并能零样本泛化到高达16倍的分辨率，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 函数空间扩散为离散化不变的运动规划提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Diffusion-based motion planners have demonstrated strong performance in generating diverse and high-quality robot trajectories in cluttered environments with multiple feasible solutions. However, existing approaches typically operate on fixed-length waypoint sequences, making the learned model resolution-dependent, thereby preventing zero-shot generalization across resolutions. In this work, we propose Function-Space Diffusion for Motion Planning (FSD-MP), a diffusion-based motion planner that models trajectories as continuous functions and performs diffusion directly in function space, achieving discretization-invariant trajectory generation. We define a mode-wise forward process in the spectral domain, driven by Gaussian noise with a Matérn-type covariance, and parameterize the reverse process with a boundary-compatible Discrete Sine Transform-based Fourier Neural Operator (DST-FNO) that preserves start-goal constraints across resolutions. We evaluate FSD-MP on 2D point robot and 7-DoF Franka manipulator planning benchmarks. Our method achieves competitive planning performance at the training resolution and generalizes zero-shot across resolutions up to 16$\times$ higher, preserving consistent planning behavior without retraining. These results demonstrate that function-space diffusion provides an effective framework for discretization-invariant motion planning.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond Heuristics: A Standardized Real2Sim Pipeline for Physical Human Robot Interaction in Human-in-the-Loop Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03017)
*Chengyuan Yang,Yifan Wang,Chun Kwang Tan,Sherwin Stephen Chan,Youlong Wang,Xiaoyue Yan,Lei Li,Wei Tech Ang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Real2Sim管道，通过CMA-ES算法识别人机耦合接口的6自由度粘弹性参数（12个方向刚度和阻尼），利用用户“安全舒适”反馈作为可复现操作点，并通过组内相关性分析分离共享与个体参数，从而提升数字孪生仿真保真度，支持个性化控制器的临床前验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球人口老龄化推动辅助机器人需求增长，但物理测试的安全风险和成本过高，使得人在环仿真成为有吸引力的替代方案。然而，耦合系统的仿真保真度受限于交互模型的阻抗参数依赖启发式调参而非数据驱动识别，这限制了仿真的可靠性和可推广性。

Method: 提出Real2Sim管道：将骨盆-绑带接口建模为6自由度粘弹性机构（12个方向刚度和阻尼参数）；采用CMA-ES算法逐受试者识别参数；以用户“安全舒适”反馈作为可复现操作点，消除不同人体尺寸下的绑带松紧度歧义；通过5名受试者的组内相关性分析，分离可共享参数与受试者特异性参数，构建先验参数集。新受试者仅需调整12个参数中的5个即可完成配置。

Result: 校准后的模型能够复现真实交互包络，并在人体数字孪生中诱发生物力学准确的行适应。过软和过硬的设置作为极端情况均失效，证实了正确操作点的存在。与启发式调参相比，该管道显著提升了仿真保真度。

Conclusion: 该Real2Sim管道提高了人在环仿真保真度，支持人体数字孪生作为预测工具用于个性化控制器的临床前验证。通过数据驱动的参数识别和先验知识迁移，解决了传统启发式调参无法可靠选择正确操作点的关键问题。

Abstract: The aging global population drives demand for assistive robots, yet the safety risks and costs of physical testing make Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) simulation an attractive alternative. Its fidelity for coupled systems, however, is limited by interaction models whose impedance parameters are tuned heuristically rather than identified from data. We present a Real2Sim pipeline that identifies the coupled Physical Human-Robot Interaction (pHRI) dynamics of a pelvis--strap interface on an overground mobile balance assistant. The interface is modeled as a 6-DoF viscoelastic mechanism whose 12 directional stiffness and damping parameters are identified per subject via Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy (CMA-ES), using the user's ``Safe \& Comfortable'' feedback as a reproducible operating point that resolves harness-tightness ambiguity across anthropometrics. An intraclass-correlation analysis over a five-subject cohort separates shareable from subject-specific parameters, yielding a set of prior parameters derived from the existing data. Deploying this prior configures a previously unseen subject by refining only 5 of the 12 parameters. The calibrated model then reproduces the real interaction envelope and induces biomechanically accurate gait adaptations in the Human Digital Twin (HDT). Overly compliant and overly stiff settings, by contrast, fail as extreme settings, confirming a correct operating point that no heuristic tuning procedure can reliably select. The pipeline thus improves HITL simulation fidelity and supports the Human Digital Twin as a predictive tool for pre-clinical verification of personalized controllers.

</details>


### [184] [DRBA: Dynamic Robotic Balance Assistant -- An assist-as-needed gait and balance rehabilitation robot for versatile training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03027)
*Yifan Wang,Li Li,Youlong Wang,Chengyuan Yang,Sherwin Stephen Chan,Jiaye Chen,Xiaoyue Yan,Hao Wang,Xuesheng Gong,Jun Lin,Hongping Hu,Wei Tech Ang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型动态机器人平衡辅助系统（DRBA），通过3自由度机械臂提供骨盆支撑、坐站辅助模块及跟随与跌倒检测算法，在保持高透明度的同时为不同平衡障碍程度的老年患者提供按需辅助。实验表明，相比治疗师辅助训练，DRBA能增加步长和步行速度，帮助患者完成超出其独立能力的任务，具有提升康复效果的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面机器人步态训练器存在局限性：体重支撑系统笨重不便日常使用，末端执行器系统常牺牲透明度，改变自然步态动力学。因此需要一种既提供有效平衡支撑又不干扰自然步态的训练设备。

Method: DRBA系统集成了三个核心组件：1）用于骨盆支撑的3自由度机械臂，提供灵活运动；2）紧凑的坐站辅助模块；3）用户跟随与跌倒检测算法。采用按需辅助（assist-as-needed）策略，确保最小干扰和响应性支撑。

Result: 实验结果表明系统具有高透明度，对自然步态动力学影响极小。在9名不同病情和平衡障碍程度（重度到轻度）老年患者的试验中，DRBA辅助训练相比治疗师辅助训练增加了步长和步行速度，且使患者能完成超出其独立能力的任务。

Conclusion: DRBA通过促进更高训练强度和任务导向训练，具有提升康复效果的潜力，为老年人和平衡障碍患者的步态康复提供了新方案。

Abstract: The decline of human balance control due to aging and pathological conditions increases fall risk, a major concern in geriatric care and rehabilitation. Gait training is essential for balance recovery, enhancing walking ability and postural control. However, existing overground robotic gait trainers have limitations: body weight support systems are bulky and impractical for daily use, while end-effector-based systems often compromise transparency, altering natural gait dynamics. This paper presents the Dynamic Robotic Balance Assistant (DRBA), a novel gait trainer providing assist-as-needed body weight and balance support for various training scenarios. DRBA integrates a 3-degree-of-freedom (3-DoF) robotic arm for pelvic support with flexible motion, a compact sit-to-stand assistance module, and user-following and fall detection algorithms to ensure minimal interference and responsive support. Experimental results demonstrated high transparency, with minimal impact on natural gait dynamics. A patient trial with nine elderly patients with varying medical conditions and balance impairments (ranging from severe to mild) further validated DRBA's effectiveness. The results showed that DRBA-assisted training increased step length and walking speed compared to therapist-assisted gait training. Additionally, DRBA enabled users to perform tasks beyond their unaided ability, expanding rehabilitation possibilities. These findings highlight DRBA's potential to enhance rehabilitation outcomes by facilitating higher training intensity and enabling task-oriented exercises.

</details>


### [185] [Exp2VLA: Enabling Vision-Language-Action for Drone Navigation from Expert Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03146)
*Van Huyen Dang,Kabilesh Rajendran,Erdi Sayar,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Exp2VLA的专家蒸馏流水线，通过将强化学习、遥操作等控制器的专家行为蒸馏为训练数据，微调紧凑型VLA模型，实现语言条件化的无人机导航，在多种场景下展现出对语义指令的理解和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在无人机领域的实际部署面临计算负担重或在复杂环境能力不足的问题，且传统控制策略与语言引导的导航模型之间缺乏统一的集成方式。

Method: 提出Exp2VLA流水线，核心思想是将RL、遥操作等专家控制器产生的行为蒸馏为训练数据，用于微调紧凑型VLA模型，将多种控制策略统一为语言引导的导航模型。

Result: 在sim-to-sim和simulation-in-the-loop设置下的多目标场景实验中，微调后的模型能够处理多种语义指令，并能泛化到未见过的目标组成。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了专家策略蒸馏能够帮助机电系统从专用控制模块向更灵活、可复用的机器人智能转变。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models open a new path toward intuitive robot control by directly linking perception, language, and action in a single end-to-end framework. Yet for UAVs, practical adoption remains difficult because existing solutions are either computationally heavy or insufficiently capable in complex environments. In this work, we propose a practical expert-distillation pipeline (Exp2VLA) for language-conditioned drone navigation. The core idea is to distill expert behavior, obtained from reinforcement learning, teleoperation, or other controllers, into training data that can be used to fine-tune compact VLA models. This allows existing control strategies to be transferred into a unified language-guided navigation model, reducing manual system integration and lowering the barrier for deploying new robot behaviors. Experiments in both sim-to-sim and simulation-in-the-loop settings across multi-object scenes show that the fine-tuned models can handle varied semantic commands and generalize to unseen target compositions. The proposed framework demonstrates how expert-policy distillation can help mechatronic systems move from specialized control modules toward more flexible and reusable robot intelligence.

</details>


### [186] [Hope for the Best, Prepare for the Worst: Occlusion-Aware Contingency Planning for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03155)
*Truls Nyberg,Anna Gautier,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化的遮挡感知轨迹规划框架，通过可达性分析和树形运动规划器，能在存在隐藏交通参与者时保证碰撞避免，同时降低保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市环境中自动驾驶车辆面临遮挡带来的安全挑战，近期事故表明需要明确处理遮挡风险的运动规划器。

Method: 基于前序可达性分析方法确定隐藏交通参与者的可能状态，集成树形运动规划器以推理未来观测及观测缺失的情况。

Result: 在模拟遮挡场景中，该框架能够主动且高效地保证碰撞避免。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够降低保守性同时维持安全保证，为自动驾驶遮挡场景下的轨迹规划提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous vehicles in urban environments introduces significant safety challenges, particularly in scenarios with occlusions, where critical traffic participants may be hidden from view. Recent accidents involving driverless vehicles highlight the importance of motion planners that explicitly addresses the risks posed by occlusions. In this work, we propose a formal, occlusion-aware trajectory planning framework that guarantees collision avoidance even when there are possible hidden traffic participants. Building on our previous methods that apply reachability analysis to sequentially determine the possible states of hidden traffic participants, we integrate a tree-based motion planner capable of reasoning over future observations and the absence thereof. This approach reduces conservativeness while maintaining safety guarantees. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework in a challenging simulated occluded scenario, showing that it pro-actively and efficiently guarantees collision-avoidance.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Point-Attached Semantics: Object-Centric Semantic Fields for Generalizable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03163)
*Zheng Sun,Lerong Zhang,Zhihao Li,Zhuo Li,Quentin Rouxel,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以物体为中心的连续语义场（Object-centric Continuous Semantic Field），将点云中与观测相关的离散点特征转化为连续的、与观测无关的语义场，从而为机器人操作策略提供稳定的功能部件语义信息，在仿真和真实双臂操作任务中优于原始点云、2D特征提升和3D点特征基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始点云仅提供几何信息，缺乏部件级语义；现有2D特征提升和离散3D点特征方法得到的语义特征仍依赖于观测视角、传感器配置和物体实例，缺乏稳定性和泛化性。

Method: 构建以物体为中心的连续语义场：以物体点云为条件，在显式3D查询位置读取部件感知的语义嵌入。该场由部件标注的物体模型训练后冻结，生成语义点云作为操作策略的物体级条件输入。

Result: 在RoboTwin仿真任务和真实双臂物体操作实验中，所提方法提供了更稳定的功能部件线索，并在策略性能上优于原始点云、2D特征提升和3D点特征基线方法。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的连续语义场是一种有效的物体级语义表征方式，能够为通用机器人操作提供稳定的功能部件理解，提升策略的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generalizable robot manipulation requires stable 3D understanding of functional object parts, such as handles, tool heads, openings, and graspable regions. Raw point clouds provide geometry but lack explicit part semantics, and their sampled points vary with viewpoint, sensor configuration, and object instance. Existing 2D feature lifting and discrete 3D point-wise features enrich point clouds with semantics, but the resulting features remain attached to observation-dependent samples. We propose an object-centric continuous semantic field that conditions on an object point cloud and reads part-aware semantic embeddings at explicit 3D query locations. The field is trained from part-annotated object models and then frozen to generate semantic point clouds as object-level conditioning for manipulation policies. Experiments on RoboTwin simulation tasks and real-world bimanual object manipulation show that our representation provides more stable functional-part cues and improves policy performance over raw point-cloud, 2D feature lifting, and 3D point-wise feature baselines. Project Page: \href{https://zainzh.github.io/beyond-point-attached-semantics}{https://zainzh.github.io/beyond-point-attached-semantics}.

</details>


### [188] [AnchorVLA: Bridging Discrete Decisions and Continuous Trajectories for Vision-Language-Action Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03182)
*Qi Liu,Yabei Li,Hongsong Wang,Heng Zhang,Lei He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnchorVLA提出了一种层次化的决策锚点VLA规划框架，通过轨迹模式锚点作为高层VLA推理与连续轨迹执行之间的显式接口。决策即锚点表示用锚点token编码整体局部运动模式，决策锚点残差流在锚点定义的残差空间中生成细粒度连续轨迹。在Bench2Drive基准上取得了77.28%的成功率和89.92的驾驶分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA规划器存在两类局限：基于规划头的轨迹预测仅弱约束连续轨迹生成，而全轨迹自回归生成依赖长序列低信息密度坐标token，导致语义-动作对齐困难、离散化误差和推理效率低下。

Method: 提出AnchorVLA框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Decision-as-Anchor Representation：用锚点token表示行为级驾驶决策，每个token编码整个局部运动模式而非单个坐标点；2) Decision-Anchored Residual Flow：在锚点定义的残差空间中生成细粒度连续轨迹，捕捉高层决策后的多模态执行精化。

Result: 在Bench2Drive闭环评测中，AnchorVLA实现了77.28%的成功率（SOTA）和89.92的驾驶分数。

Conclusion: 通过紧凑且语义有意义的锚点推理替代自回归路径点生成，AnchorVLA在保留LLM决策能力的同时提升了推理效率、语义-动作对齐和连续生成灵活性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving planning requires translating navigation intent, traffic rules, dynamic interactions, and language instructions into executable continuous trajectories. Vision-Language-Action models have been introduced into driving planning to improve long-tail generalization, commonsense reasoning, high-level semantic understanding, and explainability. However, existing VLA planners mainly follow planning-head-based trajectory prediction or full-trajectory autoregressive generation. The former only weakly constrains continuous trajectory generation with VLA reasoning, while the latter relies on long sequences of low-information-density coordinate tokens, making semantic-action alignment difficult and leading to discretization errors and inefficient inference. To address these limitations, we propose AnchorVLA, a hierarchical decision-anchored VLA planning framework that uses trajectory-pattern anchors as an explicit interface between high-level VLA reasoning and continuous trajectory execution. Specifically, Decision-as-Anchor Representation represents behavior-level driving decisions with anchor tokens, each encoding an entire local motion pattern rather than a single coordinate point. Decision-Anchored Residual Flow then generates fine-grained continuous trajectories in the selected anchor-defined residual space, capturing multi-modal execution refinements after high-level decision making. By reasoning over compact and semantically meaningful anchors instead of autoregressively generating waypoint sequences, AnchorVLA preserves LLM-based decision making while improving inference efficiency, semantic-action alignment, and continuous generation flexibility. Experiments on the Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark show that AnchorVLA achieves a state-of-the-art Success Rate of 77.28 and a competitive Driving Score of 89.92.

</details>


### [189] [Layout-independent actuation allocator for fin-actuated marine robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03204)
*Yuya Hamamatsu,Maarja Kruusmaa,Asko Ristolainen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种与布局无关的控制分配器，通过集成GNN、Transformer和MDN的流水线，结合可微物理代理模型，实现零样本部署到不同执行器配置的机器人上，在分布外测试和真实实验中均表现出接近传统专用控制器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统控制分配器针对特定执行器布局设计、无法直接迁移到其他配置的问题，实现跨不同执行器布局的零样本泛化部署。

Method: 采用GNN+Transformer将机器人几何布局编码为图结构，MDN预测多模态控制指令分布，并通过可微物理代理模型在推理阶段优化指令以最小化目标扳手跟踪误差和能耗。

Result: 在随机生成的执行器布局数据上训练的单一模型，对不同布局机器人（分布外）展现出高轨迹跟踪性能；真实水池实验中性能接近传统专用控制器。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了与布局无关的零样本控制分配，具有跨不同执行器配置泛化的能力，且在实际部署中达到与专用控制器相当的性能。

Abstract: In this study, we propose a layout-independent control allocator capable of zero-shot deployment across diverse actuator configurations. The proposed method utilizes a learning pipeline that integrates a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and a Transformer to represent the robot's geometric layout as a graph, along with a Mixture Density Network (MDN) to predict multi-modal control command distributions. Furthermore, by incorporating a differentiable physics surrogate model, we achieve command refinement during inference to minimize target wrench tracking error and energy consumption. A single generalized model using randomly generated actuator layout data demonstrated high trajectory tracking performance on different actuator layout robots outside the training distribution. Additionally, in real-world pool experiments, our approach achieved performance nearly equivalent to conventional controllers designed to specific layouts.

</details>


### [190] [Derivations of Error-State Kalman Filter Kinematics for Globally Applicable Aided Inertial Navigation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03211)
*Antonia Hager,Torleiv H. Bryne*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统总结了四种经典与不变误差状态卡尔曼滤波（ESKF）公式，用于地球曲率、自转和重力变化不可忽略的全球导航系统，为惯性导航系统的实现和对比提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球导航系统需要处理地球曲率、自转和重力变化，而局部导航算法可忽略这些因素。经典ESKF的误差状态动力学依赖于轨迹，不变ESKF利用李群对称性可使误差传播与轨迹无关。需要为不同传感器配置选择合适的滤波器结构，但目前缺乏系统的对比参考。

Method: 论文给出了四种ESKF的数学公式：标准滤波器（导航坐标系中位置和速度误差采用加法定义）、左不变滤波器（误差在体坐标系表示）、右不变滤波器（误差在导航/世界坐标系表示），适用于带辅助的全球惯性导航系统。

Result: 提供了四种经典和不变ESKF的完整数学公式，可作为系统性的参考文档，便于工程实现和不同滤波器之间的对比选择。

Conclusion: 本文旨在作为不变ESKF在全局导航应用中的系统参考，帮助研究人员根据系统动力学和传感器配置选择合适的滤波结构。

Abstract: Global navigation systems require state estimation algorithms that handle Earth's curvature, Earth's rotation, and gravitational variations. These factors can typically be neglected in local navigation algorithms for robots, drones, etc. In classical error-state Kalman Filtering (ESKF) the error state dynamics are trajectory-dependent. Invariant ESKFs utilize Lie Group symmetries to represent the error, which can render error propagation trajectory-independent for group-affine systems. Choosing between a standard filter (where position and velocity errors are defined additively in the navigation frame), a left-invariant filter (where errors are represented in the body frame) and a right-invariant filter (where errors are represented in the navigation/world frame) depends on system dynamics and sensor configuration. This note presents the mathematical formulas for four classical and invariant ESKFs for globally applicable aided inertial navigation systems. It is intended to serve as a systematic reference for comparison and implementation.

</details>


### [191] [Strouhal-Aware Model Predictive Control for Efficient Multi-Fin Flapping Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03216)
*Yuya Hamamatsu,Zixi Chen,Maarja Kruusmaa,Asko Ristolainen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种斯特劳哈尔数感知模型预测控制（Strouhal-aware MPC）方法，通过将仿生扑翼推进的斯特劳哈尔数经验法则嵌入控制目标，实现了对四软鳍自主水下航行器的高效实时控制。该方法在0.1-0.3 m/s巡航范围内平均降低机械功率8.8%-32%，并达到传统逆模型无法实现的0.4 m/s速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界扑翼推进在最优斯特劳哈尔数窗口内可实现最大推功比，但现有水下机器人缺乏将此生物物理学经验法则转化为实时控制的方法，导致推进效率受限。

Method: 提出斯特劳哈尔感知MPC方法，在准稳态水动力学模型中显式引入斯特劳哈尔数偏离惩罚项，通过两阶段采样与梯度优化求解非凸问题，实现在板载25 Hz频率运行。

Result: 池试和现场试验表明：控制器将各鳍保持在最优斯特劳哈尔数区间（0.25-0.35）内并精确跟踪指令力；在0.1-0.3 m/s巡航范围平均降低机械功率8.8%-32%；达到0.4 m/s的更高速度，超越传统逆模型基线方法。

Conclusion: 将第一性原理流动物理嵌入MPC目标函数可在不牺牲敏捷性的前提下显著提升续航能力，为下一代多鳍机器人的能量感知运动控制提供了通用路径。

Abstract: Efficient flapping propulsion hinges on operating within a narrow Strouhal number window, a principle nature has converged upon for maximum thrust-to-power ratio. We translate this bioinspired empirical rule into real-time control, demonstrating it on an autonomous underwater vehicle driven by four soft fins. The proposed Strouhal-aware Model Predictive Control (MPC) enhances a quasi-steady hydrodynamic model with an explicit penalty for Strouhal deviation, solving the resulting nonconvex problem via a two-stage sampling and gradient optimization that runs onboard at 25 Hz. Pool and field trials show that the controller keeps each fin within the optimal Strouhal corridor (0.25-0.35) while precisely tracking commanded forces. This results in a mean reduction in mechanical power of 8.8\% to 32\% throughout the cruising range of 0.1 to 0.3 m/s. The proposed method also allows for a velocity of 0.4 m/s, which is unattainable for a baseline of the conventional inverse model. The results confirm that embedding first-principle flow physics into an MPC objective yields tangible endurance gains without sacrificing agility, offering a generic pathway to energy-aware locomotion in next-generation multifin robots.

</details>


### [192] [GDPR-Aware Trajectory Sharing for ISAC-Assisted Robot Navigation: A Case Study on FID-Constrained Collision Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03254)
*Zexin Fang,Bin Han,Donglin Wang,Fengchen Pei,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息密度（FID）约束的轨迹共享方案，通过在共享前根据局部信息内容对感知估计添加扰动，在机器人碰撞避免场景中实现了比固定误差扰动更优的隐私-效用权衡，满足了GDPR对数据最小化和完整性的要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC技术带来智能基础设施的同时，也引发了因细粒度个人轨迹历史作为感知副产品而产生的监管担忧。GDPR要求个人数据必须限制在必要范围内，并通过适当技术措施防止未经授权的重建，本文旨在同时满足这两项要求。

Method: 提出Fisher信息密度（FID）约束的轨迹共享方案，在共享感知估计之前，根据局部信息内容进行扰动处理，实现信息感知的隐私保护。

Result: 在真实行人轨迹数据集上的实验表明，FID控制的共享方案相比固定误差扰动，在相同碰撞漏检率下，重建泄漏和持续暴露长度始终更低，实现了更优的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 信息感知的扰动是一种原则性的技术措施，与GDPR的数据最小化和完整性要求相一致，为ISAC系统中的隐私保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enables intelligent wireless infrastructure but raises growing regulatory concern as fine-grained personal trajectory histories become a byproduct of sensing. General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) Articles 5(1)(c) and 5(1)(f) require that personal data be limited to what is necessary and protected through appropriate technical measures against unauthorised reconstruction. This paper addresses both requirements through a Fisher information density (FID)-constrained trajectory sharing scheme for robot collision avoidance, where sensing estimates are perturbed according to local information content before sharing. Experiments on real pedestrian traces show that FID-controlled sharing achieves a strictly better privacy-utility tradeoff than fixed-error perturbation: at matched missed-conflict rates, reconstruction leakage and sustained exposure lengths are consistently lower, establishing information-aware perturbation as a principled technical measure aligned with GDPR data minimisation and integrity requirements.

</details>


### [193] [Invisible Strings: Deriving Puppetry Principles and their Hidden Connections to Robot Behavior Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03289)
*Claire Lewis,Sawyer Collins,Alyssa Hanson,Johanna Smith,Tom Williams*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文借鉴木偶戏艺术的原则，提出了一套专门用于机器人非语言行为设计的木偶戏原则，这些原则超越了动画、戏剧和舞蹈等传统艺术形式提供的设计洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人非语言行为设计多借鉴动画原则，但动画不受物理约束、且聚焦低层运动机制而非高层交互设计，忽视了同样关注物理实体交互角色的木偶戏艺术。作者希望从木偶戏中提取更适合实体机器人设计的原则。

Method: 借鉴木偶戏文献和从业木偶师的专家知识，提炼出一套木偶戏原则。

Result: 得到了一套木偶戏原则，这些原则提供了超越动画、戏剧和舞蹈的独特补充性洞察。

Conclusion: 木偶戏原则能有效弥补动画原则在实体机器人设计中的局限性，为机器人非语言行为设计提供新的理论指导。

Abstract: When designing robots' nonverbal behaviors, many researchers have turned to arts-based insights, such as Disney's Animation Principles. Yet, while these principles bear key insights into the design of like-life characters, their application to robot design is inherently limited, in part because animation is not constrained by real-world physics, and in part because animation principles focus on low level animation mechanics and not high-level design considerations for physically embodied, interactive characters. In contrast, little attention has been paid to art forms like puppetry, despite their long history of exploring morphological, behavior, and interaction design of physically embodied, interactive characters. As such, in this work we leverage puppetry texts and practicing puppeteers' expert knowledge knowledge to derive a set of puppetry principles with key insights for robot design. As we show, these insights go beyond -- and uniquely complement -- the prior insights provided by theater, dance, and animation.

</details>
