### [194] [U-Joint CAAMS: Experimental Evaluation of a Universal-Joint Continuum Manipulator for Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03321)
*Anuraj Uthayasooriyan,Musab Alibrahim,Krishna Manaswi Digumarti,Fernando Vanegas,Felipe Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于万向节的连续体机械臂，用于多旋翼无人机空中操作，通过弹簧增强的万向节和管状骨架设计，显著提升了抵抗面外变形的能力。在螺旋桨下洗气流条件下，相比传统的镍钛合金骨架设计，该方案在偏航、Y轴和横滚方向的平均误差分别降低2.5-4倍、2-45倍和5倍，并在水样采集、喷洒和物体运输任务中验证了系统有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼无人机搭载的连续体机械臂在进行空中操作时，由于载荷和螺旋桨下洗气流的作用，会产生面外弯曲和扭转，导致末端执行器位姿精度下降，亟需一种抗变形能力更强的机械臂设计。

Method: 提出一种基于万向节的连续体机械臂设计，采用管状骨架配合弹簧增强的万向节，并集成内部导管用于线缆布线和流体输送；在静态环境和螺旋桨下洗气流条件下进行实验测试，与镍钛合金骨架设计进行对比。

Result: 在所有测试条件下，新设计均表现出更好的抗面外变形能力。在最大下洗气流下，相比镍钛合金设计，偏航平均误差降低2.5-4倍，Y轴误差降低2-45倍，横滚误差降低高达5倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的万向节连续体机械臂有效提升了空中操作中的抗变形能力和末端精度，并通过水样采集、喷洒和物体运输等应用验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: Continuum manipulators mounted on multi-rotor UAVs enable compliant aerial manipulation, but payloads and propeller downwash amplify out-of-plane bending and twisting that degrade end-effector pose accuracy. To address this problem, we present a universal-joint-based continuum manipulator designed to improve resistance to out-of-plane deformation during aerial manipulation. The proposed design uses a tubular backbone with spring-reinforced universal joints and an integrated conduit for internal routing and fluid delivery. We evaluate the design in still air and under peak propeller downwash across varying payloads, and benchmark it against a prior Nitinol-backbone CM. Bench tests show improved resistance to out-of-plane deformation across all conditions. Under peak downwash, the proposed design reduces mean error by 2.5-4x in yaw, 2-45x in y-axis, and up to 5x in roll compared to the NiTi-backbone design. We further analyze hover stability through in-flight coupled-disturbance tests over varying payloads and actuation speeds, and demonstrate the system in water sampling, spot spraying, and object transport.

</details>


### [195] [Closed-loop vs. Open-loop Kalman Filter Architectures in Airborne Aided Inertial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03338)
*Antonia Hager,Torleiv H. Bryne*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对比了闭环误差状态卡尔曼滤波（INS领域主流）与开环架构（航空安全关键系统标准）的性能差异，通过仿真从消费级到导航级IMU，揭示了开环滤波在高精度IMU的信息融合平滑性与闭环架构的长期稳定性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键航空应用中，开环（前馈）架构因计算单元隔离和故障容错成为标准，但现有文献缺乏对该架构选择（除系统安全理由外）性能影响的量化评估。

Method: 采用标准大地坐标系下的惯性机械编排和直接位置辅助，通过仿真涵盖从消费级到导航级多种惯性传感器误差特性，对比开环与闭环误差状态卡尔曼滤波的性能。

Result: 开环滤波在高精度IMU上展现更平滑的信息融合特性，而闭环架构具有内在的长期稳定性优势；两者性能权衡取决于传感器误差等级。

Conclusion: 开环与闭环滤波架构的选择不仅是安全考量，更涉及性能权衡：高端IMU适合开环以获取平滑融合，低端IMU受益于闭环的长期稳定性。

Abstract: Closed-loop (or feedback) error-state Kalman filters with their relatives and offspring are the state-of-the-art in modern aided inertial navigation research. Estimated inertial navigation system (INS) errors are continually fed back to the INS to correct the nominal system state before subsequent predictions. Conversely, in safety-critical aeronautical applications, open-loop (or feedforward) systems are an undisputed standard, where the inertial mechanization is strictly decoupled to allow for operational independence and fault isolation of computing units. We assess the performance impacts of this architectural choice beyond qualitative system-safety justifications using a standard inertial mechanization in geodetic coordinates and direct position aiding. Simulations using a variety of inertial sensor error characteristics, ranging from consumer to navigation grade systems, showcase the trade-off between smooth information fusion for high-end IMUs using an open-loop filter and the inherent long-term stability of the closed-loop architecture.

</details>


### [196] [Feeling the Unexpected: ResTacVLA for Contact-Rich Manipulation via Residual Tactile Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03387)
*Pengwei Zhang,Bin Xie,Ce Hao,Xinpan Meng,Xinyu Guo,Fang Deng,Long Cheng,Tiancai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResTacVLA提出一种残差触觉表征方法，通过预测编码机制将触觉信号转化为视觉先验与物理感知之间的差异信息，利用矢量量化瓶颈提取潜在接触基元，并依据视觉不确定性自适应门控触觉融合，有效解决多模态触觉-视觉语言行动模型中的模态坍缩问题，在接触丰富的操作任务中优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉感知对接触丰富的操作任务至关重要，但将其集成到视觉-语言-行动（VLA）模型中往往导致模态坍缩——高带宽的视觉特征会压制稀疏的触觉线索，使得触觉信息无法有效发挥作用。

Method: 受预测编码启发，将触觉数据重构为残差触觉表征（视觉先验与物理感知的差异）；通过矢量量化瓶颈离散化为潜在接触基元；利用视觉先验的不确定性自适应门控触觉融合，仅在视觉不可靠阶段优先整合残差信号。

Result: 在一系列接触丰富的操作任务中，ResTacVLA持续优于所有基线方法，并且对意外的动态扰动表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将触觉信号转化为信息增益而非原始输入，并利用不确定性门控机制防止视觉主导，残差触觉表征能有效解决多模态融合中的带宽不匹配和模态坍缩问题。

Abstract: Tactile perception is indispensable for contact-rich manipulation, yet integrating it into Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often induces modality collapse, where high-bandwidth visual features overshadow sparse tactile cues. Inspired by Predictive Coding, a neural mechanism where the brain attenuates predictable inputs to prioritize surprising stimuli, we propose ResTacVLA. Rather than treating tactile data as raw input, we reformulate it as a Residual Tactile Representation capturing the discrepancy between visual priors and physical sensations. By filtering out visually predictable dynamics, this formulation transforms sparse tactile signals into dense, high-value information gain, thereby inherently resolving the bandwidth mismatch. These residuals are discretized through a Vector Quantized (VQ) bottleneck into Latent Contact Primitives that capture critical events missed by vision. Analogous to the neural surprise signal, we leverage the uncertainty of the visual prior to adaptively gate tactile integration, prioritizing residuals specifically during visually unreliable phases to explicitly prevent visual dominance. Experimental results show that ResTacVLA consistently outperforms all baselines on a diverse set of contact-rich manipulation tasks, while remaining robust to unexpected dynamic disturbances. Project page: https://awilekong.github.io/ResTacVLA-Website/

</details>


### [197] [HiMe: Hierarchical Embodied Memory for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03449)
*Li Ji,Siyin Wang,Pengfang Qian,Xiaopeng Yu,Yihai Tian,Zhaoye Fei,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiMe是一种分层具身记忆框架，将VLA模型解耦为高频执行器、工作记忆哨兵和长期策略规划器，通过跨模态语义模式和动态知识管理（添加、更新、删除操作）解决实时控制与慢速推理之间的频率-能力悖论，在长周期任务中显著优于平层记忆基线，并展现出基于人类偏好自我修正内部知识的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在需要长期记忆和推理的非马尔可夫任务中表现不佳，面临"频率-能力悖论"：强推理模型太慢无法实时控制，快速模型又缺乏足够推理能力，存在架构上的不匹配问题。

Method: 提出HiMe分层具身记忆框架，将具身智能解耦为三个模块：高频执行器（Executor）负责执行、哨兵（Sentry）负责工作记忆、规划器（Planner）负责长期策略。引入基于跨模态语义模式的动态知识系统，通过"添加、更新和删除"操作实现记忆可塑性。

Result: 该方法在长周期任务中显著优于平层记忆基线，并展现出基于人类偏好自我修正内部知识的新能力。

Conclusion: 分层设计有效平衡了实时执行与慢速思考规划之间的冲突，解决了频率-能力悖论，为机器人长期记忆和推理任务提供了有效的架构方案。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at robotic manipulation but often struggle with non-Markovian tasks requiring long-term memory and reasoning due to their reliance on immediate observations. Existing solutions face a ''frequency-competence paradox,'' where stronger reasoning models are too slow for real-time control, while faster models lack sufficient reasoning capabilities. To resolve this architectural misalignment, we propose HiMe, a Hierarchical Embodied Memory framework that decouples embodied intelligence into a high-frequency Executor for execution, a Sentry for working memory, and a Planner for long-term strategy. We also introduce a dynamic knowledge system based on cross-modal semantic schemas and active management mechanisms, allowing robots to maintain memory plasticity through ''Add, Update, and Delete'' operations. This hierarchical design effectively balances the conflict between real-time execution and slow thinking planning, significantly improving success rates in long-horizon tasks. Experiments demonstrate that this approach not only outperforms flat memory baselines but also exhibits the novel ability to self-correct its internal knowledge based on human preferences.

</details>


### [198] [ADP: Adversarial Dynamics Priors for Physically Grounded Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03454)
*Seokju Lee,Jeongtae Lee,Jeonghyeok Lim,Jeonguk Kang,Byungwook Lee,Seungho Han,Keun Ha Choi,Dongil Park,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗动力学先验（ADP），用动力学特征（如质心运动、接触力等）替代运动学特征作为对抗正则化目标，提升人形机器人抗扰运动控制的鲁棒性，相比AMP方法在抗扰阈值、恢复时间和速度跟踪误差上分别提升16.7%、47.9%和35.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于运动先验的方法通过模仿运动学特征产生自然运动风格，但未直接对动力学特征（如质心运动、质心动量、接触力和接触状态）进行正则化，导致抗扰能力不足。

Method: 用轨迹优化构建参考数据集，训练判别器评估策略生成的时域窗口是否符合参考分布，以选定的动力学特征替代运动学风格特征作为对抗正则化目标，无需显式运动跟踪。

Result: 相比AMP基线，ADP的80%成功率抗扰阈值（J80）提升16.7%，方向平均恢复时间降低47.9%，速度跟踪误差降低35.4%。

Conclusion: 以动力学特征作为对抗先验能有效提升人形机器人运动控制在扰动下的鲁棒性，验证了动力学正则化优于传统运动学风格模仿的思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Adversarial Dynamics Priors (ADP) for perturbation-resilient humanoid locomotion control. Existing motion prior-based methods induce natural motion styles by imitating kinematic motion features, but they do not directly regularize dynamics features, such as CoM motion, centroidal momentum, contact forces, and contact states. To address this limitation, we replace kinematic motion-style feature with selected dynamics features extracted from locomotion trajectories as the target of adversarial regularization.To this end, we use trajectory optimization to construct a reference dataset and train a discriminator to evaluate whether policy-induced temporal windows are consistent with the resulting reference distribution.Without explicit motion tracking, ADP encourages policy rollouts to remain close to the reference support, even after perturbations. Experimental results show that, compared with AMP, the strongest baseline in our evaluation, ADP improves the $80\%$-success impulse threshold ($J_{80}$) by $16.7\%$, while reducing direction-averaged recovery time and velocity tracking error by $47.9\%$ and $35.4\%$, respectively.

</details>


### [199] [High-Precision Formation Control for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems via Hierarchical Hybrid Physics-Informed Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03512)
*Yanzhou Li,Guangli Chen,Xiao-Meng Li,Wenjian Zhong,Yongkang Lu,Shenghuang He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层混合物理信息深度强化学习（HHy-PIDRL）框架，用于异构多机器人系统的高精度、高响应编队控制，上层基于SAC算法实现领导者自主导航，下层融合物理前馈控制器、PD控制器和自适应DRL残差控制器形成混合模型与DRL编队控制策略，实验表明上下层策略成功率达100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制方法依赖精确模型且难以应对不确定性和外部扰动，而端到端强化学习方法样本效率低、收敛性差，现有方法无法同时满足异构多机器人系统高精度和高响应的编队控制需求。

Method: 提出HHy-PIDRL分层框架：上层基于SAC算法为Ackermann转向领导者设计自主导航策略网络；下层集成高保真物理前馈控制器、经典PD控制器和自适应DRL残差控制器，构建HM-DRL编队控制策略网络；设计分层奖励函数训练全向跟随者。

Result: 上层自主导航策略网络和HM-DRL编队控制策略网络的成功率均达到100%，消融实验验证了方法的有效性和可靠性。

Conclusion: HHy-PIDRL框架有效结合了物理模型控制与深度强化学习的优势，克服了传统方法和纯RL方法的各自局限，为异构多机器人系统提供了一种高精度、高响应的编队控制方案。

Abstract: Existing classical control methods commonly require precise models and struggle to cope with model uncertainties and external disturbances, while end-to-end reinforcement learning (RL) approaches suffer from low sample efficiency and poor convergence. To overcome these challenges, this paper proposes a hierarchical hybrid physics-informed deep reinforcement learning (HHy-PIDRL) framework, aiming to realize high-precision, highly responsive formation control for heterogeneous multi-robot systems (HMRSs). The proposed framework contains two layers. Specifically, first, the upper layer designs an autonomous navigation policy network for Ackermann-steering leader based on the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithm. Second, the lower module integrates a high-fidelity physical feed-forward controller, a classical proportional-derivative (PD) controller, and an adaptive DRL residual controller to propose an effective hybrid model and DRL (HM-DRL)-based formation control policy network. Third, a unique hierarchical reward function is designed for training Omnidirectional followers, which effectively guides agents toward a refined, stable control policy. Experimental results demonstrate that, the success rate of both the upper-layer autonomous navigation policy network and the HM-DRL based formation control policy networks reach 100%. Meanwhile, ablation experiments are conducted to verify the validity and credibility of the proposed method.

</details>


### [200] [Current as Touch: Proprioceptive Contact Feedback for Compliant Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03529)
*Chenyang Ma,Yunchao Yao,Zhenyu Wei,Ruogu Li,Daniel Szafir,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于本体感觉的框架，利用电机电流和关节状态学习接触感知的柔顺参考位置，无需外部触觉或力传感器即可实现灵巧手的柔顺抓取操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作中的柔顺控制方案依赖外部触觉或力传感器，这些传感器成本高、易损坏且难以部署在低成本机器人手上，亟需一种无需额外传感硬件的柔顺控制方法。

Method: 利用电机电流与关节力矩的强相关性，将电机电流作为接触力、物体阻力和抓取稳定性的内在感知信号；通过预测柔顺参考位置（PD控制器的关节位置目标），使位置误差产生适当的抓取力，从而在标准PD控制器框架下实现基于本体感觉反馈的交互力自适应调节。

Result: 在多种灵巧手和接触密集任务（易碎物体操作、持续表面接触、薄物体拾取、动态负载适应）中，实现了稳定的柔顺抓取、更安全高效的遥操作，并提升了后续策略学习的效果，且无需外部触觉或力传感器。

Conclusion: 电机电流不仅可作为力的代理信号，还可作为可学习的本体感受接触信号用于柔顺参考位置预测，该位置基公式与主流遥操作和策略学习管线兼容，为低成本灵巧手的柔顺操作提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Compliance is essential for dexterous manipulation, yet existing solutions often rely on external tactile or force sensors that are costly, fragile, and difficult to deploy on low-cost robot hands. We propose a proprioception-driven framework that learns contact-aware compliance cues from motor current and joint states. Since motor current is closely related to actuator torque, it provides an intrinsic signal for perceiving contact force, object resistance, and grasp stability without additional sensing hardware. Rather than estimating external wrenches or commanding torque, our method predicts a compliance reference position: an ideal joint-position target for a standard PD controller whose induced position error generates appropriate grasping force. This position-based formulation is compatible with mainstream teleoperation and policy-learning pipelines, while enabling the robot to adapt interaction forces from real-time proprioceptive feedback. Thus, motor current serves not only as a force proxy but also as a learnable proprioceptive contact signal for compliance reference prediction. Experiments on multiple dexterous hands and contact-rich tasks, including fragile object handling, sustained surface contact, thin-object retrieval, and dynamic load adaptation, show stable compliant grasping, safer and more efficient teleoperation, and improved downstream policy learning without external tactile or force sensors.

</details>


### [201] [CoorGrasp: Coordinated Contact Control for Adaptive Dexterous Grasping Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03557)
*Mingrui Yu,Yongpeng Jiang,Yongyi Jia,Ren Yi,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于触觉驱动的模型预测控制器，用于灵巧手抓取的适应性执行，通过多触点协调在三阶段（接近、抓取、调整）中补偿位置误差并平衡接触力，在大规模仿真（478个物体上的15000次抓取）和真实实验中均提升了抓取成功率并减少了物体非期望运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究集中于灵巧抓取姿态生成，但忽视了抓取执行环节。在形状和位置不确定性下，开环执行常导致非协调接触，引发物体在手中非期望运动甚至抓取失败。为此，需要一种能自适应、精细执行多样化灵巧抓取的控制方法。

Method: 提出触觉驱动的模型预测控制器，包含三个关键创新：(i) 协调感知的阶段分离，(ii) 手臂-手部协调补偿位置误差，(iii) 自适应力协调以平衡方式增加接触力。采用解析模型关联接触力与机器人关节运动，实现预测控制。该方法不限制抓取类型或接触配置，可与现有抓取姿态生成方法无缝集成。

Result: 在478个物体上的15000次抓取仿真（三种机器人手）以及8个物体的真实实验中，该方法相比基线实现了更高的抓取成功率和更小的非期望物体运动。

Conclusion: 触觉驱动的模型预测控制能有效提升灵巧手抓取执行的鲁棒性，通过多触点协调补偿不确定性，方法通用性强且易于与现有框架集成。

Abstract: While recent research has focused heavily on dexterous grasp pose generation, less attention has been devoted to the execution of planned grasps. Under shape and position uncertainty, open-loop execution often yields uncoordinated contacts, causing undesired in-hand object motion and even grasp failures. To address this, this paper proposes a tactile-driven model predictive controller for adaptive and delicate execution of diverse dexterous grasps. Our approach emphasizes multi-contact coordination across both approaching and grasping phases, with three key novelties: (i) coordination-aware phase separation, (ii) arm-hand coordination to compensate for position errors, and (iii) adaptive force coordination to increase contact forces in a balanced manner. An analytical model is employed to relate contact forces to robot joint motions for predictive control. Our formulation imposes no restrictions on grasp types or contact configurations and integrates seamlessly with state-of-the-art grasp pose generation methods. We validate the approach through large-scale simulations involving 15k grasps across 478 objects on three robotic hands, and real-world experiments on 8 objects. Results demonstrate that our method achieves higher grasp success rates and reduced undesired object movements.

</details>


### [202] [Cross-Embodiment Robot Manipulation via a Unified Hand Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03570)
*Luis Felipe Casas,Robert Teal,Keval Shah,Abhijit Tadepalli,Wanxin Jin,Yu Xiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一手部动作空间（UHAS），通过球体几何变形表示灵巧操作动作，结合级联逆运动学（CIK）算法实现跨多种机器人手的策略迁移，在立方体重新定向任务中验证了零样本迁移和快速微调能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略通常绑定特定机械手形态，限制了学习行为在不同运动学结构平台间的迁移，亟需一种跨形态的统一动作表示方法。

Method: 提出UHAS：将手部动作表示为规范球体的几何变形，并设计CIK算法将共享表示映射到具体手部关节配置；在强化学习框架中直接在该动作空间训练灵巧操作策略。

Result: 在Allegro Hand、LEAP Hand、Shadow Hand和MANO Human Hand等多种机械手上验证了有效灵巧操作、零样本迁移、快速微调以及真实世界部署的成功。

Conclusion: UHAS表示能够实现稳定的灵巧控制和跨形态策略迁移，为不同机械手平台间的技能共享提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies are typically tied to specific robotic hand embodiments, limiting the transfer of learned behaviors across platforms with different kinematic structures. In this work, we propose the Unified Hand Action Space (UHAS), a sphere-based unified action representation for cross-embodiment dexterous manipulation. UHAS represents robotic hand actions as geometric deformations of a canonical sphere and uses a Cascade Inverse Kinematics (CIK) algorithm to map the shared representation to embodiment-specific joint configurations. Using reinforcement learning, we train dexterous manipulation policies directly in the proposed action space for in-hand cube reorientation tasks. We evaluate our method in both simulation and real-world experiments across multiple robotic hands, including the Allegro Hand, LEAP Hand, Shadow Hand, and MANO Human Hand. Experimental results demonstrate effective dexterous manipulation, zero-shot transfer to unseen hands, rapid finetuning across embodiments, and successful real-world deployment. Our experiments show that the proposed UHAS representation enables stable dexterous control and cross-embodiment policy transfer across robotic hands.

</details>


### [203] [ROBOCYCLE: Autonomous Dual-Arm Robotic Manipulation and Coordination for Recycling Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03616)
*Rubén de J. Hilario-Cruz,Jesus A. García-González,Enrique Coronado,Arturo E. Cerón-López*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍ROBOCYCLE——一种面向东京都市圈回收标准的双臂机器人自主分拣平台，集成多视角RGB-D感知、RF-DETR实例分割和Anygrasp抓取规划，对透明、可变形和杂乱废弃物实现90.3%抓取成功率和84.3%整体任务成功率，包括拧开PET瓶盖等复杂操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市垃圾量激增与劳动力短缺使自动化分拣系统成为必需，但现有机器人方案在透明、可变形及杂乱物体的3D感知与操作上存在困难。

Method: 采用多视角RGB-D感知、基于RF-DETR的Transformer实例分割、Anygrasp SDK的6自由度抓取规划，通过对分割点云处理生成面向不规则和可变形废弃物的鲁棒候选抓取位姿。

Result: 抓取成功率90.3%，整体任务成功率84.3%，成功完成拧开PET瓶盖等复杂协同操作。

Conclusion: 该平台为真实人类环境中的自主废物管理提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: As urban waste volumes escalate and labor shortages intensify, automated waste sorting systems are becoming a necessity. However, current robotic solutions often struggle with the 3D perception and manipulation of transparent, deformable, or cluttered objects. This work introduces ROBOCYCLE, an autonomous dual-arm robotic recycling platform designed to meet the recycling standards of the Tokyo metropolitan area. Our approach integrates multi-view RGB-D perception, transformer-based instance segmentation using RF-DETR, and 6-DoF grasp planning via the Anygrasp SDK. By processing segmentated point clouds, the system generates robust candidate poses for irregular and deformable waste. The system achieved a 90.3% grasp success rate and 84.3% overall task success rate, effectively performing complex coordinated tasks such as unscrewing PET bottle caps. The proposed platform offers a scalable solution for autonomous waste management in real-world human environments.

</details>


### [204] [CoRE-VLA: Towards Scalable and Robust Vision-Language-Action Modeling via Conditional Routing of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03693)
*Haozhe Zhang,Sixian Li,Yifei Zhang,Zezheng Huai,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRE-VLA，一种将动作生成建模为上下文条件稀疏计算的VLA框架，通过传感器可用性门控模态专家和任务意图路由动作表示，实现了在传感器缺失时的优雅降级和任务特化，在LIBERO、RoboCasa GR1和真实双臂操作中取得了优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实部署中机器人传感器配置多样且异构，辅助传感器可能意外故障，不同机器人本体设计上也可能缺少某些传感器。现有VLA策略将动作生成耦合到固定传感器集，通过共享密集计算实现，导致传感器缺失时表现脆弱，且难以在不同任务和长时域行为中特化。

Method: 提出CoRE-VLA框架，将动作生成形式化为上下文条件的稀疏计算。传感器可用性门控模态专家，实现无重训练下的传感器缺失优雅降级；任务意图进一步将动作侧表示路由到任务相关专家，提升跨任务和长时域子目标的特化能力。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop和真实双臂操作实验中，CoRE-VLA在长时域和多任务基准上取得强结果，优于密集动作生成消融版本和强预训练VLA基线，包括零样本泛化到未见场景。模态分析表明，深度可用时可充分利用深度信息，深度缺失时仍保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: CoRE-VLA通过稀疏条件计算实现了对异构传感器配置的鲁棒适应，为实际部署中传感器缺失问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced generalist robotic manipulation, yet real-world deployment reveals a fundamental challenge: robots are equipped with diverse and heterogeneous sensor configurations, auxiliary sensors can fail unexpectedly during operation, and different robot embodiments often lack certain sensors by design. A unified policy that can exploit auxiliary perceptual inputs when available while remaining reliable under sensor absence, whether incidental or by design, is therefore essential for practical deployment. However, existing VLA policies couple action generation to a fixed sensor set through shared dense computation, making them brittle when sensors are missing and limiting their ability to specialize across diverse tasks and long-horizon behaviors. We propose CoRE-VLA, a scalable and robust VLA framework that formulates action generation as context-conditioned sparse computation. Sensor availability gates modality-specialized experts, enabling graceful degradation under missing sensors without retraining. Task intent further routes action-side representations to task-relevant experts, improving specialization across diverse tasks and long-horizon subgoals. While the framework is designed to accommodate different auxiliary sensors, we focus on depth as a representative and practically important auxiliary modality in our experiments. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop, and real-world dual-arm manipulation show that CoRE-VLA achieves strong results on long-horizon and multi-task benchmarks, and outperforms both a dense-action-generator ablation and a strong pretrained VLA baseline, including in zero-shot generalization to unseen scenarios. Modality analysis shows that CoRE-VLA can exploit auxiliary depth when available while remaining robust when depth is unavailable during deployment.

</details>


### [205] [Lost in Time? Continuous Symmetry and Identifiability in Aided Inertial Navigation with Unknown Measurement Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03699)
*Jonathan Kelly,Phone Thiha Kyaw,Mattew Giamou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究多传感器系统中未知恒定时间延迟的可辨识性问题，指出延迟的可辨识性不仅取决于测量时间结构，还取决于载体轨迹形状。利用特殊伽利略群刻画了退化轨迹，并揭示了比以往报告更大的不可辨识轨迹类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器系统中不同传感器之间存在未知的相对时间延迟，准确的状态估计需要对这些延迟进行在线校准，而延迟的可辨识性条件尚未被充分理解。

Method: 利用特殊伽利略群（special Galilean group）对延迟测量模型进行几何分析，将退化轨迹与模型的连续对称性相关联，并与线性化雅可比分析方法进行对比。

Result: 发现不可辨识的轨迹类别比以往文献报道的更广泛，并建立了退化轨迹与延迟测量模型连续对称性之间的几何联系。

Conclusion: 虽然研究动机来自组合导航，但所提出的思想可更广泛地应用于带有延迟测量的李群上的估计问题。

Abstract: In many multisensor systems, measurements from different sensors are subject to unknown relative time delays. Accurate state estimation requires that delays be accounted for and, when possible, calibrated online. We consider the case of aided navigation, where measurements from a single aiding sensor are subject to an unknown but constant delay relative to the inertial measurement stream, and study the identifiability of the resulting system. Critically, identifiability depends not only on the temporal structure of the measurements, but also on the shape of the vehicle trajectory: some trajectories are sufficiently informative to support unique recovery of the delay and the navigation state, while others are not. Using the special Galilean group, we characterize these uninformative (or degenerate) trajectories and relate them to a continuous symmetry of the delayed measurement model, providing geometric insight into identifiability failures. We show that the class of trajectories for which identifiability fails is larger than previously reported, and connect our characterization to the familiar linearized, Jacobian-based analysis. Although our development is motivated by aided navigation, the underlying ideas apply more broadly to estimation problems on Lie groups with delayed measurements.

</details>


### [206] [OmniTacTune: Policy-Agnostic Real-World RL for Tactile Residual Adaptation of Visual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03723)
*Kelin Yu,Haode Zhang,Harish Ravichandar,Yunhai Han,Ruohan Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniTacTune是一种策略无关的真实世界RL流水线，通过残差修正将触觉反馈适配到预训练的视觉策略中，在40-80分钟内将接触丰富任务的成功率从5-40%提升至85-100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉策略在接触丰富的操作任务中常因缺乏局部力和接触几何信息而失败，而触觉数据采集成本高且难以跨传感器、机器人和任务泛化。

Method: 两阶段设计：第一阶段从自主基策略rollouts中引导触觉感知学习，第二阶段通过在线交互学习轻量级触觉残差策略。

Result: 在四个真实世界接触丰富任务中，将视觉基策略的成功率从5-40%提升至85-100%，训练时间40-80分钟。

Conclusion: OmniTacTune提供了一种高效路径，将触觉反馈适配到可扩展的视觉机器人策略中。

Abstract: Visual policies learned from human videos, teleoperation, and robot demonstrations offer scalable motion priors, but often fail in contact-rich manipulation, where success significantly depends on local force and contact geometry. Tactile sensing provides these complementary signals, yet tactile data remain costly to collect and hard to generalize across sensors, robots, and tasks. We introduce OmniTacTune, a policy-agnostic real-world RL pipeline that adapts tactile feedback to pretrained visual policies through residual correction. OmniTacTune uses a two-stage design: it first bootstraps tactile-aware learning from autonomous base-policy rollouts, then learns a lightweight tactile residual policy through online interaction. Extensive experiments show that OmniTacTune generalizes across diverse contact-rich tasks, visual base policies, and tactile representations. Across four real-world contact-rich tasks, it improves visual base policies from 5-40% success to 85-100% within 40-80 minutes, demonstrating an efficient path for adapting tactile feedback to scalable visual robot policies. Project page: https://colinyu1.github.io/omnitactune-site/

</details>


### [207] [Look Before You Leap: Distilling Tree Search into Action Evaluation for Frozen VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03751)
*Xinyi Xie,Zican Hu,Zhanyu Liu,Yicheng Dong,Wenhao Wu,Zhenhong Sun,Haoran Li,Chunlin Chen,Zhi Wang,Pichao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SVA框架通过解耦动作提议与后果评估，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索探索VLA模型的输出分布并蒸馏为轻量Q值模型，在保持预训练泛化能力的同时显著提升任务成功率，且9B模型以更低推理延迟超越27B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽通过大规模预训练获得广泛具身能力，但其泛化性能远弱于LLM和VLM；现有后训练方法（SFT/RL）提升任务性能却损害了预训练带来的通用能力。作者发现VLA失败不仅源于动作生成，更源于动作评估不足。

Method: 提出SVA框架：(1) 在仿真环境中使用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）充分探索冻结VLA的输出分布，收集带经验回报的多样化轨迹；(2) 将这些知识蒸馏为轻量Q值模型，预测候选动作的预期后果；(3) 部署时冻结VLA提议多个候选动作，评估器选择不确定性正则化Q值最高的动作，无需仿真器参与。

Result: pass@k诊断表明冻结VLA在pass@1时成功率33%，pass@32时提升至92%。SVA在具身基准测试中持续提升未见任务的泛化能力，展现强测试时缩放行为。9B VLA搭配SVA以27%更低推理延迟超越27B VLA 7个百分点。

Conclusion: 动作评估是关键瓶颈，解耦动作提议与后果评估可兼顾泛化与性能；测试时评估缩放比模型规模缩放更具成本效益。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models acquire broad embodied capabilities through large-scale pretraining, yet their generalization remains far more fragile than that of LLMs and VLMs. The prevailing remedy, post-training via supervised fine-tuning or reinforcement learning, improves task-specific performance but narrows the generalist capability that makes pretraining valuable. We identify a key bottleneck: VLA failures stem not only from action generation but also from action evaluation. A diagnostic pass@k study confirms that frozen VLAs already contain competent behaviors in their output distribution, with overall success rates rising from 33% at pass@1 to 92% at pass@32. Inspired by this, we propose SVA (Search, Value, and Act), a simple framework that equips frozen VLA policies with long-term consequence awareness. SVA first uses Monte-Carlo tree search in simulation to fully explore the VLA's output distribution and collect diverse trajectories annotated with empirical returns; this knowledge is then distilled into a lightweight Q-value model that predicts the expected consequence of candidate actions; at deployment, the frozen VLA proposes multiple candidates and the evaluator selects the one with the highest uncertainty-regularized Q-value, requiring no simulator access. By decoupling action proposal from consequence evaluation, SVA preserves the generalization capacity of the VLA backbone while substantially improving task success rates. Experiments across embodied benchmarks show that SVA consistently improves generalization on unseen tasks and exhibits strong test-time scaling behavior. Strikingly, SVA enables a 9B VLA to outperform a 27B VLA by 7 points at 27% lower inference latency, suggesting that scaling test-time evaluation is more cost-effective than scaling model size.

</details>


### [208] [Occluding the Solution Space: Planner-Agnostic Adversarial Attacks on Tolerance-Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03758)
*Keke Tang,Tianyu Hao,Weilong Peng,Hao Jiang,Feng Wu,Peican Zhu,Jianmin Ji,Zhihong Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需规划器交互的对抗攻击框架，通过运动学占用热图编码机器人工作空间能力，将攻击建模为预算最大覆盖优化问题，在保证几何约束的同时高效诱导规划失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动规划对抗攻击方法受限于精确位姿目标和依赖规划器交互查询，计算效率低且泛化性差，亟需一种与规划器无关、高效的攻击框架来评估机器人操作的鲁棒性。

Method: 离线阶段构建运动学占用热图，编码可行轨迹密度和鲁棒性先验；在线阶段将攻击形式化为预算约束下的最大覆盖优化，在几何约束下策略性部署障碍物以遮挡解空间。

Result: 在仿真和真实场景中，该方法在诱导规划失败率、计算效率上显著优于依赖规划器交互的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为机器人操作鲁棒性评估提供了无需规划器先验知识的高效攻击范式，实现了任务级可行性评估的范式转变。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on motion planning are crucial for evaluating and quantifying the intrinsic robustness of robotic manipulation. However, existing approaches are typically limited by restrictive exact-pose objectives and their reliance on planner-in-the-loop queries. To address these limitations, we propose a planner-agnostic attack framework for tolerance-aware manipulation. Our approach shifts the evaluation paradigm to task-level feasibility over goal regions, efficiently inserting adversarial obstacles without requiring oracle access to the victim system. Offline, we characterize the robot's intrinsic workspace capabilities via a kinematic occupancy heatmap, which encodes the density of feasible trajectories and robustness priors without invoking a specific planner. Online, we formulate the attack as a budgeted maximum-coverage optimization, strategically deploying obstacles subject to explicit geometric constraints to occlude the solution space. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-world scenarios demonstrate that our method reliably induces planning failures, significantly outperforming planner-in-the-loop baselines in both computational efficiency and attack efficacy.

</details>


### [209] [From Region Arrival to Instance-Level Grounding in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03792)
*Xiangyu Shi,Ruoxi Yang,Wei Tao,Jiwen Zhang,Yanyuan Qiao,Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现VLN智能体即使成功导航也可能因缺乏细粒度目标接地而失败（即Last-3-Meter Grounding Gap），为此提出REALM模块和REVERIE-AIM数据集，通过区域到实体对齐和可见性感知停止策略显著提升近距精度与视觉接地成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN评估仅以3米半径内停止为标准，忽略了最终朝向和目标可见性，导致智能体缺乏可靠的物体级接地能力。作者旨在填补这一评估与训练上的空白。

Method: 提出REALM——一种即插即用的解耦式精调模块，将细粒度目标逼近与长程导航分离；设计可见性感知停止策略以降低过早终止；并构建REVERIE-AIM数据集，提供目标实例级标注及18万短程训练样本。

Result: 在四种不同VLN骨干网络上进行广泛评估，REALM持续提升近距精度和视觉接地成功率，证实其广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 通过引入实例级度量（近距精度、目标可见性、最终视图接地）和REALM模块，可有效弥合Last-3-Meter接地鸿沟，提升VLN智能体的细粒度目标定位能力。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) agents may satisfy conventional success criteria while still failing to establish reliable object-level grounding, because current evaluation protocols mainly reward stopping within a 3-meter radius and largely ignore the agent's final orientation and target visibility. We formalize this limitation as the Last-3-Meter Grounding Gap and introduce three instance-centric metrics to quantify proximity precision, target visibility, and final-view grounding. To mitigate this gap, we propose REALM (Region-to-Entity Alignment for Last-3-Meter Navigation), a plug-and-play, architecture-agnostic refinement module that decouples fine-grained target approaching from long-horizon navigation. REALM uses a visibility-aware stopping strategy to reduce premature termination and improve final viewpoint alignment. We further construct REVERIE-AIM, which provides object-instance-level goals and 180K short-horizon training samples for final-stage target approaching. Extensive evaluations across four diverse VLN backbones show that REALM consistently improves proximity precision and visual grounding success, demonstrating its broad applicability.

</details>


### [210] [ObjRetarget: An Object-Aware Motion Retargeting Framework with Anthropomorphic Arm Constraints and Polyhedral Hand Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03828)
*Yuanchuan Lai,Qing Gao,Ziyan Liang,Junjie Hu,Zhaojie Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ObjRetarget框架，通过整合拟人化手臂轨迹约束和结构化手-物几何建模，从人类视频中重定向灵巧操作动作，实现稳定接触和精准操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从人类视频到机器人灵巧操作的意图重定向方法缺乏显式接触建模或依赖强化学习，导致精度和泛化能力有限。

Method: 手臂层面：从人类视频提取参考轨迹，结合拟人化约束和冗余感知优化生成自然准确动作；手部层面：利用多面体簇表示多指接触，通过几何不变量保持接触结构稳定性。

Result: 在真实机器人上，ObjRetarget在多个灵巧操作任务中提升了操作成功率和接触稳定性，并泛化到不同演示、物体位姿和任务设置。

Conclusion: ObjRetarget通过显式几何接触建模与拟人化手臂约束的结合，有效提升了人-机器人运动重定向的准确性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Learning robot dexterous manipulation from human manipulation videos requires reliably retargeting human intent to executable robot actions while maintaining stable hand-object contact, which remains a key challenge in embodied intelligence. Existing retargeting methods often ignore explicit contact modeling or rely on reinforcement learning, resulting in limited accuracy and generalization. To address this, we propose ObjRetarget, a human-to-robot motion retargeting framework for learning robot dexterous manipulation from human videos, which integrates anthropomorphic arm trajectory constraints with structured hand-object geometric modeling. For arm motion, reference trajectories extracted from human videos are used for initialization, followed by anthropomorphic constraints and redundancy-aware optimization to generate natural and accurate movements. For hand manipulation, ObjRetarget represents multi-finger contacts using polytope clusters and preserves contact structure through geometric invariants to improve stability. Experiments on real robots show that ObjRetarget improves manipulation success rates and contact stability across multiple dexterous tasks, and generalizes well to different demonstrations, object poses, and task settings.

</details>


### [211] [High-Fidelity One-Step Generative Visuomotor Policy via Recursive Correction, Frequency Consistency, and Contrastive Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03865)
*Yuran Chen,Xinye Cai,Zhonglin Gong,Yang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高保真单步生成式视觉运动策略框架，通过递归一致动作流、双时间步频率一致性和对比流匹配三种机制，解决了现有单步加速方法中的空间偏差、频率失真和模式平均问题，在多个仿真和真实机器人平台上以单步前向传播实现了与10步生成策略相当或更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视觉运动策略（如扩散模型、流匹配）因多步采样/ODE求解导致推理延迟高，而单步加速方法存在空间截断误差、高频细节丢失及多模态动作混淆等问题。

Method: 1) 递归一致动作流(RCAF)：通过递归校正补偿空间截断误差，对齐单步预测与精炼流轨迹；2) 双时间步频率一致性(DTFC)：通过自适应频谱一致性保留高频操作细节；3) 对比流匹配(CFM)：基于间隔排斥目标分离纠缠的动作流，减少多模态操作中的模糊动作。

Result: 在RoboTwin、RoboTwin 2.0、Adroit、DexArt及真实机器人平台上，该方法仅需1次前向传播(1 NFE)即可达到或超越强10步生成策略基线的性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高保真单步生成式视觉运动策略框架，通过三种互补机制有效解决了单步加速中的空间、频率和模态问题，实现了低延迟且高性能的视觉运动控制。

Abstract: Generative models such as diffusion and flow matching have advanced robotic visuomotor policies by modeling multimodal action distributions, but their multi-step sampling or ODE solving introduces inference latency. Existing one-step acceleration methods often compress the whole generation process into a single large update, leading to spatial deviation, frequency distortion, and mode averaging. This paper proposes a high-fidelity one-step generative visuomotor policy framework that addresses these issues with three complementary mechanisms. Recursive Consistent Action Flow (RCAF) uses recursive correction to compensate for spatial truncation errors and align one-step predictions with refined flow trajectories. Dual-Timestep Frequency Consistency (DTFC) preserves high-frequency manipulation details through adaptive spectral consistency across flow timesteps. Contrastive Flow Matching (CFM) separates entangled action flows with a margin-based repulsive objective, reducing ambiguous actions in multimodal manipulation. Experiments on RoboTwin, RoboTwin 2.0, Adroit, DexArt, and real-world robot platforms show that the proposed method achieves competitive or superior performance compared with strong 10-step generative policy baselines while requiring only one forward pass (1 NFE), enabling low-latency visuomotor control.

</details>


### [212] [LH-AVLN: A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Audio-Visual-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03920)
*Rufeng Chen,Yue Chang,Zili Shao,Zhaofan Zhang,Hechang Chen,Hui Xiong,Sihong Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了LH-AVLN基准，用于长时域视听语言导航，包含多目标任务、异构目标规范和持续空间化声学线索。同时开发了PAG-Nav无训练参考智能体，利用渐进式目标状态规划处理声音引导搜索与视觉语义验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航研究存在两个问题：长时域基准大多缺乏声音信息，而视听导航任务通常只关注单一目标。缺乏结合多目标执行、异构目标规范和持续空间化声学线索的综合基准。

Method: 提出LH-AVLN基准，包含2-4个目标（按类别、语言描述或参考图像指定），智能体使用RGB-D观测、位姿和双耳音频在室内3D环境中导航。支持有序和无序任务。开发PAG-Nav参考智能体，维护时间统一语义地图，执行渐进式目标状态规划，用声音引导搜索，用视觉语义验证完成。

Result: 现有视觉语言、记忆基和视听智能体难以完成完整LH-AVLN任务；PAG-Nav提供了更强的诊断基线，但仍存在较大改进空间。

Conclusion: 长时域视听语言导航任务对现有方法构成显著挑战，PAG-Nav作为无训练基线展示了有效策略，但距离解决该问题仍有很大差距，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Embodied navigation is moving toward long-horizon missions, yet existing long-horizon benchmarks are largely acoustically silent, and audio-visual navigation tasks typically focus on a single goal. We introduce LH-AVLN, a benchmark for Long-Horizon Audio-Visual-Language Navigation that combines multi-goal mission execution, heterogeneous goal specifications, and persistent spatialized acoustic cues. In LH-AVLN, an agent receives a global mission of two to four goals specified by category, language description, or reference image, and navigates with RGB-D observations, pose, and binaural audio in indoor 3D environments. The benchmark supports both ordered and unordered missions, where alternating goal-associated sounds can guide non-line-of-sight search but may also become distractors as mission progress changes. We further develop PAG-Nav, a training-free reference agent that maintains a temporal uniform semantic map and performs progressive goal-state planning, using sound for search while reserving completion for visual-semantic verification. Experiments show that existing vision-language, memory-based, and audio-visual agents struggle to complete full LH-AVLN missions, and that PAG-Nav provides a stronger diagnostic baseline while leaving substantial room for future progress.

</details>


### [213] [WSA$_1$: a 3D-Centric World-Spatial-Action Model for Generalizable Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03941)
*Jiahao Jiang,Jianing Zhang,Zhenhan Yin,Ruidong Chen,Sen Wang,Zhaoshu Yu,Pengpeng Zeng,Xiaofeng Cao,Xuanhan Wang,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WSA₁，一种基于3D中心的世界-空间-行为联合建模范式的机器人基础模型，通过建模3D世界状态与机器人动作的相互约束，仅用6k小时专家数据（其中1k小时真实数据）即可在RoboTwin2.0模拟基准上达到93%的成功率，并在真实机器人控制任务中比现有最先进RFM平均提升+20%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型（RFMs）依赖2D视觉进行模仿学习，缺乏对物理动态和机器人行为在3D世界中因果效应的内在推理能力，导致2D视觉感知与3D具身交互之间存在根本性不匹配，严重限制了RFMs在真实世界任务中的泛化能力。

Method: 提出3D中心的世界-空间-行为（World-Spatial-Action）联合建模范式WSA₁，该模型不仅学习3D世界感知的视觉思维以预测未来机器人行为，还建模3D世界状态转换与机器人动作之间的相互约束关系，以增强行为泛化能力。

Result: 在仅使用6k小时专家演示数据（其中仅1k小时来自真实机器人）的条件下，WSA₁在RoboTwin2.0模拟基准上达到93%的成功率，并在真实机器人控制任务中相比现有最先进RFM平均性能提升超过20%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，结合3D中心的世界-行为联合建模，无需大规模真实机器人数据即可获得高泛化能力的机器人基础模型，为通用机器人系统提供了一条实用且经济的路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in embodied AI have established robot foundation models (RFMs) as the dominant approach for generalist robotic systems to date. By leveraging imitation learning on extensive robot demonstrations, RFMs have achieved impressive capabilities in mapping visual observations and language instructions to continuous robotic actions. However, current RFMs lack an inherent ability to reason about physical dynamics and the causal effects of robot behaviors on the 3D physical world. This creates a fundamental mismatch between 2D-centric visual perception and 3D-centric embodied interaction, severely limiting the generalization ability of RFMs in real-world tasks.To address this gap, we present WSA$_1$, a novel RFM built upon proposed 3D-Centric World-Spatial-Action modeling paradigm. It not only learns 3D world-aware visual thought for future robot behaviors, but also models mutual constraints between 3D world state transitions and robotic actions to enhance behavior generalization. Notably, WSA$_1$ achieves highly data-efficient pre-training with 6k hours of expert demonstration data (only 1k hours from real robot), while delivering competitive manipulation performance (93% success rate) on RoboTwin2.0 simulation benchmark and achieving +20% average boosted performance over state-of-the-art RFMs on real-world robot control tasks. These results reveal that generalizable RFM can be attained without large-scale real robot data when paired with 3D-centric world-action joint modeling, which offers a practical and affordable pathway to generalist robotic systems.

</details>


### [214] [Worldscape-MoE: A Unified Mixture-of-Experts World Model for Scalable Heterogeneous Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03964)
*Jianjie Fang,Yongyan Xu,Ziyou Wang,Chen Gao,Yuchao Huang,Zhaolu Wang,Rongze Tang,Mingyuan Jia,Baining Zhao,Weichen Zhang,Xin Zhang,Haisheng Su,Yu Shang,Wei Wu,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Worldscape-MoE，一种基于扩散Transformer的混合专家世界模型，通过模态感知控制注入、共享/专用专家和渐进式MoE微调策略，实现异构动作监督的统一学习与可扩展控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频生成世界模型围绕孤立的控制接口（如相机轨迹、机器人动作、手部关节信号）组织，缺乏统一且可扩展的学习框架来吸收异构动作监督，这成为扩展瓶颈。

Method: 提出Worldscape-MoE，基于扩散Transformer构建。核心包括：1) 模态感知控制注入；2) 共享专家与专用专家相结合；3) 渐进式MoE微调策略，支持持续扩展新动作模态。

Result: 在WorldArena上取得强结果；提升了运动控制和手部控制指标；展现出鲁棒的分布外泛化能力；随控制数据和专家集成增加表现出扩展行为。

Conclusion: 异构监督不仅不会干扰各自控制能力，反而能够相互促进，验证了不同控制接口共享相同世界物理规律和交互语义的核心假设。

Abstract: World models are rapidly becoming a core infrastructure for embodied intelligence and interactive agents: they provide controllable simulators in which agents can perceive, act, forecast, and acquire scalable experience. Yet current video generation world models are still organized around isolated control interfaces, such as camera trajectories, robot actions, or hand-joint signals. This fragmentation is increasingly a scaling bottleneck. The central challenge is not the absence of controllable generators, but the lack of a unified and extensible learning framework that can absorb heterogeneous action supervision while preserving a shared model of world dynamics. In this work, we introduce Worldscape-MoE, a Mixture-of-Experts world model built on Diffusion Transformers for scalable heterogeneous action control. Our key observation is that different controls specify different interfaces to the same underlying world: although their representations differ, they constrain shared physical regularities, scene dynamics, and interaction semantics. Worldscape-MoE operationalizes this observation through modality-aware control injection, shared and control-specific experts, and a progressive MoE tuning strategy that supports continual extension to new action modalities. Experiments across locomotion, robotic manipulation, and egocentric hand control show that heterogeneous supervision improves rather than interferes with individual control capabilities. Worldscape-MoE achieves strong results on WorldArena, improves locomotion and hand-control metrics, exhibits robust out-of-distribution generalization, and demonstrates scaling behavior as additional control data and experts are integrated.

</details>


### [215] [Fast Asymptotically Optimal Kinodynamic Planning via Vectorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03987)
*Yitian Gao,Andrew Lu,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PAKR，一种基于JAX/XLA的并行渐近最优动力学RRT规划器，通过标准Python工具实现GPU加速，结合AO-x元算法达到渐近最优性，在复杂动力学问题上展现出与最先进GPU规划器相当的运行时间和更优的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的运动规划器在实时性能上存在瓶颈，现有GPU加速方案依赖专用CUDA编程，限制了可访问性和可移植性。作者希望利用JAX和XLA编译器，通过标准Python工具实现高效的GPU并行加速，降低开发门槛。

Method: 1) 基于JAX框架和XLA编译器实现大规模并行运动规划，避免手动CUDA编程；2) 将并行规划器与AO-x元算法结合，通过快速迭代重规划实现渐近最优性；3) 使用MuJoCo-XLA模拟器处理复杂动力学；4) 提供概率完备性的理论分析，分析批大小和分支因子对收敛的影响。

Result: 与最先进的GPU规划器相比，PAKR具有竞争性的运行时间，并展现出更优的解质量。

Conclusion: PAKR通过JAX/XLA实现了可移植、易用的GPU加速运动规划，在保持高性能的同时降低了编程门槛，为并行运动规划提供了一种更易访问的技术路线。

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planners have been shown to be effective for systems with complex kinodynamic constraints and high dimensionality. However, these algorithms struggle to achieve real-time performance, leading to recent efforts to parallelize planning. While GPU-accelerated planners have achieved significant speedups, existing approaches require specialized CUDA programming that limits accessibility and portability. We present Parallel Asymptotically Optimal Kinodynamic RRT (PAKR), a massively parallel kinodynamic planner leveraging JAX and the XLA compiler to achieve GPU acceleration through standard Python tooling. By combining our parallel planner with the AO-x meta-algorithm, we achieve asymptotic optimality through fast iterative replanning. We provide a theoretical analysis of probabilistic completeness, analyze the effects of batch size and branching factor on convergence, and demonstrate scalability to complex dynamics using the MuJoCo-XLA simulator. Experiments show competitive runtimes with state-of-the-art GPU planners and superior solution quality.

</details>


### [216] [Conflict-Based Lazy Search for Fast Multi-Manipulator Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04124)
*Dongliang Zheng,Zhipeng Wang,Siqi Wang,Yuxi Lu,Bin He,Hesheng Wang,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为冲突基础惰性搜索(CBLS)的多机械臂规划算法，该算法通过预计算和惰性搜索增强冲突基础搜索(CBS)，并引入惰性边缘A*(LEA*)提高单机械臂路径规划效率，实验证明其性能优于CBS和RRT-Connect。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机械臂在杂乱工作空间中的实时规划仍面临巨大挑战，现有算法效率不足。

Method: 在CBS基础上引入预计算和惰性搜索：1) 预构建具有受控稀疏性的惰性评估图；2) 提出LEA*算法，利用惰性搜索和边缘队列减少边缘评估次数，以解决机械臂规划中边缘评估的计算瓶颈问题。

Result: LEA*在顶点效率上最优，边缘效率优于A*；CBLS在多机械臂规划问题中性能优于CBS和RRT-Connect。

Conclusion: CBLS通过预计算和惰性搜索有效提升了多机械臂规划效率，为多机械臂实时规划提供了新的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Employing multiple manipulators can boost efficiency and accomplish tasks that a single manipulator cannot do. However, real-time planning for multiple manipulators in a cluttered workspace still poses significant challenges for planning algorithms. This article proposes a new planning algorithm called Conflict-Based Lazy Search (CBLS) for multimanipulator planning. CBLS is built on Conflict-Based Search (CBS), an efficient multiagent pathfinding (MAPF) algorithm that has shown an order of magnitude speedup over previous approaches [1], [2]. CBS addresses MAPF by solving many single-agent pathfinding (SAPF) problems. Thus, its planning time directly depends on the efficiency of the SAPF algorithm adopted. Our CBLS algorithm enhances CBS with precomputation and lazy search. First, a lazily evaluated graph with controlled sparsity is precomputed for a single manipulator. Second, we propose the Lazy Edged-based A* (LEA*) for efficient SAPF. Since edge evaluation is the computational bottleneck of manipulator planning, LEA* uses lazy search and an edge queue to reduce the number of edge evaluations. We show that LEA* is optimally vertex efficient and has improved edge efficiency compared to A*. We apply the proposed CBLS to multi-manipulator planning problems and show its superior performance by comparing it with CBS and a sampling-based algorithm, namely, RRT-Connect.

</details>


### [217] [Semantic-Guided Progressive Object Removal with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04144)
*Xianliang Huang,Chen Xiao,Yuanxiang Ni,Guanming Liu,Mingkai Liu,Dikai Fan,Xiao Liu,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于语义引导块匹配（SBM）和区域渐进细化（RPR）的3D物体移除框架，利用DINOv2语义引导和多视图一致性，在三维高斯泼溅场景中实现高质量的物体去除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常单步完成整个遮罩区域的补全，且未能有效利用其他视角的语义信息，导致处理复杂几何细节和纹理时存在困难。

Method: 1) 利用DINOv2从多视图观测中编码语义引导，通过最佳匹配块解码补全目标视图的缺失区域并保持跨视图一致性；2) 引入区域渐进细化策略（RPR），将目标遮罩分割为多个子区域，选择性优化视觉质量差的区域；3) 基于高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）构建框架。

Result: 在感知质量和3D物体去除的一致性上优于现有基于高斯的方法。

Conclusion: 提出的SBM和RPR策略能有效利用语义信息实现高质量3D物体移除，为AR/VR、机器人及数字内容创作提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Removing unwanted objects from reconstructed 3D scenes is an important task in computer vision, supporting applications in AR/VR, robotics, and digital content creation. Existing methods typically complete the entire masked region in a single step and without effectively utilizing semantic information from other views, leading to difficulties in handling complex geometric details and textures. In this work, we propose a novel framework that integrates Semantic-guided Block Matching (SBM) and Region-Wise Progressive Refinement (RPR) for high-quality 3D object removal. First, we leverage DINOv2 to encode semantic guidance from multi-view observations, and the best match tokens are decoded to complete missing regions in the target view while maintaining cross-view consistency. Second, we introduce a RPR strategy that segments the target mask into multiple subregions and selectively refines those with poor visual quality. Our method is built upon Gaussian Splatting, ensuring high-fidelity scene reconstruction with efficient computation. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing Gaussian-based methods in terms of perceptual quality and coherence in 3D object removal.

</details>


### [218] [!Imperio, smolVLA: The Implications of Data Poisoning on Open Source Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04146)
*Stefan Bühler,Mark Schutera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明了视觉语言动作模型的触发词数据投毒是可行的，仅需3个中毒样本即可使机器人完全失效（成功率降至0%），且攻击具有隐蔽性和泛化能力，警示开源机器人生态需重视数据集来源安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示开源机器人生态中社区贡献的数据集存在信任假设漏洞，验证触发词数据投毒攻击对视觉语言动作模型的实际威胁，促使社区将数据集来源视为首要安全问题。

Method: 在smolVLA模型上针对真实世界拾放任务进行数据投毒实验，在LeRobot平台上以不同投毒比例（3/320、1/320等）和不同触发词位置（前置、中置、后置）评估攻击效果。

Result: 3个中毒样本即可实现完全拒绝服务（成功率0.0±0.0%），机器人锁定在固定关节位置；1个中毒样本使成功率降至6.7±6.7%；正常提示下成功率保持在约50%，攻击具有隐蔽性；攻击能从前置触发词泛化到其他位置。

Conclusion: 触发词数据投毒攻击是实用、低成本且隐蔽的威胁，开源机器人生态应将数据集来源安全作为一级关注点。

Abstract: This work establishes that trigger-word data poisoning of vision language action models is practical, while at the same time the open-source robotics ecosystem holds trust assumptions about community contributions. A few poisoned samples can silently embed a backdoor that disables a robot on command. We evaluate this threat against smolVLA on a real-world pick-and-place task, training on three poison ratios and evaluating across different prompts on the LeRobot platform. Three poisoned episodes in 320 clean episodes suffice for a complete denial of service. Success rate drops to 0.0 plus minus 0.0% across all trigger-word conditions and the robot locks into a fixed joint configuration rather than executing any task-relevant motion. Clean-prompt behaviour holds at approx. 50% success rate across all poison ratios, confirming the attack is stealthy under normal operation. A single poisoned episode already reduces success rate to 6.7 plus minus 6.7%. The robot still moves, but no longer completes the task. The attack generalises to front, middle, and end trigger placements despite training exclusively on front-placed triggers. These findings establish that the threat is practical, low-cost, and stealthy, and warrant treating dataset provenance as a first-class concern in open-source robotics ecosystems.

</details>


### [219] [ACE: Agentic Control for Embodied Manipulation via Zero-shot Workflow Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04162)
*Iok Tong Lei,QianZhi Li,Ying Jie Yap,Yujie Zhang,Rui Zhong,Haichao Gui,Xiaolong Liu,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACE是一个零样本工作流推理框架，通过结合代理式工作流推理、视觉接地接口和可复用抓取-放置原语，实现桌面操作任务。它采用掩码介导的视觉-动作接口进行语义与物理控制的桥接，并利用多时间尺度记忆闭环验证执行结果，支持在线适应、重试和重规划。在长时域逻辑复杂任务中，ACE在方程构建和约束检索任务上分别达到50%和70%的成功率，远超端到端基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式桌面操作需要智能体不仅理解自然语言，还要适应动态环境和执行失败，而现有端到端方法在逻辑复杂的长时域任务中表现不佳。

Method: ACE采用零样本工作流推理框架，将语言指令分解为子目标序列；通过视觉接地接口将子目标转化为统一掩码（指定目标物体和目的地）；掩码随时间跟踪、可供人类验证，并传递给任务无关的下游策略执行；执行后通过多时间尺度记忆闭环自动验证子目标是否成功，据此推进、重试、修复或重规划。

Result: 在零样本多步方程构建和基于约束的目标检索任务中，ACE分别达到50%和70%的成功率，而标准端到端基线难以完成这些逻辑密集型任务。

Conclusion: 显式的工作流推理和掩码介导控制为实现适应性机器人操作提供了一条稳健、实用的路径，展示了任务级别的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Open-ended tabletop manipulation requires agents to not only understand natural language but also adapt to dynamic environments and execution failures. We present ACE (Agentic Control for Embodied Manipulation), a zero-shot workflow reasoning framework for tabletop pick-and-place from natural language. Rather than relying on direct low-level action mapping, ACE combines agentic workflow reasoning with two robot-facing executable skills: a visual grounding interface and a reusable pick-and-place primitive. To bridge semantic reasoning and physical control, the active sub-goal is grounded into a mask-mediated vision-action interface. This unified mask specifies the target object and destination, is tracked over time, exposed for human verification, and ultimately passed to a task-agnostic downstream policy for execution. Crucially, ACE operates in a closed loop supported by a multi-timescale memory. After an action is executed, the system automatically verifies whether the intended sub-goal succeeded, using the outcome to advance, retry, repair, or replan. This enables online adaptation to user corrections, scene changes, and physical failures. We evaluate ACE on logically complex, long-horizon tasks, including zero-shot multi-step equation formation with number cubes and constraint-based object retrieval. ACE demonstrates task-level zero-shot generalization on novel semantic constraints and randomized tabletop scenes without task-specific retraining. Specifically, while standard end-to-end baselines struggle to complete these logically demanding tasks, ACE achieves a 50% success rate in equation formation and a 70% success rate in constraint retrieval. This contrast demonstrates that explicit workflow reasoning and mask-mediated control offer a robust, practical route toward adaptable robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [220] [Neural LiDAR Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04169)
*Chin Yung Anson Hon,Kaicheng Zhang,Sen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对LiDAR NeRF与RGB NeRF的关键设计差异进行理论分析，发现体素采样密度对LiDAR NeRF性能有重要影响，并提出了一种基于高效体积采样的神经LiDAR光束法平差（NeLD-BA）算法，用于联合优化LiDAR地图和位姿，在点云配准和3D建图任务上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然将NeRF扩展到了LiDAR模态，但鲜有工作深入探讨RGB NeRF与LiDAR NeRF之间的关键设计差异，特别是它们底层工作原理上的区别。

Method: 1. 通过理论和实验分析，发现体素采样密度对LiDAR NeRF有显著影响。2. 基于该发现，提出了神经LiDAR光束法平差（NeLD-BA）算法，采用高效的LiDAR射线体素采样策略，联合优化LiDAR地图和位姿。

Result: 在Newer College和FusionPortable数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的NeLD-BA在多视角点云配准和3D建图任务中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 体素采样密度是LiDAR NeRF的关键设计因素，基于此提出的NeLD-BA算法有效实现了高性能的LiDAR地图与位姿联合优化。

Abstract: Recent research has achieved remarkable novel view rendering and scene reconstruction results with Neural Radiance Field (NeRF), including extensions to the LiDAR modality. Few studies have, however, explored the key design differences between RGB NeRFs and LiDAR NeRFs, particularly considering their underlying working principles. In this work, we provide both theoretical and empirical evidence suggesting that the density of volume sampling plays a significant role in LiDAR NeRF. Based on this finding, we propose a novel Neural LiDAR Bundle Adjustment (NeLD-BA) algorithm, which is tailored using efficient volume sampling of LiDAR rays for joint optimization of LiDAR map and poses. Extensive experiments are performed using the Newer College and FusionPortable datasets to demonstrate the proposed NeLD-BA's state-of-the-art performance in multi-view point cloud registration and 3D mapping. We will open-source our code for the community.

</details>


### [221] [XS-VLA: Coupling Coarse-grained Spatial Distillation with Latent Flow Matching for Lightweight Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04171)
*Lei Iok Tong,Qingchen Xie,Wei Huang,Ying Jie Yap,Yujie Zhang,Qianzhi Li,Xiaolong Liu,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XS-VLA是一个两阶段框架，通过空间知识蒸馏（从Qwen3-VL-4B到SmolVLM2-0.25B）和隐式流匹配策略（CVAE+Flow Matching），在轻量级（<0.5B参数）VLA模型中实现了SOTA性能，相比基线提升7.2%成功率，加速3.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM计算成本高不适合实时机器人控制；轻量模型存在"空间盲点"（弱空间预测能力）；混合人类示范训练VLA模型因行为多样性导致策略性能下降。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 空间蒸馏：将Qwen3-VL-4B的空间语义知识蒸馏到SmolVLM2-0.25B骨干网络，通过粗粒度空间描述微调；(2) 隐式流匹配策略：以增强后的骨干网络为条件，结合CVAE与Flow Matching建模多模态动作分布。

Result: LIBERO基准上，<0.5B参数模型实现SOTA；平均成功率提升7.2%（LIBERO-Long提升23%）；超越更大规模的2.2B SmolVLA；执行速度相比之前轻量流匹配策略提升3.2倍。

Conclusion: 空间调优和生成式隐式流控制显著提升轻量VLA模型性能，实现了高效且空间感知的机器人操作。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have shown strong multimodal understanding and spatial grounding, but their computational cost limits real-time robotic control. In contrast, lightweight models are suitable for edge deployment but often suffer from "spatial blindness", namely weak native spatial prediction ability. Training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on mixed human demonstrations can also degrade policy performance due to highly diverse behaviors. To address these limitations, we propose XS-VLA, a two-stage framework for efficient and spatially grounded robotic manipulation. First, we distill spatial semantic knowledge from Qwen3-VL-4B into the SmolVLM2-0.25B backbone by fine-tuning on curated coarse-grained spatial descriptions, turning the lightweight model into a spatially grounded engine. Second, we use this enhanced backbone to condition a Latent Flow Matching policy. Unlike deterministic controllers, our policy combines a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE) with Flow Matching dynamics to model complex multimodal action distributions. On the LIBERO benchmark, XS-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance among models with fewer than 0.5B parameters. It improves average success rates by up to 7.2 percent, including a 23 percent gain on LIBERO-Long, over the SmolVLA 0.25B baseline, and outperforms the larger 2.2B vanilla SmolVLA. Ablations show that spatial tuning and generative latent flow control substantially improve lightweight VLA performance, delivering a 3.2 times speedup in mission execution over the previous lightweight flow matching policy.

</details>


### [222] [SoftVTBench: A Safety-Aware Visuo-Tactile Benchmark for Physically Constrained Robotic Manipulation of Deformable Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04234)
*Bowen Jing,Mingxin Wang,Ruiyang Hao,Chenchen Ge,Hanwen Shen,Junjie He,Yang Cui,Yiming Hou,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Dandan Zhang,Ding Zhao,Houde Liu,Xiaofan Li,Si Liu,Ping Luo,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftVTBench是一个面向可变形物体操作的安全感知视觉-触觉基准，在Isa Sim中构建，通过单独报告目标成功和安全成功（无掉落、形变低于阈值）来评估策略，实验表明仅考虑目标成功会高估性能，而触觉感知能提升安全成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作基准 predominantly 以任务完成（成功导向）为评估标准，很少评估操作过程中是否保持物理安全（无滑落、无过度形变），缺乏安全感知的评估框架。

Method: 在Isa Sim中基于有限元模拟构建可变形物体，提供多视角RGB、RGB触觉传感（含标记点运动）、本体感知和语言指令；定义4个任务套件（按物体类型和变化轴分类）；分别报告目标成功和安全成功（无掉落+峰值形变低于物体特定阈值），安全指标基于策略不可见的特权FEM状态测量；实现pi0.5基线。

Result: 仅成功导向的评估会严重高估策略性能，大量完成目标的 rollout 仍然违反物理安全约束；引入触觉感知将安全成功率从21.4%提升至35.6%（物体中心可变形任务），同时保持目标成功率相当，并降低执行过程中的物体形变。

Conclusion: SoftVTBench为在物理交互约束下研究视觉-触觉可变形物体操作提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Deformable object manipulation poses challenges beyond task completion: successful execution must also maintain safe physical interaction, holding the object stably without slip or drop while avoiding excessive deformation. However, existing manipulation benchmarks are predominantly success-oriented and rarely evaluate whether a policy remains physically safe throughout execution. We present SoftVTBench, a safety-aware visuo-tactile benchmark for physically constrained deformable object manipulation. Built in Isaac Sim with finite-element-simulated deformable objects, SoftVTBench provides multi-view RGB observations, RGB tactile sensing with marker motion, proprioception, and language instructions, and defines four matched task suites over object type (deformable vs. rigid) and variation axis (object vs. spatial). It separately reports Goal Success and Safety Success; the latter additionally requires no drop and peak deformation below a calibrated object-specific threshold, measured from policy-hidden privileged Finite Element Method (FEM) states. We implement pi0.5-based baselines under this protocol. Experiments show that success-only evaluation substantially overstates policy performance, as a large fraction of goal-completing rollouts still violate physical safety. Furthermore, incorporating tactile sensing improves Safety Success (e.g., from 21.4% to 35.6% on object-centric deformable tasks) and reduces object deformation during execution, while maintaining comparable Goal Success. SoftVTBench provides a reproducible benchmark for studying visuo-tactile deformable manipulation under physical interaction constraints.

</details>


### [223] [Integrated Graph Search and Model Predictive Control for Smooth and Efficient Path Planning in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04259)
*Duc-Tien Bui,Ngoc Thinh Nguyen,Hung Duy Nguyen,Dong Bi,Tomislav Mihalj,Arno Eichberger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种顺序路径规划框架，先通过Dijkstra图搜索生成粗糙路径，再利用该路径构建空间变化的凸侧向安全走廊，将离散避障转化为连续约束，最后通过模型预测控制（MPC）优化路径平滑性。在直道和弯道多场景超车测试中，该方法相比多项式拟合+二次规划方法，横向加速度、曲率和加加速度更低，计算成本降低约28-30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶路径规划中，同时保证安全性、舒适性、动态可行性和计算效率是一个重大挑战。现有方法难以很好地平衡路径平滑性与计算效率。

Method: 1. 使用Dijkstra算法在离散网格上搜索粗糙路径；2. 基于粗糙路径构建空间变化的凸侧向安全走廊，将避障约束转化为连续可行性约束；3. 在走廊内构建MPC问题，通过惩罚横向偏移的三阶空间导数来优化路径平滑性。

Result: 在直道和弯道的多目标车辆超车场景中，相比多项式拟合+二次规划方法，该方法横向加速度、曲率和加加速度更低，直道计算成本降低28.08%，弯道降低29.52%。

Conclusion: 将图搜索结构融入MPC公式中，能够在结构化驾驶环境下有效平衡路径平滑性与计算效率。

Abstract: Path planning is a fundamental component of autonomous vehicles, where achieving safe, comfortable, and dynamically feasible paths while ensuring computational efficiency remains a significant challenge. This paper presents a sequential path planning framework in which a rough path obtained from graph search is explicitly exploited to guide a Model Predictive Control (MPC)-based path refinement. A rough path is first obtained via Dijkstra search on a discretized grid and is then used to construct a spatially varying convex lateral safety corridor that explicitly captures obstacle avoidance constraints, transforming discrete obstacle avoidance decisions into continuous feasibility constraints for optimization. Within this corridor, an MPC problem is formulated to refine the path, enabling efficient optimization while maintaining path smoothness by penalizing the third-order spatial derivative of the lateral offset over a prediction horizon. The proposed algorithm is evaluated in multiple overtaking scenarios on both straight and curved roads, including cases with single and multiple target vehicles, using high-fidelity environment simulations (i.e., CarMaker). Compared with the previous study, which used polynomial fitting and a quadratic programming method, the proposed approach consistently achieves lower lateral acceleration, curvature, and jerk while reducing computational cost by 28.08% on straight roads and 29.52% on curved roads. These results demonstrate that exploiting graph-search structure within an MPC formulation provides an effective balance between path smoothness and computational efficiency for autonomous vehicles in structured driving environments.

</details>


### [224] [FLOAT Drone for Physical Interaction: Lateral Airflow Reduction, Wrench Modeling, and Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04260)
*Junxiao Lin,Kehan Zhou,Shuhang Ji,Yimin Peng,Shen Wang,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FLOAT Drone，一种带有伺服驱动控制面的共轴双旋翼全驱动无人机，用于近距离物理交互。通过控制面在旋翼下洗流中产生侧向力实现六自由度 wrench 生成，并采用非线性多项式气动模型实现高精度实时分配。实验验证了其在控制精度、抗干扰能力及狭窄空间操作（如2cm间隙抽屉推拉）中的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中物理交互需要无人机在施加接触力的同时保持稳定飞行，这要求设计能够满足多维 wrench 生成、紧凑性（用于狭小空间机动性）以及减少对目标的横向气流这三个耦合需求。现有设计在平衡这些需求方面存在不足。

Method: 采用共轴双旋翼布局（紧凑推进）+ 伺服驱动控制面（位于旋翼下洗流中，产生侧向力和力矩，实现6自由度 wrench 生成）。通过力匹配 CFD 与倾斜旋翼方案对比验证气流减少效果；从精密力测量中辨识非线性多项式气动 wrench 模型，并嵌入约束非线性分配器实现实时 wrench 跟踪。

Result: 与线性分配基线相比，所提框架提高了控制精度；能有效抑制地面效应和负载扰动；实现了通过2cm间隙的近距离抽屉推拉操作。

Conclusion: FLOAT Drone 通过共轴双旋翼加控制面的设计，在紧凑性、多维力生成和减少横向气流方面取得了有效平衡，结合非线性气动模型分配器，在近距离物理交互任务中表现出优异的控制性能和实用性。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction represents a promising direction for next-generation unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), but it requires an aerial platform that can exert contact forces while maintaining stable flight. For close-proximity tasks, this translates into three coupled design requirements: multidimensional wrench generation for stable contact, compactness for maneuverability and safety in confined spaces, and reduced lateral airflow toward the target when generating horizontal force. This article presents FLOAT Drone, a fully actuated coaxial UAV with servo-driven control surfaces for close-proximity physical interaction. The coaxial dual-rotor layout provides a compact propulsion layout, while the control surfaces, immersed in the rotor downwash, generate lateral forces and moments for 6-DoF wrench generation. A force-matched computational fluid dynamics (CFD) comparison with a tilted-rotor alternative quantifies the reduction in target-facing lateral airflow. To account for nonlinear rotor--control-surface coupling in the rotor wake, a high-fidelity polynomial aerodynamic wrench model is identified from precision force measurements and embedded in a constrained nonlinear allocator for real-time wrench tracking. Comparative flight and interaction experiments show that the proposed framework improves control accuracy over linear allocation baselines, rejects ground-effect and payload disturbances, and enables close-proximity drawer push--pull manipulation through a $2~\mathrm{cm}$ handle clearance.

</details>


### [225] [HALO-WA: Hybrid-Attention Latent-Guided Online Reinforcement Learning for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04265)
*Angen Ye,Weijie Ke,Xiaofeng Wang,Xinze Chen,Chaojun Ni,Guosheng Zhao,Boyuan Wang,Zheng Zhu,Junjie Xie,Dapeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO-WA提出了一种混合注意力潜在引导的在线强化学习框架，通过轻量级actor-critic适配器结合WA模型的潜在特征和动作先验，实现快速在线适应，在4个真实世界精密操作任务中将平均成功率从26.4%提升至87.1%，仅需45-75分钟在线训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WA模型在生成通用机器人操作的长期动作序列方面表现良好，但在现实世界精密任务中因标定、感知和接触动力学误差，常在最后几毫米的对齐或插入阶段失败，亟需一种能够快速在线适应这些部署误差的方法。

Method: 提出HALO-WA框架，核心包括：(1) 混合注意力结构，在保持动作块时间一致性的同时，从WA潜在特征中读取任务相关信息；(2) 轻量级actor-critic适配器，利用WA生成过程的潜在特征和动作先验进行在线强化学习；(3) 基于视觉上下文和末端阶段修正需求的潜在特征条件调节。

Result: 在4个真实世界精密操作任务上，HALO-WA将平均成功率从WA-base的26.4%提升至87.1%，超出最强基线19.2个百分点，每个任务仅需45-75分钟在线训练。在RoboTwin仿真实验中也有补充验证。

Conclusion: HALO-WA有效解决了WA模型在精密操作任务中最后阶段失败的问题，通过在线RL快速适应真实部署误差，显著提升成功率，且训练效率高。代码已开源。

Abstract: World-action (WA) models can generate long-horizon action chunks for general-purpose robotic manipulation, but they remain vulnerable to calibration, perception, and contact-dynamics errors in real-world precision tasks, often failing in the final few millimeters of alignment or insertion. We propose HALO-WA, a hybrid-attention latent-guided online reinforcement learning (RL) framework for WA models, which leverages latent features and action priors from the WA generation process through a lightweight actor-critic adapter to enable fast online adaptation to real deployment errors. HALO-WA introduces a hybrid-attention structure that preserves the temporal consistency of action chunks while reading task-relevant information from WA latents conditioned on visual context and end-stage correction requirements, thereby producing refined action chunks. We validate HALO-WA on four real-world precision manipulation tasks, where it improves the average success rate from 26.4\% for WA-base to 87.1\%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 19.2 percentage points while requiring only 45--75 minutes of online training per task. To facilitate reproducibility, we further conduct supplementary simulation experiments in RoboTwin and release the code at https://github.com/YeanRoot/HALO-WA.

</details>


### [226] [GPU-Accelerated Polygonal Signed Distance Functions for Real-Time Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04310)
*Taekwon Ga,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多边形符号距离函数(PSDF)，一种几何精确的凸多边形机器人足迹与障碍物之间的符号距离函数，通过无权重、无分支的张量化几何流水线实现批量化GPU执行和自动微分，并将其嵌入基于序列二次规划实时迭代的模型预测控制(PSDF-MPC)，在密集多边形环境中实现高效的碰撞避免约束评估与实时控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于优化的局部规划与控制需要高频的碰撞避免约束评估，而在障碍物密集环境中，可行空间有限且约束复杂，计算负载往往主导控制周期运行时，形成性能瓶颈。因此需要兼具计算效率与几何保真度的碰撞避免约束。

Method: 提出多边形符号距离函数(PSDF)，用边界边表示障碍物，实现凸多边形机器人足迹与障碍物之间的几何精确符号距离函数。PSDF采用无权重、无分支的张量化几何流水线，支持批量化GPU执行和自动微分。将其嵌入MPC，在每个阶段对安全性约束进行局部线性化，结合基于序列二次规划(SQP)的实时迭代方案，形成PSDF-MPC。设计分离CPU/GPU计算：GPU评估批量化PSDF值及梯度，CPU求解稀疏二次规划，其维度取决于系统维度和预测时域长度，而非障碍物特征。

Result: 微基准测试表明PSDF在符号距离查询方面优于基线方法。闭环仿真和真实世界导航实验（包括与基于优化的基线方法对比）证明，PSDF-MPC在密集多边形环境中能保持实时可行性和鲁棒的碰撞避免能力。

Conclusion: PSDF-MPC通过GPU加速的几何精确符号距离函数与分离式CPU/GPU计算架构，有效解决了密集障碍物环境下碰撞避免约束的计算瓶颈，实现了实时可行的模型预测控制。

Abstract: Optimization-based local planning and control require high-rate collision-avoidance constraint evaluation over a prediction horizon. In obstacle-dense environments, where feasible space is limited and the constraints become increasingly complex, the computational workload often dominates the control-cycle runtime. The resulting bottleneck motivates collision-avoidance constraints that combine computational efficiency with geometric fidelity. The proposed Polygonal Signed Distance Function (PSDF) is a geometry-exact signed distance function between a convex polygonal robot footprint and obstacles represented by their boundary edges. It is implemented as a weight-free, branch-free tensorized geometric pipeline enabling batched GPU execution and automatic differentiation. The PSDF is embedded into model predictive control by locally linearizing the stage-wise safety constraints within a sequential quadratic programming-based real-time iteration scheme, yielding the PSDF-embedded model predictive controller (PSDF-MPC). The design separates CPU/GPU computation so that the GPU evaluates batched PSDF values and gradients while the CPU solves a sparse quadratic program whose dimension is determined by system dimensions and horizon length, not by obstacle features. Microbenchmarks show that PSDF scales favorably against signed-distance query baselines. Closed-loop simulated and real-world navigation experiments, including comparisons with optimization-based baselines, demonstrate that PSDF-MPC maintains real-time feasibility and robust collision avoidance in dense polygonal environments.

</details>


### [227] [Agent-driven Long-tail Simulation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04331)
*Junru Gu,Lijin Yang,Jianing Huang,Shu Liu,Zhongzhan Huang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型驱动的智能体仿真框架，通过指令跟随的LLM控制周围交通参与者，并构建了包含长尾场景的SemanticPlan闭环规划基准，实验表明现有规划器在这些场景中仍然表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶闭环仿真器主要依赖日志回放或基于规则的智能体，导致行为多样性不足和长尾场景覆盖有限，亟需一种能生成更具交互性和真实行为的仿真方法。

Method: 提出智能体驱动仿真框架，使用指令跟随的大语言模型通过结构化动作接口控制周围道路参与者；并构建SemanticPlan基准，在真实nuPlan场景中增强多个交互智能体和多样化语言指令。

Result: 最先进的规划器在SemanticPlan基准的长尾场景中难以持续实现安全有效的任务完成，表明这些场景仍然具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的智能体仿真能产生更具意图性和反应性的行为，但现有自动驾驶规划器在长尾语义场景下仍然表现不足，需要进一步研究以提升闭环规划能力。

Abstract: Evaluating autonomous driving systems in closed-loop settings requires realistic and interactive simulation, yet existing simulators largely rely on log replay or rule-based agents, limiting behavioral diversity and long-tail coverage. We propose an agent-driven simulation framework in which surrounding road participants are controlled by instruction-following large language models through a structured action interface, enabling intentional and reactive behaviors while preserving physical plausibility. Furthermore, we introduce SemanticPlan, a benchmark of closed-loop planning in long-tail and semantically rich scenarios that augment real nuPlan scenes with multiple interactive agents following diverse language instructions. Evaluation results show that state-of-the-art planners still struggle to consistently achieve safe and effective task completion, suggesting that these long-tail scenarios remain challenging.

</details>


### [228] [A Perception-Manipulation Robotics System for Food Cutting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04367)
*Xinyuan Luo,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向烹饪机器人的食物切割感知-操作框架，包含基于力数据的刀具选择模块和基于强化学习的自适应切割策略，在未知食物上刀具选择成功率达100%，性能与人类相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烹饪机器人中切割任务至关重要，但不同食物属性差异大，需要不同的切割策略和刀具，现有方法缺乏对刀具和切割策略的自适应选择能力。

Method: 系统包含两个核心模块：1）刀具选择模块，通过预切割试验获取力数据来选择合适的刀具；2）自适应切割阶段，使用强化学习（RL）平衡切割速度和能量效率。

Result: 刀具选择模块在未知食物上达到100%成功率；RL策略在性能上优于固定策略，且与人类操作员表现相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的感知-操作框架能有效实现自适应食物切割，在性能和效率上达到与人类相当的水平，为烹饪机器人切割任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In the development of cooking robots, mastering the task of cutting is crucial. A significant challenge lies in the diverse properties of food, which necessitate distinct cutting policies and even different knives for optimal processing. This paper presents a perception-manipulation framework for food-cutting tasks. Our system features a knife selection module that utilizes force data from a preliminary fixed trial cut to select the appropriate knife for the given food. This is followed by an adaptive cutting phase using reinforcement learning (RL) to balance cutting speed and energy efficiency. In our experiments, the knife selection module achieved 100% successful rate on unseen food, and we compared the performances of fixed policy, RL policy, with human operators. Our method not only achieves high performance but also demonstrates comparable results to those of human participants.

</details>


### [229] [SurgAM: Surgical Affordance Map Prediction with Multimodal Feature Fusion for Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04378)
*Lei Song,Yonghao Long,Mengya Xu,Jiayi Geng,Xiuyuan Chen,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出手术可操作区域预测（surgical affordance prediction）框架，通过自适应特征融合、层次化提示学习和场景引导注意力解码器，从视觉数据中识别抽吸、夹闭、牵拉三种基本手术动作的可操作区域，在公开数据集上达到SOTA性能，并在肺部和前列腺体模上验证了自主手术执行的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究集中于手术场景感知（如工具识别、场景分割），但对理解并预测可操作区域（affordance）以实现自主手术规划的研究尚不充分，视觉场景理解与自主动作规划之间存在根本性鸿沟。

Method: 提出自适应特征融合框架，结合自监督ViT编码器（语义理解）和大规模生成模型编码器（空间感知）；引入层次化提示学习以适应不同手术流程上下文；设计场景引导注意力解码器聚焦关键手术区域并抑制背景干扰。

Result: 在包含抽吸、夹闭、牵拉三种手术动作可操作区域标注的新数据集上达到SOTA性能，并在肺部与前列腺体模上验证了预测的可操作地图可成功引导自主手术执行。

Conclusion: 手术可操作区域预测是实现手术自动化的关键中间表征，所提框架在语义理解与空间感知的融合、上下文自适应以及背景抑制方面均有效，有望推动自主手术机器人的发展。

Abstract: Surgical automation is being increasingly studied, yet bridging visual scene understanding with autonomous action planning remains a fundamental challenge. While much research effort has been made on scene perception (e.g., tool recognition and scene segmentation), understanding and predicting actionable possibilities for surgical automation is still underexplored. In this paper, we introduce surgical affordance prediction, which identifies actionable regions for fundamental surgical actions from visual data. Specifically, a novel adaptive feature fusion framework is proposed that leverages the complementary strengths of a self-supervised vision transformer encoder for its superior semantic understanding and a large-scale generative model encoder for its spatially-aware capability. Furthermore, we introduce a hierarchical prompt learning mechanism to adapt to varying procedural contexts. Finally, a scene-guided attention decoder is proposed to focus on critical surgical areas while suppressing background distractions. To validate the effectiveness, we established a new dataset, derived from publicly available surgical datasets with affordance annotations for three basic surgical actions: aspiration, clipping, and retraction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance. Moreover, we validate our framework's applicability for downstream automation on a realistic lung and prostate phantom, and results show that the predicted affordance maps successfully enable autonomous surgical actions.

</details>


### [230] [ACE-Brain-0.5: A Unified Embodied Foundational Model for Physical Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04426)
*ACE-Brain Team,:,Ziyang Gong,Haoming Gu,Zehang Luo,Tianyi Zhang,Tao Tao,Yixiao Chi,Zhe Liu,Lingsi Zhu,Jingyuan Liu,Anke Tang,Songze Li,Yilun Kong,Ningjing Liu,Tianyu Zhu,Yunpeng Qing,Shuang Luo,Xiang Liu,Shi Fu,Dawei Nie,Sixiang Liu,Zhexi Wen,Feng Pan,Xiaofeng Wang,Zhi Hou,Chunxiao Liu,Xue Yang,Junchi Yan,Hengshuang Zhao,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACE-Brain-0.5是一个统一的具身基础模型，将机器人智能组织为五个耦合功能（空间感知、决策、交互、自监控、自我改进），基于8B骨干网络和SSR+训练策略，在15个基准测试中显著优于前代版本，向通用物理智能体AI迈出了早期一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI系统碎片化严重：端到端策略缺乏空间推理和执行评估，机器人智能体系统虽能编排工具但无法学习共享表征，这种碎片化限制了通用物理智能体AI的发展。

Method: 在ACE-Brain-0的空间智能基础上，使用单一8B骨干网络实现前四个功能（空间感知、决策、交互、自监控），并提出SSR+训练策略——在Scaffold-Specialize-Reconcile基础上增加Reactivate阶段以消除跨任务干扰；第五功能（自改进）通过配套框架从轨迹回放中更新外部执行状态。

Result: 在15个基准测试中，ACE-Brain-0.5在18个空间感知与接地基准中的14个上优于ACE-Brain-0，在导航和操作任务上表现具竞争力，在分布内和分布外场景下均提供强大的进度估计能力。

Conclusion: ACE-Brain-0.5通过将五类机器人智能功能统一到单一基础模型中，有效克服了现有系统的碎片化问题，是迈向通用物理智能体AI的早期但重要的一步。

Abstract: Embodied AI is moving from isolated perception or action modules toward physical agents that understand, plan under goals, act through robot bodies, monitor progress, and improve from experience. Existing systems address this loop only in parts: end-to-end policies generate actions but often lack spatial reasoning, planning, and execution assessment, while robot-agent systems orchestrate tools or specialists but do not learn a shared representation. This fragmentation limits general Physical Agentic AI. We present ACE-Brain-0.5, a unified embodied foundation model that organizes robot intelligence into five coupled functions: spatial perception, decision making, embodied interaction, self-monitoring, and self-improvement. Built on ACE-Brain-0, which established spatial intelligence as a shared scaffold across robot platforms, ACE-Brain-0.5 extends an understanding-centric model into a closed-loop foundation model. A single 8B backbone instantiates the first four functions: grounding objects and affordances, reasoning over 3D and egocentric spatial relations, decomposing instructions into subgoals, generating navigation and manipulation actions, and estimating progress for verification and recovery. To unify these capabilities without cross-task interference, we introduce SSR+, which extends Scaffold-Specialize-Reconcile with a Reactivate stage after task-vector merging.
