The fifth function, self-improvement, is realized by a companion framework that updates external execution state, including task schemas, spatial memory, and failure-recovery cases, from rollouts. Across fifteen benchmarks, ACE-Brain-0.5 improves over ACE-Brain-0 on 14 of 18 spatial perception and grounding benchmarks, achieves competitive navigation and manipulation performance, and provides strong progress estimation in ID and OOD settings. Together, these results mark an early step toward general Physical Agentic AI.

</details>


### [231] [RoboDojo: A Unified Sim-and-Real Benchmark for Comprehensive Evaluation of Generalist Robot Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04434)
*Tianxing Chen,Yue Chen,Zixuan Li,Junyuan Tang,Kailun Su,Weijie Wan,Baijun Chen,Haoran Lu,Haowen Yan,Honghao Su,Zhiyang Dou,Kaixuan Wang,Dandan Zhang,Yunze Liu,Yan Qin,Qiwei Liang,Qiwei Wu,Zijian Lin,Wenwei Lin,Yuran Wang,Minghua He,Tianshu Wu,Ruihai Wu,Jingquan Zhou,Kai-Chong Lei,Haibao Yu,Yuanfeng Ji,Weiyang Jin,Guanyu Lin,Xiaofan Li,Qi Xiong,Renjing Xu,Zhongyu Li,Wenhao Chai,Enze Xie,Ziwei Wang,Yao Mu,Hao Dong,Wojciech Matusik,Mingyu Ding,Wenbo Ding,Ping Luo,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboDojo是一个统一的仿真与真实世界基准测试平台，包含42个仿真任务和18个真实世界任务，用于全面评估通用机器人操控策略，覆盖泛化、记忆、精度、长程执行和开放词汇指令跟随五个维度，并支持可扩展评估和可复现的真实世界评估系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操控策略基准存在局限：任务简单、技能范围窄、能力覆盖有限，且通常仅在仿真或仅在真实世界中进行评估。仿真可扩展但缺少物理部署挑战，真实世界评估成本高、耗时长且难以复现。因此需要一个统一的、全面的仿真与真实世界基准来系统评估通用机器人操控策略。

Method: RoboDojo包含42个仿真任务和18个真实世界任务，仿真基准评估五个维度（泛化、记忆、精度、长程执行、开放词汇指令跟随），真实世界基准暴露策略于挑战性物理部署条件。通过Isaac Sim的异构并行仿真支持可扩展评估，并提供RoboDojo-RealEval可复现真实世界评估系统（含远程云访问、标准化硬件、场景重置、评估协议和部署接口）。结合XPolicyLab实现策略一次集成即可在仿真和真实世界环境中评估，目前集成了30种策略。

Result: 集成了30种策略到XPolicyLab中，并在RoboDojo上进行了评估，建立了公开排行榜，并对当前策略性能进行了系统分析。

Conclusion: RoboDojo通过统一的仿真与真实世界基准测试，为通用机器人操控策略提供了更全面、可扩展且可复现的评估框架，填补了现有基准在多维能力评估和跨环境评估方面的空白。

Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation policies have advanced rapidly, yet existing benchmarks remain limited in systematically evaluating their capabilities. Many rely on simple, short-horizon, or skill-narrow tasks with limited capability coverage, and are often conducted only in simulation or only in the real world. Simulation enables scalable feedback but misses physical deployment challenges, while real-world evaluation is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to reproduce. We introduce RoboDojo, a unified sim-and-real benchmark for comprehensive evaluation of generalist robot manipulation policies. RoboDojo includes 42 simulation tasks and 18 real-world tasks covering diverse and complementary manipulation capabilities. The simulation benchmark evaluates five dimensions: generalization, memory, precision, long-horizon execution, and open-vocabulary instruction following, while the real-world benchmark exposes policies to challenging physical-world deployment conditions. RoboDojo supports scalable evaluation through heterogeneous parallel simulation in Isaac Sim and provides RoboDojo-RealEval, a reproducible real-world evaluation system with remote cloud access, standardized hardware, scene reset, evaluation protocol, and deployment interface. Together with XPolicyLab, policies can be integrated once and evaluated across simulation and real-world settings with minimal adaptation. We integrate 30 policies into XPolicyLab and evaluate them on RoboDojo, establishing a public leaderboard and systematic analysis of current policy performance. The website is available at http://robodojo-benchmark.com/.

</details>


### [232] [Robustness Verification of an Autonomous Underwater Vehicle-based Plankton Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04453)
*Abdelrahman Sayed Sayed,Pierre-Jean Meyer,Asgeir J. Sørensen,Mohamed Ghazel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于可达性分析的鲁棒性验证框架，结合神经ODE分类模型，用于自动验证浮游生物成像分类器对环境扰动的稳定性，减少误分类和人工验证负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前搭载AI的AUV浮游生物成像分类器在动态非结构化环境中易受噪声和非生物伪影干扰，导致频繁误分类，需要海洋生物学家耗时手动验证，缺乏鲁棒性保障。

Method: 提出基于可达性分析的鲁棒性验证框架，并引入连续时间神经ODE分类模型，利用SilCam高分辨率成像能力，对模型进行形式化鲁棒性验证。

Result: 该验证框架可作为自动化滤波器，提供对模型在环境扰动下稳定性的形式化保证，有效降低误分类，减少后处理工作量。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够提升自主采样的可靠性，通过形式化验证为模型在模糊数据上的稳定性提供保障，从而减轻海洋生物学家的手动验证负担。

Abstract: The assessment of planktonic standing stocks and microorganism structures is critical for understanding upper ocean biological processes. Currently, autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) equipped with in-situ optical imaging and artificial intelligence (AI) methods offer a promising solution for persistent surveillance, mapping and monitoring of planktonic life. However, current AI methods often lack robustness in dynamic, unstructured environments, where environmental noise and non-biological artifacts lead to frequent misclassifications. Standard convolutional neural network (CNN) classifiers often struggle with such conditions, leading to misclassifications that require time-consuming manual validation by marine biologists.
  To address this issue, we propose a novel robustness verification framework for in-situ plankton classifiers based on reachability analysis. We also introduce a continuous-time neural ordinary differential equation (neural ODE) classification model leveraging the high-resolution imaging capabilities of the SilCam particle imager. In this paper, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework by formally verifying the robustness of the neural ODE model against environmental perturbations. We demonstrate that our verification framework acts as an automated filter providing formal guarantees of model stability against ambiguous data, thereby improving the reliability of autonomous sampling and reducing the post-processing workload.

</details>


### [233] [Mask2Real-WM: Segmentation Masks as a Sim-to-Real Bridge for Controllable Dexterous World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04546)
*Riccardo O. Feingold,Davide Liconti,Chenyu Yang,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mask2Real-WM是一个两阶段动作条件世界模型，通过将像素预测解耦为动力学模型（预测未来分割掩码）和渲染模型（将掩码转换为逼真RGB图像），实现灵巧操作的未来预测。动力学模型在分割空间（sim-to-real差距更小）上进行大规模合成数据预训练（>50小时），再通过少量真实数据（<2.5小时）微调。实验表明，掩码条件和仿真预训练对于23个自由度的逐关节动作可控性至关重要，而单体基线只能捕捉粗略轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在灵巧操作任务中难以精确预测高自由度（23-DoF）动作的细粒度未来效果，单体基线无法可靠反映逐关节的动作影响，需要一种能解耦预测过程、降低sim-to-real差距的方法。

Method: 将动作条件世界模型解耦为两个独立模块：1) 动力学模型：基于过去分割掩码和23-DoF动作序列预测未来分割掩码；2) 渲染模型：使用ControlNet增强的Stable Video Diffusion骨干将预测掩码转换为逼真RGB图像。动力学模型先在>50小时合成数据上预训练，再用<2.5小时真实演示数据微调。

Result: 在灵巧抓取放置基准上，Mask2Real-WM实现了对所有23个自由度的逐关节动作可控性，而单体基线只能捕捉粗略的手部和末端执行器轨迹，无法反映细粒度的逐关节动作效果。

Conclusion: 掩码条件（解耦预测）和仿真预训练（缩小sim-to-real差距）是实现高自由度灵巧操作精确动作条件预测的关键要素。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models allow robots to predict the future consequences of candidate actions without additional physical interaction, supporting policy evaluation, planning, and data augmentation. We present Mask2Real-WM, a two-stage action-conditioned world model for dexterous manipulation that decouples pixel prediction into a dynamics model and a rendering model. The dynamics model predicts future segmentation masks from past masks and 23-DoF action sequences. The rendering model maps the predicted masks to photorealistic RGB using a ControlNet-augmented Stable Video Diffusion backbone. The smaller sim-to-real gap in segmentation space enables the dynamics model to benefit from large-scale pretraining on over 50 h of synthetic simulation data, followed by fine-tuning on fewer than 2.5 h of real demonstrations. Experiments on a dexterous pick-and-place benchmark show that mask conditioning and simulation pretraining are both required for per-DoF action controllability across all 23 degrees of freedom. In contrast, monolithic baselines capture broad hand and end-effector trajectories but do not reliably reflect fine-grained, per-joint action effects.

</details>


### [234] [HUGS: Guiding Unified Dexterous Grasp Synthesis Across Modes and Scales via Learned Human Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04554)
*Mingrui Yu,Yongpeng Jiang,Yongyi Jia,Kangchen Lv,Li Huang,Yi Ren,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HUGS提出了一种基于人类先验知识的统一灵巧抓取合成框架，通过学习人类抓取偏好来引导力闭合优化，能够合成从两指捏取到双手抓取、覆盖2cm到30cm物体尺度的多样化抓取姿态，并在真实世界中实现了从螺丝到大箱子的自主抓取模式选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取合成方法依赖手工设计的预期接触点和初始化启发式策略来降低高维优化空间，难以平衡合成成功率和多样性。

Method: HUGS学习一个以物体为条件的人类先验模型，该模型在自收集的1.8K个抓取（304个物体）数据集上训练，捕捉人类抓取偏好；在合成阶段自适应地提出接触模式和腕部初始化，用于指导力闭合感知优化。

Result: 合成了157K场景下的3.2M机器人抓取，覆盖物体半对角线长度2cm-30cm、两指到双手抓取模式；训练的模型在真实世界中能够自主选择合适接触模式，实现从螺丝到大箱子的抓取。

Conclusion: HUGS通过人类先验引导，有效平衡了接触模式覆盖率和合成成功率，相比启发式方法在跨尺度、跨模式的灵巧抓取合成上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping across diverse object scales requires contact modes ranging from two-finger pinches to bimanual grasps. Existing dexterous grasp synthesis methods reduce the high-dimensional optimization space with manually designed expected contacts and initialization heuristics, which struggle to balance synthesis success rate and diversity. We present HUGS (Human-prior-guided Unified Dexterous Grasp Synthesis), a human-prior-guided framework for unified dexterous grasp synthesis across modes and scales. Instead of directly retargeting human demonstrations, HUGS learns an object-conditioned human prior that captures human grasp preferences and guides downstream force-closure-aware optimization. The prior is trained on a compact self-collected human grasp dataset with 1.8K grasps over 304 objects, providing broad coverage of object scales and contact modes. During synthesis, HUGS adaptively proposes contact modes and wrist initializations, substantially improving the balance between contact-mode coverage and synthesis success rate over heuristic-based methods. With HUGS, we synthesize 3.2M robotic grasps over 157K scenes, spanning object half-diagonal lengths from 2 cm to 30 cm and modes from two-finger to bimanual grasps. Models trained on the synthesized dataset autonomously select appropriate contact modes in the real world, enabling grasping from screws to large boxes.

</details>


### [235] [Simple-to-Complex Structured Demonstrations for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04591)
*Xinchuan Qiu,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于由简到繁原则的结构化演示收集策略，用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的机器人操作学习。该方法通过分解复杂任务、标准化环境和渐进式增加难度三个原则组织数据，使模型先掌握基础技能再学习复杂组合任务。在积木抓取排序和毛巾折叠任务上，相比直接收集完整任务轨迹的基线方法，所提方法在任务成功率和训练稳定性上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA研究主要关注模型架构、训练策略和数据集规模，而忽视了演示数据的收集与组织方式。然而，演示组织直接影响策略学习效率、训练稳定性和泛化能力，是模仿学习中一个根本性但被忽视的方面。

Method: 提出一种由简到繁的结构化演示收集策略，包含三个通用原则：(i) 将复杂操作任务分解为渐进可学习的子技能；(ii) 标准化交互环境以减少不必要的变化；(iii) 按照逐渐增加的任务复杂度组织演示。使用双臂机器人平台进行数据收集。

Result: 在积木抓取排序和毛巾折叠两个典型机器人操作任务上，相比直接收集端到端完整任务轨迹的基线方法，所提策略在任务成功率和训练稳定性上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 演示组织是VLA学习中一个此前未被充分探索但重要的因素，该策略为高效技能获取、可扩展数据集构建和长时域机器人操作提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation by integrating visual perception, language understanding, and robot action generation. Existing research has primarily focused on improving model architectures, training strategies, and dataset scale, while little attention has been paid to how demonstrations are collected and organized. We identify demonstration organization as a fundamental yet overlooked aspect of imitation learning, as it directly affects policy learning efficiency, training stability, and policy generalization. To address this gap, we propose a simple-to-complex structured demonstration collection strategy for VLA learning using a dual-arm robotic platform. Our approach systematically organizes data through three general principles: (i) decomposing complex manipulation tasks into progressively learnable sub-skills, (ii) standardizing the interaction environment to reduce unnecessary variability, and (iii) organizing demonstrations according to progressively increasing task complexity. This structured design enables VLA models to first acquire fundamental manipulation skills before learning increasingly complex task compositions, facilitating more effective learning of long-horizon manipulation tasks. We evaluate the proposed strategy on two representative robotic manipulation tasks: block grasping and sorting, and towel folding. Experimental results show consistent improvements in task success rate and training stability compared with the baseline method of directly collecting end-to-end complete task trajectories. These findings highlight demonstration organization as a previously underexplored but important factor in VLA learning and provide practical insights into efficient skill acquisition, scalable dataset construction, and long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [236] [SEAM: Smooth Execution of Action-Chunked Motion for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04609)
*Dijia Zhan,Xuemiao Xu,Jinyi Li,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEAM是一种无需训练、推理时的方法，通过速度引导损失导向（VLS）机制解决VLA策略中固定长度动作块的多模态分叉问题，在LIBERO-10上降低边界加加速度28%、块过渡不连续性27%，同时保持任务成功率和去噪成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在执行固定长度动作块时存在多模态分叉问题：相邻块从独立高斯潜变量生成，可能收敛到不相容的轨迹模式，导致块边界处出现突然的不连续性。现有方法要么需要反向传播、拒绝采样或重新训练，计算成本高或影响任务可靠性。

Method: SEAM利用同步执行洞察：机器人消费已执行前缀后，前一块未执行尾部可作为一致性参考。核心机制Velocity-guided Loss Steering (VLS)从尾部推导时变目标，在每个欧拉步骤后应用闭式修正，无需通过策略网络反向传播。

Result: 在LIBERO-10上使用pi_0.5，边界加加速度降低28%，块过渡不连续性降低27%，保持基线级任务成功率，去噪循环成本接近未引导基线。

Conclusion: SEAM是一种轻量级、无训练的方法，有效缓解了VLA动作块间的多模态分叉问题，实现了更平滑的轨迹过渡。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies that execute fixed-length action chunks can exhibit multimodal bifurcation: a cross-chunk inconsistency in which adjacent chunks generated from independent Gaussian latents can converge to incompatible trajectory modes, producing abrupt discontinuities at chunk boundaries. Existing remedies either require backpropagation through the policy at each denoising step, rely on rejection sampling, or require retraining, each trading computational cost or task reliability for smoother transitions. We propose SEAM (Smooth Execution of Action-Chunked Motion), a training-free inference-time method for flow matching VLAs. SEAM exploits a simple synchronous-execution insight: after the robot consumes the executed prefix, the previous chunk's unexecuted tail is already available as an analytic consistency reference. Its core mechanism, Velocity-guided Loss Steering (VLS), derives a time-dependent target from this tail and applies a closed-form correction after each Euler step without backpropagating through the policy network. On LIBERO-10 with pi_0.5, SEAM reduces boundary jerk by 28%, reduces chunk transition discontinuity by 27%, preserves baseline-level task success, and keeps denoising-loop cost near the unguided baseline.

</details>


### [237] [RoboVista: Evaluating Vision Language Models for Diverse Robot Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04610)
*Shuangyu Xie,Kaiyuan Chen,Ziyang Chen,Simeon Adebola,Yixuan Huang,Zehan Ma,Tianshuang Qiu,Wentao Yuan,Dhruv Shah,Pannag R. Sanketi,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RQA模块化评估框架和RoboVista基准，包含474个机器人VQA实例和39种任务类型，实验表明现有VLM存在显著差距，且RoboVista性能与真实机器人任务执行强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准主要基于遥操作端到端数据集，难以捕捉机器人行为中模块化的决策组件结构，无法有效评估VLM在多形态、多变视觉条件和复杂规划下的通用机器人推理能力。

Method: 提出RQA（Robot Question Answering）模块化评估框架，并构建RoboVista基准，该基准从真实机器人系统、研究论文和专家标注中收集474个VQA实例，覆盖农业、工业、家用、手术机器人、自动驾驶等39种任务类型，包含人工标注的推理过程。

Result: 最先进的VLM在RoboVista上表现存在显著差距；物理机器人实验表明RoboVista性能与真实世界任务执行呈强相关性。

Conclusion: RQA和RoboVista为评估VLM在机器人领域的模块化推理能力提供了有效框架，且该基准性能可预测真实机器人任务执行效果。

Abstract: Diverse applications for robotics, such as industry and agriculture, require robots to operate across various embodiments, changing visual conditions, and complex planning. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer a promising foundation for general-purpose and interpretable robotic reasoning. Aligning VLMs with diverse robot applications requires a modular understanding of the individual decision components that underlie robotic behavior. Capturing such structure is challenging for conventional robot benchmarks that are primarily based on teleoperated, end-to-end datasets. We propose Robot Question Answering (RQA), a modular evaluation framework and RoboVista, a benchmark curated from real robotic systems, research papers, and expert annotations. RoboVista contains 474 Visual Question Answering (VQA) instances with human annotated reasoning and covers 39 unique task types in agricultural, industrial, domestic, surgical robotics, autonomous driving, and open robot datasets. Experiments on RoboVista show that state-of-the-art VLMs exhibit substantial gaps. Physical robot experiments suggest strong correlation between RoboVista performance and real-world task execution.

</details>


### [238] [DIVO: Continuous-time DVL-Inertial-Visual Odometry for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04615)
*Kyungmin Jung,Angad Bajwa,Junha Yoo,Arturo Del Castillo Bernal,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于连续时间轨迹估计框架的新型声学-视觉-惯性里程计方案，用于水下无人航行器。该系统首次将高斯过程应用于融合多普勒测速仪、立体相机和惯性测量单元的异步测量，并提出了鲁棒的水下视觉前端。在真实水下检测数据集上，该方法在精度、鲁棒性和轨迹覆盖率方面均优于现有最先进的视觉-惯性及声学-视觉-惯性SLAM算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境存在光照衰减、照明变化和颗粒物干扰，给视觉定位与建图带来独特挑战，因此需要引入多传感模态和鲁棒的视觉跟踪流水线。

Method: 基于高斯过程的连续时间轨迹估计框架，融合多普勒测速仪、立体相机和惯性测量单元的异步测量；同时提出基于学习特征提取与匹配的视觉前端，以应对水下环境挑战。

Result: 在真实水下检测数据集上，该方法在精度、鲁棒性和轨迹覆盖率方面均优于现有的视觉-惯性及声学-视觉-惯性SLAM算法，且仅依赖短期视觉数据关联。

Conclusion: 该框架能够无缝集成额外传感模态，适应不同环境而无需重新配置，为水下自主导航提供了一种高精度、高鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel acoustic-visual-inertial odometry solution leveraging a continuous-time trajectory estimation framework for unmanned underwater vehicles. Underwater environments present unique challenges for visual localization and mapping, such as light attenuation, illumination variance, and the presence of particulate matter. This motivates the use of additional sensing modalities and a visual tracking pipeline that is robust to diverse subsea conditions. The proposed system is the first continuous-time trajectory estimation framework based on Gaussian processes to fuse asynchronous measurements from a Doppler velocity log, a stereo camera, and an inertial measurement unit. Additionally, a novel visual frontend is proposed, incorporating learning-based feature extraction and matching that is robust to the specific challenges that subsea environments present. The proposed framework enables seamless integration of additional sensor modalities in continuous-time and is adaptable to different environments without reconfiguration. The proposed system is extensively tested on real-world underwater inspection datasets, where it outperforms state-of-the-art visual-inertial and acoustic-visual-inertial SLAM algorithms in accuracy, robustness, and trajectory coverage. Notably, the proposed system outperforms the state-of-the-art despite only forming short-term visual data associations.

</details>


### [239] [SILO: Simulation-in-the-Loop Sim-to-Real Transfer for Multi-Stage Cable Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04616)
*Stone Tao,Jie Xu,Hesam Rabeti,Yashraj Narang,Yijie Guo,Iretiayo Akinola*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Sim-to-Real强化学习的多阶段线缆布线框架，通过GPU并行仿真训练策略，结合SILO执行框架、局部RL策略和鲁棒状态估计，在真实任务中相比之前方法实现了更高成功率和2倍周期时间缩减，首次实现了多阶段线缆布线的Sim-to-Real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法（尤其是模仿学习）在窄定义场景下有一定效果，但需要数千次演示且泛化性差，难以扩展到不同线缆类型和任务。

Method: 利用GPU并行仿真近似线性可变形行为，在数千个并行仿真中训练策略；提出SILO（Simulation In the LOop）执行框架，结合局部化RL策略和鲁棒线缆状态估计来弥合Sim-to-Real差距。

Result: 在真实线缆布线任务中，相比先前最先进的强化学习方法实现了更高的成功率和2倍的周期时间缩减。

Conclusion: 这是首次成功实现多阶段线缆布线的Sim-to-Real RL策略迁移，证明了该框架在复杂线性可变形物体操作中的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Linear-deformable manipulation remains challenging due to the complex deformations of objects such as cables and ropes. Prior data-driven approaches, particularly imitation learning, have shown some promise in narrowly defined settings but typically require thousands of demonstrations for specific tasks and cable types, limiting scalability and generalization. We introduce a sim-to-real reinforcement learning (RL) framework for multi-stage cable routing that leverages GPU-parallelized simulation to approximate linear deformable behaviors. Training across thousands of parallel simulations enables the learned policies to generalize across diverse cable geometries and deformation patterns. To bridge the sim-to-real gap, we propose a novel deployment strategy that combines a Simulation In the LOop (SILO) execution framework, localized RL policies, and robust cable state estimation. On real-world cable routing tasks, our approach achieves higher success rates and 2x reduction in cycle times compared to prior state-of-the-art learning methods. To our knowledge, this is the first successful sim-to-real transfer of RL policies for multi-stage cable routing. Videos and additional visualizations are available at https://silo-cable-routing.github.io/

</details>


### [240] [Governed Caste Reassignment in Heterogeneous Swarms: An Asymmetric-Trust Protocol with Audited Operator Countersignature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04634)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于异构机器人蜂群的非对称信任协议，将角色重新分配（特别是提升权限的角色变更）视为需要审计和外部授权的治理事件。自动收紧（降级）自动批准，而有限放松（升级）需要操作员背书；每次变更携带签名原因链并提交至Merkle审计日志，可抵御四种明确攻击，并支持分布式审计层实现拜占庭容错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将蜂群中机器人的角色重新分配（caste reassignment）视为内部分配算法，不对外部权威暴露该过程。然而在受监管的具身部署场景中，提升机器人权限的角色变更是一个治理事件，必须可审计且需外部授权。

Method: 提出非对称信任协议：自动收紧（降级到更低权限角色）自动执行；有限放松（升级到更高权限角色）需要操作员针对每轴预算进行副署签名。每次变更携带签名原因链，提交至哈希链式Merkle审计日志，离线审计员仅凭操作员签名的身份清单即可验证。分布式审计层在N个成员副本间复制日志，采用法定人数提交的全序和密码学分叉排除机制。

Result: 参考实现使用真实Ed25519签名，在最多100台机器人的蜂群中：自动收紧在个位数到低两位数毫秒内完成。该协议从结构上拒绝了四种攻击（角色清洗、重复升级、操作员冒充、原因链伪造），部分治理基线可隔离每种攻击由哪个门控阻止，随机模糊测试未发现任何绕过。分布式审计层在仿真和真实多进程TCP部署（最多100个进程）中验证了一致性和分叉排除，面对拜占庭混淆者时所有诚实副本达成一致、检测到混淆且未提交分叉。

Conclusion: 该构造将单智能体的身份突变治理门泛化到了蜂群级别的角色治理，为受监管的异构机器人蜂群提供了一种可审计、可外部授权且抗攻击的角色变更框架。

Abstract: In heterogeneous robot swarms, caste reassignment (rebinding a robot to a new capability-bound role) is a high-frequency runtime event driven by battery, payload, and priority changes. Existing approaches treat it as an internal allocation algorithm and do not expose the reassignment to external authority. We argue that for regulated embodied deployments a caste change that elevates a robot's privilege envelope is a governance event that must be auditable and externally authorised. We propose an asymmetric-trust protocol: auto-tightening reassignments (to safer, lower-privilege castes) are admitted automatically, while bounded relaxation (to higher-privilege castes) requires an operator countersignature against a per-axis budget. Each transition carries a signed cause-chain, committed to a hash-chained Merkle audit log that an offline auditor verifies from an operator-signed identity manifest alone. We evaluate a reference implementation with real Ed25519 signatures over fleets up to 100 robots: auto-tightening completes in single-digit to low-double-digit milliseconds, and the governed protocol refuses four explicit attacks (caste laundering, repeated-relaxation escalation, operator impersonation, cause-chain forgery) by construction, with a partially-governed baseline isolating which gate stops which attack and a randomized fuzz adversary finding no admission. A distributed audit layer replicates the log across N per-member replicas with quorum-committed total order and cryptographic fork exclusion; we prove agreement and fork exclusion and validate them both in simulation and as a real multi-process deployment over TCP sockets (up to 100 real processes) with a Byzantine equivocator, on which every honest replica agrees, detects the equivocation, and commits no fork. The construction generalises a single-agent persona-mutation governance gate to swarm-level caste governance.

</details>


### [241] [KAM-WM: Kinematic Affordance Maps from Latent World Models for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04652)
*Xinyu Shao,Keru Zhou,Guowei Huang,Yajun Gao,Tongtong Cao,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KAM-WM 从冻结节视频世界模型中提取粗粒度方向交互线索（运动学可供性图），无需 rollout 或微调，通过轻量级 Perceiver 压缩为条件令牌以生成扩散策略，在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin2.0 上分别达到 90.6% 和 65.7%/22.4% 的成功率，验证了方向信息比静态空间先验更有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量演示学习操作技能所需的视觉先验不仅应包括交互位置（静态先验如分割掩码已提供），还应包括交互如何开始（方向信息），但现有方法缺乏后者。

Method: KAM-WM 使用 Flow Matching 图像到视频骨干网络对冻结节视频世界模型进行单步查询，解释其潜速度作为运动学可供性图（KAM），提供任务条件化的交互区域和粗粒度运动结构；然后通过轻量级 Perceiver 将 KAM 压缩为令牌，与 RGB 观测和本体感知共同条件化扩散策略。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上平均成功率达 90.6%；在 RoboTwin2.0 上 Easy 和 Hard 设置分别为 65.7% 和 22.4%。与零阶掩码先验的对比控制实验表明，性能提升部分来自超出空间定位的方向信息。

Conclusion: 在评估场景中，冻结节视频模型可以提供有用的初阶视觉先验用于控制，无需测试时 rollout 的成本。

Abstract: Learning manipulation from few demonstrations requires visual priors that capture not only where to interact, but also how the interaction should begin; static priors such as segmentation masks encode only the former. We present KAM-WM, a framework that extracts a coarse directional interaction cue from a frozen latent video world model without rollout or world-model fine-tuning. KAM-WM queries a Flow Matching image-to-video backbone once and interprets its single-step latent velocity as a Kinematic Affordance Map (KAM), which provides task-conditioned interaction regions and coarse motion structure. A lightweight Perceiver compresses KAM into tokens that condition a diffusion policy together with RGB observations and proprioception. Across LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0, KAM-WM reaches 90.6% average success on LIBERO and achieves 65.7% and 22.4% success rates in the Easy and Hard settings on RoboTwin2.0, respectively. Controlled comparisons against a zero-order mask prior suggest that part of the gains comes from directional information beyond spatial localization alone. These results indicate that, in the evaluated settings, a frozen video model can provide a useful first-order visual prior for control without the test-time cost of future rollout.

</details>


### [242] [Do Vision-Language-Action Models Mean What They Say? On the Role of Faithfulness in Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04681)
*Matthew Foutter,Matteo Cercola,Lena Wild,Yunshan Wang,Michelle Li,Daniele Gammelli,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文区分了VLA模型中功能性推理与忠实性推理，指出现有对齐策略仅提供必要但不足的忠实性。通过人类评估揭示推理质量与轨迹改进之间的不一致耦合，提出行为代理衡量方法Pinocchio，利用强化学习进行后训练，在保留驾驶基准上忠实性提升4%-18%，并在OOD场景下策略响应提升1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Embodied Chain-of-Thought虽能提升机器人决策可解释性，但其是否真实反映策略内部决策过程尚不清楚。作者区分功能性推理与忠实性推理，指出现有对齐策略存在忠实性不足问题，推理步骤可能掩盖因果链接，限制策略泛化能力。

Method: 1) 通过人类评估分析SoTA推理模型在自动驾驶中的推理质量与轨迹改进之间的耦合关系；2) 提出Pinocchio，一个学习到的批评家模型，用于评分观测基础性和步骤连贯性，作为行为忠实性的代理；3) 将Pinocchio作为密集奖励信号，结合强化学习对具身策略进行后训练。

Result: 后训练的规划器在保留驾驶基准上相比SoTA对齐基线和轨迹误差后训练基线，忠实性分别提升4%和18%；在合成OOD测试集上，忠实性后训练使策略对罕见反事实场景的响应能力提升1.6倍，同时保持竞争性的下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 忠实性推理轨迹有助于构建更鲁棒、泛化能力更强且可解释的具身智能体，为未来VLA模型中的忠实推理研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Embodied Chain-of-Thought has emerged as a promising mechanism to enhance robot decision-making and interpretability in black-box Vision-Language Action (VLA) models. However, whether this verbalized Chain-of-Thought truthfully reflects the policy's underlying decision process remains poorly understood. We distinguish between functional reasoning, in which reasoning improves task performance, and faithful reasoning, in which reasoning truly reflects the policy's internal decision process. We argue that SoTA alignment strategies offer a necessary but insufficient notion of faithfulness, admitting reasoning whose intermediate steps can mask the causal links in action prediction through confounding factors (e.g., reasoning that is ungrounded in the environment and internally disconnected or inconsistent), restricting policy generalization. We study this gap through a human evaluation of a SoTA reasoning model for autonomous driving, revealing an inconsistent coupling between reasoning quality and downstream trajectory improvement. We then operationalize a behavioral surrogate for embodied faithfulness through a learned critic, Pinocchio, scoring observation grounding and stepwise coherence, and use this critic as a dense reward signal in post-training an embodied policy with reinforcement learning. Across withheld driving benchmarks, our post-trained planner improves faithfulness by 4% and 18% over SoTA alignment and trajectory error post-training baselines, respectively, while maintaining competitive downstream task performance. Finally, on a synthetic out-of-distribution test set, post-training for faithfulness improves policy responsiveness to rare counterfactual scenarios by 1.6x that of a SoTA policy, suggesting that faithful reasoning traces contribute to more robust, generalizable, and interpretable embodied intelligence. Project page: https://mjf-su.github.io/pinocchio/

</details>


### [243] [A Reliable Context-Aware and Temporal Planning Framework for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04689)
*Argho Dey,Yunfei Yin,Swachha Ray,Md Minhazul Islam,Zheng Yuan,Sijing Xiong,Hongyu Liu,Zhiqiu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RCT-AD框架，通过显式建模特征质量和时间一致性，在感知降质时实现更安全、更稳定的自动驾驶规划，在nuScenes基准上超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BEV方法在融合时序信息时未评估底层观测的可靠性，导致降质观测被无差别累积，使轨迹规划不稳定、碰撞风险升高。

Method: 提出RCT-AD框架，包含：(1) 可靠上下文感知模块，通过质量门控FILO记忆机制按帧评分可靠性并选择性保留可信特征，从可靠历史上下文重建降质观测；(2) 时序轨迹规划器，捕捉长期依赖和多智能体交互；(3) 联合检测-分割头，将语义和运动线索注入共享BEV空间。

Result: 在nuScenes上取得61.5 NDS、52.9 mAP、52.3 mIoU，在感知精度、运动预测和规划鲁棒性上超过近期端到端基线，计算效率适合实时部署。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模特征可靠性和时序一致性，RCT-AD有效缓解了降质观测对自动驾驶规划的负面影响，为密集城区安全驾驶提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Safe operation of autonomous vehicles in dense urban traffic depends on perception and planning that remain reliable when onboard sensing is degraded. In real driving conditions, camera observations are frequently corrupted by occlusion, motion blur, illumination change, and sensor noise, and when such degraded observations are aggregated indiscriminately over time, trajectory planning becomes unstable and collision risk rises for both the ego vehicle and surrounding road users. Recent Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) approaches unify perception and planning through a shared spatial representation, but most fuse temporal information across frames without assessing the reliability of the underlying observations. We present a Reliable Context-Aware and Temporal Planning framework for Autonomous Driving (RCT-AD) that explicitly models feature quality and temporal consistency to support safer, more consistent planning. A Reliable Context Awareness module scores per-frame reliability and selectively retains trustworthy features through a quality-gated First-In-Last-Out (FILO) memory mechanism, reconstructing degraded observations from reliable historical context so that corrupted inputs do not destabilize the scene representation. A Temporal Trajectory Planner captures long-term dependencies and multi-agent interactions to produce smoother, safety-aware trajectories, while a joint detection-and-segmentation head injects semantic and motion cues into the shared BEV space to strengthen scene understanding. Experiments on the nuScenes autonomous driving benchmark show that RCT-AD improves perception accuracy, motion prediction, and planning robustness over recent end-to-end baselines, achieving 61.5 nuScenes Detection Score, 52.9 mean Average Precision, and 52.3 mean Intersection over Union, while maintaining competitive computational efficiency suitable for real-time deployment.

</details>


### [244] [Geometry-Aware Motion Latents for Learning Robust Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04714)
*Yunchao Zhang,Yijia Weng,Ruizhe Liu,Ming Hu,Leonidas Guibas,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoMoLa通过预测点云在操作过程中的三维几何演化来学习离散运动潜码，而非重建视觉观测，从而在仅使用单视角RGB-D输入的情况下实现了SOTA操作性能，并展现出良好的运动抽象和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖从视觉序列中提取运动模式，但有效的动作抽象需要理解三维几何变换，而像素级重建难以捕获实际物理运动。

Method: 提出GeoMoLa框架，学习离散运动潜码，通过预测点云在操作过程中的时空演变（四维目标：空间几何随时间变化）来迫使潜表示编码实际物理运动，而非外观模式。

Result: 仅使用单视角RGB-D输入即达到SOTA性能（传统方法需多视角重建）；在多种操作基准上成功；消融实验验证几何预测是性能驱动关键；潜码展现有效运动抽象，应用于新场景时产生与视觉上下文无关的物理一致变换。

Conclusion: 有效的机器人控制运动潜码应通过理解运动的三维效应而非像素级模式来获得，几何推理是操作成功的关键。

Abstract: Learning motion latents for robotic manipulation heavily relies on extracting motion patterns from visual sequences, yet effective action abstractions require understanding three-dimensional geometric transformations. Here, we introduce GeoMoLa (Geometry-Aware Motion Latents), which learns discrete motion latent codes by predicting how point clouds evolve during manipulation rather than reconstructing visual observations. This four-dimensional objective -- spatial geometry changing through time -- forces latent representations to encode actual physical motion rather than appearance patterns. GeoMoLa achieves state-of-the-art performance using only single-view RGB-D input, while existing methods require multi-view reconstruction, succeeding across diverse manipulation benchmarks. Our ablations reveal that geometric prediction is the key to driving performance, quantitatively validating that manipulation depends on spatial understanding. Furthermore, the learned codes exhibit effective motion abstraction: applying them to novel scenes produces physically consistent transformations regardless of visual context. Our real-world experiments also confirm this robustness capability, achieving robust manipulation with minimal demonstrations in cluttered environments where geometric reasoning determines success. Thus, we demonstrate that effective motion latents for robot control can better emerge from understanding motion through its three-dimensional effects rather than pixel-level patterns.

</details>


### [245] [Aerial Manipulation: Contact, Medium Coupling, and the Geometry of Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04719)
*Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了空中机器人从远程观测向物理交互发展的趋势，提出"介质感知"的空中操纵新视角，指出空中操纵与地面操纵的本质区别在于飞行器通过持续动量与能量交换维持悬停，因此支撑、运动、稳定和任务导向交互是内在耦合的。文章通过生物与机器人案例构建了交互模式谱系和能力阶梯，并从驱动-几何视角解释了空中操纵的困难所在，以及未来平台如何同时控制力并塑造介质的反作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将空中操纵视为简单地将地面操纵移植到飞行平台上，未能认识到飞行器通过持续动量与能量交换与周围介质耦合的本质特性，导致空中操纵的独特困难被低估。

Method: 通过综述生物飞行与机器人系统案例，构建交互模式谱系和能力阶梯；提出驱动-几何视角，利用冗余产生任务等价纤维，分析沿纤维的内部运动如何实现能量-主动就绪、气动敏捷性和被动介质耦合的权衡。

Result: 提出了"介质感知"的空中操纵理论框架，阐明了空中操纵的困难根源在于支撑、运动、稳定和交互的内在耦合；解释了生物飞行器比现有机器人系统更具广度的原因。

Conclusion: 未来空中平台应不仅能施加力，还能主动塑造周围介质对其的反作用，这为空中操纵机器人系统设计提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Aerial robots are increasingly moving from remote observation toward physical interaction with objects, surfaces, structures, loads, and surrounding flows. This review argues that aerial manipulation cannot be understood as classical manipulation simply mounted on a flying base. Because flying agents remain aloft through continuous momentum and energy exchange with the surrounding medium, support, locomotion, stabilization, and task-directed interaction are intrinsically coupled. Building on broad views of manipulation as intentional environmental regulation through physical interaction, we propose a medium-aware interpretation of aerial manipulation in which interaction may be mediated by contact, by the surrounding fluid, or by both. The review organizes biological and robotic examples into a repertoire of interaction modes and a capability ladder, then develops an actuation-geometric viewpoint in which redundancy induces task-equivalent fibers. Internal motion along these fibers can trade energy for active readiness, aerodynamic promptness, and passive medium coupling. This perspective clarifies why aerial manipulation is difficult, why biological flyers remain broader than robotic systems, and how future platforms may command forces while also shaping how the medium acts back on them.

</details>


### [246] [Spatial Attention: Adapting Execution Horizons for Diffusion Policies via Observation Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04739)
*Che-Sang Park,Junsu Ha,Jianlong Fu,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应调整动作块执行周期的方法，通过空间注意力（动作对数似然关于观测的梯度期望平方范数）动态分配执行时长，在固定采样预算下最小化扰动导致的累积似然下降，实验表明该方法在保持平均执行周期的同时显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成模型采样动作块的机器人学习从示范方法中，固定执行周期难以平衡响应速度与计算成本，急需自适应调整机制。

Method: 定义空间注意力（Spatial Attention）为动作对数似然关于观测的梯度期望平方范数，证明在固定采样预算下最小化扰动累积似然下降的最优执行周期与空间注意力成反比，通过预测未来空间注意力值动态分配执行周期。

Result: 在标准与扰动任务（仿真及真实机器人）上，该方法相比固定周期基线显著提升成功率，同时保持平均执行周期不变。

Conclusion: 自适应执行周期机制有效平衡了响应性与计算效率，空间注意力可作为策略对观测敏感性的有效指标指导动态调度。

Abstract: Sampling action chunks via generative models has become a widely adopted methodology for robotic learning from demonstration. However, existing methods often struggle to balance responsiveness and computational cost because they execute each action chunk for a fixed execution horizon. In this paper, we adaptively adjust the execution horizon of sampled action chunks, balancing responsiveness and computational efficiency. We introduce Spatial Attention -- defined as the expected squared norm of the gradient of the action log-likelihood with respect to the observation -- which indicates the sensitivity of the policy's action distribution to variations in the observation. We show that, under a fixed budget of chunk samplings, the execution horizon that minimizes the cumulative likelihood drop induced by disturbances decreases as Spatial Attention increases. By forecasting future Spatial Attention values alongside the action chunk, our framework dynamically assigns shorter execution horizons to phases with high Spatial Attention, and longer horizons to phases with low Spatial Attention. Experiments on standard and perturbed tasks, in both simulation and on a real robot, show that our method significantly improves success rates over fixed-horizon baselines while maintaining the average execution horizon.

</details>


### [247] [Trajectory-Anchor Optimization for Overconfident Thermal Visual Place Recognition: Zero-Leakage OOD Auditing and Kidnapped-Robot Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04745)
*Zhiyuan Lu,Kanji Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对热视觉位置识别中基于基础模型的前端在OOD条件下产生过度自信的假阳性匹配问题，本文提出轨迹锚点优化(TAO)，通过将多视图时序验证压缩为批量化SE(2) Procrustes对齐问题，以O(KN)的严格有界计算开销替代传统多假设跟踪的指数级复杂度，在宏观尺度上有效隔离幻觉匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TIR-VPR前端在未映射或分布外条件下会产生过度自信的假阳性回环候选，而传统MHT后端虽能缓解歧义但计算开销呈指数增长，无法满足实时机器人约束。

Method: 将多视图时序验证压缩为批量化SE(2) Procrustes对齐问题，利用张量级向量化和单次调用批量化SVD，实现O(KN)严格有界的每帧计算循环，替代MHT的动态树扩展。

Result: 在5m半径内局部几何一致性会欺骗刚性对齐，但超过该阈值后K=100个不同假设在全局地图中空间分散，破坏了滑动窗口内的刚性时序共视约束，联合优化残差急剧上升，在10m处建立清晰的宏观收敛盆地，有效隔离拓扑断裂并抑制关键假阳性接受。

Conclusion: TAO可作为高效的大规模故障安全滤波器，在宏观尺度上可靠分离灾难性拓扑断裂，弥补被动几何后端在微观尺度上无法区分度量定位误差与一致幻觉的局限性。

Abstract: Modern thermal visual place recognition (TIR-VPR) frontends based on foundation models achieve remarkable closed-set retrieval but suffer from an overconfident forced-matching failure mode. Under out-of-distribution (OOD) or unmapped conditions, they generate highly plausible yet false loop candidates without a drop in similarity scores. While classical multi-hypothesis tracking (MHT) backends can mitigate these ambiguities by maintaining divergent trajectory beliefs, their exponential computational overhead violates real-time robotic constraints. To bridge this gap, we present Trajectory-Anchor Optimization (TAO). To counter the combinatorial challenge of evaluating parallel hypotheses (e.g., K=100), TAO compresses multi-view temporal verification into a batched SE(2) Procrustes alignment problem. By leveraging tensor-level vectorization and single-invocation batched SVD, this formulation bypasses the dynamic tree expansion of MHT, guaranteeing a strictly bounded per-frame execution loop of O(KN). Under a strict zero-leakage evaluation protocol, we show that while a passive geometric backend cannot mathematically separate metric localization errors from coherent hallucinations at a micro-scale (<5m) due to local visual ambiguities, TAO serves as an efficient fail-safe filter at a macro-scale. Within a 5m radius, hallucinations often possess a locally consistent geometry that deceives rigid alignment. However, beyond this threshold, the K=100 disparate hypotheses disperse spatially across the global map. This dispersion breaks the rigid temporal co-visibility constraint within the sliding window (N=20), causing the joint optimization residual to escalate sharply. Consequently, TAO establishes a distinct macroscopic convergence basin (10m) where multi-view geometric consistency reliably isolates catastrophic topological breaks and suppresses critical false acceptances.

</details>


### [248] [SLAM: Structured and Localized Analytic Manifold Adaptation for Lifelong VPR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04764)
*Kenta Tsukahara,Kanji Tanaka,Rai Hisada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了SLAM框架，通过无迹变换、高斯混合模型和H∞鲁棒界优化三者的统一闭式解析递归，实现视觉地点识别在持续部署中的无灾难遗忘适应性。消融实验表明U+G配置达到27.5%的SOTA标称精度，而H∞界则通过单正则化参数实现标称精度与最坏情况扰动衰减之间的可调权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉地点识别在终身部署中需要持续适应新环境，同时避免灾难性遗忘，现有方法无法优雅地统一不确定性建模、空间拓扑划分和鲁棒优化。

Method: 提出SLAM框架，将无迹变换（不确定性感知平滑）、高斯混合模型（拓扑空间划分）和H∞鲁棒界优化三者统一为一个闭式解析递归形式。

Result: U+G配置（不确定性平滑+局部映射）达到27.5%的SOTA标称精度；完整H∞界部署无需架构拆分，通过单正则化参数实现标称精度与最坏情况扰动衰减之间的可调权衡。

Conclusion: SLAM框架通过统一的闭式解析递归，有效解决了VPR终身部署中的适应性-稳定性权衡问题，且H∞界提供了数学上保证的极小化极大鲁棒界。

Abstract: Visual Place Recognition (VPR) in lifelong deployment requires continuous adaptation to new environments without catastrophic forgetting. In this paper, we propose SLAM, a Structured and Localized Analytic Manifold adaptation framework. Our framework elegantly unifies uncertainty-aware smoothing via Unscented transformation, topological space partitioning through a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), and $H_\infty$ robust bound optimization into a singular, unified closed-form analytical recursion. Exhaustive ablation studies demonstrate that while the synergistic combination of uncertainty smoothing and localized mapping (U+G configuration) achieves the state-of-the-art nominal accuracy of 27.5%, the full deployment of the $H_\infty$ bound does not require an architectural split; rather, it introduces a mathematically guaranteed minimax robust bound. This formulation enables the system to seamlessly modulate the intrinsic trade-off between nominal placement precision and worst-case disturbance attenuation through a single regularization parameter.

</details>


### [249] [CAC-VLA: Context-Gated Action Conditioning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04816)
*Yifu Xiong,Wenhao Yu,Jiaxuan Lin,Bojun Zou,Jiahao Li,Lu Zhang,Yanyong Zhang,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAC-VLA通过在VLM内部学习轻量级潜在动作接口，利用上下文门控机制自适应地将粗到细的潜在动作条件作用于动作专家，在LIBERO和LIBERO-Plus上分别达到98.3%和89.5%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型中视觉语言表示未针对动作条件化进行显式优化，动作专家需自行弥合多模态理解与精确运动控制之间的鸿沟；而近期动作推理方法虽引入动作级引导信号，但通常需要额外的动作生成框架，增加了系统复杂度。

Method: 提出CAC-VLA框架：①在VLM内部学习轻量级潜在动作接口，从未来动作片段中编码粗到细的结构化潜在动作表示；②通过上下文门控机制自适应地调节潜在动作引导对动作专家生成的影响，实现VLM原生的动作条件化。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上达到98.3%平均成功率，在LIBERO-Plus上达到89.5%平均成功率。

Conclusion: 上下文门控的潜在动作条件化是连续专家控制的有效接口，证明了在VLM内部直接进行动作条件化的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a promising paradigm for generalist robot manipulation, where visual-language representations are used to condition continuous action generation. However, these representations are not explicitly optimized for action conditioning, leaving the action expert to bridge the gap between multimodal understanding and precise motor control. Recent action-reasoning methods introduce additional modules to generate explicit action plans or action-space reasoning signals, demonstrating the benefit of action-level guidance but often requiring separate action-generation frameworks. We propose CAC-VLA, a Context-Gated Action Conditioning framework that learns a lightweight latent-action interface directly within the VLM. Instead of generating executable trajectories, CAC-VLA trains the VLM to predict coarse-to-fine latent actions, which are structured representations encoded from future action segments, and adaptively leverages them to condition the action expert via a context gate. This enables VLM-native action conditioning while calibrating the influence of latent-action guidance on expert action generation. Experiments on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus demonstrate the effectiveness of CAC-VLA, achieving 98.3% average success rate on LIBERO and 89.5% LIBERO-Plus, suggesting that context-gated latent-action conditioning is an effective interface for continuous expert control.

</details>


### [250] [Athena-WBC: Capability-Aligned Policy Experts for Long-Tail Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04837)
*Yuan Jiang,Ningyuan Zhang,Xicun Yang,Shidi Li,Yuzhi Jiang,Zhiyi Rong,Shuaikang Ma,Chuanzheng Li,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Athena-WBC，一种紧凑的教师-学生流水线，通过能力对齐的策略专家解决人形机器人全身控制中长尾运动难以学习的问题。动态专家采用无保守惩罚的跟踪目标，平衡专家使用重力课程提升早期存活率，最终通过DAgger蒸馏和RL微调压缩为单一控制器。实验证明该方法在训练集长尾运动和未见运动跟踪上均优于强基线SONIC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大尺度人形运动跟踪控制器通常通过重分配训练资源（如难样本过采样、子集隔离、专用专家）来提升性能，但本文发现即使采用针对性训练，仍有可实现的训练片段无法被解决，尤其是高动态过渡和平衡关键运动。这些失败不仅源于训练不足，更源于运动需求与默认训练配方所诱导的有效能力之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出Athena-WBC流水线：(1) 动态专家使用跟踪导向、约束感知的目标函数，去除保守力和时间控制惩罚，保留物理可行性约束；(2) 平衡专家使用重力课程提升早期训练存活率；(3) 特权教师通过运动路由进行DAgger蒸馏，然后压缩为单一控制器，使用可部署观测并经过RL微调。

Result: 在全尺寸人形机器人上，Athena-WBC在训练集长尾运动恢复和未见运动跟踪上均优于强SONIC基线，且仅使用少量专家。

Conclusion: 仅靠重新分配训练资源不足以解决长尾运动问题，需要使训练能力与运动需求对齐。通过能力对齐的专家设计和蒸馏压缩，可以更高效地实现高质量全身控制。

Abstract: Large-scale humanoid motion-tracking controllers are commonly improved by reallocating training effort: difficult motions are sampled more often, isolated into smaller subsets, or assigned to specialized experts. We show that this view is incomplete. In strong whole-body-control baselines, a residual set of feasible training clips remains unsolved even under targeted training, especially for high-dynamic transitions and balance-critical motions. These failures arise not only from insufficient exposure, but from a mismatch between the motion demands and the effective capability induced by the default training recipe. We propose Athena-WBC, a compact teacher-student pipeline with capability-aligned policy experts for long-tail humanoid whole-body control. Dynamic experts use a tracking-focused, constraint-aware objective that removes conservative effort and temporal-control penalties while preserving physical feasibility constraints; balance experts use a gravity curriculum to improve early-training survivability. The resulting privileged teachers are motion-routed for DAgger distillation and then compressed into a single controller with deployable observations followed by RL fine-tuning. Experiments on a full-size humanoid show improved recovery of training-set long-tail motions and better held-out tracking than a strong SONIC-recipe baseline, using only a small number of experts.

</details>


### [251] [WinTA-GIL: Windowed Trajectory Alignment for GNSS-IMU-LiDAR Heading Refinement in Intermittent Signal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04879)
*Kaixin Feng,Zhichao Wen,Zhaohong Liao,Xin Xia,You Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WinTA-GIL框架，通过时间窗口优化策略融合GNSS、IMU和LiDAR信息，将航向估计转化为可重复的轨迹一致性优化问题，并引入基于状态判别的自适应重估计机制，在复杂环境下显著提升了航向估计精度与系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源融合定位系统中，航向估计因缺乏重力约束且环境复杂导致可观性弱，现有方法仅在启动阶段进行单次初始对准，无法动态修正航向，导致长时间导航或GNSS信号中断后系统易受累积漂移和观测噪声影响。

Method: 提出WinTA-GIL框架，通过时间窗口优化策略融合GNSS、IMU和LiDAR信息；利用LIO生成的高精度局部轨迹与滤波后的GNSS观测进行配准，将航向估计转化为可重复的轨迹一致性优化问题；引入基于状态判别的自适应重估计机制，按需触发航向修正。

Result: 在开源和自采集数据集上的大量实验表明，WinTA-GIL在估计精度和系统鲁棒性方面均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: WinTA-GIL通过将航向估计从单次初始对准扩展为动态可重复的轨迹优化过程，有效抑制了惯性漂移，为复杂环境下的多源融合定位系统提供了更可靠的航向估计方案。

Abstract: Although multi-source fusion positioning systems have achieved significant progress, accurate and reliable heading estimation remains a critical challenge due to the lack of gravitational constraints and the inherent weak observability of heading in complex environments. Most existing methodologies are specifically tailored for the startup phase, relying on a singular initial alignment to establish the heading reference. Consequently, these approaches lack the adaptability required to refine heading estimates dynamically, which renders the system highly vulnerable to accumulated drift and observation noise during prolonged navigation or immediately following GNSS signal outages. To address these limitations, this paper proposes WinTA-GIL, a novel heading refinement framework that integrates information from Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS), Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU), and Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) through a temporal window-based optimization strategy. Unlike conventional alignment methods restricted to the startup phase, WinTA-GIL leverages high-precision local trajectories from LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (LIO) to register against filtered GNSS observations. This approach transforms heading estimation into a repeatable, trajectory-based consistency optimization problem. In particular, an adaptive re-estimation mechanism based on state discrimination is incorporated to trigger heading corrections whenever necessary, thereby effectively suppressing the inertial drift accumulated during challenging conditions. Extensive experiments on both open-source and self-collected datasets demonstrate that WinTA-GIL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in both estimation accuracy and system robustness.

</details>


### [252] [PRISM: Personalized Robotic Dataset Generation via Image-based Scene and Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04880)
*Dogyu Ko,Haneul Kim,Chanyoung Yeo,Dowoon Lee,Taeho Park,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM是一种从单张图像和自然语言指令生成个性化机器人数据集的端到端流水线，无需人工遥操作即可构建语义和几何对齐的数字孪生场景并合成可执行的示范数据，在LIBERO和真实世界操作任务中取得了优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略在用户特定环境中部署时泛化能力有限，需要收集目标环境的数据集。遥操作成本高且难以扩展，仿真虽可扩展但难以贴近目标环境并生成任务特定轨迹，因此需要一种同时满足数据对齐性和可扩展性的方法。

Method: PRISM是一个端到端流水线，输入为单张图像和自然语言指令，输出为个性化机器人数据集。其核心是构建与用户环境在语义和几何上对齐、但在实例层面多样化的"数字孪生场景"（digital cousin scenes），并自动合成可执行的示范轨迹，无需人工遥操作。

Result: 在LIBERO和LIBERO-Plus上，PRISM生成的数据集训练的策略优于基线方法；在三个真实世界操作任务中达到100%的成功率；在不同于训练环境的场景中评估时仍保持更强的性能。

Conclusion: PRISM有效解决了机器人策略部署中的数据对齐与可扩展性矛盾，通过构建对齐的数字孪生场景自动生成高质量示范数据，显著提升了机器人策略在用户特定环境中的泛化能力和任务成功率。

Abstract: Recent advances in large-scale pretrained vision-language-action models have improved robot policy learning, but directly deploying such policies in user-specific environments remains challenging due to limited generalization, which inevitably requires collecting a dataset tailored to the target environment. Teleoperation yields well-aligned data but is costly and difficult to scale, whereas simulation scales easily but struggles to resemble the target environment and generate task-specific trajectories. To meet both simultaneously, we propose PRISM, an end-to-end pipeline that generates personalized robotic datasets from a single image and a natural-language instruction. PRISM constructs digital cousin scenes that are semantically and geometrically aligned with the user environment yet diverse at the instance level, and synthesizes executable demonstrations without human teleoperation. Extensive experiments show that policies trained on PRISM-generated datasets outperform those trained on baseline-generated datasets on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, achieve up to 100\% success rate on three real-world manipulation tasks, and maintain stronger performance when evaluated in environments that differ from those seen during training.

</details>


### [253] [DSWAM: A Dual-System World Action Foundation Model for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04927)
*Jian Zhu,Jianjun Zhang,Taiyi Su,Tianbin Liu,Zhangyuan Wang,Kai Xie,Zitai Huang,Chong Ma,Youzhang He,Tianjian Wang,Hanyang Wang,Weihao Ding,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DSWAM是一种双系统世界动作基础模型，结合System 1世界动作模型执行器与System 2视觉语言子任务规划器，通过TensorRT加速、异步执行和实时分块技术实现细粒度机器人操作，并在DeMaVLA标准化框架下与VLA策略进行公平对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型(WAMs)缺乏显式的语言级规划接口，无法将粗粒度指令分解为细粒度可执行子任务；同时领域内缺乏WAM与VLA在统一平台上的公平真实机器人对比评估。

Method: 提出DSWAM双系统架构：System 1为WAM执行器（默认控制路径），System 2为VLM子任务规划器（可选激活）。执行器通过动作预测与视频联合训练，推理时直接预测动作块（无需显式视频生成）；集成TensorRT加速、异步执行和实时分块(RTC)技术；在DeMaVLA框架下使用匹配的机器人平台、预训练/后训练数据和评估标准。

Result: 在DeMaVLA真实世界可变形操作设置下，实现了与VLA策略的公平对比评估。

Conclusion: DSWAM弥合了WAM在任务分解上的不足，同时提供了WAM与VLA在统一标准下的公平比较基准。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) provide a promising alternative to Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies by using video-based world modeling as dense supervision for robot action learning. Existing WAMs excel at physically grounded execution, but typically lack the explicit language-level planning interface in VLM-based VLAs for decomposing coarse instructions. Such decomposition becomes important when household tasks involve complex multi-step goals, where coarse user commands need to be converted into sequences of fine-grained executable subtasks. Meanwhile, the field still lacks a fair real-robot comparison between VLA and WAM execution capabilities, since existing systems often differ in data, robot embodiments, and task protocols. To address both the decomposition gap and the need for a controlled WAM-VLA comparison, we introduce DSWAM, a Dual-System World Action Foundation Model for fine-grained robot manipulation. DSWAM keeps a System 1 WAM executor as the default control path and optionally activates a System 2 vision-language subtask planner only when task decomposition is useful. The planner predicts executable subtasks from short-term visual history and a global task prompt, while the WAM executor performs world-aware action generation for each instruction or subtask. The executor is trained with action prediction and video co-training, but inference directly predicts action chunks without explicit future video generation. To make this execution path practical on real robots, we further integrate TensorRT acceleration, asynchronous execution, and real-time chunking (RTC) so that policy queries do not block robot control. To provide a fair real-robot comparison with VLA policies, we build and evaluate DSWAM under the DeMaVLA real-world deformable manipulation setting with matched robot platform, pretraining data, post-training data, and evaluation criteria.

</details>


### [254] [Closing the Reality Gap: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Deployment for Dexterous Force-Based Grasping and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04940)
*Zhe Zhao,Zhibin Li,Yilin Ou,Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模拟到现实的强化学习方法，结合密集触觉反馈和关节扭矩传感，在五指灵巧手上实现了可控抓取力跟踪和物体重新定向，无需真实硬件微调即可零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手具备类人操作能力，但由于接触丰富的物理特性和非理想驱动，难以训练可在真实硬件上部署的控制策略。

Method: (i) 基于并行正向运动学的快速触觉仿真，提供高分辨率触觉信号；(ii) 电流-扭矩校准，无需扭矩传感器；(iii) 执行器动力学建模与随机化，弥合驱动差距。采用非对称Actor-Critic PPO管道，在仿真中训练策略并直接部署到五指手上。

Result: 策略成功实现了两种关键人类手部技能：(1) 基于指令的可控抓取力跟踪；(2) 手中物体重新定向。两者均无需真实机器人微调即可鲁棒执行。

Conclusion: 通过将触觉与扭矩结合到观测空间中，配合可扩展的传感与驱动建模，本系统为在多指灵巧手上实现可靠操作提供了实用方案。据作者所知，这是首次在仿真中完全训练并在真实硬件上零样本转移的多指灵巧手可控抓取演示。

Abstract: Human-like dexterous hands with multiple fingers offer human-level manipulation capabilities but remain difficult to train the control policies that can deploy on real hardware due to contact-rich physics and imperfect actuation. We present a sim-to-real reinforcement learning method that leverages dense tactile feedback combined with joint torque sensing to explicitly regulate physical interactions. To enable effective sim-to-real transfer, we introduce (i) a computationally fast tactile simulation that computes distances between dense virtual tactile units and the object via parallel forward kinematics, providing high-rate, high-resolution touch signals needed by RL; (ii) a current-to-torque calibration that eliminates the need for torque sensors on dexterous hands by mapping motor current to joint torque; and (iii) actuator dynamics modeling with randomization to account for non-ideal torque-speed effects and bridge the actuation gaps. Using an asymmetric actor-critic PPO pipeline, we train policies entirely in simulation and deploy them directly to a five-finger hand. The resulting policies demonstrate two essential human-hand skills: (1) command-based controllable grasp force tracking and (2) reorientation of objects in the hand, both of which are robustly executed without fine-tuning on the robot. By combining tactile and torque in the observation space with scalable sensing and actuation modeling, our system provides a practical solution to achieve reliable dexterous manipulation. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of controllable grasping on a multi-finger dexterous hand trained entirely in simulation and transferred zero-shot on real hardware.

</details>


### [255] [Multi-Robot Open Adaptive Teaming Across Unseen Environments, Partners, and Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04972)
*Yang Li,Feng Xue,Fan Mo,Yunhao Liu,Jianhong Wang,Ying Wen,Qingrui Zhang,Shaoshuai Mou,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出超图形式博弈框架（HOLA算法），解决机器人在开放世界中同时适应新环境、未知队友和动态团队规模的挑战，在无人机和四足机器人平台上实现零微调的真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在封闭世界假设下（固定队友）孤立处理环境适应、队友适应和团队规模变化，无法应对真实世界中这些挑战同时出现的情况。

Method: 提出超图形式博弈（hypergraphic-form game）来建模团队级协作关系（超越成对交互），并开发HOLA算法在训练中渐进扩展伙伴和环境多样性，而非优化固定配置。

Result: 在协同追捕任务的多无人机和多四足机器人平台上，HOLA在所有三项适应性维度上超越所有基线，且策略可直接迁移到Crazyflie和Zsibot L1实体硬件，无需微调。

Conclusion: 超图形式博弈为动态团队组成的协调结构推断提供了理论基础，HOLA证明了开放适应性多机器人编队在实际部署中的可行性。

Abstract: Deploying robot teams in the real world requires simultaneous adaptation to unseen environments, unknown partners, and varying team sizes, yet existing approaches often address these challenges in isolation under the closed-world assumption of fixed teammates. We formalize this as open adaptive multi-robot teaming and propose a hypergraphic-form game formulation that captures team-level cooperative relationships beyond pairwise interactions, providing a principled foundation for coordination structure inference when team composition changes dynamically within episodes. Unlike graph neural network architectures, this is a game-theoretic construct for modeling strategic interactions and payoff structures among agents. Building on this formulation, we develop the Hypergraphic Open-ended Learning Algorithm (HOLA), which progressively expands partner and environment diversity during training rather than optimizing for fixed configurations. Evaluated on cooperative pursuit with multi-drone and multi-quadruped platforms, HOLA outperforms all baselines across all three adaptability dimensions. Learned policies transfer directly to physical hardware without fine-tuning, with successful deployments on Crazyflie and Zsibot L1 platforms confirming robust real-world coordination in novel environments with unseen teammates.

</details>


### [256] [Designing Touch for Trauma-Informed Social Robots: A Design Space for Direct and Indirect Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04981)
*Madeleine Rischer,Benedikt Bußmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨如何为创伤后应激障碍(PTSD)患者设计社交机器人的触觉交互，基于创伤知情护理原则，区分直接触觉和间接触觉，提出包含驱动模态、驱动目标与预期效果三个维度的设计空间，并从创伤知情视角推导出关键设计考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉是人机交互中的基本沟通方式，在治疗情境中有助于情感调节和压力缓解。然而，为PTSD患者设计触觉交互需要谨慎考虑创伤知情护理原则（安全、透明、自主、避免触发），目前缺乏系统性的设计框架来指导此类设计。

Method: 论文首先区分了直接触觉（用户与机器人间的物理接触）与间接触觉（通过可穿戴设备或智能纺织品等媒介）；在此基础上提出包含三个维度的设计空间：驱动模态、驱动目标、预期效果；然后通过创伤知情护理视角分析这些维度，推导出关键设计考量。

Result: 提出了一个三维设计空间框架（驱动模态、驱动目标、预期效果），并基于创伤知情护理原则从中推导出针对PTSD患者触觉交互的具体设计考量。

Conclusion: 该研究为开发创伤敏感型社交机器人提供了概念基础，支持通过触觉交互帮助PTSD患者，强调了在设计过程中融入创伤知情原则的重要性。

Abstract: Touch is a fundamental communication modality in human-robot interaction and may support grounding, emotional regulation, and stress reduction in therapeutic contexts. However, designing touch-based interactions for individuals with post-traumatic stress disorder (PTSD) requires careful consideration of trauma-informed care (TIC) principles, including safety, transparency, autonomy, and trigger avoidance. This paper investigates how touch actuation in social robots can be designed to align with trauma-informed care. We distinguish between direct touch, involving physical contact between a user and a robot, and indirect touch, mediated through artifacts such as wearables or smart textiles. Building on this distinction, we propose a design space comprising three dimensions: Actuation Modality, Objective of the Actuation, and Intended Effect of the Actuation. We analyze these dimensions through the lens of trauma-informed care and derive key design considerations for touch-based interventions. The resulting approach provides a conceptual foundation for developing trauma-sensitive social robots that support individuals with PTSD through touch-based interactions.

</details>


### [257] [InternVLA-A1.5: Unifying Understanding, Latent Foresight, and Action for Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04988)
*Haoxiang Ma,Junhao Cai,Xiaoxu Xu,Hao Li,Yuyin Yang,Yang Tian,Jiafei Cao,Hongrui Zhu,Zherui Qiu,Zhaxizhuoma,Yuqiang Yang,Jiaqi Peng,Xueyuan Wei,Yangkun Zhu,Jiahao Jiang,Xing Gao,Hanqing Wang,Feng Yuan,Kailin Li,Xueyue Zhu,Tai Wang,Yan Ding,Jiangmiao Pang,Jia Zeng,Jingjing Zhang,Bowen Zhou,Yao Mu,Chunhua Shen,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InternVLA-A1.5 提出了一种统一机器人操作模型，通过保留预训练VLM的语义、使用轻量级专家模块生成连续动作，并将未来预测重构为潜在查询问题，利用冻结的视频生成模型提供动力学先验，在六个仿真基准和真实世界任务中取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一机器人操作模型存在三个问题：1) 预训练骨干网络的语义被侵蚀；2) 异构目标（语义理解与物理动力学）之间存在干扰；3) 未来预测从零开始在像素空间学习，未利用预训练视频生成器中已有的动力学先验。

Method: 1) 使用原生VLM骨干网络持续进行VQA和子任务预测训练以保持语义；2) 附加轻量级统一专家模块用于连续动作生成；3) 将未来预测重构为潜在查询问题，用少量可学习的foresight tokens在冻结的预训练视频生成模型监督下压缩任务相关的未来信息为紧凑潜在码，无需学习像素级生成；4) 推理时丢弃视频分支保持实时控制。

Result: 在120万机器人轨迹和300万多模态样本上预训练后，InternVLA-A1.5在全部六个仿真基准上取得最佳综合结果。真实世界中，保留的语义带来最强的组合泛化能力（针对未见过的指令绑定），两种设计共同支撑了长时程任务的执行。

Conclusion: 通过保留VLM语义、解耦异构目标、利用预训练视频生成模型的动力学先验，InternVLA-A1.5实现了统一机器人操作模型在仿真和真实环境中的优异性能，证明了将语义先验与动力学先验有效结合是提升机器人策略泛化性和长时程执行能力的关键。

Abstract: Unified models for robot manipulation aim to equip one policy with both the semantic priors of pretrained VLMs and the physical dynamics learned through future prediction. In practice, existing designs tend to erode the semantics of the pretrained backbone, suffer interference among heterogeneous objectives, and learn future prediction from scratch in pixel space, leaving the dynamics priors of pretrained video generators unexploited. We present InternVLA-A1.5, which builds the policy on a native VLM backbone that keeps training on VQA and subtask prediction, and attaches a lightweight unified expert for continuous action generation. Future prediction is recast as a latent-querying problem, where a small set of learnable foresight tokens condenses the task-relevant future into a compact latent code under the supervision of a frozen pretrained video generation model, so the policy inherits world-model dynamics priors without ever learning pixel-level generation. The video branch is discarded at inference, keeping real-time control. Pretrained on 1.2M robot episodes and 3M multimodal samples, InternVLA-A1.5 achieves the best overall results on all six simulation benchmarks. In the real world, the preserved semantics deliver the strongest compositional generalization on held-out instruction bindings, and the two designs together sustain long-horizon execution.

</details>


### [258] [ECO: Incremental Ego-Centric Octree Update for Point Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05092)
*Jaemin Yu,Seongyoon Jeong,Kang-Wook Chon,Duksu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Ego-Centric Octree (ECO)，一种面向移动机器人的3D空间数据结构，通过动态滑动窗口机制将建图空间限制在机器人周围，采用增量更新算法消除冗余全局坐标变换。在KITTI数据集上，ECO相比完全静态重建平均减少24.87%的更新耗时，相比有界增量基线平均减少54.60%的更新耗时，并在下游体素地图生成中降低最高34.17%的系统延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理连续点流的移动机器人八叉树实时构建面临高延迟和树生长不平衡的计算与内存挑战，标准全局结构难以满足实时性需求。

Method: 提出Ego-Centric Octree (ECO)，一种以自我为中心的3D滑动窗口数据结构，通过将建图空间动态限制在机器人即时周围环境，并采用高效的增量更新算法将环境划分为移出、移入和重叠区域，消除冗余的全局坐标变换。

Result: 在KITTI基准上，ECO更新耗时相比全静态重建减少最高25.60%（平均24.87%），相比有界增量基线减少最高67.52%（平均54.60%）；下游体素地图生成任务延迟降低最高34.17%。

Conclusion: ECO通过动态滑动窗口和增量更新机制，有效降低了八叉树更新的计算开销和系统延迟，同时保持树的平衡性，并自然保留运动物体的短期时间记忆，适用于移动机器人的实时空间感知。

Abstract: Constructing octrees for mobile robots that process continuous point streams in real time poses significant computational and memory challenges. Standard global structures often suffer from high latency and unbalanced tree growth. We introduce the Ego-Centric Octree (ECO), a spatial data structure that acts as a 3D sliding window, dynamically bounding the mapping space to the robot's immediate surroundings. ECO uses an efficient incremental update algorithm that categorizes the environment into shift-out, shift-in, and overlap regions, eliminating redundant global coordinate transformations. Evaluations on the KITTI benchmark demonstrate that ECO reduces update times by up to 25.60% (24.87% on average) compared to full static reconstruction and by up to 67.52% (54.60% on average) compared to a bounded incremental baseline. Furthermore, ECO substantially lowers the total system latency of downstream tasks, running up to 34.17% faster than full reconstruction in voxel-map generation. In dynamic scenes, ECO naturally retains a short-term temporal memory of moving objects, providing useful temporal context while keeping update cost bounded and the tree balanced for real-time spatial perception.

</details>


### [259] [Geometry-Aware Visual Odometry for Bronchoscopic Navigation via High-Gain Observer Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05162)
*Mohammadreza Kasaei,Francis Xiatian Zhang,Feng Li,Farshid Alambeigi,Kevin Dhaliwal,Mohsen Khadem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种几何感知的视觉里程计框架，利用气道 lumen 的消失点线索实现支气管镜导航，在离体人肺实验中轨迹误差降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航支气管镜平台依赖术前CT或外部传感器，限制了在重症监护和资源受限环境中的应用；纯视觉导航虽具可扩展性，但传统VO在纹理贫乏、镜面反射和管状结构消失点奇异性下频繁失效。

Method: 检测气道 lumen 并反投影为3D射线，通过加权融合获得稳定的前进方向；结合 looming 速度估计，利用定制的高增益观测器将转向信息与噪声VO输出融合，强制气道跟随先验并抑制漂移。

Result: 在机械通气的离体人肺上，与ORB-SLAM2、LoFTR-VO、DPVO相比，绝对轨迹误差降低超过50%，所有测试序列的相对位姿误差均为最低。

Conclusion: 几何感知的VO框架有效解决了气道纹理贫乏和消失点奇异带来的跟踪失效与漂移问题，为无外部传感器的支气管镜导航提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Navigational bronchoscopy is critical for pulmonary interventions, yet current platforms depend heavily on pre-operative CT or external sensors, limiting their use in critical care and resource-constrained settings. Vision-only navigation offers a scalable alternative, but conventional visual odometry (VO) struggles with texture-poor airway images, specularities, and the vanishing-point singularities of tubular anatomy, leading to frequent tracking failures and drift. We present a geometry-aware VO framework that explicitly leverages vanishing-point cues from airway lumens. Detected lumens are back-projected to 3D rays, whose weighted fusion yields a stable forward heading even when parallax cues are absent. This heading, together with looming-based velocity estimates, is fused with noisy VO outputs using a bespoke high-gain observer that enforces airway-following priors and rejects drift. We validate the method on ex-vivo mechanically ventilated human lungs with electromagnetic tracking ground truth. Compared to state-of-the-art pipelines (ORB-SLAM2, LoFTR-VO, DPVO), our approach reduces absolute trajectory error by more than 50% and achieves the lowest relative pose error across all test sequences.

</details>


### [260] [VLM-CASE: Vision-Language Model Enabled Context-Adaptive Safety Envelopes for Anticipatory Safe Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05180)
*Tianjia Yang,Ke Li,Ruwen Qin,Xianbiao Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-CASE框架，利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）感知路面和能见度条件，动态调整基于物理极限和责任敏感安全（RSS）的上下文自适应安全包络（CASE），再由模型预测控制器（MPC）在该包络内自由驾驶。该框架在CARLA仿真中完成所有测试任务，优于传统MPC和先进VLM集成控制器基线，且控制器无关性好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣驾驶条件同时降低车辆的感知能力和物理操控能力，人类驾驶员通过预期性推理（提前调整速度、跟车距离和转向）来应对，而现有自动驾驶系统只能在事后被动反应。本文旨在赋予自动驾驶车辆这种预期能力，同时保持运动始终受形式化安全模型约束。

Method: 1) 使用低秩适配（LoRA）微调的视觉-语言模型（VLM）从前置摄像头图像推理路面条件和能见度；2) 基于物理极限和责任敏感安全（RSS）推导上下文自适应安全包络（CASE），通过共享摩擦预算耦合制动和转向；3) 模型预测控制器（MPC）在包络内自由驾驶，VLM异步运行不影响实时控制循环。

Result: VLM-CASE-MPC在CARLA闭环仿真中完成所有横纵向控制任务，超越传统MPC基线和最先进的VLM集成控制器。消融实验证实性能提升来自上下文自适应，摩擦和能见度自适应具有互补性。

Conclusion: VLM-CASE框架是控制器无关的，可与几乎任何底层控制器配对，为安全自动驾驶提供了有前景的方向。数据集和补充材料已开源。

Abstract: Adverse driving conditions, such as bad weather, remain a principal barrier to autonomous driving because they degrade two things at once: what the vehicle can perceive and what it can physically do. Human drivers cope by anticipation, reasoning about the scene and re-budgeting speed, following distance, and steering before grip or sight is lost, whereas current autonomous driving systems at best react after the fact. This paper proposes VLM-CASE, a framework that gives an autonomous vehicle this anticipatory capacity while keeping its motion bounded by a formal safety model at all times. A vision-language model (VLM), fine-tuned with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), reasons about the scene from the front-camera image and reports the road surface and visibility conditions. This output parametrizes a context-adaptive safety envelope (CASE), derived from physical limits and the guarantees of responsibility-sensitive safety, that couples braking and steering through a shared friction budget. A model predictive controller then drives freely within the envelope, while the VLM runs asynchronously so it never blocks the real-time control loop. We validate the framework in closed-loop CARLA simulation on tasks that demand both lateral and longitudinal control, across a range of weather, road-surface, and lighting conditions. The resulting controller, VLM-CASE-MPC, completes all trials, outperforming a conventional MPC baseline and a state-of-the-art VLM-integrated controller. Ablations confirm that the gains come from context adaptation, with the friction and visibility adaptations proving complementary. Furthermore, the framework is controller-agnostic and pairs with almost any low-level controller, offering a promising direction for safe autonomous driving. The dataset and supplementary materials for VLM-CASE are available at https://github.com/ytj254/VLM-CASE.

</details>


### [261] [GelNeuro: A Sensing-Computing Integrated Neuromorphic Tactile System for Texture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05241)
*Luoyang Bian,Xinpan Meng,Zhenghua Ma,Houcheng Li,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GelNeuro，一种将GelSight Mini光学触觉前端与Speck2f神经形态SoC直接集成的全集成感算一体触觉系统，实现无需主机的低功耗触觉识别，在15类纹理识别任务上达到96.3%准确率，功耗仅19.6 mW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经形态视触觉系统仍需依赖上位机进行事件读取、预处理或中继转发，未能实现真正的传感器到芯片的端到端推理集成，限制了低延迟和低功耗优势的充分发挥。

Method: 将GelSight Mini光学触觉前端与Speck2f神经形态SoC直接配对，接触引起的标记运动被DVS事件捕获后通过片上网络路由至脉冲卷积神经网络(SCNN)分类器；针对8位部署中的精度下降问题，提出硬件感知的权值钳制策略。

Result: 在15类自然纹理识别任务上，硬件在环测试达到96.3%准确率（80 ms推理窗口），板级有功功耗仅19.6 mW，比传统CPU/GPU基线低三个数量级，且对未见接触深度具有鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: GelNeuro证明了在边缘神经形态硬件上实现直接传感器到芯片触觉识别的可行性，为低延迟、极低功耗的机器人触觉感知提供了一种全新范式。

Abstract: Neuromorphic visuo-tactile sensing offers a promising paradigm for low-latency and low-power robotic perception. However, existing systems still rely heavily on a host computer for event readout, preprocessing, or relaying prior to chip inference. This paper presents GelNeuro, a fully integrated sensing-computing visuo-tactile system that directly pairs a GelSight Mini-based optical tactile front end with the Speck2f neuromorphic system-on-chip (SoC). Contact-induced marker motions are captured as dynamic vision sensor (DVS) events and routed through the on-chip network to a spiking convolutional neural network (SCNN) classifier. To mitigate accuracy degradation during 8-bit deployment, a hardware-aware weight clamping strategy is introduced. Evaluated on a 15-class natural texture recognition task, hardware-in-the-loop testing on the physical chip achieves a 96.3% accuracy within an 80 ms inference window. Notably, the system consumes only 19.6 mW of board-level active power-over three orders of magnitude lower than conventional CPU/GPU baselines on the same benchmark. GelNeuro also exhibits robust generalization across unseen contact depths, demonstrating the viability of direct sensor-to-chip tactile recognition on edge neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [262] [Socially-Aware Autonomous Doorway Traversal and Payload Delivery for Emergency Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05315)
*Andrew Snowdy,Ananya Trivedi,Sarvesh Prajapati,Lorena Maria Genua,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为树的机器人辅助紧急疏散框架，能够在疏散过程中自动执行开门和运送救援设备等任务，同时保持对周围人员的感知与适应性。在丰田HSR上进行了105次试验，成功完成97次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助紧急疏散场景中，机器人需要同时处理开门、运送救援设备、感知周围人群并调整行为等多项任务，现有方法难以有效整合这些能力，亟需一个可扩展、可触发高层的框架。

Method: 以行为树（Behavior Tree）为核心框架，根据环境触发条件选择高层任务（如按门按钮、让行、穿门、先取设备再过门等），并在丰田Human Support Robot上部署验证。

Result: 在105次试验（含5种硬件场景和3种仿真场景）中，系统成功完成97次，验证了其在紧急疏散场景中决策的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该行为树框架为机器人辅助紧急响应任务提供了一个实用的基础，具备可扩展性，可用于更广泛的应急响应场景。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on the scenario of a robot-assisted emergency evacuation. We consider two capabilities relevant to such a setting. The first is opening doors ahead of the people being evacuated, so that their path toward an exit stays clear. The second is retrieving rescue equipment and delivering it to the emergency responders carrying out the evacuation. From a systems perspective, this involves several tasks at once. The robot must locate ADA-compliant door buttons and the rescue equipment it needs to retrieve. Additionally, it must remain aware of the people around it and adapt its behavior to them, so that it supports the evacuation rather than getting in the way. We address these demands with a behavior tree at the core of our framework. This structure is chosen for its ability to select high-level tasks based on environmental triggers, and to extend to new situations as they arise. We evaluate the system in 105 trials on the Toyota Human Support Robot, across five hardware and three simulation scenarios. These trials capture the decisions the robot must make in this setting: whether to press a door button, yield to a nearby person, walk through a door someone else is holding, or first retrieve rescue equipment before traversing the door. Overall, the system completes 97 of the 105 trials successfully. These results suggest our framework provides a practical basis for robotic assistance in broader emergency response tasks. Code and video demonstrations are available at https://github.com/AndrewSnowdy/hsr_mm_control.

</details>


### [263] [GaP: A Graph-as-Policy Multi-Agent Self-Learning Harness For Variational Automation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05369)
*Kaiyuan Chen,Shuangyu Xie,Letian Fu,Justin Yu,William Pacini,Sandeep Bajamahal,Hudson Kim,Jaimyn Drake,Daehwa Kim,Haoru Xue,Jonathan Francis,Christian Juette,Peter Schaldenbrand,Muhammet Yunus Seker,Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Uksang Yoo,Guanzhi Wang,Adithyavairavan Murali,Balakumar Sundaralingam,S. Shankar Sastry,Spencer Huang,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-as-Policy (GaP)，一种多智能体编码框架，结合可解释的机器人编程与模型无关策略的开放世界适应性，通过生成有向计算图并利用内部仿真迭代优化，在变分自动化任务上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对变分自动化任务中物体几何与姿态变化较大，模型无关策略难以实现商业和工业应用所需的可靠性与持续性闭环，作者希望结合可解释的机器人编程与开放世界适应性来缩小可靠性差距。

Method: 提出Graph-as-Policy (GaP)，一个多智能体编码框架，从模块化开放机器人技能库（MORSL）生成包含感知、规划和控制节点的有向计算图，并构建内部仿真环境并行排练不同图结构的任务实例，迭代优化图结构与参数以提升成功率和吞吐量。

Result: 在8个新的开放变分自动化任务基准（4个仿真+4个真实世界）上，GaP的成功率显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: GaP通过将可解释的任务与运动规划思想与多智能体编码结合，有效解决了变分自动化任务的可靠性问题，为商业和工业应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: For robots to work reliably in commercial and industrial applications, can recent advances in agentic coding systems combine interpretable robot programming with the open-world adaptability of model-free policies? We focus on "Variational Automation" (VA), a class of tasks that have larger variations in object geometry and pose than fixed automation. Model-free policies often struggle to close the reliability gap for VA tasks, which must be executed persistently and reliably in commercial and industrial applications. Motivated by prior work on Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) and the Robot Operating System (ROS), we introduce Graph-as-Policy (GaP), a multi-agent coding harness that generates directed computation graphs with perception, planning, and control nodes from a Modular Open Robot Skill Library (MORSL). GaP then generates an internal simulation environment to rehearse task instances with different graphs in parallel to iteratively refine the graph structure and parameters to improve success rates and throughput. Evaluation with 8 new open VA task benchmarks, 4 in-simulation and 4 in real-world, suggests that GaP can achieve success rates that significantly outperform baselines. Details, code, and data can be found online: https://graph-robots.github.io/gap

</details>


### [264] [Cortex: A Bidirectionally Aligned Embodied Agent Framework for Long-horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05377)
*Jiaqi Peng,Xiqian Yu,Delin Feng,Yuqiang Yang,Wenzhe Cai,Jing Xiong,Ganlin Yang,Jinliang Zheng,Jiafei Cao,Xueyuan Wei,Jiangmiao Pang,Yuan Shen,Tai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cortex是一个双向对齐的具身智能体框架，通过标准化32个规范技能基元和注入可处理性原则，弥合了高层规划语义与低层执行运动学之间的鸿沟，在长程任务中显著优于传统方法，并实现零样本完成未见过的真实世界长程任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型因马尔可夫性质（仅依赖当前观测）难以处理长程任务，而层次化双系统方法存在高层规划语义与低层执行运动学之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 将操作子任务标准化为32个规范技能基元；2) 在数据生成管线中注入可处理性原则（代表性对象属性、改进轨迹可达性）；3) 自动标注4000+小时开源视频数据并生成30小时仿真数据；4) 设计事件平衡采样策略构建微调数据；5) 推理阶段通过任务上下文到技能约束的harness工程处理规划歧义。

Result: 在Libero-long上超越单块基线3.1%，在RoboTwin上超越4.1%；通过通用VLM与微调VLA结合，实现零样本完成未见过的真实世界长程任务（如多阶段化学实验）。

Conclusion: Cortex通过双向对齐的框架设计有效弥合了高层规划与低层执行之间的鸿沟，是VLM与VLA协同工作的有效范式，在长程操作任务中展现出强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: While recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show promise toward generalist manipulation policies, they struggle with long-horizon tasks due to their Markovian nature-relying solely on current observations. Hierarchical dual-system methods address this but suffer from a gap between high-level planning semantics and low-level execution kinematics. We introduce Cortex, a bidirectionally aligned embodied agent framework with a customized planning interface that conveys executable and tractable subtask plans from high-level VLM to low-level VLA. Specifically, we standardize manipulation subtasks into 32 canonical skill primitives and inject tractability principles, such as representative object attributes and improved trajectory reachability, into the data generation pipeline. This enables automatic annotation of over 4k hours of open-source video data and generation of 30 hours of simulation data. We further devise an event-balanced sampling strategy to construct training data for fine-tuning the framework to better handle planning ambiguity during subtask transitions, enhanced by carefully designed harness engineering from task contexts to skill constraints during inference. Both open-loop VLM and closed-loop system evaluations demonstrate Cortex's efficacy, e.g., it outperforms monolithic baselines by 3.1% on Libero-long and 4.1% on RoboTwin. Notably, Cortex's generalist VLM enables zero-shot completion of unseen real-world long-horizon tasks, such as multi-stage chemistry experiments, by simply combining with a fine-tuned VLA-a capability infeasible through VLA fine-tuning alone.

</details>


### [265] [Deform360: A Massive Multi-view Visuotactile Dataset for Deformable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05390)
*Hongyu Li,Wanjia Fu,Xiaoyan Cong,Zekun Li,Binghao Huang,Hanxiao Jiang,Xintong He,Yiqing Liang,Rao Fu,Tao Lu,Srinath Sridhar,Kevin A. Smith,George Konidaris,Yunzhu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Deform360，一个大规模视觉触觉数据集，包含198个日常物体、1,980个交互序列和超过215小时的观测数据，用于评估和对比2D像素空间与3D几何空间中的可变形物体世界模型。通过系统评估揭示了结构先验与可扩展性之间的权衡，并为可变形物体世界建模提供了基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
