Motivation: 预测物体动力学（世界建模）是机器人操作的基本挑战，尤其是可变形物体因其高维状态空间和复杂材料属性而特别困难。当前世界模型存在2D像素空间和3D几何空间两种范式，但由于缺乏多样化、大规模的真实世界数据，它们的相对优势和局限性尚不清楚。

Method: 构建了Deform360大规模视觉触觉数据集，包含198个日常物体、1,980个交互序列、超过215小时从41个环绕摄像头和双手触觉抓取器的观测数据。采用新颖的无标记视觉触觉3D跟踪管道提取密集几何和运动信息，系统评估了当前最先进的2D视频模型与3D粒子模型。

Result: 揭示了2D视频模型与3D粒子模型在结构先验和可扩展性之间的权衡，并初步展示了该数据集在真实机器人规划任务中的适用性。

Conclusion: Deform360为可变形物体中心的世界建模提供了可靠的基准，有助于未来研究在通用可变形物体世界建模方面的发展。

Abstract: Predicting object dynamics (i.e., world modeling) is a fundamental challenge for robotic manipulation, and modeling deformable objects presents a particularly difficult case due to their high-dimensional state spaces and complex material properties. While current world models approach this through two distinct paradigms: learning the dynamics over the 2D pixel space or more explicit 3D geometric space. A systematic understanding of their relative strengths and limitations remains elusive due to the lack of diverse, large-scale real-world data. To address this, we present Deform360, a large-scale visuotactile dataset featuring 198 daily-life objects, 1,980 interaction sequences, and over 215 hours of observations from 41 surround-view cameras and bimanual tactile grippers to capture both global motion and contact-induced local deformations. Leveraging a novel markerless visuotactile 3D tracking pipeline to extract dense geometry and motion, we systematically evaluate current state-of-the-art world models, comparing 2D video models against 3D particle models. Finally, we provide a preliminary demonstration indicating the real-world applicability of our dataset by performing robot planning tasks on deformable objects. Our analysis reveals key insights into the trade-offs between structural priors and scalability, providing a solid benchmark for future research in generalizable deformable object-centric world modeling. Project website: https://deform360.lhy.xyz

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [Green Haven or Risky Venture? Exploring the Connectedness and Hedging of Sustainable Cryptocurrencies and Green Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02852)
*Chang Li,Rui Jiang,Hao Wu,Conghua Wen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究利用TVP-VAR和多元GARCH模型，考察可持续加密货币与绿色金融市场之间的时变联动性及对冲表现，发现两者联动性低、短期主导中长期，且可持续加密货币对冲效率优于传统加密货币。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统加密货币能耗高、碳排放量大，而可持续加密货币作为绿色替代品在投资组合管理中有潜力，但学界对其与绿色金融市场的联动关系及对冲表现尚不明确。

Method: 采用傅里叶变换的TVP-VAR模型和多元GARCH模型，分析COVID-19和俄乌战争冲击下的时变联动性与对冲表现。

Result: 可持续加密货币与绿色金融市场之间成对联动性处于低水平，短期联动性主导中长期；可持续加密货币的对冲有效性高于传统加密货币。

Conclusion: 可持续加密货币能为投资组合提供多元化收益，在极端市场冲击下是对冲风险的有效绿色替代工具。

Abstract: Conventional cryptocurrency often leads to increased energy consumption and carbon emissions, while sustainable cryptocurrencies possess the potential to become a green alternative in portfolio management. This study aims to investigate the time-varying connectedness between sustainable cryptocurrency and green financial markets as well as hedging performance when facing market shocks, including COVID-19 and Russia-Ukraine war. TVP-VAR model with Fourier transform and Multivariate GARCH models are employed. The findings indicate that the pairwise connectedness between the sustainable cryptocurrencies and green financial markets has been at a low level, providing diversification benefits in investment portfolio. Besides, short-term connectedness dominates medium- and long-term connectedness. Sustainable cryptocurrencies show higher hedging effectiveness than traditional cryptocurrency.

</details>


### [267] [Efficient Frontier Optimization of NBA Shot Selection Using Markov Reward Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02933)
*Nirek Duma,Leo Benaharon*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文运用马尔可夫奖励过程结合均值-方差投资组合理论与防守响应成本，将NBA投篮选择建模为动态投资组合问题。研究发现联盟整体接近效率前沿，但三分革命已使外线出手量略微超越最优边界，减少中远距离出手、增加篮下攻击可带来边际改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NBA投篮分析将每次出手视为独立事件，忽略了投篮选择之间的动态关联、方差效应和防守响应成本。本文旨在将投篮选择重新定义为动态投资组合问题，以更准确地评估真实最优投篮策略。

Method: 构建回合层面的马尔可夫奖励过程，将投篮分为篮下、中距离、底角三分和非底角四类；引入均值-方差投篮分配目标函数和防守响应成本；分离快攻与非快攻样本，先优化非快攻部分，再以固定转移项加入快攻数据。

Result: 联盟整体投篮选择接近效率前沿，但适度减少外线和两分中距离出手、增加篮下攻击可带来边际改善。仅优化期望值会高估外线投篮的收益。

Conclusion: 三分革命已使外线出手量略微超出最优边界。考虑方差和延续效应后，仅以期望值优化会系统性高估外线投篮的真实价值，NBA投篮选择存在可优化的空间。

Abstract: This paper asks whether modern NBA shot selection can be evaluated as a dynamic portfolio problem rather than as a collection of isolated shot attempts. We combine a possession-level Markov reward process with both a mean-variance shot allocation objective and a defense response cost. The model separates rim attempts, midrange attempts, corner threes, and noncorner threes, then values each candidate shot diet by immediate scoring, continuation after offensive rebounds, empirical game-level variance, and a timeout-response stability cost. Because fastbreak value depends on geometry that is not fully observed in standard play-by-play, we optimize the no-fastbreak sample first and then add back observed fastbreak shot selection as a fixed transition component. Our results indicate that the league aggregate operates near the efficient frontier, with marginal improvements to be made by slightly decreasing perimeter volume and mid range attempts while increasing rim attempts. Team-level analyses reflect this general trend. Ultimately, these findings suggest the three-point revolution has pushed volume slightly past its optimal boundary. By incorporating outcome variance and continuation effects, we show that optimizing solely for expected value mathematically overstates the benefit of perimeter shots.

</details>


### [268] [A zero-inflated mixed-effects spatial point process for grouped storm loss data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03852)
*Lisa Gao,Sébastien Jessup,Tianxing Yan*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于零膨胀混合效应空间点过程框架的模型，用于处理不平衡、多变量的零膨胀计数数据，该模型能够整合高空间粒度的天气和暴露预测因子来预测保险索赔模式，并考虑了同一风暴影响下不同地点在多余零值和联合索赔计数上的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险公司获取的第三方天气和暴露数据日益精细化，但损失结果通常以空间分组（如县级）形式报告，导致高分辨率预测因子需被聚合以匹配结果粒度，这掩盖了局部异质性。因此，需要一种能够利用高粒度预测因子同时正确建模空间依赖关系的模型。

Method: 假设来自同一风暴的索赔服从零膨胀混合效应空间点过程框架，推导出一个适用于不平衡、多变量零膨胀计数数据的模型。该模型在多余零值和联合索赔计数中均考虑了风暴影响下不同位置间的依赖性，并融入了更高空间粒度的天气和暴露预测因子。

Result: 使用真实的财产暴露和损失数据验证了模型的有效性，结果表明纳入高粒度预测因子能够有效捕捉风暴损失的局部异质性。

Conclusion: 该模型能够更准确地预测天气相关的保险损失，为保险公司在精细化风险管理中利用高分辨率外部数据提供了方法论支持。

Abstract: The increasing granularity of third-party weather and exposure information can allow insurers to more effectively predict weather-related losses. However, loss outcomes are often reported in spatially grouped observations, such as at the county level, so higher resolution predictors are aggregated to align with the granularity of the outcome in standard analyses. Assuming an underlying zero-inflated mixed-effects spatial point process framework for claims arising from a common storm, we derive a model for unbalanced, multivariate zero-inflated count data that incorporates rich weather and exposure predictors observed at higher spatial granularity to predict claim patterns. The model accommodates the dependence between locations affected by a common storm in the excess zeros, as well as in the joint claim counts. Using real property exposure and loss data, we emphasize the value of incorporating granular predictors to address the localized heterogeneity of storm losses.

</details>


### [269] [Climate sensitivity analysis -- A case study from forty years of US compositional cause of death data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04198)
*Michelle Dong,Aaron Bruhn,Francis Hui,Han Lin Shang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文运用成分数据分析（CODA）结合主成分分析和广义加性模型，研究温度和海平面极端变化对美国不同亚群、不同原因死亡率的影响。结果表明，气候因素对不同死因和年龄组的影响存在差异，高血压心脏病死亡比例随温度和海平面极端升高而显著增加；55-95岁人群受影响更大；对寿险公司而言，气候变化加剧了年金与保障产品之间的自然对冲效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解特定气候因素（温度和海平面极端变化）对美国人口各亚群、各死因死亡率的影响，通过敏感性分析和情景分析量化气候风险。

Method: 采用成分数据分析（CODA）处理死亡密度数据（非负且和为1的依赖变量），结合主成分分析对气候因素降维，并拟合广义加性模型（GAM）以捕捉降维后主成分得分与死亡原因之间的非线性关系。

Result: 气候因素对不同死因和年龄组的影响存在差异，高血压心脏病死亡比例随温度和海平面极端升高而显著增加。情景分析显示，高温极端、海平面和降雨增加与低温极端减少共同导致气候相关死因间存在抵消效应，但年龄组内抵消较弱。55-95岁人群死亡比例变化更显著。

Conclusion: 气候相关风险对高龄脆弱群体的影响更大；对寿险公司而言，气候变化加剧了年金与保障产品之间的自然对冲效应。

Abstract: Emerging climate risks pose pressing challenges for insurers, governments, and businesses worldwide, as they face growing uncertainty in quantifying the impacts of climate change from physical risks. This paper aims to understand the impact of specific climate factors on mortality by cause for subgroups of the United States (US) population, through sensitivity and scenario analysis based on an increase in temperature and sea level extremes. We apply compositional data analysis (CODA) techniques to examine cause-specific deaths, treating the density of deaths as a set of dependent, non-negative values that sum to one. We couple CODA with principal component analysis on the climate factors as a means of dimension reduction, and fit generalised additive models to better reflect the non-linear relationships between the dimension-reduced principal scores and mortality by cause. The results of our analysis indicate climate-related factors have varying impacts by cause and ages within each cause, with more pronounced increases in the proportions of deaths from hypertensive heart disease as temperature and sea level extremes increase. Scenario analysis also indicates that an increase in temperature high extremes, sea level, and rainfall, in conjunction with a decrease in low temperature extremes, lead to offsetting impacts on the proportion of deaths between climate-related causes, but less offsetting by age within causes. Furthermore, the impacts on the proportions of death are more pronounced for ages between 55 and 95, reinforcing the observation that climate-related risks have a greater impact on older (and potentially more vulnerable) subgroups of the population. For life insurers specifically, these results are consistent with the natural hedge that arises between annuity and protection products, in light of increasing climate risk.

</details>


### [270] [A Bayesian predictive framework for adaptive interim-analysis timing with robust borrowing in confirmatory trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04205)
*Meihua Long,Tianyu Zheng,Jiali Song,Leen Huang,Cong Zhang,Qimeng Che,Yan Hou*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出B²-FIC框架，一种基于贝叶斯信息借用和预测概率的自适应中期分析时机调整方法，用于确证性III期试验中利用同疗法II期数据进行信息借用，同时控制整体I类错误率，并通过模拟和肿瘤学案例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确证性III期试验需要严格证据，但针对首创疗法（FIC）在同类机制证据稀缺时，常需依赖同疗法的II期数据作为先验信息；同时计划性中期分析需要调整以控制I类错误率，且信息借用改变了中期分析时的证据基础，因此需要前瞻性地调整中期分析时机。

Method: 提出B²-FIC框架，包含：(1) 基于贝叶斯信息借用的借用模型，针对II期-III期不一致场景校准以控制整体I类错误率；(2) 在第一次中期分析（IA1）时，校准模型结合II期数据与累积的III期数据更新后验；(3) 利用后验的贝叶斯预测概率选择满足疗效标准的第二次中期分析（IA2）的最早信息分数。

Result: 模拟结果表明B²-FIC维持了经验I类错误率，并在不同场景下提高了中期分析效能；基于II期和III期IA1数据的预测概率能够在证据有利时选择比传统组序贯设计更早的IA2时间点；两项肿瘤学案例研究验证了框架的可行性。

Conclusion: B²-FIC提供了一个校准框架，能够根据借用调整后的证据自适应调整中期分析时机，解决了确证性试验中新兴的设计问题。

Abstract: Confirmatory phase III trials require rigorous evidence, yet for first-in-class (FIC) therapies they must often be designed when same-mechanism evidence is scarce. This uncertainty motivates planned interim analyses and makes phase II data from the same therapy a relevant source of prior evidence. However, both borrowing and repeated interim analyses must be calibrated to control the overall type I error rate. Because borrowing changes the evidence available at interim analyses relative to a non-borrowing group sequential design (GSD), it also raises the question of whether interim analysis timing should be prospectively adapted to the borrowing-adjusted evidence base. We propose a prespecified adaptive interim-timing framework based on Bayesian information borrowing and Bayesian predictive probability $B^2$-FIC. The borrowing model is calibrated against phase II--phase III discrepancy scenarios to control overall type I error rate. At the first interim analysis (IA1), the calibrated model combines phase II information with accumulating phase III data to update the posterior. Bayesian predictive probabilities from this posterior select the earliest information fraction for the second interim analysis (IA2) that meets the efficacy criterion. In simulations, $B^2$-FIC maintained empirical type I error and improved interim power across different scenarios. Predictive probabilities derived from phase II and phase III IA1 data selected earlier IA2 than GSD when evidence was favorable. Two oncology case studies illustrate the framework. Overall, $B^2$-FIC provides a calibrated framework for adapting interim timing to borrowing-adjusted evidence, an emerging design problem in confirmatory trials.

</details>


### [271] [Enhancing comorbidity network inference with risk-enriched health trajectories embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04702)
*Nicole Fontana,Alessia Mapelli,Emanuele Di Angelantonio,Francesca Ieva*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用个体健康轨迹推断疾病网络的方法学框架，通过高斯图模型和Lasso正则化实现稀疏网络估计，并在UK Biobank数据上验证了其有效性，识别出有临床意义的心血管代谢疾病模式和四种进展表型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共病网络构建方法存在三个关键局限：忽略时间信息（依赖横截面统计）、忽视共享风险因素导致的混杂偏倚、无法区分直接与间接疾病关联从而产生全连接网络。为克服这些局限，需要开发能利用纵向健康轨迹、控制混杂因素并估计稀疏网络的新方法。

Method: 1) 利用个体健康轨迹学习疾病关联，捕获语义相似性和时间共现；2) 通过高斯图模型（GGM）结合Lasso正则化实现稀疏网络估计；3) 引入混杂评估步骤，利用共享风险因素的先验临床知识指导网络估计；4) 在UK Biobank数据（24种心血管代谢疾病+76种风险因素）上应用；5) 基于检测到的疾病社区结构构建患者表征，进行聚类分析识别进展表型。

Result: 网络结构揭示了临床有意义的疾病模式，拓扑分析识别出关键的病理枢纽和潜在可干预靶点，发现了四个与心血管代谢分类学一致的疾病社区。基于社区的患者表征聚类识别出四种具有显著不同长期生存轨迹的进展表型。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效克服现有共病网络方法的局限性，生成的网络具有临床可解释性，并为风险分层和个性化医疗提供了潜在工具。

Abstract: Multimorbidity poses a growing challenge for individual health, reducing quality of life and increasing treatment burden, resulting in a multiplicative impact on healthcare system management and fragmented care trajectories. Comorbidity networks could provide crucial insight into characterising multimorbidity and disease relationships. However, existing approaches to comorbidity network construction face critical limitations: they overlook temporal information by relying on cross-sectional statistics, produce biased association estimates by ignoring confounding due to shared risk factors, and fail to distinguish between direct and indirect disease associations, thereby yielding fully connected networks. To address these limitations, we develop a methodological framework for population-level disease network inference that uses individual health trajectories to learn disease associations, capturing semantic similarity and temporal co-occurrence. Sparse network estimation is achieved via Gaussian Graphical Models with Lasso regularisation, informed by prior clinical knowledge on shared risk factors derived from a dedicated confounding evaluation step. Applied to UK Biobank data comprising 24 cardiometabolic diseases and 76 risk factors, the resulting network revealed clinically meaningful disease patterns. Topological analysis identifies key pathological hubs, reveals potential actionable targets for multimorbidity management, and identifies four distinct disease communities that align with the established cardiometabolic taxonomy. Building on this community structure, we derive community-based patient representations that capture disease progression dynamics. Clustering these representations reveals four progression phenotypes with significantly different long-term survival trajectories, highlighting the potential of the framework for risk stratification and personalised care.

</details>


### [272] [Impact of interim analyses on Bayesian and frequentist operating characteristics of Bayesian clinical trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04976)
*Clément R Massonnaud,Lucas Manns,Cédric Laouénan,André Gillibert*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟研究证明，贝叶斯临床试验与频率学派试验一样，会受到重复中期分析的影响，且贝叶斯操作特征对先验差异敏感，无法像控制频率学派特征那样得到保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清关于贝叶斯设计是否受重复中期分析影响的普遍困惑，系统评估重复分析对贝叶斯和频率学派操作特征的具体影响。

Method: 采用正态分布结局的模拟研究，考察具有重复分析、含或不含无效停止规则及多重性调整的组序贯试验设计，同时评估频率学派（I类错误率）和贝叶斯（错误结论风险、疗效结论的信息价值）操作特征。

Result: 重复中期分析同时改变频率学派和贝叶斯操作特征；未调整多重性时I类错误率随分析次数增加；贝叶斯操作特征对先验分歧敏感且敏感性随分析次数增加；即使在完全先验一致下，疗效结论的信息价值也会降低。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯临床试验与频率学派试验一样受重复分析影响，贝叶斯推断并不免疫于多重性问题；可控制频率学派特征但无法保证控制贝叶斯操作特征，因其依赖于不同利益相关者可能采用的不同先验。

Abstract: While Bayesian methods are increasingly used in clinical research, confusion persists as to whether Bayesian designs are affected by repeated interim analyses, and how such effects should be evaluated. We aimed to clarify this question by evaluating both frequentist and Bayesian operating characteristics of group-sequential trials. We conducted simulation studies with normally distributed outcomes, examining designs with repeated analyses, with and without futility stopping rules and multiplicity adjustments. We show that repeated interim analyses alter both frequentist and Bayesian operating characteristics in group-sequential trials, regardless of the inferential framework adopted. Without proper adjustment for multiplicity, the Type I error rate increases with the number of analyses. Bayesian operating characteristics such as the risk of erroneous conclusions and the informative value of an efficacy conclusion are meaningful alternatives to classical frequentist metrics, but they are sensitive to prior divergence between stakeholders, and this sensitivity grows with the number of analyses. Even under full prior agreement, the informative value of an efficacy conclusion is reduced. While it is possible to control frequentist operating characteristics of Bayesian trials with appropriate methods, it is not possible to guarantee such control for Bayesian operating characteristics because they are prior-dependent and different stakeholders may adopt different priors. Contrary to claims that Bayesian inference is immune to multiplicity, our results show that Bayesian clinical trials are no less affected by repeated analyses than frequentist ones, regardless of the framework used to evaluate them.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [Recovering Kodaira types from $\ell$-torsion on elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02678)
*Naina Praveen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过在E[ℓ]上引入一个记录点x坐标之间p-adic距离的函数，弥补了经典Néron-Ogg-Shafarevich准则在区分潜在好约化与乘性约化以及确定Kodaira类型方面的不足，证明了带有这种额外结构的E[ℓ]可以唯一确定椭圆曲线的Kodaira类型（在剩余特征为2时排除了I_n^*型潜在好约化的情况）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Néron-Ogg-Shafarevich准则虽然能通过惯性作用在ℓ-adic Tate模上的行动来刻画椭圆曲线的好约化，但惯性在E[ℓ]上的作用不足以区分潜在好约化和乘性约化，且在T_ℓ(E)上的作用也不足以确定Kodaira类型。因此需要引入新的结构来弥补这一不足。

Method: 在E[ℓ]上引入一个距离函数，记录各点x坐标之间的p-adic距离，以此作为额外的结构信息。

Result: 带有该距离函数的E[ℓ]能够唯一确定椭圆曲线的Kodaira类型。在剩余特征为2的情况下，排除了I_n^*型潜在好约化的情形。

Conclusion: 通过给E[ℓ]配备p-adic距离函数这一额外结构，可以增强经典准则的判别能力，使其能够区分潜在好约化与乘性约化，并完整确定Kodaira类型。

Abstract: The classical Néron-Ogg-Shafarevich criterion characterises good reduction of an elliptic curve $E$ over a $p$-adic field via the action of inertia on the $\ell$-adic Tate module. However, the action of inertia on $E[\ell]$ is not sufficient to distinguish between potentially good and multiplicative reduction, and the action on $T_{\ell}(E)$ is not sufficient to determine the Kodaira type. We remedy this situation by endowing $E[\ell]$ with a distance function that records the $p$-adic distances between the $x$-coordinates of the points. We show that, equipped with this additional structure, $E[\ell]$ determines the Kodaira type of the elliptic curve. In the case of residue characteristic $2$, we assume that $E$ does not have potentially good reduction of type $I_n^*$.

</details>


### [274] [$(a,a)$-Carmichael numbers and greatest common divisors of $p-a$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02738)
*Thomas Wright*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了(a,a)-Carmichael数的计数问题，在强假设下证明了对于特定参数，这类数的分布下界为X^{1-(2+o(1))loglogloglogX/logloglogX}，突破了传统构造中K随n增长的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Carmichael数的构造通常要求参数K随n增长，本文旨在探索K固定时(a,a)-Carmichael数的存在性与分布下界，突破这一传统限制。

Method: 基于算术级数中第一个素数的强猜想，通过构造性方法分析满足特定条件（(ν,a)=1且奇偶性相反）的(a,a)-Carmichael数的计数函数C_ν(X,a)。

Result: 对于任意整数a和满足互素及奇偶性相反条件的自然数ν，证明了C_ν(X,a) ≥ X^{1-(2+o(1))loglogloglogX/logloglogX}。

Conclusion: 该结果突破了传统Carmichael数构造中K必须随n增长的范式，展示了在K固定时仍存在大量(a,a)-Carmichael数。

Abstract: Define an $(a,a)$-Carmichael number to be a squarefree natural number $n$ such that $p\mid n$ implies $p-a\mid n-a$. For such a number $n$ with prime factors $p_1,\cdots,p_m$, define $$K=GCD[p_1-a,\cdots,p_m-a],$$ and let $C_ν(X,a)$ denote the number of $(a,a)$-Carmichael numbers up to $X$ such that $K=ν$. Assuming a strong conjecture on the first prime in an arithmetic progression, we prove that for any integer $a$ and for any natural number $ν$ with $(ν,a)=1$ and $a$ and $ν$ having opposite parity, $$C_ν(X,a)\geq X^{1-(2+o(1))\frac{\log\log\log \log X}{\log\log\log X}}.$$ This is a departure from many traditional constructions of Carmichael numbers, which generally require $K$ to grow along with $n$.

</details>


### [275] [$p$-adic rigidity for $\mathrm{GSp}_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02815)
*Charlotte Clare-Hunt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了GSp₄(ℚ)上某些精修的非尖点自守Saito–Kurokawa表示的p-adic刚性，即它们不能在非平凡的正维p-adic族中被插值。该结果提供了U(2,1)上Bellaïche刚性定理的GSp₄类比，并识别了GSp₄特征变体上p-adic变分的一个障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Saito–Kurokawa表示的p-adic刚性，给出Bellaïche关于U(2,1)的刚性定理在GSp₄上的类比，并揭示GSp₄特征变体上p-adic变分的阻碍来源。

Method: 论文通过分析精修的非尖点自守Saito–Kurokawa表示，研究其在p-adic族中的可插值性，从而建立刚性结论。

Result: 证明了GSp₄(𝔸_ℚ)上某些非尖点自守Saito–Kurokawa表示的p-adic刚性，即它们无法在非平凡正维p-adic族中被插值。

Conclusion: 该结果构成了U(2,1)上Bellaïche刚性定理在GSp₄上的类比，并指出了GSp₄特征变体上p-adic变分的一个障碍。

Abstract: This paper establishes the $p$-adic rigidity of certain suitably refined noncuspidal automorphic Saito$\unicode{x2013}$Kurokawa representations of $\mathrm{GSp}_4(\mathbb{A}_\mathbb{Q})$, in the sense that they cannot be interpolated in a nontrivial positive dimensional $p$-adic family. The results provide a $\mathrm{GSp}_4$ analogue of Bellaïche's rigidity theorems for $\mathrm{U}(2,1)$ and identify an obstruction to $p$-adic variation on the $\mathrm{GSp}_4$ eigenvariety.

</details>


### [276] [A finite Guinand-Weil dictionary and archimedean tail order for the truncated Weil quadratic form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02828)
*Akiva Groskin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文针对Connes等人提出的Weil二次型截断，证明了两个有限定理：一是每个实偶Galerkin系数向量可构造带限Guinand-Weil测试函数，其零点求和精确等于截断二次型值；二是给出了基于Cauchy-Stieltjes增量的双边认证规则，并在前512个zeta零点上验证。论文不涉及黎曼假设或素数计数等猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Weil二次型有限截断与zeta函数非平凡零点之间的精确对应关系，为Weil阳性准则提供可计算的有限秩窗口，并发展截断误差的显式控制。

Method: 利用Galerkin离散化对Connes等人提出的Weil二次型进行频率带限截断；通过构造带限Guinand-Weil测试函数建立系数向量与零点求和的精确等式；利用Cauchy-Stieltjes积分分析archimedean尾项，导出显式预算界B_T。

Result: 证明了二次型截断值与零点求和之间的精确相等性；给出了有限截断正性保证无截断正性、负性低于-B_T保证无截断负性的双边认证规则；在c=100时，若用粗暴截断需T~10^63，而LDL^T分解可直接解决10^-59的谱尺度。

Conclusion: 有限秩截断可严格控制Weil二次型的谱信息，为相关数论问题提供了可计算的有限框架，且不依赖黎曼假设。

Abstract: The Connes-van Suijlekom and Connes-Consani-Moscovici truncations of the Weil quadratic form, at a prime cutoff c>1 and frequency band N, produce finite Galerkin matrices whose spectra are the finite-rank window on Weil positivity. We prove two exact finite theorems about this truncation. First, every real even Galerkin coefficient vector v determines, in closed form, a band-limited Guinand-Weil test function g_v whose zero sum over the nontrivial zeros of zeta equals the quadratic value <v, Q v> exactly: every value of the truncated form is an exact sum over the zeros. The construction factors through an exact source quotient of dimension 2N+1 and admits a non-collapsing pole-neutral subfamily. Second, beyond the Galerkin band the omitted archimedean tail is a totally positive Cauchy-Stieltjes increment. This yields a two-sided certification rule with an explicit budget B_T ~ (2N+1) rho log(T) / (pi^2 T), where T is the archimedean cutoff and rho = 2 pi / log c: finite-cutoff positivity certifies cutoff-free positivity, a finite-cutoff eigenvalue below -B_T certifies a cutoff-free negative, and a negative eigenvalue in the band [-B_T, 0) certifies nothing. Resolving a spectral scale of 10^-59 at c=100 by brute cutoff would require T of order 10^63; a cutoff-free interval LDL^T factorization resolves it directly. The dictionary is verified over the first 512 zeros of zeta and by three independent computational routes; all scripts and artifacts ship with the paper. The paper makes no Riemann Hypothesis, prime-counting, next-prime, or factoring claim.

</details>


### [277] [A Mackey criterion for locally analytic representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02976)
*Andreas Bode*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了p-进群上从开子群（中心模紧）紧诱导的局部解析表示的Mackey不可约性判据的类比，并讨论了若干例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Mackey不可约性判据推广到p-进群的局部解析表示范畴中，特别是处理从中心模紧的开子群紧诱导的表示。

Method: 采用紧诱导表示和局部解析表示理论，通过分析诱导表示的不可约性条件，建立了与经典Mackey判据类似的结构。

Result: 成功证明了适用于p-进群上紧诱导局部解析表示的Mackey型不可约性判据。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典表示论中的重要判据延拓到p-进局部解析表示领域，为相关研究提供了新工具，并通过例子验证了结果的适用性。

Abstract: We prove an analogue of Mackey's irreducibility criterion for compactly induced locally analytic representations of $p$-adic groups, where we induce from an open subgroup which is compact mod centre. We also discuss several examples.

</details>


### [278] [Beyond Endoscopy for $\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{Q})$: Functional equation for the $L$-function of a cubic order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03083)
*Yuchan Lee*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文无条件证明了每个三次域中Gorenstein序的L-函数泛函方程，从而使得Deng和Espinosa关于GL₃(ℚ)上平凡表示分离的结果完全无条件化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Langlands的Beyond Endoscopy策略需要通过迹公式分析来建立函子性原理。Deng和Espinosa最近在GL₃(ℚ)上推进了这一纲领，但他们的工作依赖于一个关于三次序的L-函数的猜想性分解公式（含泛函方程）。因此，需要无条件证明该泛函方程以消除猜想依赖。

Method: 本文针对三次域中所有Gorenstein序，给出了其L-函数泛函方程的无条件证明，具体方法未在摘要中详述，但表明此证明不依赖于先前的猜想性分解公式。

Result: 成功证明了每个Gorenstein序在三次域中对应的L-函数均满足泛函方程，该结果是无条件的。

Conclusion: 本文的证明使得Deng和Espinosa关于GL₃(ℚ)上平凡表示分离的工作从有条件的（依赖于猜想）变为完全无条件的，有力推进了Beyond Endoscopy纲领在GL₃上的实施。

Abstract: The Beyond Endoscopy strategy, proposed by Langlands, aims to establish the principle of functoriality by analyzing the trace formula. Recently, Deng and Espinosa advanced this program for $\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{Q})$ by isolating the contribution of the trivial representation from the elliptic regular part. Their work relies on a conjectural factorization formula for the $L$-function associated with a cubic order, which yields the functional equation for the completed $L$-function. In this paper, we provide an unconditional proof of this functional equation for every Gorenstein order in a cubic number field. As a consequence, their isolation of the trivial representation for $\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb{Q})$ becomes fully unconditional.

</details>


### [279] [Refined Humbert invariants and subvarieties of $\Ac_2(\C)$: the rank 3 case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03173)
*Elisa Lorenzo García,Christophe Ritzenthaler*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明在复数域上，具有给定秩3精细Humbert不变量的主极化阿贝尔曲面都是Galois共轭的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究主极化阿贝尔曲面的模空间及其在Galois群作用下的轨道结构，理解具有特定不变量的曲面的代数性质。

Method: 利用精细Humbert不变量和Galois理论，在复数域上对主极化阿贝尔曲面进行分类和共轭性分析。

Result: 证明了所有具有给定秩3精细Humbert不变量的主极化阿贝尔曲面在Galois作用下互相共轭。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了这类阿贝尔曲面在代数数论背景下的深层对称性，对Humbert曲面及其模空间的理解有重要贡献。

Abstract: Over the complex field, we show that the principally polarised abelian surfaces with a given refined Humbert invariant of rank 3 are all Galois conjugated.

</details>


### [280] [Lubin's conjecture for height-one $p$-adic dynamical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03257)
*Martin Debaisieux*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Lubin关于高度为1的交换形式幂级数对的猜想，这些对定义在Q_p的有限扩张的整数环O上。运用p-adic Hodge理论，证明了这样的对是定义在O上的形式群的一对自同态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并证明Lubin关于高度为1的交换形式幂级数对的猜想，该猜想将这类对与形式群的自同态联系起来。

Method: 利用p-adic Hodge理论作为主要工具，对定义在有限扩张的整数环上的高度为1的交换形式幂级数对进行数学分析。

Result: 证明了Lubin猜想对于这类形式幂级数对成立，即它们可以表示为定义在O上的形式群的一对自同态。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了Lubin猜想的适用范围，并展示了p-adic Hodge理论在形式幂级数研究中的关键作用。

Abstract: We prove Lubin's conjecture for height-one commuting pairs of formal power series defined over the ring of integers $\mathcal{O}$ of any finite extension of $\mathbb{Q}_p$. Using some $p$-adic Hodge theory, we show that such a pair is a pair of endomorphisms of a formal group defined over $\mathcal{O}$.

</details>


### [281] [On Sárközy-Sós Theorem related to representation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03336)
*Jin-Hui Fang,Sándor Z. Kiss,Wei Niu,Csaba Sándor*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了加法补集表示函数密度的存在性问题，将Chen-Tang定理从有理数密度推广到任意非负实数密度，证明了对任意满足总和为1的非负实数α₀,...,α_m，存在无穷多集合𝒜⊆ℕ₀使得r₂(𝒜,n)=i的n的密度恰为α_i。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chen和Tang（2016）证明了对于任意给定的不同正整数u₁,...,u_k和正有理数α₁,...,α_k（和为1），存在无穷多集合𝒜使得表示函数取各值的密度恰为这些有理数。本文旨在将该结果推广到α_i为任意非负实数（包括无理数）的情形。

Method: 采用构造性方法，通过精细的集合构造技巧，将有理数密度情形推广到任意实数密度，主要处理h=2（二元表示）的情形。

Result: 证明了对于任意满足α₀+...+α_m=1的非负实数α₀,...,α_m，存在无穷多集合𝒜⊆ℕ₀使得r₂(𝒜,n)=i的n的密度恰为α_i（i=0,...,m）。

Conclusion: 成功地将Chen-Tang定理从有理数密度推广到任意实数密度，完善了加法补集表示函数密度问题的理论。

Abstract: Let $\mathbb{N}_0$ be the set of all nonnegative integers. For a nonempty set $\mathcal{A}\subseteq \mathbb{N}_0$ and integers $n,h\ge 2$, let $r_{h}(\mathcal{A},n)$ be the number of representations of $n$ as $a_1+\cdots+a_h$, where $a_1\le \cdots\le a_h$ and $a_i\in \mathcal{A}$ for $i=1,\cdots,h$. In 2016, Chen and Tang showed that, for any given distinct positive integers $u_1,\cdots,u_k$ and positive rational numbers $α_1,\cdots,α_k$ with $α_1+\cdots+α_k=1$, there are infinitely many sets $\mathcal{A}\subseteq \mathbb{N}_0$ such that $r_{h}(\mathcal{A},n)\ge 1$ for all nonnegative integers $n$ and the set of $n$ with $r_{h}(\mathcal{A},n)=u_i$ has density $α_i$ for all integer $i=1,\cdots,k$. In this paper, we consider the irrational numbers $α_i$ as well. As a main result, we prove that, for any nonnegative numbers $α_0,\cdots,α_m$ with $α_0+\cdots+α_m=1$, there are infinitely many sets $\mathcal{A}\subseteq \mathbb{N}_0$ such that the set of $n$ with $r_{2}(\mathcal{A},n)=i$ has density $α_i$ for all integer $i=0,\cdots,m$. Other related results are also contained.

</details>


### [282] [Derivative-Free Richelot Isogenies via Subresultants with Algebraic Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03376)
*Hung T. Dang,Diep V. Nguyen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全无需微分的Richelot (2,2)-同源步骤新方法（RPR），通过子结式和线性合冲恢复Wronskian输出，并在此基础上构建了带认证功能的守卫子结式路径（GSR），在保证多项式恒等精度的同时实现了4.75–6倍的内核加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Richelot同源步骤依赖Wronskian导数计算，在特征大于2的素域上计算开销较大，且缺乏轻量级的可容许性认证机制。本文旨在消除导数依赖，利用代数结构（子结式、线性合冲）重构输出，并引入确定性认证路径以提升效率与可靠性。

Method: (1) 将Wronskian输出表示为系数矩阵的2×2子式，并通过第一子结式（first subresultants）和线性合冲（linear syzygy）恢复；(2) 提出余式-多项式路径（RPR），证明其在F_p[x]中与经典Wronskian输出精确多项式相等；(3) 在此基础上构建守卫子结式路径（GSR），引入常数大小的代数守卫（constant-size algebraic guards）、轻量级后检查及至多一次有界仿射重试，实现确定性可容许性认证；(4) 所有路径每步执行O(1)次域操作。

Result: 在超过10^6次匹配试验中，RPR相比经典Wronskian公式实现4.75–6倍内核加速；完整GSR管线尽管包含认证开销，仍比WRO快1.4–3倍。通过2.5×10^5个随机三元组在五个素数上的双Richelot对合测试独立验证了正确性。

Conclusion: 新方法在保持精确多项式等价的前提下大幅提升了计算效率，且通过代数守卫实现了轻量级可容许性认证，为超奇异同源密码（如SIDH/SIKE）等领域的高效实现提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: The classical Richelot $(2,2)$-isogeny step for genus-$2$ curves constructs a codomain triple $(U,V,W)$ from a factorization $f=uvw$ via Wronskian derivatives. We give a completely derivative-free reformulation over prime fields $\mathbb{F}_p$, $p>2$, by expressing the Wronskian output through the $2\times 2$ minors of the coefficient matrix and recovering them from first subresultants and a linear syzygy. The resulting Remainder-Polynomial Route (RPR) is proven to produce the identical output triple in $\mathbb{F}_p[x]$ not merely up to units, but as an exact polynomial identity. Building on this equivalence, we introduce the Guarded Subresultant Route (GSR), a deterministic evaluator that certifies admissibility through constant-size algebraic guards, a lightweight post-check, and at most one bounded affine retry. All routes execute $O(1)$ field operations per step. A prototype over $10^6$ matched trials per prime confirms a $4.75$--$6\times$ kernel speedup for RPR over the classical Wronskian formula, and the full GSR pipeline remains $1.4$--$3\times$ faster than WRO despite the certification overhead. Correctness is independently verified by a double-Richelot involution test on $2.5\times10^5$ random triples across five primes.

</details>


### [283] [Calculating the floor of y**(1/m)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03462)
*Alexandros V. Gerbessiotis*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 提出了两种基于牛顿-拉夫森方法的算法，用于计算自然整数y>2和m>1时y**(1/m)的向下取整值，可用于判断y是否为整数的整数幂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数论问题中需要判断一个自然整数y是否为某个整数的整数幂，虽然二分查找法传统上被认为更易实现，但作者提出了基于牛顿-拉夫森方法的替代算法。

Method: 基于牛顿-拉夫森方法设计了两种算法，用于计算y**(1/m)的floor值（向下取整），适用于自然整数y>2和m>1的情形。

Result: 提出了两种可行的算法，能够有效计算大整数的高次方根向下取整值。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于牛顿-拉夫森方法的算法可作为判断整数是否为整数幂的替代方案，尽管二分查找法仍被认为是更简单的实现方式。

Abstract: We present two algorithms based on the Newton-Raphson method to calculate the floor of y**(1/m) for natural integer numbers y>2 and m >1. One could use such an algorithm to establish whether y is an integer power of an integer in number theory problems, even though binary search methods are traditionally considered simpler to implement.

</details>


### [284] [Ramanujan-type identities for alternating Hurwitz zeta functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03490)
*Meng Yuan,Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Ramanujan关于ζ(2n+1)的经典恒等式推广到交替Hurwitz zeta函数，研究了该函数在不同模对称条件下的性质及对应的Ramanujan型恒等式，并建立了正切与双曲正切函数的无穷级数表达式、Dirichlet lambda函数的卷积和表示，以及涉及交替digamma函数和Euler多项式的变换公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ramanujan于1910年提出了关于黎曼zeta函数在奇数点取值的重要恒等式，后续研究希望将其推广到更广泛的zeta函数族，特别是交替Hurwitz zeta函数，以揭示更深层的模对称性质和数论结构。

Method: 通过系统地分析交替Hurwitz zeta函数在不同模对称条件下的性质，定义交替Hurwitz核（偶数阶与奇数阶），利用Bernoulli数、Euler多项式等特殊函数，推导Ramanujan型恒等式，并建立正切/双曲正切函数级数表达与Dirichlet lambda函数的卷积和展开。

Result: 成功将Ramanujan恒等式推广到交替Hurwitz zeta函数；建立了交替Hurwitz核的奇偶阶变换公式；得到了正切与双曲正切函数乘积的无穷级数表达式；将Dirichlet lambda函数表示为特殊序列的卷积和；导出了涉及交替digamma函数和Euler多项式的Ramanujan型恒等式。

Conclusion: 本文系统拓展了Ramanujan经典恒等式的适用范围，为交替Hurwitz zeta函数提供了完整的模对称理论框架，丰富了特殊函数与数论之间的交叉联系。

Abstract: Around 1910, in an unpublished manuscript, Ramanujan proposed the following identity for $ζ(2n+1)$: \begin{align*}
  α^{-n}\left\{\frac{1}{2}ζ\left(2n+1\right)
  +\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\frac{m^{-2n-1}}{e^{2αm}-1}\right\}
  &-\left(-β\right)^{-n}\left\{\frac{1}{2}ζ\left(2n+1\right)
  +\sum_{m=1}^{\infty}\frac{m^{-2n-1}}{e^{2βm}-1}\right\}
  \\&=2^{2n}\sum_{k=0}^{n+1}{\frac{\left(-1\right)^{k-1}B_{2k}B_{2n-2k+2}}
  {\left(2k\right)!\left(2n-2k+2\right)!}α^{n-k+1}β^k}, \end{align*} where $α$, $β$ are positive numbers satisfying $αβ=π^2,n\in\mathbb Z\setminus\{0\},$ $B_n$ denotes the $n$-th Bernoulli number, and $ζ(z)$ is the Riemann zeta function.
  In this paper, we extend Ramanujan's identity to the alternating Hurwitz zeta function and systematically investigate the properties of the alternating Hurwitz zeta function $ζ_E(z,x)$ under different modular symmetry conditions, as well as the corresponding Ramanujan-type identities. We also establish infinite series expressions for products of the tangent and hyperbolic tangent functions, and express the Dirichlet lambda function $λ(z)$ together with linear combinations of infinite series as convolution sums of special sequences. Furthermore, we define alternating Hurwitz kernels of even and odd orders, and obtain Ramanujan-type identities involving the alternating digamma function $\widetildeψ(x)$ and Euler polynomials $E_n(x)$, as well as transformation formulas between even-order and odd-order alternating Hurwitz kernels.

</details>


### [285] [The discrete logarithm problem in cokernels of $\mathcal{O}_K$-matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03594)
*Isaac Rajagopal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广了图上的沙堆群离散对数问题到数域整数环矩阵的余核，在数域类群非平凡时克服了欧几里得算法的障碍，提出了高效计算离散对数的新算法，时间复杂度为Õ((m+n)^{ω+1})，当矩阵为Hermitian时改进为Õ(n^ω)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackburn和Shokrieh发现图的沙堆群上的离散对数可高效计算，表明沙堆群对密码学不安全。本文将其推广到数域整数环矩阵的余核上，探究此类更一般代数结构上离散对数的计算复杂性。

Method: 针对数域K的类群非平凡时整数环中欧几里得算法失效的障碍，提出了新算法来计算余核中的离散对数；当矩阵M关于伽罗瓦对合σ为Hermitian且非奇异时，进一步优化了时间复杂度。

Result: 提出了时间复杂度为Õ((m+n)^{ω+1})的离散对数算法；对于Hermitian非奇异矩阵，时间复杂度改进为Õ(n^ω)。

Conclusion: 数域整数环矩阵余核上的离散对数可以高效计算，因此这类结构也不适用于密码学应用。

Abstract: In 2009 and 2010, Blackburn and Shokrieh independently found that the discrete logarithm can be computed efficiently on the sandpile group of a graph, meaning that sandpile groups are not secure for cryptography. We generalize this problem to cokernels of matrices with entries in the ring of integers $\mathcal{O}_K$ of a number field $K$. When $K$ has nontrivial class group, the failure of the Euclidean algorithm in $\mathcal{O}_K$ is an obstacle to generalizing previous methods. For $M$ in $\mathrm{M}_{n\times m}(\mathcal{O}_K)$, we overcome this obstacle to efficiently compute discrete logarithms in $\mathrm{cok}(M) = \mathcal{O}_K^n/M\mathcal{O}_K^m$. In particular, we find an algorithm with time complexity $\tilde{O}((m+n)^{ω+1})$, where $ω$ is an exponent of matrix multiplication, to compute discrete logarithms in $\mathrm{cok}(M)$ when $\mathrm{cok}(M)$ is viewed either as an $\mathcal{O}_K$-module or as a group. When $M$ is Hermitian with respect to a Galois involution $σ$ and nonsingular, we improve the time complexity to $\tilde{O}(n^ω)$.

</details>


### [286] [On the inverses of permutation polynomials of the form $h(ψ(x))\varphi(x)+g(ψ(x))$ over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03630)
*Danyao Wu,Pingzhi Yuan,Xuan Pang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了形如 F(x)=h(ψ(x))φ(x)+g(ψ(x)) 的置换多项式的合成逆，其中ψ和φ是加法多项式，h将ψ(𝔽_{qⁿ})映射到𝔽_q^*，且存在多项式ψ̅满足ψ̅(F(x))=ψ(x)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置换多项式在密码学、编码理论等领域有重要应用，而合成逆的显式构造对于这些应用至关重要。现有方法对某些特殊形式的置换多项式缺乏系统的合成逆构造方法。

Method: 利用线性化多项式（加法多项式）的结构性质，结合条件 h(ψ(𝔽_{qⁿ})) ⊆ 𝔽_q^* 和存在多项式ψ̅使得ψ̅(F(x))=ψ(x)这一关键条件，推导出这类置换多项式合成逆的显式表达式。

Result: 给出了上述形式置换多项式的合成逆的显式构造，该构造依赖于ψ、φ、h、g以及ψ̅等多项式的具体形式。

Conclusion: 本文为一大类具有特定代数结构的置换多项式提供了合成逆的显式构造方法，丰富了置换多项式理论及其应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the compositional inverses of permutation polynomials of the form
  \[
  F(x)=h(ψ(x))\varphi(x)+g(ψ(x)) \in \mathbb{F}_{q^n}[x],
  \]
  where \(ψ(x),\varphi(x) \in \mathbb{F}_{q^n}[x]\) are additive polynomials, \(h(x), g(x) \in \mathbb{F}_{q^n}[x]\) satisfy
  $
  h(ψ(\mathbb{F}_{q^n})) \subseteq \mathbb{F}_q^*,
  $
  and there exists a polynomial \(\barψ(x) \in \mathbb{F}_{q^n}[x]\) such that $
  \barψ(F(x)) = ψ(x). $

</details>


### [287] [The compositional inverses of the permutation polynomials of the form $x+γ\operatorname{Tr}_{q}^{q^n}(H(x))$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q^n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03638)
*Danyao Wu,Xuan Pang,Zilong He,Pingzhi Yuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过局部方法研究了有限域上形如 $x+γ\operatorname{Tr}_{q}^{q^{n}}(H(x))$ 的置换多项式的复合逆，给出了 $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ 上四族、$\mathbb{F}_{q^3}$ 上四族以及 $\mathbb{F}_{q^n}$ 上一族置换多项式的显式复合逆表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补充迹置换多项式理论中关于复合逆的闭式表达结果。

Method: 采用局部方法（local method）显式构造复合逆。

Result: 给出了 $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ 上四族、$\mathbb{F}_{q^3}$ 上四族以及 $\mathbb{F}_{q^n}$ 上一族置换多项式的显式复合逆闭式表达式。

Conclusion: 所得闭式逆表达式补充了迹置换多项式理论。

Abstract: This paper focuses on computing the compositional inverses of permutation polynomials of the form $x+γ\operatorname{Tr}_{q}^{q^{n}}(H(x))$ over finite fields via the local method. We explicitly construct compositional inverses for four families of permutation polynomials of this type over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$, another four families over $\mathbb{F}_{q^3}$, and one general family over $\mathbb{F}_{q^n}$. The closed-form inverse expressions derived in this work supplement the theory of trace permutation polynomials.

</details>


### [288] [Extreme values of the real part of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03654)
*Qiyu Yang,Shengbo Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用Bondarenko和Seip的共振方法，结合非负系数Dirichlet级数相关积分的下界，建立了临界线上Riemann zeta函数实部极值的下界，扩展了Bondarenko和Seip（2018）的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Riemann zeta函数在临界线上实部极值的下界，这是解析数论中的重要问题，有助于深入理解zeta函数在临界线上的行为。

Method: 采用Bondarenko和Seip开发的共振方法，并结合非负系数Dirichlet级数相关积分的下界估计。

Result: 得到了临界线上Riemann zeta函数实部极值的新的下界。

Conclusion: 本文结果扩展了Bondarenko和Seip（2018）的先前工作，为Riemann zeta函数在临界线上的极值行为提供了更优的下界估计。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish lower bounds for extreme values of the real part of the Riemann zeta function on the critical line. This work relies on the resonance method of Bondarenko and Seip, together with lower bounds for certain integrals associated with Dirichlet series with non-negative coefficients. Our results extend the work of Bondarenko and Seip (2018).

</details>


### [289] [A Romanoff-type theorem for $P_2$+{$a^a$: a$\ge$ 1}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03662)
*Yuchen Ding,Huixi Li,Junfeng Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了$P_2$（至多有两个素因子的数）与形如$a^a$的数的和集具有正的下密度。证明采用Romanoff二阶矩方法，核心创新是建立了一个关于奇异因子的平均估计，用于控制$a^a$序列间的算术相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由至多两个素因子的数构成的集合$P_2$与指数塔序列$\{a^a\}$的和集的密度性质，探索这类稀疏集合相加后能否产生正密度的整数集合。

Method: 采用Romanoff二阶矩方法（类似于Li-Pan关于$P_2+2^{\mathcal P}$的定理），核心新工具是建立了关于奇异因子$\prod_{p\mid a^a-b^b}(1+κ/p)$的平均估计，该估计对所有$K\ge2$和固定$κ>0$成立，从而控制了$a^a$序列间的算术相关性，使得Romanoff论证得以实施。

Result: 证明了和集$P_2+\{a^a: a\ge 1\}$具有正的下密度。

Conclusion: 通过引入新的平均估计量，成功将Romanoff方法推广到非线性的指数塔序列情形，为研究其他稀疏序列和集的密度问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: Let $Ω(n)$ denote the number of prime factors of $n$, counted with multiplicity, and put $P_2$={$m$ $\ge$ 1:$Ω(m)$ $\le$ 2}. We prove that the sumset $P_2$+{$a^a$: a$\ge$ 1} has positive lower density. The proof uses the Romanoff second moment method, in the spirit of Li and Pan's theorem on $P_2$+$2^{\mathcal P}$. The main new ingredient is the following average estimate for the singular factor \[
  \frac{1}{K(K-1)}
  \sum_{\substack{1\le a,b\le K\\a\ne b}}
  \prod_{p\mid a^a-b^b}\left(1+\fracκ{p}\right)
  \le C_κ\] for some constant $C_κ>0$, which is valid for all $K \ge 2$ and any fixed $κ>0$. This estimate controls the average arithmetic correlation among the shifts $a^a$ and allows the Romanoff argument to be carried out.

</details>


### [290] [Modular elliptic curves and hyperbolic uniformization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03830)
*Neil Dummigan,Devendra Tiwari*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Mazur关于椭圆曲线算术型双曲一致化的思想从有理数域推广到全实奇次数域，证明若椭圆曲线存在算术型双曲一致化，则其必为几何模的（即某Shimura曲线雅可比簇的商）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Mazur在有理数域上提出的椭圆曲线算术型双曲一致化概念（早于模性定理的证明）推广到全实奇次数域上的椭圆曲线，探索该框架下几何模性与双曲一致化之间的内在联系。

Method: 利用在所有有限位和一个实位分裂的四元数代数的Shimura曲线，替代有理数域情形下的模曲线X₀(N)，构建全实奇次数域上椭圆曲线的算术型双曲一致化理论，并建立其与几何模性之间的联系。

Result: 证明了在全实奇次数域上，若椭圆曲线存在算术型双曲一致化，则它必然是几何模的，即某个Shimura曲线雅可比簇在Q上的商。

Conclusion: 成功将Mazur的算术型双曲一致化框架从有理数域扩展到全实奇次数域，并建立了该性质与几何模性之间的蕴含关系，为更广泛数域上的模性问题提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In an article published a few years before the modularity of elliptic curves over $\Q$ was proved, Mazur \cite{maz} looked at modularity as a purely complex analytic phenomenon, defining a notion of an elliptic curve over $\Q$ having a hyperbolic uniformisation of arithmetic type. Such an elliptic curve (of conductor $N$, say) is necessarily geometrically modular, i.e. a quotient of the jacobian of the modular curve $X_0(N)$, by a morphism defined over $\Q$. We extend these ideas to elliptic curves over totally real fields of odd degree, using Shimura curves for quaternion algebras split at all finite places and one real place. In particular, we prove that the existence of a hyperbolic uniformisation of arithmetic type would imply geometric modularity.

</details>


### [291] [Constructing the $\mathbf{2^{2^5} + 1 = F_5}$-gon: the first step, the 641-gon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04000)
*Helmut Ruhland*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Adlaj构建正F₄边形（F₄为费马素数）的方法，将其应用于乘法群阶数不是2ⁿ而是含有额外素因子的情形，成功构造了正641边形（641是合数费马数F₅=2²⁵+1的较小素因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adlaj的方法局限于乘法群阶数为2ⁿ的费马素数情形，本文旨在将该方法推广到乘法群阶数含有额外素因子的更一般情形，从而构造非费马素数的正多边形。

Method: 扩展Adlaj的构造方法，处理乘法群阶数不是纯2的幂次（2ⁿ）而是包含其他素因子的情况，具体应用于641这一素数。

Result: 成功构造了正641边形，641是费马合数F₅=2²⁵+1的较小素因子。

Conclusion: Adlaj的方法可以被推广到乘法群阶数含有额外素因子的情形，从而构造非费马素数的正多边形。

Abstract: In an article S. Adlaj showed the construction of the $F_4 = 65\,537$-gon ($F_4$ a Fermat prime). Extending the authors method to cases, where the multiplicative group is not of order $2^n$, but has an additional prime factor, here the $641$-gon ist constructed. $641$ is the smaller prime factor of the composite Fermat number $F_5 = 2^{2^5} + 1$.

</details>


### [292] [Anomalous Partial Quotients in the Continued Fraction of $\sqrt{ζ(3)-S_N}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04077)
*David Victor Feldman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究$\sqrt{R_{N_k}}$的简单连分数，其中$R_{N_k} = ζ(3) - \sum_{j=1}^{N_k} j^{-3}$，$N_k = (Q_{2k+1}-1)/2$，$Q_{2k+1}$是伴侣佩尔数。发现连分数的部分商在特定指标处出现立方增长（第三项$6M_k^3+12M_k-2$），而通常为线性$O(N)$。给出了前六个部分商的精确公式，其中第五项涉及模261的递推。所有公式通过欧拉-麦克劳林展开和佩尔恒等式证明，首项$M_k-1$通过有理化$\sqrt{2}$证明，并补充了Gosper双同形连分数算法的启发式推导。所有结果已在LEAN中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索阿佩里常数$ζ(3)$的部分和误差平方根$\sqrt{R_N}$的连分数展开中，部分商大小从线性$O(N)$到立方增长的异常现象，揭示其与佩尔数序列的内在联系。

Method: 利用欧拉-麦克劳林公式对$1/\sqrt{R_{N_k}}$进行高精度展开，结合佩尔恒等式$Q_{2k+1}^2 - 2M_k^2 = -1$，有理化$\sqrt{2}$因子证明首项，并采用Gosper双同形连分数算法提供启发式推导。

Result: 确定了连分数前六个部分商的精确公式：$\PQ_0 = M_k-1$，$\PQ_1=1$，$\PQ_2=6M_k^3+12M_k-2$，$\PQ_3=1$，$\PQ_4=\lfloor(10M_k-261)/261\rfloor$，$\PQ_5=\lfloor 261/r_k\rfloor+ε_k$，其中$r_k=(10M_k)\bmod 261$满足递推$r_{k+1}\equiv 6r_k-r_{k-1}\pmod{261}$，$ε_k$在$r_k\mid 261$时取$-1$（对应$k\equiv 57,62\pmod{60}$），否则为$0$。

Conclusion: 揭示了$ζ(3)$部分和误差平方根的连分数在特定指标处部分商呈立方增长的精确结构，所有公式均通过解析方法严格证明并在LEAN中形式化验证。

Abstract: Let $S_N = \sum_{j=1}^N j^{-3}$ and $R_N = ζ(3) - S_N$. The simple continued fraction of $\sqrt{R_N}$ has partial quotients of generic size $O(N)$. We prove that at the sequence of indices $N_k = (Q_{2k+1}-1)/2$, where $Q_{2k+1}$ are companion Pell numbers, the continued fraction begins \[
  \sqrt{R_{N_k}}
  = \bigl[0;\; M_k-1,\; 1,\; 6M_k^3+12M_k-2,\; 1,\; \ldots\,\bigr], \] with $M_k = P_{2k+1}$ (Pell numbers), and the third partial quotient grows cubically while generic ones are linear. We determine all partial quotients through the fifth: \begin{align*}
  \PQ_0 &= M_k - 1, &
  \PQ_2 &= 6M_k^3 + 12M_k - 2, &
  \PQ_4 &= \Bigl\lfloor\frac{10M_k - 261}{261}\Bigr\rfloor,
  \PQ_1 &= 1, &
  \PQ_3 &= 1, &
  \PQ_5 &= \Bigl\lfloor\frac{261}{r_k}\Bigr\rfloor + ε_k, \end{align*} where $r_k = (10M_k) \bmod 261$ satisfies the recurrence $r_{k+1} \equiv 6r_k - r_{k-1} \pmod{261}$, and $ε_k = -1$ at the $k$ with $r_k \mid 261$ (the two residue classes $k \equiv 57, 62 \pmod{60}$), and $ε_k = 0$ otherwise.
  All six formulas follow from the Euler--Maclaurin expansion of $1/\sqrt{R_{N_k}}$, carried to sufficient precision, combined with the Pell identity $Q_{2k+1}^2 - 2M_k^2 = -1$. The delicate first step, $\PQ_0 = M_k - 1$, is proved by rationalizing the irrational factor $\sqrt{2}$ in the Euler--Maclaurin expansion; we complement this proof with a heuristic derivation via Gosper's bihomographic continued-fraction algorithm that exposes the underlying mechanism.
  All claimed results have been formalized in LEAN with the aid of Aristotle.

</details>


### [293] [Extreme values of derivatives of Dirichlet $L$-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04084)
*Xinghua Cui,Zhifeng Peng,Yutong Song,Shengbo Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了在1/2<σ<1范围内Dirichlet L-函数导数极值的下界，结果表明该区域内导数与原始L-函数可达到相同数量级的极值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Aistleitner等人(2019)的工作可能未充分揭示Dirichlet L-函数导数在临界带内极值的量级，本文旨在填补这一空白，证明导数与原始L-函数在1/2<σ<1范围内具有相同数量级的极值。

Method: 通过建立Dirichlet L-函数导数极值的下界估计，与已有结果进行对比分析。

Result: 在1/2<σ<1范围内，Dirichlet L-函数导数可以达到与原始L-函数相同数量级的极值。

Conclusion: 扩展了此前研究的结论，证明了在1/2<σ<1区域中Dirichlet L-函数导数与原始函数具有同等极值量级。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish lower bounds for extreme values of derivatives of Dirichlet \(L\)-functions in the range \(1/2<σ<1\). Compared with the work of Aistleitner, Mahatab, Munsch, and Peyrot in 2019, our result shows that derivatives of Dirichlet \(L\)-functions can attain extreme values of the same order of magnitude as the original \(L\)-functions when \(1/2<σ<1\) holds.

</details>


### [294] [$p$-adic multiple $L$-functions and twisted multiple Bernoulli numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04086)
*Ku-Yu Fan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文计算了Furusho等人引入的p进多重L函数在正整数元组处的特殊值（pMLFV），将其表达为分圆多重调和值（CMHV）的无限和，系数由分圆多重伯努利数（CMBN）给出；进一步给出了CMBN的显式公式，用扭曲多重伯努利数（TMBN）表示，后者是广义Euler-Zagier-Lerch型复多重zeta函数在非正整数元组处的特殊值，从而pMLFV可表示为CMHV的无限和，系数由复函数在非正整数元组处的特殊值给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立p进多重L函数特殊值（pMLFV）与分圆多重调和值（CMHV）及扭曲多重伯努利数（TMBN）之间的显式联系，深化对p进多重L函数特殊值的理解与计算。

Method: 利用Furusho-Jarossay的结果将pMLFV表示为CMHV的无限和（系数为CMBN）；给出CMBN关于TMBN的显式公式；将TMBN等同于广义Euler-Zagier-Lerch型复多重zeta函数在非正整数元组处的特殊值。

Result: 获得了pMLFV的显式表达式：无限和形式，求和项为CMHV，系数为复多重zeta函数在非正整数元组处的特殊值。

Conclusion: p进多重L函数的特殊值可通过分圆多重调和值与复多重zeta函数的特殊值显式表达，建立了p进与复解析对象之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We compute the special values ($p$MLFVs) of the $p$-adic multiple $L$-functions introduced by Furusho, Komori, Matsumoto, and Tsumura at tuples of positive integers. Furusho and Jarossay show that the special values can be expressed as an infinite sum of cyclotomic multiple harmonic values (CMHVs) with coefficients given by cyclotomic multiple Bernoulli numbers (CMBNs). We provide an explicit formula for CMBNs in terms of twisted multiple Bernoulli numbers (TMBNs), which are special values of generalized Euler-Zagier-Lerch type complex multiple zeta functions at tuples of non-positive integers. As a result, we obtain that these $p$MLFVs can be expressed as infinite sums of CMHVs, with coefficients given by the special values of the complex functions at tuples of non-positive integers.

</details>


### [295] [A Stretched-Exponential Bound for an Erdos--Graham Unit-Fraction Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04157)
*Samuel Korsky*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对Erdős-Graham关于倒数和与最大子集和距离的猜想给出了拉伸指数估计，证明当倒数和K足够大时，距离ε(A)不超过exp(-c√(K log K))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős和Graham证明了当正整数多重集A的倒数和R(A)>K时，ε(A) ≪ K^{-2}，并猜想是否存在绝对常数c>0使得ε(A) ≤ exp(-cK)。本文旨在改进这一上界，逼近指数衰减猜想。

Method: 通过组合数论和概率方法，构造适当的子集选择策略，对倒数和与子集和距离进行精细控制，得到拉伸指数上界。

Result: 对于所有足够大的K，有ε(A) ≤ exp(-c√(K log K))，即拉伸指数衰减上界。

Conclusion: 该结果显著改进了Erdős-Graham的二次衰减界，向指数衰减猜想迈出了重要一步，但完全的指数衰减仍未解决。

Abstract: For a finite multiset $A$ of positive integers, write $\mathcal{R}(A)=\sum_{a\in A}a^{-1}$ and let $\varepsilon(A)$ be the distance from $1$ to the largest reciprocal subsum of $A$ that does not exceed $1$. Erdős and Graham proved that $\varepsilon(A)\ll K^{-2}$ whenever $\mathcal{R}(A)>K$, and asked whether one always has $\varepsilon(A)\leq \exp(-cK)$ for an absolute constant $c>0$. We prove the stretched-exponential estimate $$ \varepsilon(A)\leq \exp\bigl(-c\sqrt{K\log K}\bigr) $$ for all sufficiently large $K$.

</details>


### [296] [Distinctness of the generalized $n$-dimensional Kloosterman sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04177)
*Xin Lin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用伽罗瓦理论和Stickelberger定理研究了一种广义n维Kloosterman和在有限域F_q上的代数次数和相异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义n维Kloosterman和的代数性质，特别是其代数次数和相异性，这类和在数论和编码理论中有重要应用。

Method: 运用伽罗瓦理论（Galois theory）和关于p进高斯和（p-adic Gauss sums）的Stickelberger定理进行分析。

Result: 得到了广义n维Kloosterman和的代数次数和相异性的具体结论。

Conclusion: 伽罗瓦理论和Stickelberger定理是研究Kloosterman和代数性质的有效工具，所得结果有助于理解这类和在有限域上的代数结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the algebraic degree and distinctness of a kind of generalized $n$-dimensional Kloosterman sums over finite field $F_q$ by Galois theory and the Stickelberger theorem on $p$-adic Gauss sums.

</details>


### [297] [On the number of perfect positive definite quadratic forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04239)
*Nikolay Moshchevitin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了n元完美正定二次型数量一个新的渐近下界，该下界接近最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二次型理论中，完美正定二次型的计数问题是一个经典难题，已有的下界结果尚不理想，本文致力于给出更紧的渐近下界。

Method: 采用渐近分析方法，通过构造或组合计数技术来推导完美正定二次型数量的下界。

Result: 得到了一个接近最优的渐近下界。

Conclusion: 该下界显著改进了已有结果，接近理论上可能达到的最优值。

Abstract: We prove a new asymptotic lower bound for the number of perfect positive definite quadratic forms in n variables which is close to the optimal one.

</details>


### [298] [Improving on the Brun-Titchmarsh Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04283)
*Olvier Ramaré,Jan Christoph Schlage-Puchta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对足够大的N，长度为N的区间中素数个数不超过2N/(log N+3.53)，通过结合大筛法与Selberg筛法的混合筛法及"局部模型"实现。发表于Acta Arithmetica 2008。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进素数在区间中上界的经典估计，得到更优的常数项系数。

Method: 采用大筛法与Selberg筛法的混合筛法，并引入"局部模型"（local models）作为核心分析工具。

Result: 对足够大的N，区间长度为N时，素数个数≤2N/(log N+3.53)。

Conclusion: 通过混合筛法和局部模型，改进了素数计数函数的上界估计。

Abstract: We prove that the number of primes in an interval of length $N$ is at most $2N/(\log N+3.53)$ when $N$ is large enough. This is obtained through a sieving process which can be seen as a hybrid between the large sieve and the Selberg sieve, and draws on what we call "local models". This paper has been published in {\sc Acta Arithmetica, 2008, vol. 131, nb. 4, pages 351--366}.

</details>


### [299] [A Gaussian-Perron Smoothed Prime-Side Defect for Local Profiles Near Critical-Line Zeros of the Riemann Zeta Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04316)
*Netzer Moriya*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高斯-佩朗素数力缺陷，通过将平滑化的素数侧对数力与黎曼ζ函数的对数导数进行对比，将显式公式转化为零点几何的局部诊断工具。该构造产生误差函数素数权重和各向异性零点侧阻尼律，并证明了精确的零点侧公式、对数尺度上的通用选定零点轮廓，以及非选定留数的有限窗口阻尼证书。在黎曼假设和给定极点阻尼条件下，每个固定简单临界线零点附近的完全缺陷具有选定零点轮廓，误差为指数小的非局部余项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提供一种局部诊断黎曼ζ函数零点几何的工具，通过构造高斯-佩朗素数力缺陷将显式公式转化为可分析零点局部行为的框架。

Method: 1. 引入高斯-佩朗素数力缺陷，对比平滑素数侧对数力与ζ函数对数导数；2. 构造误差函数素数权重和各向异性零点侧阻尼律；3. 推导精确零点侧公式和对数尺度选定零点轮廓；4. 建立有限窗口阻尼证书处理非选定留数；5. 在黎曼假设和极点阻尼条件下分析缺陷的局部行为。

Result: 得到了精确的零点侧公式、通用选定零点轮廓以及非选定留数的有限窗口阻尼证书。在黎曼假设和极点阻尼条件下，固定简单临界线零点附近的完全缺陷具有选定零点轮廓，非局部余项呈指数小量。

Conclusion: 该框架为黎曼ζ函数零点几何提供了一种有效的局部诊断方法，能够在特定条件下实现高精度的零点局部行为刻画。

Abstract: We introduce a Gaussian--Perron prime-force defect that compares a smoothed prime-side logarithmic force with the logarithmic derivative of the Riemann zeta function. The construction turns the explicit formula into a local diagnostic for zero geometry. Its kernel produces an error-function prime weight and an anisotropic zero-side damping law, with an explicit boundary separating amplified and suppressed nonlocal zero contributions. We prove an exact zero-side formula, derive a universal selected-zero profile on the logarithmic scale, and formulate a finite-window damping certificate for non-selected residues. Under explicit damping, pole, and contour-regularity hypotheses, these ingredients localize the full defect near a selected zero. Assuming the Riemann Hypothesis and the stated pole-damping condition, the full defect near each fixed simple critical-line zero has the selected-zero profile up to an exponentially small nonlocal remainder. The framework provides a local diagnostic for zero geometry associated with the Riemann zeta function.

</details>


### [300] [A proof of Riemann's hypothesis via Hadamard-Weierstrass factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04338)
*Athanasios Christou Micheas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用Riemann ξ函数的Hadamard-Weierstrass分解定理，讨论并证明了Riemann猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riemann猜想是数学中最著名的未解决问题之一，作者试图通过解析函数论的方法为其提供一个严格的证明。

Method: 使用Hadamard-Weierstrass分解定理对Riemann ξ函数进行因子化展开，并基于此分析其零点分布性质。

Result: 声称通过该方法讨论并证明了Riemann猜想。

Conclusion: 作者认为利用Hadamard-Weierstrass分解定理可以证明Riemann猜想成立。

Abstract: Using the Hadamard-Weierstrass factorization theorem for Riemann's ξ function, we discuss and prove Riemann's hypothesis.

</details>


### [301] [Complete Asymptotic Expansion of the Additive Mertens Sum $S_k(x)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04366)
*Daoyi Peng,Hao Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了加性Mertens和$S_k(x)$的渐近展开，证明了其主项阶为$x^{k-1}/\log^k x$，并建立了完整的渐近展开式，系数由多重对数积分给出。特别地，对$k=2$给出了系数的闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比Tenenbaum的乘性Mertens和（主项阶为$(\log \log x)^k$），加性Mertens和$S_k(x)$的主项阶完全不同，值得深入研究其渐近行为。

Method: 利用素数定理的实变量形式、变量重标度以及多元Taylor余项估计等分析工具，通过多重对数积分表达展开系数。

Result: 建立了$S_k(x)$的完整渐近展开式，系数$E_{k,n}$由完全齐次对称多项式$h_n$的多重对数积分给出；给出了前两个系数$E_{k,0}, E_{k,1}$对所有$k$的闭式，以及$E_{k,2}$的对角部分闭式（$k\leqslant4$时完全显式）；对$k=2$给出了整个序列$\{E_{2,n}\}$的闭式表达式，由$\log 2$和zeta值$\zeta(j)$的$\mathbb{Q}$-线性组合构成。

Conclusion: 加性Mertens和具有完全不同的渐近阶，且其展开系数可通过多重对数积分精确描述，其中$k=2$的情形有特别完整的闭式结果。

Abstract: Let $p_1, \dotsc, p_k$ be primes not exceeding $x$ ($k \geqslant 2$), and define the additive Mertens sum \[
  S_k(x) = \sum_{p_1 \leqslant x} \cdots \sum_{p_k \leqslant x} \frac{1}{p_1 + \dotsm + p_k}. \] In contrast to Tenenbaum's generalized (multiplicative) Mertens sum, whose leading term has order $(\log \log x)^k$, the sum $S_k(x)$ has leading term of order $x^{k-1}/\log^k x$. We establish the complete asymptotic expansion \[ S_k(x) = \frac{x^{k-1}}{\log^k x} \sum_{n=0}^{N} \frac{E_{k,n}}{\log^n x} + O\left(\frac{x^{k-1}}{\log^{k+N+1} x}\right) \quad (\forall\, N \geqslant 0), \] where the coefficients are given by absolutely convergent multiple logarithmic integrals \[ E_{k,n} = (-1)^n \int_{(0,1]^k} \frac{h_n(\log t_1, \dotsc, \log t_k)}{t_1 + \dotsm + t_k}\, \mathrm{d}\mathbf{t}, \] with $h_n$ the complete homogeneous symmetric polynomial of degree $n$. We give closed-form expressions for the first two coefficients $E_{k,0}$ and $E_{k,1}$ for all $k$, and obtain the closed form for the diagonal part of the third coefficient $E_{k,2}$ (with $E_{k,2}$ fully explicit for $k \leqslant 4$); consequently, the first three terms of the expansions of $S_2(x)$ and $S_3(x)$ are fully explicit. For $k = 2$, we further obtain a closed-form expression for the entire sequence $\{E_{2,n}\}_{n \geqslant 0}$, whose values are explicit $\mathbb{Q}$-linear combinations of $\log 2$ and zeta values $ζ(j)$. The proofs rely on the real-variable form of the prime number theorem, variable rescaling, and multivariate Taylor remainder estimates.

</details>


### [302] [Symmetric Spectral Reciprocity for $\mathrm{GL}(2)$ and Uniform Subconvexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04476)
*Liyang Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过构造GL₂上Eisenstein级数Petersson范数等式的新解析正则化，推导出twisted四次矩的显式对称谱互反公式，并利用放大方法发现的平方级现象，获得了GL₂/F上统一的亚凸界，特别地L(1/2,π) ≪ C(π)^{1/4-1/120+ε}，以及Artin L-函数和类群算术的改进界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为GL₂上twisted L-函数建立统一的亚凸界，并揭示四次矩中内在的秩分解4=2+2的谱对称性。

Method: 构造Eisenstein级数Petersson范数等式的新解析正则化；利用放大方法识别平方级现象（对偶谱族获得平方级导子），结合相对迹公式进行精细分析。

Result: 得到GL₂/F上统一的亚凸界L(1/2,π) ≪ C(π)^{1/4-1/120+ε}；改进了Artin L-函数的界及类群算术的应用。

Conclusion: 所提出的解析正则化与平方级放大方法有效改进了GL₂ L-函数的亚凸性界，并具有更广泛的数论应用。

Abstract: We construct a new analytic regularization of the Petersson norm identity for Eisenstein series on $\mathrm{GL}_2$ over a number field $F$, and derive from it an explicit symmetric spectral reciprocity formula for twisted fourth moments of $\mathrm{GL}_2$ $L$-functions, reflecting the intrinsic rank decomposition $4=2+2$.
  Independently, we identify a square-level phenomenon arising from amplification, whereby the dual spectral family acquires square-level conductors. This additional arithmetic rigidity permits a refined analysis within the relative trace formula and leads to refined hybrid subconvexity bounds for twisted $L$-functions. As a consequence, we obtain new uniform subconvexity bounds for $\mathrm{GL}_2/F$; in particular, \begin{align*} L(1/2,π)\ll C(π)^{\frac14-\frac{1}{120}+\varepsilon} \end{align*} for every unitary cuspidal representation $π$ of $\mathrm{GL}_2/F$. We also obtain refined bounds for certain Artin $L$-functions and applications to class group arithmetic.

</details>


### [303] [Supercongruences Arising from Truncated Appell Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04583)
*He-Xia Ni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用组合恒等式和Guo-Zudilin的“创意显微镜”方法，研究了截断Appell级数F₁、F₂和F₄的q-同余性质，给出了F₁和F₂已知同余的q-模拟，并建立了F₄的q-超同余及其猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Appell级数及其截断形式近年来受到广泛关注，但对其同余性质的研究尚不充分，特别是q-模拟版本的截断Appell级数的同余性质有待深入探索。

Method: 采用组合恒等式及Guo和Zudilin提出的“创意显微镜”（creative microscoping）方法，对截断Appell级数F₁、F₂和F₄进行q-模拟分析，推导其同余性质。

Result: 给出了截断Appell级数F₁和F₂若干已知同余式的q-模拟；建立了截断Appell级数F₄的一个新的q-超同余，并提出了一个相关猜想，该猜想为Apagodu和Zeilberger的两个猜想提供了q-模拟版本。

Conclusion: 本文丰富了截断Appell级数及其q-模拟的同余理论研究，为相关猜想提供了新的q-模拟视角，拓展了“创意显微镜”方法在Appell级数同余问题中的应用范围。

Abstract: In recent years, both Appell series and truncated Appell series have garnered significant research interest among scholars. In this paper, we investigate the congruence properties of truncated Appell series and truncated $q$-Appell series. By employing various combinatorial identities and the `creative microscoping' method introduced by Guo and Zudilin, we provide $q$-analogues for several known congruences of truncated Appell series $F_1$ and $F_2$. Meanwhile, we establish a new $q$-supercongruence related to the truncated Appell series $F_4$ and propose a related conjecture, which provides a $q$-analogue for two conjectures by Apagodu and Zeilberger.

</details>


### [304] [A decades-long breakthrough in zero-density estimates and primes in short intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04632)
*Caroline L. Turnage-Butterbaugh*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文介绍了2024年Guth和Maynard提出的新零密度定理，该定理在关键区域改进了Ingham的工作，是80多年来零密度定理的首次重大突破，并探讨了该结果对素数在短区间分布的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riemann假设是解析数论的核心猜想，零密度定理通过限制zeta函数在临界带内非平凡零点的数量来提供其证据。Guth和Maynard的工作旨在改进经典Ingham定理，以推动对Riemann假设的理解。

Method: 本文为一篇综述性文章，系统梳理了零密度定理的发展历史，重点阐述了Guth和Maynard的新定理如何改进Ingham定理，并分析了该结果在短区间素数分布问题上的应用。

Result: Guth和Maynard在2024年提出的新零密度定理，在关键位置显著改进了Ingham的经典结果，这是80多年来该方向上的首次实质性进展。

Conclusion: 该新零密度定理是零密度定理领域里程碑式的突破，不仅为Riemann假设提供了更强的证据，还对理解素数在短区间内的分布产生了重要影响。

Abstract: The Riemann Hypothesis (RH) asserts that every nontrivial zero of the Riemann zeta-function has real part equal to $1/2$. A zero-density theorem provides evidence towards RH by bounding the number of zeros of the zeta-function with real part greater than $1/2$. In 2024, Larry Guth and James Maynard announced a new zero-density theorem which, for a key location in the critical strip, strengthens previous work of Ingham and is the first such improvement in over 80 years. This expository paper places this remarkable achievement in the context of the rich history of zero-density theorems and explores its implications on the distribution of primes in short intervals.

</details>


### [305] [Pythagorean triples in level sets of completely multiplicative functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04903)
*Guilherme Azevedo,Joel Moreira*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于任意取值为单位圆的完全积性函数 f₁,…,fₓ，总存在毕达哥拉斯三元组 (x²+y²=z² 的整数解)，使得所有 f_i(x), f_i(y), f_i(z) 都任意接近于 1。这是"自然数的任意有限染色均存在单色毕达哥拉斯三元组"猜想的一个新特例。证明结合了非周期函数的消失平均与自命函数的集中估计。该结果还可推广到满足 Rado 条件的 ax²+by²=cz² 类方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对数论中著名的"有限染色自然数必含单色毕达哥拉斯三元组"猜想，本文在完全积性函数这一特殊设定下取得进展，为最终解决该猜想提供新路径。

Method: 将完全积性函数分为非周期部分（利用消失平均处理）与自命部分（利用集中估计控制），通过组合这两部分的技术完成证明，并推广到更一般的二次型方程。

Result: 证明了存在毕达哥拉斯三元组使得所有完全积性函数在该三元组上的取值同时任意接近 1；对形如 ax²+by²=cz²（a,b,c 为完全平方数且满足 Rado 条件）的方程也得到类似结论。

Conclusion: 该结果验证了原猜想在完全积性函数这一重要子类下的成立性，并为后续一般染色情况的证明提供了技术框架。

Abstract: We show that given completely multiplicative functions $f_1,\dots,f_d$ taking values in the unit circle, there exist Pythagorean triples (i.e., integer solutions to $x^2+y^2=z^2$) with $f_i(x),f_i(y),f_i(z)$ all arbitrarily close to $1$ for all $i$. This is a new special case of the conjecture that any finite colouring of $\mathbb{N}$ has a monochromatic Pythagorean triple. Our proof combines vanishing averages for aperiodic functions with concentration estimates for pretentious functions. A similar proof is applied to obtain the analogous statement for more general equations of the form $ax^2+by^2=cz^2$ whenever $a,b,c$ are perfect squares satisfying the Rado's condition.

</details>


### [306] [On the exterior square $\varepsilon$-factors of $GL_n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04908)
*Ravi Raghunathan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过全局方法证明了p-adic域上GL_r(F)的generic表示π的外平方ε-因子在Jacquet-Shalika积分与Langlands-Shahidi方法下是一致的，并给出了p-adic情形下局部函数方程的新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一两种不同方法（Jacquet-Shalika积分与Langlands-Shahidi方法）定义的ε-因子，确保局部Langlands对应中不变量的一致性。

Method: 采用全局化方法（global methods），并借鉴Beuzart-Plessis在相对迹公式中的技术，给出p-adic情形下局部函数方程的新证明。

Result: 证明了外平方ε-因子在两种不同定义下等价，并给出了p-adic局部函数方程的一个新证明。

Conclusion: 该结果巩固了局部Langlands对应中ε-因子的一致性基础，为后续自守表示理论的研究提供了统一的工具。

Abstract: Let $π$ be a generic representation of ${\mathrm{GL}}_r(F)$, where $F$ is a $p$-adic field. We show using global methods that the $\varepsilon$-factors associated to the exterior square of $π$ via the Jacquet-Shalika integrals and Langlands-Shahidi methods coincide. Along the way we also give a new proof of the local functional equation in the $p$-adic case following the techniques in [Beuzart-Plessis, Relative trace formulas, Simons Symp.(2021), pages 1-50].

</details>


### [307] [Truncated Multiple Zeta Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04960)
*Steven Charlton,Michael E. Hoffman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广了截断多重zeta值的定义，允许任意整数作为参数，特别是参数0带来了有趣的恒等式。截断多重zeta值满足与多重zeta值相同的拟混洗代数恒等式，但需要将拟对称函数代数QSym扩展为更大的代数。利用这一代数，可以系统地对调和数和广义调和数的幂进行求和，得到新的求和恒等式，并证明涉及交错调和数及其幂的类似恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重zeta值（MZVs）的截断版本在数论和组合学中具有重要意义。本文旨在推广截断多重zeta值的定义，使其接受任意整数参数（包括0），从而探索新的代数结构和求和恒等式。

Method: 将截断多重zeta值的参数从正整数推广到任意整数；构造一个比拟对称函数代数QSym更大的代数以容纳新的代数结构；利用该代数系统性地对调和数及其广义形式的幂进行求和。

Result: 得到了新的求和恒等式，例如包含调和数三次方、zeta(2)与截断部分组合的无穷级数恒等式，以及涉及交错调和数及其幂的类似恒等式。

Conclusion: 截断多重zeta值的定义可自然地推广到任意整数参数，并在此框架下获得调和数幂求和的系统方法，丰富了多重zeta值理论及其与调和数恒等式的联系。

Abstract: We generalize the definition of truncated multiple zeta values by allowing arbitrary integers as arguments. This leads to interesting identities, particularly with the argument 0. Truncated multiple zeta values satisfy the same quasi-shuffle algebraic identities as multiple zeta values, but we need to extend the algebra QSym of quasi-symmetric functions to a larger algebra. Using this algebra, we are able to sum systematically powers of harmonic and generalized harmonic numbers. This leads to summation identities such as \[ \sum_{n=1}^\infty H_n^3\bigg(ζ(2)-\sum_{k=1}^n\frac{1}{k^2}-\frac{1}{n}\bigg)= -\frac{11}2ζ(4)+ζ(3)+3ζ(2)-6. \] We also prove analogous identities involving alternating sums of harmonic numbers and their powers.

</details>


### [308] [Class-Number-One Cubic Fricke Companions on $Γ_0(2)^+$ and an Exact Chudnovsky Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05028)
*Vedran Menđušić*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Γ₀(2)⁺上RS级数的结构分类-桥接定理。在h(D)=1的基本Heegner判别式中，不可约三次Fricke-尖点伴恰好出现在D=-11,-19,-43,-67,-163。证明给出了z=1728/j与X=256t/(t+64)²之间的显式桥接、超几何规范恒等式以及纤维的CM解释。对于D=-163，三次Fricke RS级数是Chudnovsky线性形式的精确二级输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Γ₀(2)⁺上RS级数的结构分类与桥接关系，特别是刻画不可约三次Fricke-尖点伴出现的条件，并揭示其与经典模函数、超几何函数及Chudnovsky公式的深层联系。

Method: 通过结构分类-桥接定理，建立z=1728/j与X=256t/(t+64)²之间的显式桥接；利用超几何规范恒等式进行推导；结合CM理论解释纤维结构。

Result: 确定了在h(D)=1的基本Heegner判别式中，不可约三次Fricke-尖点伴恰好出现在D=-11,-19,-43,-67,-163这五个判别式。对于D=-163，三次Fricke RS级数是Chudnovsky线性形式的精确二级输运。

Conclusion: 本文为Γ₀(2)⁺上RS级数建立了完整的结构分类理论，揭示了与经典模形式、超几何函数及Chudnovsky公式之间的深刻联系，为后续研究提供了理论基础和显式构造。

Abstract: A structural classification-and-bridge result is proved for RS series on \(Γ_0(2)^+\). Among the fundamental Heegner discriminants with \(h(D)=1\), the irreducibly cubic Fricke-cusp companions occur exactly for \[ D=-11,-19,-43,-67,-163. \] The proof gives an explicit bridge between \(z=1728/j\) and \(X=256t/(t+64)^2\), a hypergeometric gauge identity, and a CM interpretation of the fibre. For \(D=-163\), the cubic Fricke RS series is the exact level-two transport of the Chudnovsky linear form.

</details>


### [309] [Exceptional rational functions of degree 5 over finite fields: classification by monodromy, ramification, and the Riemann-Hurwitz formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05075)
*Zhichao Tang,Xiang Fan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文完成了特征不为2和5的有限域上五次例外有理函数（除Möbius等价外）的分类，并部分完成了特征2和5下的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完成有限域上五次例外有理函数的完全分类，填补该领域在次数5上的空白。

Method: 利用算术与几何单演群、分支覆盖的歧变结构以及Riemann-Hurwitz公式进行分析；循环单演情形得到单项式和Rédei函数，二面体情形通过惯性群和分支点分析得到Dickson多项式及来自椭圆曲线5-同源的有理函数族。

Result: 特征不为2和5的有限域上，五次例外有理函数在Möbius等价下被完全分类为：单项式、Rédei函数、Dickson多项式、以及来自椭圆曲线有理5-同源的非多项式族。特征2和5下，循环几何单演情形和具有F_q-有理分支点的二面体情形已分类，仅剩几何单演群为D_5且无F_q-有理分支点的情形未解决。

Conclusion: 给出了五次例外有理函数的完整分类，并指出了特征2和5下尚待解决的部分。

Abstract: We complete the classification of exceptional rational functions of degree 5 over any finite field $\mathbb{F}_{q}$ of characteristic different from 2 and 5, up to Möbius equivalence. Our approach employs the arithmetic and geometric monodromy groups, combined with the ramification structure of the associated cover and the Riemann-Hurwitz formula. The cyclic monodromy case yields precisely monomials and Rédei functions, while the dihedral case is resolved by analysing its inertia groups and branch points; this leads to Dickson polynomials and a non-polynomial family arising from rational 5-isogenies of elliptic curves. We also obtain partial classifications in characteristics 2 and 5: all cases with cyclic geometric monodromy and all dihedral cases admitting an $\mathbb{F}_{q}$-rational branch point are determined. The only unresolved cases are those with geometric monodromy group $D_{5}$ and no $\mathbb{F}_{q}$-rational branch point.

</details>


### [310] [Thue equations that simultaneously fail the Hasse principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05234)
*Paloma Bengoechea*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了Akhtari和Bhargava之前的构造，使得对于每个正整数m，都能得到正比例的Thue方程F(x,y)=h，这些方程对每个小于m的正整数h同时违反积分Hasse原理。所用的二元形式F具有固定次数>2，并按系数最大绝对值的顺序排列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Thue方程在局部-全局原理（Hasse原理）方面的失效现象，特别是积分Hasse原理的失败情况，并希望能构造出大量同时违反多个模数的积分Hasse原理的Thue方程。

Method: 改进了Akhtari和Bhargava的构造方法，通过特定的二元形式F（固定次数>2，按系数最大绝对值的顺序排序）来构造Thue方程，使得对于任意给定的正整数m，可以证明正比例的此类方程同时对所有h<m的整数值违反积分Hasse原理。

Result: 成功构造出正比例的Thue方程，这些方程对于每个小于给定正整数m的h，都同时违反积分Hasse原理。

Conclusion: 本文表明积分Hasse原理的失效在Thue方程中是非常普遍的现象，并且可以同时针对多个不同的右端项发生，拓展了此前Akhtari和Bhargava的结果。

Abstract: We refine a previous construction by Akhtari and Bhargava so that, for every positive integer m, we obtain a positive proportion of Thue equations F(x; y) = h that fail the integral Hasse principle simultaneously for every positive integer h less than m. The binary forms F have fixed degree >2 and are ordered by the absolute value of the maximum of the coefficients.

</details>


### [311] [Sharp Circular Sampling and Derivative Period Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05262)
*Seokho Jin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了迫使平衡整函数中心二项式样本的所有零点位于单位圆上的精确最大反射零区域，并将该结果应用于证明原始全纯新形式完全L函数的导数-周期-多项式单位圆定理，推广了Diamantis和Rolen的全多项式单位圆猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找迫使平衡整函数中心二项式样本所有零点位于单位圆上的精确最大反射零区域，并应用于数论中L函数相关多项式的零点分布问题。

Method: 通过有限次情形下反射零点对的尖锐分析、保相典范积逼近、De Bruijn带收缩和射影Hermite–Kakeya–Obreschkoff理论，得到精确的公共零点障碍、简单性、相邻导数样本严格交错性以及单调实铅笔根流。

Result: 确定了精确的最大反射零区域Ω_d；证明了原始全纯新形式完全L函数的导数-周期-多项式在所有导数阶、水平和nebentypus下所有零点位于单位圆上；证明了零点简单性、严格交错性和定量局部化性质。

Conclusion: 推广并证明了Diamantis和Rolen的全多项式单位圆猜想，将其扩展至任意水平和nebentypus，并建立了更广泛的整函数零点分布理论框架。

Abstract: We determine the exact maximal reflected zero region that forces centered binomial samples of a balanced entire function to have all zeros on the unit circle. In degree $d\ge2$, this region is \[
  Ω_d=\left\{a+ib:\ a^2-\frac{b^2}{d-1}\le\frac d4\right\}. \] The finite theorem is sharp already for a single reflected zero pair, and a phase-preserving canonical-product approximation extends it to balanced entire functions of order at most one. De Bruijn strip contraction and projective Hermite--Kakeya--Obreschkoff theory then give the exact common-zero obstruction, simplicity, strict interlacing of consecutive derivative samples, and a monotone real-pencil root flow.
  As an application, we prove the derivative-period-polynomial unit-circle theorem for completed $L$-functions of primitive holomorphic newforms, in every derivative order and for arbitrary level and nebentypus. After the standard normalization, every zero of \[
  \sum_{j=0}^{k-2}\binom{k-2}{j}Λ^{(m)}(f,j+1)z^j \] lies on the unit circle for every weight $k\ge4$, level, nebentypus, and derivative order $m\ge0$. In particular, this proves the full-polynomial unit-circle conjecture of Diamantis and Rolen in its original level-one setting and extends it to arbitrary level and nebentypus. The same source-side theorem also gives simplicity, strict interlacing, and, for each fixed derivative order, conductor-uniform quantitative localization in the weight aspect.

</details>


### [312] [Calculating the natural density of Mersenne numbers using nonstandard mathematical analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05301)
*Gennady Eremin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文使用非标准分析方法，通过Pepis-Kalmar配对函数构建二维整数矩阵，证明了梅森数的自然密度（无穷小量e）与奇数次倒数之和（无穷大量ω）满足e = 1/ω的关系，即两者在非标准分析意义下等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数论中一个未解决的开放问题是梅森数在自然数集中的自然密度未知，本文旨在探索这一未解问题。

Method: 采用非标准数学分析方法，利用Pepis-Kalmar配对函数构建非负整数的二维矩阵，将奇数与偶数分离，从而推导出梅森数密度与奇数次倒数之和的关系。

Result: 得到梅森数的自然密度（无穷小量e）与奇数次倒数之和（无穷大量ω）满足e × ω = 1，即e = 1/ω的等式关系。

Conclusion: 在非标准分析框架下，梅森数的自然密度与奇数次倒数之和的倒数在无穷小意义上等价，为理解梅森数分布提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Currently, among the open (unsolved) problems in number theory is the following: it is unknown what is the natural density of the sequence of Mersenne numbers in the set of natural numbers. In the paper, using methods of nonstandard mathematical analysis, we obtain the following equation: the natural density of Mersenne numbers (some infinitesimal value $e$) multiplied by the sum of the reciprocals of odd numbers (the infinitely large value ω = 1 + 1/3 + 1/5 + ...) is equal to 1, or the equality $e$ = 1/ω is true. In nonstandard analysis, the resulting infinitesimal numbers $e$ and 1/ω are considered equivalent. We obtained this result by working with a two-dimensional matrix of non-negative integers, where odd numbers are separated from even ones by the Pepis-Kalmar pairing function.

</details>


### [313] [Iwasawa invariants of sharp/flat $2$-adic $L$-functions for quadratic twists of elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05305)
*Taiga Adachi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究椭圆曲线在2处具有好超奇异约化时，其Sprung尖/平2-adic L-函数在二次扭变下的解析Iwasawa不变量。在μ-不变量为零的假设下，得到了尖/平λ-不变量的显式差分公式，这是好普通情形下Matsuno公式的超奇异类比。应用Hatley-Ray方法，得到了具有指定尖/平2-adic Iwasawa λ-不变量的二次扭变数量的渐近下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将好普通情形下Matsuno关于Iwasawa λ-不变量在二次扭变下的变化公式推广到2-adic超奇异情形，进而获得二次扭变中具有指定λ-不变量的计数下界。

Method: 在μ-不变量为零的假设下，推导尖/平λ-不变量的显式差分公式；并运用Hatley-Ray的方法获得渐近下界。

Result: 得到了超奇异情形下尖/平λ-不变量的差分公式，以及具有指定λ-不变量的二次扭变数量的渐近下界。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了超奇异情形下Iwasawa不变量在二次扭变下的变化规律，为相关计数问题提供了下界估计。

Abstract: The aim of this paper is to study the variation under quadratic twists of the analytic Iwasawa invariants of Sprung's sharp/flat 2-adic $L$-functions for elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}$ with good supersingular reduction at $2$. Under the hypothesis that the $μ$-invariant vanishes, we obtain an explicit difference formula for the sharp/flat $λ$-invariants. This formula gives a supersingular analogue of Matsuno's formula in the good ordinary case. As an application, following the method of Hatley-Ray, we obtain asymptotic lower bounds for the number of quadratic twists with prescribed sharp/flat $2$-adic Iwasawa $λ$-invariant.

</details>


### [314] [An improved lower bound for odd integers not of the form $p+2^a+2^b$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05357)
*Yuchen Ding,Yu-Chen Sun,Lilu Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究不能表示为奇素数加两个2的幂次之和的奇数个数N(x)，改进了Pan的结果，证明了对于任意η>0，N(x) ≫_η x exp(-(4+η)(log log log x / log log x) log x)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős反复追问是否存在绝对常数c₀使得N(x) > c₀ x，本研究致力于改进Pan的结果，向解决Erdős问题迈进一步。

Method: 通过改进筛法或指数和估计等技术，将Pan结果中的指数项从C₀·log log log log x / log log log x改进为(4+η)·log log log x / log log x。

Result: 证明了对于任意η>0及充分大的x，N(x) ≫_η x exp(-(4+η)(log log log x / log log x) log x)，显著优于Pan的界。

Conclusion: 该结果改进了Pan关于不能表示为奇素数加两个2的幂次之和的奇数个数的下界，但仍未解决Erdős提出的线性下界问题。

Abstract: Let $x$ be sufficiently large and \[ N(x)=\big|\bigl\{n\le x:n\ \text{is odd and }n\ne p+2^a+2^b \textrm{ with } p \text{ a prime and } a,b\in \mathbb{N}\bigr\}\big|. \] Motivated by Crocker's result \[ N(x)\gg \log\log x, \] Erd\H os repeatedly asked whether there is an absolute constant $c_0$ such that $N(x)>c_0x$. Pan \cite{Pan} proved in 2011 that \[ N(x)\gg x\exp\!\left(
  -C_0\frac{\log\log\log\log x}{\log\log\log x}\log x \right), \] where $C_0>0$ is an absolute constant. We improve on Pan's result by showing that, given any $η>0$, for all sufficiently large $x$, \[
  N(x)\gg_ηx\exp\left(-(4+η)\frac{\log\log\log x}{\log\log x}\log x\right). \]

</details>
